### [1] [AcFlow: Controlling Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers via Learned Conditional Activation Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10723)
*Junran Wang,Zehao Jin,Tianyu Luan,Xinjie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AcFlow 是一种推理时的控制器，通过在冻结的 DiT 中将中间层图像 token 激活沿概念条件速度场进行传输，实现对风格强度与概念抑制的连续控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散 Transformer（DiT）虽然生成能力强，但直接提示对风格强度的控制有限，且难以抑制不期望的概念。作者希望在不重新训练基座模型的前提下，引入可控且可泛化的控制接口。

Method: 提出 AcFlow：在推理阶段保持基座 DiT 冻结，利用学习到的、以概念为条件的速度场，将中间层图像 token 激活进行传输。文本概念描述指定干预目标，积分时长作为连续控制参数；速度场产生随 token 变化且依赖激活状态的更新。同一任务族内概念共享参数，从而支持细致描述并泛化到训练未见概念，无需逐概念拟合。

Result: 在风格控制上，AcFlow 在高风格对齐区间取得评估基线中最佳的风格—内容权衡。固定操作点下，风格/内容对齐为 0.5365/0.2860，优于风格对齐最高基线的 0.4397/0.2684。定性结果显示可抑制多种概念，包括直接提示失败的情形。分析表明学习到的速度场是一种自适应控制机制，更新方向随 token 变化并依赖其激活状态。

Conclusion: AcFlow 提供了一种高效、可泛化的推理时控制方法，通过概念条件速度场实现细粒度风格调节与概念抑制，且无需微调基座 DiT，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion transformers (DiTs) are powerful generators, yet direct prompting provides limited control interface for style intensity and can fail to suppress unwanted concepts. To enable these controls, we introduce AcFlow, an inference-time controller that transports intermediate layer image-token activations through a learned concept-conditioned velocity field while keeping the base DiT frozen. A textual concept description specifies the desired intervention, while the integration horizon provides a continuous control parameter. The field produces token-varying, activation-dependent updates. With parameters shared across concepts within each task family, the field supports fine-grained descriptions and generalizes to concepts unseen during training without per-concept fitting. On style control, AcFlow achieves the best style--content trade-off among the evaluated baselines in the high-style-alignment regime. At a fixed operating point, AcFlow attains style--content alignment of 0.5365/0.2860, compared with 0.4397/0.2684 for the baseline with the highest style alignment. Qualitative results demonstrate suppression of diverse concepts, including cases where direct prompting fails. Our analyses support the learned velocity field as an adaptive control mechanism, with update directions varying across tokens and depend on their activation states. Our code is available at https://github.com/Nove1yst/AcFlow.

</details>


### [2] [MHE-Former: Multi-Hypothesis Transformers via Entropy Maximization for 3D Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10743)
*Boshu Jia,Rongyu Chen,Linlin Yang,Zihao Liu,Yingjie Chen,Zhongqun Zhang,Zhulin Tao,Shaohui Lin,Xiaoyu Wu,Libiao Jin,Baochang Zhang,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"探索-利用"范式的多假设学习与选择框架，用于单目3D手部和身体网格恢复，以缓解遮挡与歧义问题。探索阶段提出基于Transformer的多假设方法MHE-Former，利用概率建模与熵最大化生成多样且合理的假设；利用阶段提出上下文感知的假设选择机制，借助VLM的视觉理解与推理能力，支持用户结合额外证据和自然语言意图挑选最优估计。实验表明该框架在准确性与多样性上达到SOTA，用户偏好研究验证了其假设选择流程的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D手部与身体网格恢复面临严重遮挡与歧义，传统确定性方法只回归单一最优解，导致预测过度自信，无法表达多解可能性。因此需要一种能生成并利用多个合理假设的方法来应对歧义。

Method: 提出"探索-利用"范式：探索阶段在概率建模与熵最大化基础上设计了MHE-Former，一种基于Transformer的多假设框架，兼顾训练效率与标签友好性，生成合理且多样的假设；利用阶段提出假设选择（Hypothesis Selection），对多个预测进行上下文感知处理，特别借助VLM强大的视觉理解与推理能力，使用户可结合额外证据和自然语言意图选择最合理、最符合需求的估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在多个数据集上的准确性与多样性均达到SOTA性能；用户偏好研究进一步证明了假设选择流程的实用性。

Conclusion: 多假设学习结合上下文感知的假设选择，可有效缓解单目网格恢复中的歧义与过度自信问题，并在精度、多样性及实用性方面取得优异表现。

Abstract: Monocular 3D hand and body mesh recovery often suffers from severe occlusion and ambiguity. Traditional deterministic methods typically regress a single optimal solution, leading to overconfident predictions. In this paper, we introduce an exploration--exploitation paradigm for ambiguous mesh recovery with multi-hypothesis learning and selection. Specifically, during exploration, based on our probabilistic formulation and entropy maximization, we propose a novel multi-hypothesis method referred to as MHE-Former. It is a Transformer-based multi-hypothesis framework, ensuring high training efficiency and label friendliness while generating plausible and diverse hypotheses. During exploitation, we propose Hypothesis Selection, a context-aware process for multiple predictions. Especially leveraging VLM's powerful visual understanding and reasoning capabilities, it allows users to choose the most plausible and desired estimate with additional evidence and natural language intent. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance in accuracy and diversity across multiple datasets. The user preference study further shows the practicality of our hypothesis selection process.

</details>


### [3] [GRADE: Single-Frame Generative Radar Depth Estimation Under Visual Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10756)
*Bin Zhao,Patrick Chiou,Nakul Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRADE将预训练生成先验与单帧毫米波雷达几何相结合，在烟雾、雾、黑暗等光学退化场景下估计高保真度量深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集3D深度感知在烟雾、雾和黑暗条件下失效，因为光学传感器无法穿透空气中的颗粒物。毫米波雷达在这些条件下仍可使用且测距准确，但其小孔径限制了角分辨率。因此需要在保持雷达鲁棒性的同时恢复高保真、度量准确的稠密深度。

Method: GRADE先将原始4D雷达频谱映射为粗略度量深度；然后以潜在扩散骨干恢复结构细节，并在每一步去噪中对该深度估计进行条件化；此外引入像素空间适配器，在相机线索可用时利用残差信息，并在清晰、烟雾退化和遮挡输入上训练，使可见度下降时整体输出趋近于雷达条件路径。

Result: 在12栋建筑、约95K帧含真实烟雾的数据上训练和评估，GRADE在清晰场景MAE为0.303 m，烟雾下为0.313 m，优于现有基线。

Conclusion: GRADE证明以单帧雷达几何为条件、结合预训练生成先验，可以在严重光学退化条件下实现高保真度量深度估计，并保持跨可见度的鲁棒性。代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Dense 3D depth perception fails under smoke, fog, and darkness because optical sensors cannot penetrate airborne particulates. mmWave radar remains usable and measures range accurately under these conditions, but its small aperture limits angular resolution. We present GRADE, which grounds a pretrained generative prior in single-frame radar geometry to estimate high-fidelity metric depth. GRADE first maps raw 4D radar spectra to coarse metric depth. A latent diffusion backbone then recovers structural detail while conditioning every denoising step on this estimate. A pixel-space adapter uses residual camera cues when available and is trained across clear, smoke-degraded, and occluded inputs so the full output approaches the radar-conditioned path as visibility degrades. Trained and evaluated on ~95K frames across 12 buildings with real smoke, GRADE achieves an MAE of 0.303 m in clear scenes and 0.313 m under smoke, outperforming existing baselines. Code and datasets are available at https://phi-lab-rice.github.io/GRADE.

</details>


### [4] [How Much Velocity Does Off-Ball Space Value Need? A Broadcast-Viewport Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10801)
*Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探究"速度信息"在广播视角无球分析中究竟在哪一层起作用：结论是速度对插补几乎无用、对控制面是一阶重要、对球队判决影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播视角下约半数球员位于屏幕外，且屏幕内速度来自漂移的标定；作者希望明确速度信息在离屏插补、控制面估计、球队判决三个层次中各自的价值，以指导分析流程的资源分配。

Method: 沿用离屏插补协议（3场Metrica比赛、44 m视野、块自助法置信区间），设置四种速度机制（无、视口内合法观测、真值仅给可见者、真值给全员），在插补、控制面、球队判决三层分别与速度感知的真值对比；另做噪声梯度（11档抖动、含σ_v匹配对）与11段SoccerNet-GSR片段验证。

Result: 速度对插补仅-0.2 pp（无速度面MAE为12–14 pp）；对控制面为一阶重要（-1.5至-1.8 pp，占该MAE的11–15%）；对判决仅-0.12至-0.19 pp，小十倍。真正重要的是可见通道：完美遮挡球员速度仅贡献可见增益的2–6%。省略速度使控制面模糊（逐帧|e| 2.2–2.6 pp）但时均偏差小（每格≤0.4 pp）；插补误差则对防守方深区形成结构化偏差（5–9 pp）。固定速度窗口下，噪声梯度按σ_v一阶排序，盈亏平衡约1 m/s；实测σ_v=1.65 m/s仍挽回24–36%的收益，其中43%方差为帧间公共成分（控制面可容忍），残差重尾且聚集，高斯对照无法复现（+0.03 vs +0.36）。速度可去除的无速度误差份额随视野增宽而上升（36 m为7%，60 m为21%）。

Conclusion: 应"窄镜头修插补、宽镜头重速度"：速度的价值高度分层且集中在控制面与可见通道，判决层几乎不敏感；噪声影响可由σ_v单轴一阶刻画，但残差结构需非高斯处理。代码与日志已开源。

Abstract: Velocity-aware pitch control is standard, but under a broadcast viewport half the players are off screen and on-screen velocities come from a drifting calibration. We ask at which layer of broadcast off-ball analysis velocity changes the answer. Inheriting our off-screen imputation protocol (three Metrica matches, 44 m viewport, block-bootstrap CIs), we score four velocity regimes -- none, viewport-legal observed, true-for-visible, true-for-all -- against a velocity-aware ground truth at three layers: imputation, the control surface, and team verdicts. Velocity is nearly useless for imputation (-0.2 pp against a 12--14 pp velocity-free surface MAE), first-order for the surface (-1.5 to -1.8 pp, 11--15% of that MAE), and ten times smaller for verdicts (-0.12 to -0.19 pp). The velocity that matters is the visible channel: perfect occluded-player velocity adds 2--6% of the visible gain, and no last-seen decay policy we tested exceeds that. Omitting velocity blurs the surface (per-frame |e| 2.2--2.6 pp) with small time-averaged bias (per cell <=0.4 pp), whereas imputation error is a structured bias against the defending team's deep zone (5--9 pp). At a fixed velocity window, a noise ladder of eleven jitter settings, including sigma_v-matched pairs, is ordered to first order by one velocity-noise axis sigma_v with break-even ~1 m/s; eleven SoccerNet-GSR clips from one match through our pipeline measure sigma_v=1.65 m/s yet recover 24--36% of the benefit: 43% of the variance is frame-common, which the surface tolerates, and the residual is heavy-tailed and clustered, which Gaussian controls matched on component RMS do not reproduce (+0.03 vs. +0.36). The share of velocity-free error that velocity removes grows with viewport width (7% at 36 m, 21% at 60 m): fix imputation on tight shots, velocity on wide ones. Code and logs are released.

</details>


### [5] [TrajFusionNet+: Transformer-Based Prediction of Pedestrian Crossing Intention via Fusion of Trajectory Representations and Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10806)
*François G. Landry,Moulay A. Akhloufi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrajFusionNet+，一种基于Transformer的行人过街意图预测模型，融合轨迹序列、视觉与图结构三种表征，在PIE和JAAD数据集上取得最优性能，并提出联合训练评估协议验证泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人过街意图预测是自动驾驶的关键任务，需要从自车视角判断行人是否可能横穿马路。单一模态（如轨迹序列或视觉信息）难以充分捕捉行人行为与场景上下文之间的复杂关系，因此需要多模态融合方法来提升预测准确性与泛化能力。

Method: TrajFusionNet+在前期TrajFusionNet基础上扩展，采用Transformer架构，包含三个分支：序列注意力模块（SAM）处理过去与预测轨迹的序列表征；视觉注意力模块（VAM）将观测与预测的边界框叠加到场景图像上，利用视觉表征；图注意力模块（GAM）从分割场景图像中提取以行人为中心的图，捕捉行人同交通元素间的依赖关系。

Result: 在PIE和JAAD两个主流行人过街意图数据集上均取得改进的SOTA性能。此外，在提出的联合训练（PIE+JAAD）、分别评估的新协议下，TrajFusionNet+相较现有方法展现出更优的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 融合序列、视觉与图结构三种表征的Transformer架构能有效提升行人过街意图预测性能，并在跨数据集联合训练设定下表现出更强的泛化性，为自动驾驶行人行为预测提供了有力方案。

Abstract: The pedestrian crossing intention task involves predicting whether pedestrians are likely to cross the road from the point of view of an autonomous vehicle. We introduce TrajFusionNet+, a novel transformer-based model for pedestrian crossing intention prediction. TrajFusionNet+ combines sequential and visual representations of pedestrian trajectory with a graph-based representation of the scene context in order to predict pedestrian crossing intention. The proposed architecture builds upon our previous model, TrajFusionNet, and comprises three branches: a Sequence Attention Module (SAM), which processes a sequential representation of past and predicted pedestrian trajectories; a Visual Attention Module (VAM), which utilizes a visual representation of the pedestrian trajectories by overlaying observed and predicted bounding boxes onto scene images; and a Graph Attention Module (GAM), which extracts pedestrian-centric graphs from segmented scene images and captures the relational dependencies between pedestrians and traffic elements. TrajFusionNet+ achieves improved state-of-the-art performance on the two most widely used pedestrian crossing intention datasets, PIE and JAAD. Furthermore, we introduce a new evaluation protocol in which models are trained jointly on the PIE and JAAD datasets but evaluated separately on each. Under this setting, TrajFusionNet+ demonstrates superior generalization compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [6] [Overpainting: Localized Context-aware Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10811)
*Sam Sartor,Iliyan Georgiev,Michael Fischer,Valentin Deschaintre,Pieter Peers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"overpainting"图像编辑操作：以trimap控制编辑区域（白=必改、灰=可改、黑=不可改），通过联合注意力与低秩适配改造预训练扩散模型，并设计自动训练数据生成流水线，支持多样编辑任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法难以同时兼顾编辑位置的可控性与对原始内容的感知；用户既需要精确指定编辑区域，也需要灵活松散的编辑控制。

Method: 提出overpainting操作，用trimap（白/灰/黑）界定必改、可改、不可改像素；在预训练图像编辑扩散模型上结合joint attention与low-rank adaptation，并跨输入图像施加attention-dropout以平衡噪声、源图像与掩码信息流；构建自动训练数据生成流水线：生成候选图像对、筛选、提取trimap。

Result: 在广泛的编辑任务上验证了overpainting模型的多功能性，兼具精确与松散的编辑控制能力。

Conclusion: overpainting通过trimap与扩散模型适配实现了可控且内容感知的图像编辑，自动数据生成流程支撑了模型训练，方法具备通用性。

Abstract: We present "overpainting", an image editing operation which offers both control over the location of the edit and awareness of the previous content in that location. The overpainted area is given by a trimap, where white-annotated pixels must be edited, gray-annotated pixels may be edited, and black-annotated pixels must not be edited. This enables both precise and loose control, depending on user intent.
  We implement overpainting by adapting a pretrained image editing diffusion model using a combination of joint attention and low-rank adaption across input images with attention-dropout to balance the information flow between noise, source and mask images. We present a novel, automated, training data generation pipeline that (1) generates a set of candidate image pairs leveraging existing language-based editing models, (2) carefully curates those pairs, and (3) extracts a trimap from each usable pair. We demonstrate the versatility of our overpainting model on a wide range of editing tasks.

</details>


### [7] [BodyCam-VQA: Enhanced Body-Worn Camera Video Captioning via Multimodal Reasoning and Probe Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10815)
*Karish Gupta,Matthew Alex,Alex Li,Yang Wu,Yun-Wei Chu,Kashif Munir,Xiaotian Zhou,Zhengping Ji,Xiaozhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向执法场景的自适应VQA框架，通过结构化推理提取细粒度视觉证据，实验比较多种问题生成模型，证明该架构能提供更可靠、客观、详细的执法事件记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 警用随身摄像头（BWC）视频对法律透明、警员问责和公民权利保护至关重要，但其多模态特性导致处理困难：画面混乱、视觉质量低、动作交互快速、音频噪声大，即使SOTA多模态模型也难以理解。现有视觉语言模型常忽略关键取证细节（如证据存在、嫌疑人与警员互动的潜在细微差别），而这些对公正裁决和人员安全至关重要。

Method: 提出一个面向高风险执法场景的自适应视觉问答（VQA）框架，采用结构化推理方式提取传统描述系统无法捕捉的细粒度视觉证据；并实验多种问题生成模型，包括基础模型和微调的开源权重模型，以观察不同问题生成实现带来的性能差异。

Result: 实验表明，不同问题生成模型实现之间存在性能差异；所提出的VQA驱动架构能够提供更可靠、客观且详细的执法事件记录。

Conclusion: 该VQA框架可作为AI辅助取证工具，提升执法记录的清晰度与客观性，从而保护执法人员和公众双方利益。

Abstract: Police body-worn camera (BWC) footage has emerged as a critical aspect of law enforcement that ensures legal transparency, officer accountability, and the protection of civil rights. However, effectively processing this data remains a significant challenge due to its multimodal video format. BWC videos, in many cases, comprise chaotic scenes with low visual quality, rapid movement/interactions, and high-noise audio that make visual understanding a challenge for even SOTA multimodal models. Current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently overlook critical forensic details, such as the presence of valuable evidence or the latent nuances of suspect-officer interactions, which are vital for fair legal outcomes and civilian/officer safety. To address these limitations, we propose an Adaptive Visual Question Answering (VQA) framework engineered for high-stakes law enforcement. Our framework employs a structured reasoning approach to extract fine-grained visual evidence that traditional captioning systems fail to capture. We experiment with multiple question generation models, including foundation models and fine-tuned open-weight models, to observe performance variation among question generation model implementations. Our results demonstrate that this VQA-driven architecture provides a more reliable, objective, and detailed record of enforcement events, ultimately serving as a powerful tool to protect both law enforcement officers and the public through AI-assisted forensic clarity.

</details>


### [8] [Are We Really Doing Few-Shot Learning? A Critical Examination of Pre-Training Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10851)
*Alejandro Galan-Cuenca,Marcelo Saval-Calvo,Antonio Javier Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文质疑少样本学习中"类不重叠的域内预训练"协议能否真实反映低数据学习，系统比较了无预训练、类不重叠域内预训练、有监督跨域预训练与无标签跨域预训练三种设定，发现域重叠带来约9.66个百分点的乐观偏差，并提出基于描述符的源域选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本学习通常在大规模辅助集上预训练，该辅助集与目标episode类别不重叠但同域。作者质疑这种协议是否真正体现低数据学习，因为类不重叠并不等于消除了目标域数据的影响，而现实中目标域数据往往稀缺，域内预训练会高估性能。

Method: 在八个数据集、三种少样本架构、多种way-shot设置下，系统比较四类协议：无预训练、类不重叠域内预训练、有监督跨域预训练、无标签跨域预训练；并提出基于增强的无标签预训练策略，以及基于描述符的源域选择策略（在预训练前估计源域适配度）。

Result: 域内预训练较无预训练平均提升33.41个百分点，有监督跨域预训练提升23.75个百分点，二者相差9.66个百分点，即域重叠带来的乐观偏差；无标签增强策略平均提升27.71个百分点，接近有监督跨域预训练（27.97个百分点）；基于描述符的源域选择与oracle选择的中位差距仅1.37个百分点；跨域预训练效果高度依赖源域与目标域的兼容性。

Conclusion: 仅靠类不重叠不足以消除目标域数据影响，不应再将域内预训练作为少样本学习的默认评测协议，因为它会在目标域数据稀缺的现实场景中高估性能；应转向更真实的跨域预训练设定，并可通过源域选择策略在预训练前预估源域适用性。

Abstract: Few-shot learning is commonly evaluated under protocols that pre-train a model on a large auxiliary set whose classes are disjoint from the target episodes yet drawn from the same visual domain. This paper examines whether such protocols truly reflect low-data learning. We systematically compare no pre-training, class-disjoint in-domain pre-training, supervised out-of-domain pre-training, and label-free out-of-domain pre-training across eight datasets, three few-shot architectures, and multiple way-shot settings. Our results show that class disjointness alone is insufficient to remove the influence of target-domain data. In-domain pre-training improves over no pre-training by 33.41 percentage points on average, whereas supervised out-of-domain pre-training yields 23.75 percentage points, revealing a 9.66-point optimistic bias associated with domain overlap. Although out-of-domain pre-training is more realistic in applications where target-domain data are scarce, its effectiveness depends strongly on the compatibility between source and target domains. We further show that labeled source data are not strictly required, with an augmentation-based label-free strategy reaching an average gain of 27.71 percentage points and closely matching supervised out-of-domain pre-training at 27.97 percentage points. Finally, we introduce a descriptor-based source-selection strategy that estimates source-domain suitability before pre-training, reaching a median gap of only 1.37 percentage points to oracle selection. These findings highlight the need to move beyond in-domain pre-training as the default few-shot evaluation protocol, since it can overestimate performance in realistic scenarios where target-domain data are scarce.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluation of Vision-Language Models Across Diverse Coastal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10855)
*Seth Knoop,Chad R. Samuelson,Gabriel R. Slade,Brady Moon,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个含1000余张图像、18个语义类别、7400余个标注实例的密集标注海岸数据集（夏威夷Oahu三区域七次任务），通过文本到掩码、掩码到掩码、掩码到文本三类实验评测七个现代视觉语言模型。结果显示：景观大类识别最佳，海岸概念最具挑战，但同类常规类别在海岸与陆地数据集间无一致差异；掩码到掩码匹配表现相近，替换文本标签可显著提升部分海岸概念识别，表明海岸类别性能偏低主要源于分割与语言表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型通过将视觉观测与自然语言概念关联，支撑机器人感知，但其在海岸环境中的表现尚未得到充分探索；该研究旨在填补这一空白并量化海岸场景下的模型能力边界。

Method: 构建密集标注的海岸数据集（1000余张图像、3个Oahu区域、7次任务、18个语义类别、7400余个实例标注）；选取7个现代视觉语言模型；设计三类互补实验，分别度量文本到掩码、掩码到掩码与掩码到文本的对齐；并对比海岸与陆地上共享常规类别，以及尝试替代文本标签。

Result: 广域景观类别的识别准确率普遍高于常规物体类与海岸类，海岸概念最具挑战性；但共享常规类别在海岸与陆地数据集间未表现出一致的性能差异，说明仅环境背景并不导致性能下降。掩码到掩码匹配在常规与海岸类别间相近，而采用替代文本标签可显著改善若干海岸概念的识别。

Conclusion: 在所评估的物体/查询类别上，海岸类别性能偏低更多受分割质量与语言表征（文本标签选择）影响，而非环境背景本身；改进分割与语言表示是提升海岸视觉语言感知的关键方向。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable robotic per- ception by associating visual observations with natural-language concepts. Yet their performance in coastal environments remains largely unexplored. We introduce a densely labeled coastal dataset containing more than 1,000 images collected across seven missions in three regions of Oahu, Hawaii, with 18 semantic classes and over 7,400 annotated instances. We evaluate seven modern VLMs through three complementary experiments mea- suring text-to-mask, mask-to-mask, and mask-to-text alignment. Broad landscape classes are generally recognized more accurately than conventional object and coastal classes, with coastal con- cepts presenting the greatest challenge. However, comparisons of shared conventional classes across coastal and terrestrial datasets reveal no consistent performance difference attributable solely to environmental context. Mask-to-mask matching also remains similar across conventional and coastal classes, while alternative textual labels substantially improve recognition of several coastal concepts. These results suggest that lower performance on coastal classes (at least on the objects/query categories evaluated) is heavily influenced by segmentation and linguistic representation.

</details>


### [10] [Symmetry-aware super-resolution of crystal orientation maps via invariant latent-space learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10898)
*Umang Garg,Warren Zamudio,McLean P. Echlin,Samantha H. Daly,Tresa M. Pollock,B. S. Manjunath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SG-SRAN，一种晶体对称群感知的超分辨率注意力网络，将EBSD取向数据映射到局部等距隐空间进行超分辨，仅用27-49k可训练参数即匹配15-16M参数骨干网络，并在FCC/HCP基准上取得最低p68误差、最高极图保真度及零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD晶体取向图本质上是仅定义到晶体对称意义的物理场，数据位于商空间SO(3)/G上。采集时间约束限制了其空间分辨率，而常规欧氏插值会混合对称等价表示，从而模糊晶界。因此需要一种在设计中内嵌晶体对称性与边界保持机制的超分辨率方法。

Method: 提出SG-SRAN（对称群感知超分辨率注意力网络）。首先用冻结的局部等距编码器将对称等价取向映射到共同隐表示，使该空间中欧氏距离近似错取向角；随后在该隐空间执行超分辨率，每个高分辨率token被限制在特征一致的局部支撑域内，以防止跨晶界混合；最后用基于字典的解码器恢复合法取向。

Result: 在FCC与HCP基准上，SG-SRAN仅用27-49k可训练参数即可匹配15-16百万参数骨干网络的性能，同时取得最低的p68误差、最高的反极图保真度，并实现对未见合金的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 通过在网络设计中显式融入晶体对称群结构与边界保持约束，SG-SRAN在极低参数量下显著提升EBSD超分辨率性能与泛化能力，验证了对称群感知表示学习在材料取向数据超分辨任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Crystal-orientation maps are physical fields defined only up to crystal symmetry; electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) resolves them experimentally, but acquisition-time constraints limit spatial resolution. Unlike conventional images, EBSD data lie on the quotient space $\mathrm{SO}(3)/G$, where $G$ is the crystal-symmetry group. Standard Euclidean interpolation can therefore mix symmetry-equivalent representations and blur grain boundaries. We introduce the Symmetry-Group-Aware Super-Resolution Attention Network (SG-SRAN), which incorporates crystal symmetry and boundary preservation by design. A frozen, locally isometric encoder maps equivalent orientations to a common latent representation in which Euclidean distance approximates misorientation. Super-resolution is performed in this space, with each high-resolution token restricted to a feature-consistent local support to prevent cross-boundary mixing. A dictionary-based decoder then recovers valid orientations. Across FCC and HCP benchmarks, SG-SRAN matches 15-16 million parameter backbones using only 27-49k trainable parameters, while achieving the lowest p68 errors, highest inverse-pole-figure fidelity, and zero-shot transfer to unseen alloys.

</details>


### [11] [HiPerViT: A Hierarchical Perceiver-Vision Transformer Architecture for Multi-Scale Texture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10917)
*João Pedro C. A. de Sá,Odemir Martinez Bruno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPerViT 通过在 ViT 识别流程中显式注入二阶统计先验（以紧凑双线性描述子编码为统计 token，并经 Perceiver 式潜在蒸馏与一阶空间表示交互），在六个纹理识别基准上取得一致提升，其增益对骨干深度与融合顺序不敏感，表明二阶统计信息作为一等表示信号是主要改进来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉模型在纹理识别上面临困难，因为判别性证据往往由高阶空间统计而非物体形状承载。Vision Transformer 虽具备强长程建模能力，但其标准的目标中心表示并未显式暴露这种统计结构，从而限制了细粒度识别场景中的纹理敏感性。

Method: 提出 HiPerViT，一种仅视觉的紧凑架构，将显式二阶统计先验注入基于 Transformer 的识别流程。方法结合全局与局部图像视图，并将紧凑双线性描述子编码为统计 token，通过 Perceiver 式潜在蒸馏将该 token 与一阶空间表示整合，使空间 token 与二阶特征共现统计直接交互，无需多模态预训练或集成构建。

Result: 在六个纹理识别基准上，HiPerViT 在报告评测协议下相比强仅视觉基线取得一致提升，包括 DTD 上 +3.05 个百分点、GTOS-Mobile 上 +10.48、1200Tex 上 +10.10。分析还显示，这些增益基本不随提取二阶统计的骨干深度以及交互与蒸馏阶段顺序变化。

Conclusion: 改进的主要来源并非特定融合拓扑，而是二阶统计信息作为一等表示信号的显式可用性。结果支持显式统计 tokenization 作为纹理中心视觉识别中一种有效且稳健的设计原则。

Abstract: Texture recognition remains challenging for modern vision models because discriminative evidence is often carried by higher-order spatial statistics rather than by object shape alone. While Vision Transformers provide strong long-range modeling capacity, their standard object-centric representations do not explicitly expose such statistical structure, which limits texture sensitivity in fine-grained recognition settings. We present HiPerViT, a compact vision-only architecture that injects an explicit second-order statistical prior into a transformer-based recognition pipeline. The method combines global and local image views with a compact bilinear descriptor encoded as a statistical token, and integrates this token with first-order spatial representations through Perceiver-style latent distillation. This design enables direct interaction between spatial tokens and second-order feature co-occurrence statistics, providing the model with explicit access to texture-relevant information without requiring multimodal pretraining or ensemble construction. Across six texture recognition benchmarks, HiPerViT achieves consistent improvements over strong vision-only baselines under the reported evaluation protocols, including gains of +3.05 percentage points on DTD, +10.48 on GTOS-Mobile, and +10.10 on 1200Tex. Beyond benchmark performance, our analyses show that these gains are largely invariant to the backbone depth used to extract second-order statistics and to the ordering of interaction and distillation stages. This pattern suggests that the primary source of improvement is not a specific fusion topology, but the explicit availability of second-order statistical information as a first-class representational signal. These results support explicit statistical tokenization as an effective and robust design principle for texture-centric visual recognition.

</details>


### [12] [New Evidence, Same Choice: Testing Physical Experiment Selection in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11022)
*Sourajit Saha,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Nobin Sarwar,Shaswati Saha,Yuxuan Jiang,Siyuan Li,Qiheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个受控基准，评估视觉语言模型在物理推理中"何时停止作答、何时选择最经济实验"的证据选择决策能力，发现当前模型存在严重的动作僵化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理推理基准通常只评估最终答案，无法直接衡量模型"决定是否立即作答、以及在证据不足时选择哪个实验"的决策能力，存在评测盲区。

Method: 构建受控评测：每题提供一张测量图像，以及由两种质量和另一相关属性的两种取值组合而成的四个可能物理世界；模型须选择停止作答或选取能解决问题的最廉价附加实验。设计匹配问题对，使改变观测测量或改变问题会改变最优动作，并因所有可能世界与实验成本已知而可显式确定最优选择。测试对象为6个开源模型、144个物理参数集。

Result: 直接回答时，模型在95.1%至100%的图像对上重复相同动作，即使正确动作已改变；简短推理可改善动作切换，但最佳模型在两个匹配问题上均正确的比例仅为5.9%。进一步分析揭示测量解读、物理推理和响应格式三方面失败。

Conclusion: 将证据选择与最终答案分离评估，可暴露传统答案准确率所忽视的物理推理局限；表明现有视觉语言模型在主动决策式物理推理上能力不足。

Abstract: A model first sees an image from one physical measurement experiment, such as how far a block coasted, and must answer a question about a new trial, such as whether the block will pass a target after a fixed push. The initial experiment may provide enough information to answer, or the model may need another measurement, such as the object's mass, friction, restitution, or spring stiffness. We study whether vision language models can decide when to answer immediately and, when more evidence is needed, which experiment to perform. Current physical reasoning benchmarks usually evaluate only the final answer, so they do not directly measure this decision-making ability. We introduce a controlled evaluation where each problem provides one measurement image and four possible physical worlds created by combining two possible masses and two possible values of another relevant property. The model must either stop and answer or select the cheapest additional experiment that can resolve the question. We construct matched problem pairs where changing either the observed measurement or the question changes the optimal action. Since all possible worlds and experiment costs are known, we can explicitly determine the optimal choice. Across six open models and 144 physical parameter sets, direct responses repeat the same action for 95.1% to 100% of image pairs even when the correct action changes. Brief reasoning improves action switching, but the best model makes both decisions correctly for only 5.9% of image pairs. Additional analysis reveals failures in measurement interpretation, physical reasoning, and response formatting. By evaluating evidence selection separately from final answers, our benchmark reveals limitations in physical reasoning that conventional answer accuracy can overlook.

</details>


### [13] [Toward Interpretable Multimodal Fusion: Heat Conduction Modeling for Hyperspectral and LiDAR Joint Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11040)
*Kan Wei,Jiahui Cui,Jing Yao,Xinyu Zhao,Lei Wang,Pedram Ghamisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出M2Heat，一个受热传导物理启发的遥感多模态融合框架，通过各向异性热传导模块与跨频率融合策略，在多个基准上实现高效且可解释的高光谱-LiDAR地物分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱与LiDAR数据融合可联合利用光谱、空间与结构信息以提升地物分类性能，但现有方法难以在保持计算效率的同时建模长程依赖与复杂的各向异性交互。

Method: 提出M2Heat框架，核心为物理驱动的视觉热传导模块（vHeat）与增强的频率值嵌入（FVEs），模拟各向异性信息流；结合名为跨频率融合（CFF）的混合空域-频域融合策略，生成判别性强且鲁棒的特征表示。

Result: 在Trento、Houston2013和Augsburg三个基准上取得具有竞争力的整体性能，同时提供可解释的热传导引导视角。

Conclusion: 结果表明确实存在热传导引导神经算子用于高效、可解释遥感多模态融合的潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: The fusion of hyperspectral (HS) and Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) data plays a crucial role in enhancing land-cover classification by jointly exploiting spectral, spatial, and structural cues. However, existing multimodal fusion methods still struggle to model long-range dependencies and complex anisotropic interactions while maintaining computational efficiency. This paper introduces M2Heat, a physics-inspired framework that investigates multimodal fusion through the lens of heat conduction. At its core, a physics-driven visual heat conduction module (vHeat) and enhanced Frequency Value Embeddings (FVEs) simulate anisotropic information flow, enabling the capture of global dependencies with sub-quadratic complexity and physical interpretability. This mechanism, combined with a hybrid spatial-frequency fusion strategy named Cross-Frequency Fusion (CFF) module, produces highly discriminative and robust feature representations. M2Heat achieves competitive overall performance on three benchmarks, i.e., Trento, Houston2013, and Augsburg, while providing an interpretable heat-conduction-guided perspective for multimodal feature fusion. These results indicate the potential of heat-conduction-guided neural operators for efficient and interpretable RS multimodal fusion. The source code is publicly available at https: /github.com/Weikan0425/M2Heat_HSI_LiDAR.

</details>


### [14] [Meta-Learning for Classifier Selection in Image Datasets: A Feature-Driven Framework for Accuracy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11041)
*Zahra Nabizadeh_Shahre_Babak,Farzaneh Koohestani,Nader Karimi,Shahram Shirani,Shadrokh Samavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个元学习框架，利用刻画数据集复杂度的元特征，在不进行完整训练的情况下预测分类器在图像数据集上的性能，并对相似表现模式进行分类器聚类以简化推荐。在56个多样化图像数据集上平均排名预测准确率超过86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No Free Lunch定理表明不存在普遍最优的分类器，任何在特定图像分布上的性能提升都会在其他问题集合上产生性能损失，因此为图像数据集选择最合适的分类器十分关键却因图像内在的复杂性与多样性而困难。穷举训练评估计算成本高昂，亟需无需完整训练即可预测性能的方法。

Method: 构建元学习框架，提取刻画数据集复杂度的综合元特征；采用自编码器、预训练网络和降维技术进行特征提取与选择；训练回归模型估计分类器准确率；并利用聚类技术将性能模式相近的分类器分组以简化推荐。数据集涵盖自然、动物、数字、摩托车、医学图像和人体等多种概念以保证泛化性。

Result: 在56个多样化图像数据集上评估，平均排名预测准确率超过86%，验证了该方法在指导模型选择方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展且具有可解释性，为在降低计算成本的同时提升分类性能提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: No Free Lunch theorem implies that any performance gains achieved by a classifier on a particular image distribution are necessarily offset by a loss of performance over the set of all possible problems; thus, no single model is universally optimal. Selecting the most suitable classifier for image datasets is a critical yet challenging task due to the intrinsic complexity and diversity of images. This paper proposes a meta-learning framework that leverages a comprehensive set of meta-features capturing dataset complexity to predict classifier performance without exhaustive training. By extracting and selecting features using methods such as autoencoders, pre-trained networks, and dimensionality reduction techniques, we train regression models to efficiently estimate classifier accuracies. Additionally, clustering techniques are employed to group classifiers with similar performance patterns, simplifying the recommendation process. The datasets used span a wide range of concepts, including nature, animals, numbers, motorcycles, medical images, and human bodies, to ensure broad generalization. Evaluated on 56 diverse image datasets, our approach achieves an average ranking prediction accuracy exceeding 86%, demonstrating its effectiveness in guiding model selection. This scalable and interpretable framework provides a practical solution to improve classification performance while reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond Visual Quality: Evaluating Physical Consistency under Ego-Motion with EgoGenEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11172)
*Yilin Long,Chenming Zhu,Zitang Gou,Jingli Lin,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoGenEval——一个基于几何、无需位姿的自我运动物理一致性基准（1,400 案例、2,360 目标视角），并构建 EgoGen-Train 进行 SFT 研究，发现现有生成器难以同时兼顾相机运动接地与场景状态保持，指出成对教师强制目标是关键瓶颈，倡导轨迹中心范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期视觉生成器虽能生成高保真图像，但在自我运动下常违反物理一致性，限制了其用于空间推理与具身规划。现有基准多聚焦孤立图像或单步质量，对该问题探索不足。

Method: (1) 构建 EgoGenEval 基准，含 1,400 案例与 2,360 目标视角，覆盖单步与多步自我运动，分别度量相机运动接地（CMG）与场景状态保持（SSP），两指标均经盲测人工判断验证；评估 16 个无位姿生成器与 2 个位姿条件参考模型。(2) 基于同一几何管线构建 EgoGen-Train，开展受控 SFT 研究。

Result: 现有模型难以在执行相机运动的同时保持场景状态，无系统能同时在两个维度上表现良好。SFT 研究表明，成对监督无法可靠地同时提升 CMG 与 SSP：即使使用完整训练池与最长训练预算，场景保持的增益仅为相机运动增益的一小部分。

Conclusion: 瓶颈在于成对教师强制目标本身，这促使研究转向轨迹中心范式，将自条件 rollout 与显式位姿和可见性监督相结合。

Abstract: Recent visual generators produce high-fidelity images yet often violate physical consistency under ego-motion, limiting their use for spatial reasoning and embodied planning. Existing benchmarks largely focus on isolated images or single-step quality, leaving this challenge underexplored. We introduce EgoGenEval, a geometry-grounded, pose-free benchmark designed to evaluate the physical consistency of visual generators under ego-motion, and organize our study into two parts. (1) EgoGenEval contains 1,400 cases and 2,360 target views spanning single-step and multi-step ego-motion. It separately measures Camera Motion Grounding (CMG) and Scene State Preservation (SSP), with both metrics validated against blinded human judgments. Evaluating 16 pose-free generators together with two pose-conditioned references reveals that current models struggle to execute camera motion while maintaining scene state, and that no system performs well on both axes at once. (2) To examine whether benchmark-derived data can improve these capabilities, we build EgoGen-Train from the same geometry-grounded pipeline and run controlled SFT studies. These show that pairwise supervision does not reliably improve camera-motion grounding and scene-state preservation together: even at the full training pool and the longest budget, scene preservation gains a fraction of what camera motion does. This points to the pairwise teacher-forced objective itself as the binding constraint, motivating a trajectory-centric paradigm that couples self-conditioned rollouts with explicit pose and visibility supervision.

</details>


### [16] [A Multi-View and Confusion-Guided Ensemble Framework for Robust Synthetic Image Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11188)
*Zuomin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多视角、混淆引导的集成框架用于合成图像溯源（SIA），融合FFT-ConvNeXt、DINOv2、CLIP与Xception，结合数据增强、二分类专家与类自适应置信度校准，公开榜99.53%、私有榜99.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着文本到图像生成模型快速发展，合成图像溯源愈发重要；但现代扩散生成器之间相似度不断提高，加之多样的后处理操作，导致准确识别生成图像来源模型仍然困难。

Method: 构建多视角、混淆引导的集成框架：整合FFT-ConvNeXt、DINOv2、CLIP、Xception等互补架构，从频域、语义、取证视角提取溯源线索；训练时采用模拟压缩、缩放、灰度化、模糊等真实后处理的强数据增强提升鲁棒性；分析集成模型混淆模式，针对Stable Diffusion 3与3.5的严重歧义引入专用二分类专家分类器并在低置信度条件下选择性激活；对Tencent Hunyuan等难分类别采用类自适应置信度校准。

Result: 在ICANN 2026 DLMMDD Workshop合成图像溯源挑战赛公开榜取得99.53%，私有榜取得99.20%；源代码与实现细节已公开于 https://github.com/ZOMIN28/SIA。

Conclusion: 多视角集成结合混淆引导的专家分类与置信度校准，可有效应对生成模型相似性与后处理干扰，显著提升合成图像溯源的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Synthetic image attribution (SIA) has become increasingly important with the rapid advancement of text-to-image generation models. However, accurately identifying the source model of a generated image remains challenging due to the growing similarity among modern diffusion-based generators and the presence of diverse post-processing operations. In this report, we present a multi-view and confusion-guided ensemble framework for the Synthetic Image Attribution Challenge of the DLMMDD Workshop at ICANN 2026. Our approach integrates multiple complementary architectures, including FFT-ConvNeXt, DINOv2, CLIP, and Xception, to capture diverse attribution cues from frequency, semantic, and forensic perspectives. To improve robustness against unknown degradations and image manipulations, extensive data augmentation strategies are employed during training, simulating realistic post-processing operations such as compression, resizing, grayscale conversion, and blur. Furthermore, we analyze the confusion patterns of the ensemble model and observe severe ambiguity between Stable Diffusion 3 and Stable Diffusion 3.5. To address this issue, we introduce a dedicated binary expert classifier that is selectively activated under low-confidence conditions. We additionally apply class-adaptive confidence calibration to improve the discrimination of challenging classes such as Tencent Hunyuan. The proposed framework achieved 99.53% on the public leaderboard and 99.20% on the private leaderboard. The source code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/ZOMIN28/SIA.

</details>


### [17] [CEM-TUDASR: Computationally efficient multi-modality transformer based unsupervised domain adaptive super-resolution approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11201)
*Anjali Sarvaiya,Jay Kadel,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CEM-TUDASR：一种基于无监督Transformer的计算高效WCE图像超分框架，通过域自适应退化网络合成逼真LR图像，实现无配对训练，在多项无参考指标上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线胶囊内镜（WCE）因微型光学元件、传感器及无线传输限制，图像分辨率低、诊断关键结构可见性差；同时临床缺乏配对低分（LR）与高分（HR）数据，现有超分方法难以直接应用。

Method: 提出CEM-TUDASR无监督Transformer超分框架：1）域自适应退化网络从HR常规内镜图像合成逼真的WCE风格LR图像，缩小域间差异以支持无配对学习；2）SR生成器集成深度注意力块（DAB）与融合注意力块（FAB），兼顾长程上下文依赖与局部细节；3）在Kvasir Capsule衍生数据集训练，并在KID、GIANA上评估跨数据集泛化。

Result: 在BRISQUE、PIQE、NIQE及领域专用EndoQM等无参考指标上一致优于现有无监督超分方法；定性结果改善黏膜纹理、血管形态及临床相关解剖细节的恢复；视网膜图像跨域实验验证框架可迁移性；模型仅2.67M参数、169.94 GFLOPs。

Conclusion: CEM-TUDASR在保持计算高效的同时实现高质量WCE图像重建，适用于资源受限的临床与嵌入式内镜应用。

Abstract: Wireless Capsule Endoscopy (WCE) enables non-invasive visualization of the gastrointestinal tract, but its miniaturized optics, sensor limitations, and wireless transmission constraints result in low-resolution images with reduced visibility of diagnostically important structures. This paper proposes CEM-TUDASR, a computationally efficient unsupervised Transformer-based super-resolution framework for WCE image enhancement without paired low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) training data. A domain-adaptive degradation network synthesizes realistic WCE-like LR images from HR conventional endoscopy images, reducing the domain gap and enabling effective unpaired learning. The SR generator integrates Deep Attention Blocks (DABs) and a Fusion Attention Block (FAB) to capture long-range contextual dependencies and fine local structures while preserving perceptual and structural fidelity. The model is trained on a curated dataset derived from Kvasir Capsule and evaluated on KID and GIANA for cross-dataset generalization. No-reference quality metrics, including BRISQUE, PIQE, NIQE, and the domain-specific EndoQM, show that CEM-TUDASR consistently outperforms existing unsupervised SR methods. Qualitative results further demonstrate improved restoration of mucosal textures, vascular patterns, and clinically relevant anatomical details. Cross-domain experiments on retinal images additionally demonstrate the adaptability of the framework. With only 2.67 million parameters and 169.94 GFLOPs, CEM-TUDASR achieves high-quality reconstruction while maintaining computational efficiency, making it suitable for resource-constrained clinical and embedded endoscopic applications.

</details>


### [18] [Tri-DehazeGS: Scene--Medium Decoupled Gaussian Splatting with Transmittance-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11223)
*Kui Jiang,Yang Gu,Jiacheng Liu,Shiyu Liu,Youyu Chen,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Tri-DehazeGS，将干净场景用高斯基元表示、介质用独立三平面场建模，通过物理散射模型合成雾图，并引入MD-TGC补偿低透射区被抑制的梯度，从而提升雾天多视角下的干净新视角重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雾天多视角图像中，雾衰减场景辐射并引入大气散射。现有散射感知高斯泼溅方法在图像空间施加退化，或将介质相关变量绑定到高斯基元，导致干净场景辐射与大气效应纠缠；同时低透射区域对高斯优化监督减弱，远距离或浓雾区域重建不足。作者认为需要场景–介质解耦与透射率感知的优化再平衡。

Method: 提出Tri-DehazeGS场景–介质解耦高斯泼溅框架：用高斯基元表示干净场景，用独立且视角共享的三平面场建模参与介质，并通过物理散射模型合成雾天观测。进一步提出Medium-Decoupled Transmittance Gradient Compensation（MD-TGC），在介质冻结后补偿被雾抑制的梯度，且不改变前向渲染。

Result: 在真实与合成雾天基准上的实验表明，Tri-DehazeGS提升了干净新视角重建效果。代码已开源于https://github.com/aptx46/Tri-DehazeGS。

Conclusion: 通过场景–介质解耦表示与透射率感知的梯度补偿，Tri-DehazeGS缓解了雾天多视角重建中的辐射纠缠与低透射区欠监督问题，实现了更优的干净场景重建。

Abstract: Recovering clean 3D scenes from hazy multi-view images is challenging because haze attenuates scene radiance and introduces atmospheric scattering. Recent scattering-aware Gaussian Splatting methods introduce physical haze models into reconstruction, but they often apply degradation in image space or bind medium-related variables to Gaussian primitives, which can entangle clean scene radiance with atmospheric effects. Moreover, low-transmittance regions provide weakened supervision for Gaussian optimization, causing distant or dense-haze areas to be under-reconstructed. We argue that clean reconstruction under haze requires both scene--medium disentanglement and transmittance-aware optimization rebalancing. To this end, we propose Tri-DehazeGS, a scene--medium decoupled Gaussian Splatting framework. It represents the clean scene with Gaussian primitives, models the participating medium using an independent view-shared tri-plane field, and composes hazy observations through a physical scattering model. We further introduce Medium-Decoupled Transmittance Gradient Compensation (MD-TGC), which compensates haze-suppressed gradients after medium freezing without altering forward rendering. Experiments on real and synthetic haze benchmarks show that Tri-DehazeGS improves clean novel-view reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/aptx46/Tri-DehazeGS.

</details>


### [19] [When is Test-Time Adaptation Identifiable From Unlabeled Evidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11235)
*Kartik Jhawar,Lipo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个先决问题：用于选择TTA更新的无标签证据本身是否包含足够信息以决定最优动作。作者证明即使选择器完美也可能信息不足，并在有限批量高斯TTA模型中刻画了精确边界，同时用CIFAR-100-C和DomainNet-126验证了该失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应（TTA）方法试图从无标签测试数据预测哪种更新有效，以避免错误更新损害源模型。但这些方法忽略了更前置的问题：观测通道所提供的信息是否足以区分最优动作。本文动机是区分"选择器弱"与"信息通道根本无法支持决策"这两种通常被混淆的失效模式。

Method: 将问题形式化为观测通道的可辨识性：若某通道使两个部署看起来相同但TTA排序不同，则该通道无法可靠选择。在有限批量高斯TTA模型中精确刻画边界：小偏移下"不更新"优于均值重中心化，超过唯一临界偏移后重中心化占优，且边界以1/√n速率收缩。在CIFAR-100-C和DomainNet-126上对现代TTA方法进行公开基准研究。

Result: 理论证明即使选择器完美，信息不足也会导致选择不可能；信息通道只有在能消解相关歧义时，更丰富的证据才能恢复决策。基准实验显示同样失效模式：仅改变部署结构即可逆转oracle动作，而全局顺序无关证据保持不变。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用方法，将"弱选择器"与"信息通道无法支持目标决策"两类失效模式分离，为TTA中选择证据设计提供了理论边界与实证依据。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers many ways to update a deployed model without labels, but choosing the wrong update can make a strong source model worse. Recent methods therefore try to predict which adaptation will work from unlabeled test data. We ask a prior question: does the evidence given to the selector contain enough information to determine the best action at all? We show that this is not guaranteed, even with a perfect selector. If an observation channel makes two deployments look the same while their TTA rankings differ, reliable selection is impossible from that channel; richer evidence can restore the decision only when it resolves the relevant ambiguity. We make this boundary exact in a finite-batch Gaussian TTA model, where doing nothing beats mean recentering for small shifts, recentering wins beyond a unique critical shift, and the boundary shrinks as $1/\sqrt n$. Public benchmark studies on CIFAR-100-C and DomainNet-126 show the same failure mode with modern TTA methods: changing only deployment structure can reverse the oracle action while global order-blind evidence remains unchanged. The result is a practical way to separate two failure modes that are usually mixed together: a weak selector versus an information channel that cannot support the desired decision in the first place.

</details>


### [20] [HALDETECT at ImageEval 2026 Shared Tasks: Answer-First Contrastive Grounding with QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11236)
*Syed Mohaiminul Hoque,Md Sakhawat Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 HALDETECT 系统，用于 ImageEval 2026 英文学术幻觉检测任务（Task 1b）：给定图像与三条文化上合理的陈述，识别唯一视觉有依据者。系统将任务建模为对比决策，先输出答案再给解释，推理围绕颜色/纹理、形状/形式、语境组织；最佳方案用 4-bit QLoRA 微调 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 并冻结视觉编码器，在 1000 项测试集上取得 CI 0.035，8 支队伍中排名第三。开发实验表明答案顺序的影响可能超过模型规模，适配优于纯提示；回看配对分析确认 QLoRA 相对最佳提示的增益，但未确认两个适配器间的小差距，重设种子显示数据缩放曲线不稳健；35 个残余错误集中于功能、材质与识别层面的文化合理区分，简单适配器投票无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型常以流畅方式产生视觉细节幻觉，限制了其在细粒度解释场景中的部署。因此需要一个能判别陈述是否具备视觉依据的检测系统，以提升多模态输出的可靠性。

Method: 将任务建模为单一对比决策，先输出答案再生成解释，并按颜色/纹理、形状/形式、语境三类结构组织推理。最佳模型为 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct，采用 4-bit QLoRA 微调，视觉编码器保持冻结。此外进行开发实验，考察答案顺序、模型规模、提示与适配的差异，并对已发布金标做回顾性配对分析，对四种训练规模重设种子检验数据缩放。

Result: 最佳提交适配器在 1000 项测试集上达到 Contrastive Instability (CI) 0.035，8 支队伍中排名第三。开发实验显示答案顺序可能比模型规模更重要，适配优于单纯提示。配对分析确认 QLoRA 相对最佳提示的增益，但未能确认 devtest 所选适配器与最佳测试适配器之间的小差距；重设种子后，表面上的数据缩放曲线不再成立。35 个残余错误属于文化上合理的功能、材质与识别区分，朴素适配器投票无帮助。

Conclusion: HALDETECT 通过对比式建模、结构化推理及 QLoRA 适配，在多模态幻觉检测任务上取得较好表现；但小差距与数据缩放效应不稳健，残余错误涉及文化合理区分，提示后续需关注答案顺序、种子稳定性与细粒度文化语境判别。

Abstract: Large multimodal models tend to hallucinate visual detail fluently, which limits their deployment for fine-grained interpretation. We present HALDETECT, our system for the English hallucination-detection track (Task 1b) of ImageEval 2026, in which a system must identify, from an image and three culturally plausible statements, the single visually grounded one. We frame the item as one contrastive decision, emit the answer before its explanation, and structure reasoning around colour/texture, shape/form, and context. Our best submitted adapter fine-tunes Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct with 4-bit QLoRA while freezing the vision encoder and reaches Contrastive Instability (CI) 0.035 on the 1,000-item test set; we placed third of eight teams. Development experiments show that answer order can matter more than model scale and that adaptation beats prompting alone. Retrospective paired analysis of the released gold labels confirms the QLoRA gain over the best prompt but not the small gap between the devtest-selected and best-test adapters, and reseeding all four training sizes shows that the apparent data-scaling curve does not survive a seed change. The 35 residual errors are culturally plausible function, material, and recognition distinctions; naive adapter voting does not help.

</details>


### [21] [SCINTILLA-SNN: A Spiking Multi-Scale Selective Aggregation Network for Perineural Invasion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11237)
*Youngung Han,Yului Jeong,Kyeonghun Kim,Dohyun Kweon,Suah Park,Hyunsu Go,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Jinyong Jun,Yunho Choe,Yunjin Seo,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCINTILLA-SNN脉冲神经网络，通过分层骨干与多尺度脉冲聚合模块，实现胆管癌神经周围侵犯的术前MRI预测，AUROC 0.748，推理能耗降低23.18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胆管癌（CCA）神经周围侵犯（PNI）的术前预测具有临床价值，但MRI上PNI相关线索细微、稀疏且局限于肿瘤边界，密集或空间均匀处理的3D CNN与Transformer会稀释这些证据，且3D特征网格上的乘加运算量巨大。

Method: 提出SCINTILLA-SNN，一种由四阶段分层骨干网络与多尺度脉冲聚合（MSSA）模块组成的3D脉冲网络。骨干通过脉冲卷积阶段与局部脉冲窗口调制阶段提取分层体数据表征；MSSA将每个空间token映射为可学习的内容值，并用由放电率与逐时间步膜电位变异性导出的脉冲动力学门控进行调制，得到诊断token分数，用于选择性聚合稀疏的PNI相关证据。

Result: 在182例CCA患者10年回顾性队列上，5折交叉验证AUROC达0.748，同时相比同网络的纯密集MAC计算，估计推理能耗降低23.18倍。

Conclusion: SCINTILLA-SNN以脉冲稀疏计算有效聚合肿瘤边界处的细微PNI线索，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时显著降低推理能耗，为CCA术前PNI预测提供了高能效方案。

Abstract: Preoperative prediction of perineural invasion (PNI) in cholangiocarcinoma (CCA) is clinically valuable but remains challenging because PNI-related cues on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) are subtle, sparse, and spatially localized around the tumor boundary. Standard 3D CNN and transformer architectures process volumetric data in a dense or spatially uniform manner, which can dilute subtle PNI-related evidence while requiring a large number of multiply-accumulate operations over 3D feature grids. To address these limitations, we propose SCINTILLA-SNN, a 3D spiking network composed of a four-stage hierarchical backbone and a Multi-Scale Spike Aggregation (MSSA) module for PNI prediction. The backbone extracts hierarchical volumetric representations through spiking convolutional stages and local spike window modulation stages. Given the resulting stage-wise representations, MSSA maps each spatial token to a learnable content value and modulates it with a spike-dynamics gate derived from firing rate and timestep-wise membrane-potential variability. The resulting score, referred to as the diagnostic token score, is used to selectively aggregate sparse PNI-related evidence. Experiments on a 10-year retrospective cohort of 182 CCA patients show that SCINTILLA-SNN achieves an AUROC of 0.748 under 5-fold cross-validation, while reducing the estimated inference energy by 23.18$\times$ compared with dense MAC-only computation of the same network.

</details>


### [22] [Fast and Accurate Monomodal 3D High Resolution Deep Registration of Drosophila Larval Brain Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11240)
*Daniel Reisenbüchler,Yousef Sadegheih,Michael Dittrich,Pratibha Kumari,Muhammad Usman,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出深度学习的果蝇幼虫脑配准流水线，单次前向传播即完成高分辨率可变形配准，精度与速度均优于经典及深度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果蝇幼虫是紧凑的神经科学模型，其遗传工具可在特定神经群体中表达荧光标记，而跨个体比较表达模式需将每个脑配准到共享解剖参考空间。现有流水线多基于经典配准方法，需对每个体数据单独优化、常需逐例调参，且单个脑耗时可达数分钟，难以作为常规预处理步骤。

Method: 提出训练好的深度配准流水线，在一次前向传播中完成幼虫脑到参考模板的高分辨率可变形对齐，处理体素量远超通常报道的学习型3D配准的体数据；并配套完整的预处理与解剖锚定评估流程。在具有不同采集条件与质量分层的留出数据集上，与11种经典方法和7种学习型基线进行比较。

Result: 该流水线精度最高，在解剖标志点局部互信息上较最强经典基线提升23个百分点；配准速度比经典可变形流水线快1至2个数量级；随采集质量下降，其精度保持能力优于所有其他方法。

Conclusion: 深度配准可显著提升幼虫脑配准的精度、速度与鲁棒性，适合作为常规预处理；网络、训练权重及完整流水线已开源为深度幼虫脑配准框架。

Abstract: The larval stage of Drosophila melanogaster is a compact model system for neuroscience whose genetic toolkit allows fluorescent markers to be expressed in defined neural populations, and comparing the resulting expression patterns across animals requires every brain to be registered into a shared anatomical reference space. Existing pipelines for this task are predominantly based on classical registration methods, which perform a new optimization for each volume, often require per-case parameter tuning, and can take minutes per brain, limiting their use as a routine preprocessing step. We present a trained deep registration pipeline that deformably aligns a larval brain to a reference template in a single forward pass at high spatial resolution, on volumes that hold several times more voxels than those learned 3D registration is normally reported on, together with the preprocessing and anatomy-anchored evaluation pipeline required to apply it. Against eleven classical and seven further learned baselines on a held-out collection acquired with different acquisition and quality strata, the proposed pipeline is the most accurate, improving on the strongest classical baseline by 23 percentage points of anatomical landmark-local mutual information. It registers a volume one to two orders of magnitude faster than the classical deformable pipelines, and it retains more of its accuracy than any other method as acquisition quality degrades. The network, its trained weights and the full pipeline are released as the open-source deep larval brain registration framework: https://github.com/agentdr1/deep-larval-brain-reg

</details>


### [23] [Order-Aware 2.5D Multiple Instance Learning for Preoperative MRI-Based Perineural Invasion Risk Assessment in Intrahepatic Cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11271)
*Hyunsu Go,Youngung Han,Kyeonghun Kim,Jinyong Jun,Junbeom Lee,Dohyun Kweon,Yului Jeong,Suah Park,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OAS-MIL弱监督框架，将肿瘤MRI裁剪表示为有序2.5D切片序列，通过集合注意力与双向序列注意力双分支聚合，实现术前ICC患者级PNI预测，AUROC达0.770。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝内胆管癌（ICC）的神经周围侵犯（PNI）是重要不良预后标志物，但通常只能术后病理确认。术前T2加权MRI有望无创预测PNI，然而仅有患者级标签，缺乏切片级或体素级标注，亟需弱监督方法。

Method: 提出Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning（OAS-MIL）：以肿瘤为中心裁剪MRI，构造由连续轴位切片组成的重叠2.5D slab有序序列；共享编码器提取slab级特征；再由置换不变的集合注意力分支与双向序列注意力分支共同聚合；采用患者级五折标签分层交叉验证评估。

Result: OAS-MIL取得平均AUROC 0.770，优于所对比的体素级与常规MIL基线方法，验证了轴位顺序作为归纳偏置的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，轴位切片顺序为MRI弱监督PNI预测提供了有用的归纳偏置，OAS-MIL可作为术前无创预测ICC神经周围侵犯的有效框架。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is an adverse histopathologic marker in intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (ICC), but it is usually confirmed only after resection. Preoperative T2-weighted MRI may provide noninvasive imaging cues predictive of PNI, although labels are available only at the patient level without slice- or voxel-level annotations. We propose Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning (OAS-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level PNI prediction. Each tumor-centered MRI crop is represented as an ordered sequence of overlapping 2.5D slabs formed from contiguous axial slices. A shared encoder extracts slab-level features, which are aggregated by a permutation-invariant set-attention branch and a bidirectional sequence-attention branch. Using five-fold label-stratified cross-validation at the patient level, OAS-MIL achieved a mean AUROC of 0.770, outperforming the evaluated volumetric and MIL baselines. These results suggest that axial order provides a useful inductive bias for weakly supervised PNI prediction from MRI.

</details>


### [24] [SAMV-DUSt3R: Instance-Centric 3D Scene Decoupling from Sparse Multi-Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11279)
*Langxu Zhao,Zuan Gu,Yingdan Zhang,Pengfei Zhao,Tianhan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端模型SAMV-DUSt3R，将SAM2的2D掩码注入MV-DUSt3R重建流程，实现物体级解耦与形状精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景中物体解耦（将物体从场景中分离）需求日益增长，且现有方法通常依赖多阶段流程。

Method: 将SAM2的2D掩码注入MV-DUSt3R重建；设计Cross Flow Mask Block利用掩码引导网络聚焦目标实例；引入轻量级Spatial RankGNN选择最优参考视角。

Result: 参考视角选择准确率73.5%；相比SOTA基线，多指标平均重建精度提升11%；展现出强实例解耦能力。

Conclusion: 该方法无需多阶段流程即可联合提升形状精度并实现物体级解耦，对自动驾驶、机器人、AR/VR及遗产数字化具有明显价值。

Abstract: With the rising demand to decouple objects from 3D scenes, we propose SAMV-DUSt3R, an end-to-end model that injects SAM2 2D masks into MV-DUSt3R reconstruction. A Cross Flow Mask Block uses these masks to steer the network toward the target instance, jointly improving shape accuracy and achieving object-level disentanglement without multi-stage pipelines. To ensure reconstruction stability, a lightweight Spatial RankGNN selects the optimal reference view with a selection accuracy of 73.5\%. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method boosts average reconstruction precision by 11\% across various metrics compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results reveal a strong instance-disentanglement capability and clear benefits for driving, robotics, AR/VR, and heritage digitisation.

</details>


### [25] [Your Model Already Knows Don't Teach It, Learn to Ask It: Soft Prompting for Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11310)
*Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare,Siyi Li,Hewei Wang,Cesar Daniel Hernandez,Wei Zhao,Wolfgang M. Pauli,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在视觉-语言模型（VLM）的少样本、跨域目标检测中，soft prompting（仅训练少量连续提示词元、冻结主干）可在参数量少逾2万倍的条件下匹配最佳LoRA配置，且不产生灾难性遗忘，所学词元可迁移、可文本化，并扩展至机器人操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM适配方法主要为离散提示优化与LoRA微调。前者受限于离散搜索空间，后者虽有效但需训练大量参数并导致预训练能力遗忘。作者希望重新审视第三种被忽视的选项——soft prompting，探究在仅有10张标注图像的跨域场景（航拍、工业、医疗）下能否以极小代价达到可比性能，同时保留模型的通用能力。

Method: 冻结预训练VLM主干，仅优化少量连续提示词元。研究两个关键设计维度：（1）提示词元位置——跨模态边界（视觉与文本词元之间）优于其他放置方式；（2）初始化——以空空间词元（empty space token）初始化优于语义初始化与随机初始化。平均仅需1–3个可学习词元（约7,168参数）。并在RoboCasa操作任务上，将soft prompting应用于冻结的π0.5视觉-语言-动作策略，词元置于梯度瓶颈处。

Result: 在Roboflow20-VL（10-shot）上，7,168参数的soft prompting达到14.2 mAP，匹配最佳LoRA配置，训练参数量少逾20,000倍；跨模态边界放置为10.0 mAP vs. 其他放置8.4 mAP。soft prompting优化难度更高，跨随机种子方差更大。但无遗忘：匹配精度的LoRA使NaturalBench VQA相对下降35%（最大秩时达56%），soft prompting则保持预训练性能不变。所学词元表现为提示而非权重：无需重训练即迁移至Qwen3.5-9B（+0.8 mAP），可文本化为可读提示，与提示搜索方法竞争（匹配DetPO、优于GEPA）。在RoboCasa三项任务中的两项上匹配LoRA基线。

Conclusion: 现代VLM已编码了专业领域所需的大部分知识，真正的挑战在于"如何提问"。soft prompting以极少参数实现高效跨域适配，兼具零遗忘、可迁移、可解释的优势，为少样本跨域检测与更广泛的多模态决策任务提供了LoRA之外的轻量替代方案。

Abstract: We address few-shot object detection with vision-language models (VLMs) in out-of-domain settings such as aerial, industrial, and medical imagery, using only ten annotated images for supervision. Existing adaptation methods are discrete prompt optimization and LoRA fine-tuning. We revisit a third option: soft prompting, where a small number of continuous prompt tokens are optimized while the pretrained backbone remains frozen.
  We identify two key design choices. First, placing prompt tokens at the cross-modal boundary between visual and text tokens outperforms other placements (10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP). Second, initializing prompts from the empty space token outperforms semantic and random initialization.
  With these choices, one to three learned tokens (7,168 parameters on average) match the best LoRA configuration on Roboflow20-VL (14.2 mAP, 10-shot) while training over 20,000x fewer parameters. Soft prompting remains harder to optimize, exhibiting higher variance across random seeds. Unlike LoRA, however, it causes no forgetting: the LoRA rank matching our accuracy reduces NaturalBench VQA accuracy by 35% relative, rising to 56% at the largest rank, whereas soft prompting leaves pretrained performance unchanged.
  The learned tokens behave like prompts rather than weights. They transfer to a newer model without retraining (+0.8 mAP on Qwen3.5-9B) and can be verbalized into readable prompts competitive with prompt-search methods (matching DetPO and outperforming GEPA).
  The approach also extends beyond detection. On RoboCasa manipulation tasks, the frozen $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy benefits from soft prompting, matching the LoRA baseline on two of three tasks when tokens are placed at the gradient bottleneck. These results suggest modern VLMs already encode much of what is needed for specialized domains; the challenge is learning how to ask.

</details>


### [26] [Mi-Ripple: Restoring Images Degraded by Iterative AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11317)
*Jiayin Chen,Yicheng Xu,Muting Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mi-Ripple 是一种诊断引导的修复流程，用于抑制迭代参考条件图像编辑中的数字波纹（网格状与颗粒状纹理），在保护图像结构的同时实现可测量的伪影去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代参考条件图像编辑会引入网格状与颗粒状纹理，即所谓"数字波纹"（digital ripple）。现有方法往往只优化频谱评分，无法与视觉上的清洁度对应，且直接滤波可能抹除合法细节，因此需要一种兼顾伪影抑制与结构保护的方法。

Method: Mi-Ripple 将周期性晶格伪影与内容纠缠的颗粒纹理分离，随后结合三种手段：选择性频谱陷波（selective spectral notching）、结构感知平滑（structure-aware smoothing）以及干净参考再生（cleaned-reference regeneration）。当伪影在频谱上可分离时采用低失真滤波；当滤波会抹除合法细节时改用视觉重建。

Result: 在十四次仅陷波（notch-only）执行中，全图残差标准差为 0.08–0.44 CIELAB 明度单位。在一个配对再生示例中，参考图清洗使输出碎片密度降低 45%。

Conclusion: Mi-Ripple 将可测量的伪影减少与视觉上更干净的生成图像联系起来，而非仅优化单一频谱评分。

Abstract: Iterative reference-conditioned image editing can introduce grid-like and granular textures, commonly described as digital ripple. We present Mi-Ripple, a diagnosis-guided restoration workflow that suppresses this digital ripple while protecting image structure. Mi-Ripple separates periodic lattice artifacts from content-entangled granular texture, then combines selective spectral notching, structure-aware smoothing, and cleaned-reference regeneration. This separation enables low-distortion filtering when artifacts are spectrally isolated and visual reconstruction when filtering would erase legitimate detail. Across fourteen notch-only executions, whole-image residual standard deviation is 0.08--0.44 in CIELAB lightness units. In a paired regeneration example, reference cleaning reduces output debris density by 45\%. Mi-Ripple links measurable artifact reduction to visibly cleaner generated images, rather than optimizing a spectral score alone.

</details>


### [27] [MC-DeTra: Motion-Consistent Joint Object Detection and Socially-Aware Trajectory Forecasting in Bird's-Eye-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11717)
*Vladislav Diuzhev,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MC-DeTra：在开源复现DeTra的基础上，通过运动一致性机制（基于历史运动、社会占用上下文以及航向-运动方向一致性约束的辅助监督）提升统一检测与轨迹预测模型在动态目标上的精度，且所有新增损失仅在训练阶段生效，不增加推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一检测与轨迹预测模型（如DeTra）在自动驾驶中用于直接在BEV图像上精修目标轨迹，但动态运动目标的预测仍是任务中最困难的部分，且最强的DeTra模型没有公开实现。因此作者旨在提供开源复现并进一步提升动态目标预测精度。

Method: 作者首先开源复现了DeTra并记录其近似实现，随后在其之上提出MC-DeTra：一族运动一致性机制，通过两个由标注导出的辅助信号（目标观测到的历史运动、构成其社会上下文的周围交通占用情况）进行监督，并引入一个输出间一致性约束，使目标预测的航向与其预测的运动方向对齐。所有新增损失仅用于训练，测试时移除，不增加推理延迟。

Result: 在Waymo开放数据集上，采用严格的、以检测为条件的预测协议评估，MC-DeTra提升了动态且具社会情境的轨迹预测，同时保持或改善了检测精度。基于梯度的损失校准分析揭示了辅助目标在共享骨干网络上的竞争关系，消融实验识别出贡献最大的信号。

Conclusion: MC-DeTra通过训练期辅助监督机制有效提升了统一检测-预测模型对动态目标的预测能力，且推理零开销；作者公开了代码、配置和评估工具。

Abstract: Unified models for object detection and trajectory forecasting aim to merge perception and prediction for autonomous driving, refining actor trajectories directly over shared bird's-eye-view (BEV) images rasterized from LiDAR and high-definition maps. Their accuracy on dynamic, moving actors, however, remains the hardest part of the task, and the strongest such model, DeTra, has no public implementation. We contribute an openly released DeTra reimplementation with documented approximations, and on top of it MC-DeTra: a family of motion-consistency mechanisms that add supervision through two annotation-derived auxiliary signals -- each actor's observed past motion and the occupancy of the surrounding traffic that forms its social context -- and one inter-output consistency constraint that aligns an actor's predicted heading with its predicted direction of motion. Every proposed loss is train-only and inference-safe: it shapes the shared BEV representation during training and is removed at test time, adding no inference latency. On the Waymo Open Dataset, evaluated under a strict, detection-conditioned forecasting protocol, MC-DeTra improves dynamic, socially-situated trajectory forecasting while preserving or improving detection accuracy; a gradient-based loss-calibration analysis exposes how the auxiliary objectives compete at the shared backbone, and our ablation identifies which signals contribute most. We release code, configurations, and evaluation tooling at https://github.com/diuzhevVlad/MC-DeTra.

</details>


### [28] [R4Tun: LLM-guided adaptive segmental tunnel lining segmentation in point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11360)
*Xinghui Tao,Zehao Ye,Guangming Wang,Jelena Ninić,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R4Tun，一种LLM驱动的自适应框架，在专家管线SAM4Tun基础上引入记忆、状态、知识三类结构化上下文进行有界参数调优，实现管片隧道衬砌点云的无标签自适应分割，在Seg2Tunnel数据集上显著提升mIoU与总体精度，且跨LLM表现一致，定位为机制性贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管片隧道衬砌的自动化检测需要从3D点云中进行自适应分割，但现有专家调参管线在隧道条件变化时性能下降，缺乏对工况变化的适应能力。

Method: 提出R4Tun，一种LLM驱动的自适应框架，扩展专家设计的SAM4Tun管线，引入结构化的记忆(m)、状态(s)、知识(k)上下文，指导有界参数调整；在30个Seg2Tunnel子集（13个规则、17个复杂）上用三种LLM评估，共270次运行（30隧道×3 LLM×3上下文设置），并配有可审计的调参理由日志。

Result: 相比静态SAM4Tun基线，完整m+s+k设计将mIoU从0.18提升至0.43–0.48，总体精度OA从0.42提升至0.59–0.65；近似参考的规则（交错）子集mIoU达0.784–0.796。三种LLM呈现相似的参数调整趋势（平均增益的95%置信区间重叠），并一致调整了一组关键共享参数。

Conclusion: R4Tun在SAM4Tun–Seg2Tunnel测试场景下是一种受控、无标签、跨LLM的自适应机制，能够稳定提升精度；作者将其定位为机制性贡献而非可部署的最终检测系统，其每次有界参数变化均可通过日志理由审计。

Abstract: Automated inspection of segmental tunnel linings requires adaptive segmentation from 3D point clouds, yet expert-tuned pipelines often degrade when tunnel conditions vary. This paper presents R4Tun, a large language model (LLM)-driven adaptation framework that extends an expert-designed pipeline (SAM4Tun) with bounded parameter tuning informed by structured context: memory ($m$), state ($s$), and knowledge ($k$). Evaluated on 30 selected Seg2Tunnel subsets (13 regular, 17 complex) across three LLMs, the full $m+s+k$ design raised mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) from 0.18 to 0.43--0.48 and overall accuracy (OA) from 0.42 to 0.59--0.65 relative to the static SAM4Tun baseline, with the near-reference regular (staggered) subsets reaching mIoU 0.784--0.796 across LLMs. Across 270 (30 tunnels $\times$ 3 different LLMs $\times$ 3 context settings) runs, the LLMs showed similar parameter-adjustment trends (with overlapping 95\% CIs on mean gains) and consistently adjusted a shared set of critical parameters. These results support R4Tun as a controlled, label-free, cross-LLM adaptation mechanism in the tested SAM4Tun--Seg2Tunnel setting, demonstrating consistent accuracy gains; we position R4Tun as a mechanism contribution rather than a deployable final-inspection system, in which each bounded parameter change is auditable via logged rationales.

</details>


### [29] [Vision Transformer-Based Multi-Level Feature Fusion for Multi-Label Sewer Defect Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11375)
*Xu Fang,Zhuoran Wang,Qing Li,Shengyu Zhang,Guanzhi Deng,Jianbiao He,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sewer-Transformer-ML 及两个轻量模型 Sewer-MobileNet-ML、Sewer-Mobile-TransNet，用于下水道缺陷多标签自动分类，兼顾精度与计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在大规模多标签下水道缺陷分类中难以兼顾分类精度与计算复杂度，且需适配资源受限的巡检场景。

Method: 构建带多级特征融合的层次化视觉 Transformer（Sewer-Transformer-ML），并提出两个轻量架构 Sewer-MobileNet-ML 与 Sewer-Mobile-TransNet；开展消融实验比较直接拼接与注意力融合对不同特征的有效性。

Result: Sewer-ML 测试集上，Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base 取得 F2_CIW 65.68%、F1_Normal 92.68%，公开榜单第一，F2_CIW 领先第二名 7.6 个百分点；Sewer-MobileNet-ML 以仅 17M 参数达到 F2_CIW 65.73%，参数约减少 95%；标准 Sewer-Capsule 划分下 Sewer-Mobile-TransNet 分类准确率 96.43%；训练集缩减至 1,177 张时，Sewer-ML 预训练持续提升性能。消融显示 Transformer 特征适合直接拼接，多尺度 CNN 特征更适合注意力融合。

Conclusion: 研究为下水道自动化巡检、轻量模型设计及跨土木基础设施检测平台适配提供了计算依据。

Abstract: Automated classification of sewer defects is essential for infrastructure condition assessment and maintenance decision-making, but existing deep learning methods struggle to balance classification accuracy and computational complexity in large-scale multi-label scenarios. This study develops Sewer-Transformer-ML, a hierarchical vision Transformer with multi-level feature fusion, together with two lightweight architectures, Sewer-MobileNet-ML and Sewer-Mobile-TransNet, for resource-constrained inspection scenarios. On the Sewer-ML test set, Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.68% and an $F1_{\text{Normal}}$ of 92.68%, ranking first on the public leaderboard and exceeding the second-ranked method by 7.6 percentage points in $F2_{\text{CIW}}$. Sewer-MobileNet-ML achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.73% with only 17 M parameters, representing an approximately 95% parameter reduction relative to the base model. Under the standard Sewer-Capsule data split, Sewer-Mobile-TransNet achieved 96.43% classification accuracy. When the training set was reduced to 1,177 images, pretraining on Sewer-ML consistently improved model performance. Ablation experiments further showed that direct concatenation was more effective for Transformer features, whereas attention-based fusion better supported multiscale CNN features. These findings provide a computational basis for automated sewer inspection, lightweight model design, and adaptation across civil infrastructure inspection platforms.

</details>


### [30] [DINO-Med: A Unified Patch-Based Adaptation Framework for Multi-Modal Medical Image Analysis Applied to Liver Fibrosis Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11380)
*Boya Wang,Ruizhe Li,Chao Chen,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的、基于图像块的框架，将自然图像基础模型DINOv3适配到多模态医学影像，用于肝纤维化分期，并在CARE 2025 Liver Track 4数据集上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然彩色图像与多通道医学扫描之间存在显著域差距，将DINOv3等自然图像基础模型直接迁移到多模态医学影像面临挑战，需要有效的适配策略来跨越这一域鸿沟。

Method: 提出统一的基于图像块的框架：对原始多模态影像进行免训练配准、自动定位与掩膜过滤的块提取，并采用分层策略将块级特征聚合成受试者级诊断。以肝纤维化分期为案例，对比四种块级特征表示（手工Radiomics特征、ResNet学习特征、预训练SAM-Med2D特征、冻结的DINOv3特征），所有模型使用相同的轻量MLP头，并在刚性与形变配准两种设置下评估。训练仅针对轻度纤维化（S1）与肝硬化（S4）两类。

Result: 在CARE 2025 Liver Track 4队列的360名受试者上进行10次随机训练（90%）/测试（10%）划分评估，基于DINOv3的框架显著优于所有基线，S1最佳分类准确率为78.4%，S4为75.8%。

Conclusion: 冻结的DINOv3特征结合轻量MLP头可有效迁移至多模态医学影像任务，单一分类器即可同时完成显著纤维化检测与肝硬化分期，验证了自然图像基础模型在医学影像中的适配潜力。

Abstract: Adapting natural-image foundation models like DINOv3 to multi-modal medical imaging is challenging due to the significant domain gap between natural color images and multi-channel medical scans. We present a unified, patch-based framework that processes raw multimodal imaging through training-free registration, automated localization, and mask-filtered patch extraction. This architecture culminates in a hierarchical strategy that aggregates patch-level insights into subject-level diagnostics. Using liver fibrosis staging as a case study, we evaluate four patch-level feature representations: handcrafted Radiomics features, learned ResNet features, pre-trained foundation model SAM-Med2D features, and frozen DINOv3 features. To ensure a controlled comparison, all models utilize the same lightweight MLP head and are evaluated across both rigid and deformable registration settings. Our training protocol focuses on mild fibrosis (S1) and cirrhosis (S4) classes only, enabling a single classifier to address both substantial fibrosis detection and cirrhosis staging. Evaluated via 10 random train (90%)/ test (10%) splits on 360 subjects from the CARE 2025 Liver Track 4 cohort, our DINOv3-based framework significantly outperforms all baselines, achieving the best classification accuracy of 78.4% for S1 and 75.8% for S4.

</details>


### [31] [Multi-Modal Controlled Coherent Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11439)
*Yifei Liu,Qiong Cao,Hongwei Yi,Huaiguang Jiang,Changxing Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOCO：一种基于扩散模型的多模态3D数字人动作生成框架，通过解耦式迭代去噪整合语音、文本与轨迹输入，无需对齐多模态数据即可生成协调自然的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可自然地同时行走与交谈，但现有3D虚拟人动作生成方法受限于对齐多模态数据的稀缺，通常采用顺序组合或加权求和的方式融合各模态动作，导致动作不匹配或缺乏真实感。因此需要一种能在无对齐多模态数据条件下处理并发多模态输入的方法。

Method: 提出MOCO扩散框架，可同时处理语音音频、文本描述与轨迹数据。核心创新在于解耦动作生成过程：在每个去噪步骤中，扩散模型基于输入噪声为各模态独立生成动作，并按预定义的空间规则拼装身体部位；组合后的动作再经扩散处理，作为下一步去噪的输入噪声。该迭代机制使各模态能在整体动作语境中不断修正自身贡献，逐步实现跨模态的动作协调。

Result: 在专门构建的多模态基准上评估，实验结果表明MOCO优于现有基线方法，推动了3D虚拟人多模态动作生成领域的发展。

Conclusion: MOCO通过解耦-组合-再扩散的迭代策略，解决了多模态动作生成中对齐数据依赖与动作不协调问题，能够生成连贯同步、自然流畅的3D虚拟人动作，并在多模态基准上取得优于基线的性能。

Abstract: It is natural for humans to walk and talk simultaneously. This paper tackles the challenge of replicating such natural behaviors in 3D avatar motion generation driven by concurrent multimodal inputs, such as a text description of a man walking alongside speech audio. Existing methods, constrained by the scarcity of aligned multimodal data, typically combine motions from individual modalities sequentially or through weighted sums. However, they often result in mismatched or unrealistic movements. To overcome these limitations, we propose MOCO, a novel diffusion-based framework capable of processing multiple simultaneous inputs, including speech audio, text descriptions, and trajectory data, to generate coherent and lifelike motions without requiring aligned multimodal data. Our key innovation lies in decoupling the motion generation process. During each denoising step, the diffusion model independently generates motions for each modality from the input noise and assembles the body parts according to predefined spatial rules. The resulting combined motion is then diffused and serves as the input noise for the subsequent denoising step. This iterative approach enables each modality to refine its contribution within the context of the overall motion, progressively harmonizing movements across modalities. Consequently, the generated motions become increasingly natural and fluid with each iteration, achieving coherent and synchronized behaviors. We evaluate our approach using a purpose-built multimodal benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate that MOCO outperforms existing baselines, advancing the field of multimodal motion generation for 3D avatars.

</details>


### [32] [Recursive Code World Models: Building Complex Worlds through Recursive Scene Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11499)
*Zhiqi Li,Yuxuan Liao,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出递归代码世界模型（RCWM）框架，利用递归场景程序表示与递归调用自身的构建求解器，从单张参考图像重建复杂3D世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码世界模型将世界表示为可执行程序，但该表示本身无法决定如何构建复杂世界，缺乏从视觉证据构建可执行世界的构建原则。

Method: RCWM将递归场景程序（RSP）表示与递归求解器耦合。RSP以组合式场景代码表示可执行世界；每次求解调用遵循"整体—递归重建未解决部分—回归整体精修组合"的全局—局部—全局递归流程。参考对齐视图跨层级传播共享相机投影；父级回归处理局部精修后出现的边界、空间关系与共享误差；视觉-语言编码代理通过比较参考图像与场景渲染来指导精修、递归下降与返回。

Result: 在复杂场景上，RCWM优于先前的基于代码的图像到场景重建方法。消融研究支持递归构建的收益，并表明更深的调用可改善更细尺度的重建。

Conclusion: RCWM为从视觉证据构建复杂可执行世界提供了递归构建原则。

Abstract: Code world models represent worlds as executable programs, but this representation alone does not determine how to construct a complex world. We introduce Recursive Code World Models (RCWM), a framework for reconstructing complex 3D worlds in code from a single reference image. RCWM couples a Recursive Scene Program (RSP) representation with a construction solver that recursively calls itself. An RSP represents the executable world as compositional scene code, while each solver call follows the same complete process: establish the whole, recursively reconstruct unresolved parts, and revisit the whole to refine their composition. This global-local-global recursion gives fine-scale structures their own perception-and-editing loops while preserving scene-wide geometry and relationships. Reference-aligned views propagate a shared camera projection across levels, while parent revisitation addresses boundaries, spatial relations, and shared errors that emerge after local refinement. A vision-language coding agent directly compares reference images with scene renders to guide refinement, recursive descent, and return. Across complex scenes, RCWM outperforms prior code-based image-to-scene reconstruction methods. Ablation studies further support the benefits of recursive construction and suggest that deeper calls can improve finer-scale reconstruction. RCWM provides a recursive construction principle for building complex executable worlds from visual evidence.

</details>


### [33] [Harnessing Intrinsic Subject-Aware Attention for Controllable Multi-Subject Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11507)
*Niange Yu,Ye Tian,Biaolong Chen,Miao Lu,Aixi Zhang,Hao Jiang,Yunhai Tong,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DIAL框架，通过挖掘DiT内部的Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map (ISGM) 实现无需重训的保真度强度控制，并免费构建偏好对用于强化学习，缓解多主体视频生成中的语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体视频生成存在两大挑战：保真度强度不可控，以及潜在的语义漂移。作者希望通过分析Diffusion Transformers的内部机制来同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 作者发现某些注意力块天然形成Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map (ISGM)，可精确定位参考主体。基于此提出Dual-phase Intrinsic Attention Leveraging (DIAL)框架：在低噪声阶段，用ISGM引导注意力机制，实现推理时无需重训的保真度强度精确控制；在高噪声阶段，用同一批映射以零额外成本自动构建偏好对用于强化学习，从而将模型注意力锚定到参考主体并缓解语义漂移。

Result: 在OpenS2V-Eval基准上，DIAL显著优于基线模型，持续提升身份一致性，并支持可控的保真度强度。

Conclusion: 利用DiT内部固有的空间定位信号，可在训练与推理两个阶段分别实现可控保真度与语义漂移抑制，为多主体视频生成提供了一种高效统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-subject video generation faces two key challenges: uncontrollable fidelity strength and potential semantic drift. We address these by analyzing the internal mechanisms of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs). We found that certain attention blocks naturally form an Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map (ISGM) that precisely locates reference subjects. Building on this insight, we propose Dual-phase Intrinsic Attention Leveraging (DIAL), a framework that uses these internal signals for both training and inference. In low-noise stages, we use ISGM to guide the attention mechanism, allowing precise control over fidelity strength during inference without retraining. In high-noise stages, we use these same maps to automatically build preference pairs at no additional cost for Reinforcement Learning (RL). This RL procedure effectively anchors the model's attention to reference subjects and mitigates semantic drift. Extensive experiments show that DIAL significantly outperforms baseline models on the OpenS2V-Eval benchmark, consistently improving identity consistency and enabling controllable fidelity strength.

</details>


### [34] [Prototype Matters: Modality-unified Prototype Self-distillation for Unsupervised Visible-infrared Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11514)
*Menglin Wang,Xiaojin Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PoSeD框架，通过模态统一原型对比与原型引导自蒸馏，实现无监督可见光-红外行人重识别中更可靠的跨模态关联与模态不变性学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督可见光-红外行人重识别中，跨模态关联估计至关重要。现有最优传输方法采用硬标签分配，过于刚性且未考虑聚类噪声；同时仅施加跨模态对比也有缺陷，无法联合优化模态内与模态间的相似度关系。

Method: 提出统一框架：(1) 用模态统一的原型替代跨模态原型进行对比，同时联合优化模态内和模态间的相似度关系以增强模态不变性；(2) 以自身原型作为稳定教师，通过原型引导的在线自蒸馏精炼实例-原型关系；两部分在统一框架内联合优化。

Result: 在标准VI-ReID基准上进行了大量对比与分析，验证了所提方法的有效性。代码已开源于 https://github.com/Terminator8758/PoSeD。

Conclusion: 通过充分挖掘原型信息，模态统一原型对比与原型引导自蒸馏相结合，构成一个简单而有效的模型，缓解了硬标签分配与单一跨模态对比的局限。

Abstract: Estimating reliable cross-modality association is crucial to unsupervised visible-infrared person re-ID. While optimal transport is shown to be a practical solution for cross-modality association, it suffers from the rigidness of hard label assignment without considering the impact of cluster noise. Moreover, enforcing only cross-modality contrast is also suboptimal, as it fails to jointly optimize the similarity relation within and across modality. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for cross-modality learning by well exploitation of prototypes: First, instead of contrasting with cross-modality prototypes, we show that modality-unified prototypical contrast facilitates better modality invariance by jointly and simultaneously optimizing similarity relation within and across-modality. Taking self-prototype as a steady teacher, we further refine the instance-prototype online relation through prototype-guided self-distillation. The two components are optimized in a unified framework, leading to a simple yet effective model. On standard VI-ReID benchmarks, we perform extensive comparison and analysis, validating the effectiveness of our proposed method. Code is available at: https://github.com/Terminator8758/PoSeD.

</details>


### [35] [Learning Interaction between Image and Layout Priors for Joint Image-Layout Generation in Design Templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11519)
*Shirong Yang,Bo Yang,Ying Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InterIL，将背景图与版面布局在单一生成过程中联合生成，通过可学习通信模块连接冻结的预训练图像与布局扩散模型主干，显式建模双向图文布局交互，并支持测试时引导，显著提升模板的和谐度与真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平面设计生成多采用顺序范式，逐个生成设计元素，难以忠实刻画背景与布局之间的依赖关系及联合分布，从而限制生成模板的质量。

Method: InterIL在单一生成过程中联合生成背景图像与版面布局两种模态；用可学习通信模块连接预训练图像扩散模型与布局扩散模型的主干，显式建模双向图像-布局交互。训练时冻结两个主干以保留并利用大规模单模态先验，仅更新通信模块。模型不引入设计专属归纳偏置，并引入测试时引导策略以施加用户偏好。

Result: 实验表明，相比先前方法，模型在图像、布局及图文布局协调性上均取得显著更优结果，输出更接近真实样本，并可在推理时无需重训练地灵活满足用户偏好。

Conclusion: 联合生成图像与布局并仅训练交互模块，能有效捕捉联合图像-布局分布，提升设计模板的构图和谐度与真实感，且具备无重训练的推理期偏好控制能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of graphic design template creation, which generates a background image and a layout of foreground elements over the background to form a harmonious composition from an input text.
  Prior work on graphic design generation mostly adopts a sequential paradigm, where design elements are generated sequentially. We argue that such a sequential scheme falls short of faithfully capturing the dependency between the background and layout (and thus the joint image-layout distribution), which limits the quality of generated design templates.
