  To overcome this limitation, we propose a model, InterIL, which jointly generates the two modalities, background image and layout, in a single generative process. The novel design of our joint model connects the backbones of pretrained image and layout diffusion models with a learnable communication module to explicitly model bidirectional image-layout interaction. During training, the image and layout backbones are frozen to maintain and leverage the vast pretrained single-modality prior knowledge, while only the communication module is updated, so that the model can focus on learning image-layout interaction and thereby better capture the joint image-layout distribution for improved composition harmony.
  Our model has no design-specific inductive bias, which allows it to better preserve the original characteristics of realistic designs. We further introduce a test-time guidance strategy to enable users to impose their specific preferences on generated results.
  Our experiments show that, compared with prior approaches, our model can generate significantly better results in terms of image, layout and image-layout harmonization, producing outputs closer to real samples. We also demonstrate the flexibility of our model in enforcing user preferences at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [36] [A Comparative Evaluation of Pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks for Melanoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11550)
*Wagner Moreno Schmitz,Marco Antonio de Castro Barbosa,Thiago Magalhães Amaral,Dalcimar Casanova,Jefferson Tales Oliva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估5种预训练CNN在皮肤病变分类中的表现，发现模型性能因影像模态（皮肤镜/组织病理）而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑色素瘤早期诊断对提高患者生存率至关重要，但病变类型间视觉相似度高、图像采集条件多变，导致准确区分黑色素瘤与其他皮肤病变仍是临床难题。尽管已有众多预训练CNN架构，但针对特定成像模态如何选择最合适的架构仍是未解决的挑战。

Method: 在HAM10000、ISIC 2018和CR-AI4SkIN数据集上，采用统一的训练协议，评估ResNet50、VGG16、VGG19、MobileNet和InceptionV3五种预训练CNN架构，分别针对皮肤镜图像和组织病理图像进行皮肤病变分类。

Result: 皮肤镜图像上准确率为71%（InceptionV3，ISIC 2018）至84%（ResNet50，HAM10000）；组织病理图像（CR-AI4SkIN）上准确率为72%（VGG19）至83%（ResNet50）。结果表明模型性能在皮肤镜与组织病理模态间存在差异，在皮肤镜图像上表现相近的架构在组织病理数据上表现不同。

Conclusion: 预训练CNN性能依赖于成像模态，架构选择需结合具体影像类型；在皮肤镜图像上表现相似的模型未必在组织病理图像上同样有效。

Abstract: Early diagnosis of melanoma is critical for improving patient survival rates. However, accurately distinguishing melanoma from other skin lesions remains a significant clinical challenge due to the high visual similarity among lesion types and variability in image acquisition conditions. Artificial intelligence, particularly machine learning, has emerged as a promising tool to support dermatological diagnosis by automating feature extraction from medical images. Among the available approaches, convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have demonstrated strong performance in image classification tasks, making them well-suited for analyzing both dermatoscopic and histopathological images, given their ability to capture hierarchical visual patterns relevant to lesion characterization. Nevertheless, despite numerous pre-trained CNN architectures having been proposed, selecting the most appropriate one for a given imaging modality remains an open challenge. In this study, we evaluate pre-trained convolutional neural networks (CNNs) for skin lesion classification using dermatoscopic and histopathological image datasets. Experiments were conducted on the HAM10000, ISIC 2018, and CR-AI4SkIN datasets, evaluating the ResNet50, VGG16, VGG19, MobileNet, and InceptionV3 architectures under the same training protocol. The experimental evaluation showed that the models achieved accuracies ranging from 71% (InceptionV3 on ISIC 2018) to 84% (ResNet50 on HAM10000) on dermatoscopic images. For histopathological images, accuracies ranged from 72% (VGG19) to 83% (ResNet50) on the CR-AI4SkIN dataset. The results demonstrate that model performance differs between dermatoscopic and histopathological image modalities, showing that architectures exhibiting similar performance on dermatoscopic images exhibit different performance on histopathological data.

</details>


### [37] [Learn the Solid, Not the File: Canonical Inputs for Neural Networks on CAD Boundary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11573)
*Heinrich Jiang,Hager Yasser Mohamed,Alexander Hitt,Valeriia Lomakina,Henning Jiang,Jennifer Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示现有B-rep编码器对同一实体不同B-rep表示缺乏鲁棒性，并提出一种从实体本身导出的规范区域图表示，在保持基准性能的同时实现了对重新划分与刚性变换的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CAD系统采用边界表示（B-rep）作为参数化3D模型的标准格式，但同一实体可由不同B-rep表示：不同操作、几何内核重建文件、导出设置重新划分面等都会产生不同的B-rep，而底层实体不变。作者希望解决编码器对这种表示差异不鲁棒的问题。

Method: 作者首先系统性地验证现有B-rep编码器在标准基准扰动、CAD软件固有的自然变化，以及通过FreeCAD构建的人类设计数据集（同一零件的不同建模方式）下的鲁棒性；随后提出"规范区域图"（canonical region graph）作为输入表示，其节点、特征与坐标系均从实体本身导出，并给出对重新划分（repartitioning）和刚性运动的理论不变性保证。

Result: 现有B-rep编码器在上述扰动下性能常出现灾难性崩塌，表明其不鲁棒；而所提出的规范区域图在标准基准上与最强基线持平，并在所有测试扰动下保持稳定。

Conclusion: B-rep表示的非唯一性是现有编码器的关键弱点；将表示锚定到实体本身（规范区域图）可在不牺牲标准基准性能的前提下获得表示不变性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-rep) is the standard format used by modern CAD systems for parametric 3D models. It turns out, the exact same solid can be represented by different B-reps: for example, two engineers using different operations, a geometry kernel rebuilding the file, and an export setting repartitioning faces will lead to different B-reps even though the underlying solid remains the same.
  We show that existing B-rep encoders are not robust to variation in the B-rep with the same solid on perturbations applied to standard benchmarks, naturally occurring variations inherent to CAD software, and differences in how designers model the same part via a human dataset we created in FreeCAD. The performance of popular B-rep encoders often collapses catastrophically.
  We propose the canonical region graph, an input representation whose nodes, features and coordinate frame are derived from the solid itself and show theoretical invariance guarantees on repartitioning and rigid motions. It matches the strongest baseline on standard benchmarks, and is stable under every perturbation we test.

</details>


### [38] [MMGait: Benchmarking and Unifying Gait Recognition across Heterogeneous Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11601)
*Saihui Hou,Chenye Wang,Qingyuan Cai,Aoqi Li,Yongzhen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多传感器步态基准MMGait（含可见光、红外、深度、LiDAR、雷达）与统一识别框架OmniGait++，实现单模态、跨模态与多模态步态识别的统一建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态识别多依赖RGB视频或其衍生的轮廓与姿态，人体行走产生的光度、几何与运动等多源异构线索无法在RGB为中心的基准上被系统考察；且单模态、模态对与融合配置通常需分别训练专家模型，存在可扩展性问题。

Method: 构建MMGait基准，使五类传感器观测在序列级对齐，覆盖外观、轮廓、几何、运动与身体结构模态，并采用共享的冒充者增强协议评估单模态、跨模态定向检索与多模态识别；提出Omni-Modal Gait Recognition任务与OmniGait++模型，采用模态专属前端加共享身份编码器，辅以锚点引导融合模块，可在无需帧级同步的情况下聚合不同规模与组成的模态子集，并用单次联合训练覆盖三种识别设置。

Result: 实验显示模态排序随探测条件变化，跨模态对齐仍然困难，融合常带来互补增益；OmniGait++在多数共享设置下与任务专属专家相当，并可扩展至固定模态对模型无法支持的高基数融合。

Conclusion: MMGait可作为异构步态感知的通用测试平台，验证了在模态可用性变化条件下实现统一步态识别的可行性。

Abstract: Gait recognition is commonly studied using RGB videos or their derived silhouettes and poses. Yet human walking produces heterogeneous photometric, geometric, and motion cues that cannot be systematically examined with RGB-centered benchmarks. We present MMGait, a large-scale multi-sensor benchmark that brings visible, infrared, depth, LiDAR, and radar observations into sequence-level correspondence. It provides diverse modalities spanning appearance, contours, geometry, motion, and body structure. Under a shared impostor-augmented protocol, we evaluate single-modal recognition, cross-modal recognition via directed retrieval, and multi-modal recognition using task-specific experts. Across settings, modality rankings vary with probe conditions, cross-modal alignment remains difficult, and fusion often provides complementary gains. This analysis exposes a scalability problem: individual modalities, modality pairs, and fusion configurations are typically handled by separately trained experts. We formulate Omni-Modal Gait Recognition, which unifies single-modal, cross-modal, and multi-modal recognition within a shared identity space. OmniGait++ uses modality-specific front ends followed by a shared identity encoder to preserve modality-dependent cues while learning comparable identity descriptors. An anchor-guided fusion module aggregates modality subsets of varying size without frame-level synchronization. A jointly trained checkpoint covers all three recognition settings and accommodates modality subsets of different compositions and cardinalities. Experiments show OmniGait++ remains competitive with task-specific experts in many shared settings and extends to higher-cardinality fusion unavailable to fixed-pair models. The results establish MMGait as a common testbed for heterogeneous gait sensing and demonstrate the feasibility of unified recognition under varying modality availability.

</details>

*Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Jintao Chen,Xu Wang,Deyuan Liu,Jungang Li,Dechuang Chen,Ming Lin,Jingjiang Zhou,Haopeng Jin,Qi Jia,Xiaohang Wang,Yaole Wang,Zhanqiang Zhang,Ran Li,Zhengkun Huang,Shuyue Xiong,Yuji Wang,Zikun Dai,Hui He,Yang Luo,Mang Ning,Weiqi Feng,Chengyang Ye,Xinyue Lin,Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Hengkai Tan,Zeyuan Wang,Chendong Xiang,Kaiwen Zheng,Zhijie Deng,Fan Bao,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以满足实时交互与流式编辑需求，作者希望将数字人驱动与视频编辑统一到实时框架下，并进一步验证实时空间视频生成的可行性。

Method: 提出 Vidu S2 系统，含 Vidu S2-Avatar 与 Vidu S2-Editing 两个实时模型：Avatar 支持 720p 实时生成、可随时更新的动态参考与更强指令跟随（如舞蹈）；Editing 支持对视频流进行实时风格渲染、服装替换、角色替换与背景替换；并探索两者的实时空间视频生成。

Conclusion: Vidu S2 在实时数字人交互与实时视频编辑任务上达到领先水平，并初步验证了实时空间视频生成的可行性。

Abstract: We present Vidu S2, which comprises Vidu S2-Avatar, a real-time interactive digital-character model, and Vidu S2-Editing, a real-time video editing model. Moreover, we explore the feasibility of real-time spatial video generation for both Vidu S2-Avatar and Vidu S2-Editing. Compared with Vidu S1, Vidu S2-Avatar supports real-time 720p video generation, generation with dynamic references that can be updated at any moment, and stronger instruction following, such as dancing. Vidu S2-Editing supports editing a video stream in real time, including style rendering, clothing replacement, character replacement, and background replacement. Experiments show that Vidu S2 outperforms all baselines. A playable online demo is available at https://vidu.com/vidu-stream.

</details>


### [40] [Self-Supervised Cardiac Phase Detection via Single-Parameter Latent Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11650)
*John Bonnici,Matthew Baugh,Aleksandra Kulbaka,Sarah Cechnicka,Bernhard Kainz,Alberto Gomez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将心脏相位视为一维信号，约束潜空间运动成单参数"潜轨道"，经正弦非线性映射得到有序振荡信号，实现无标注下ED/ES直接识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图中ED和ES的准确识别是心室功能量化的基础，但人工选取主观性强、存在临床显著的观察者间变异。现有自监督方法或强制严格周期轨迹（可解释但假设过强），或学习无约束低维运动子空间（相位隐式，需事后几何处理恢复ED/ES）。

Method: 把"心脏相位是一维信号"这一生理观察转化为先验：将潜运动分量约束为单参数潜轨道，即由有界标量相位变量索引的全局线性潜空间轨迹；再通过正弦非线性映射该变量，得到具有一致时间顺序的振荡运动信号，从而直接从学习到的相位信号识别ED和ES。

Result: 在EchoNet-Dynamic上无标注训练，最小化单参数心脏相位模型学得有效潜轨道，ED定位显著优于此前最优方法，ES定位与之持平，同时使用更受约束的表示和更少训练轮数。

Conclusion: 原理性的生理归纳偏置可匹配甚至超越更复杂表示的性能，说明约束性先验在可解释的心脏相位建模中具有价值。

Abstract: Accurate identification of end-diastole (ED) and end-systole (ES) in echocardiography underpins the quantification of ventricular function, yet manual selection of these key frames is subjective and introduces clinically significant inter-operator variability. Recent self-supervised methods either prescribe strict periodic trajectories or learn an unconstrained low-dimensional motion subspace from reconstruction or registration objectives. The former offers interpretability but imposes restrictive assumptions on temporal progression, whereas the latter leaves cardiac phase implicit and ED/ES must be recovered through post-hoc geometric processing of the learned trajectory. We translate the physiological observation that cardiac phase is a one-dimensional signal into a prior by constraining the latent motion component to a single-parameter latent orbit, i.e., a global linear trajectory in latent space indexed by a bounded scalar phase variable. Mapping this variable through a sinusoidal nonlinearity yields an oscillatory motion signal with consistent temporal ordering, enabling direct identification of ED and ES from the learned phase signal. This inductive bias allows the model to capture an interpretable representation of the cardiac cycle, while maintaining flexibility to capture irregular heartbeats. Trained on EchoNet-Dynamic without annotations, our minimal single-parameter cardiac phase model learns an effective latent orbit, significantly improves upon the previous state of the art in ED localisation and matches it in ES localisation while using a more constrained representation and fewer training epochs. This demonstrates that a principled physiological inductive bias can match or exceed the performance of more complex representations. Code is available at: https://github.com/BonniciJ/OrbitalEcho/

</details>


### [41] [Multimodal Taxonomic Conditioning for Generative Plankton Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11673)
*Daniela Ivanova,Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对浮游生物成像数据的长尾分布问题，本文采用基于分类学条件生成的合成图像方法：先在大规模浮游生物语料上以排序对比目标适配CLIP编码器（支持深层、不规则分类层级），再冻结该编码器以条件化一个参数高效的扩散Transformer，从分布保真度和下游分类器效用两个维度评估合成样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化浮游生物成像产生的数据集呈严重长尾分布，最具生态学意义的稀有类群图像数量过少，难以可靠地训练或评估分类器。

Method: 生成以分类学为条件的合成浮游生物图像：在大规模浮游生物语料上适配CLIP编码器，采用扩展到深层、不规则（ragged）分类层级的排序对比目标进行训练，随后冻结该编码器，用以条件化一个参数高效的扩散Transformer（diffusion transformer）。

Result: 评估涵盖两方面：合成样本的分布保真度（distributional fidelity），以及合成数据对下游分类器的效用（downstream classifier utility）。

Conclusion: 通过分类学条件化的参数高效扩散生成模型，可为稀有浮游生物类群补充合成图像，以缓解长尾数据下的训练与评估困难；其有效性由分布保真度与下游分类器效用共同衡量。

Abstract: Automated plankton imaging produces severely long-tailed datasets, where the rare taxa of greatest ecological interest have too few images to train or evaluate classifiers reliably. We generate synthetic plankton imagery conditioned on taxonomy: a CLIP encoder is adapted on a large plankton corpus with a ranked contrastive objective extended to deep, ragged taxonomies, then frozen to condition a parameter-efficient diffusion transformer. We evaluate synthetic sample quality on distributional fidelity and downstream classifier utility.

</details>


### [42] [Single-Stream Multi-Feature Fusion with Temporal Robustness for Gait Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11680)
*Shirong Lyu,Silu Quan,Yixuan Ding,Chengpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SV-GCN：单流多特征融合、时间不变的3D骨架步态情感识别框架，通过帧内相对运动特征与全局掩码引导的有效帧时空图卷积模块，实现帧率不敏感与跨序列长度/帧率泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D骨架步态情感识别面临标注成本高、数据稀缺，以及对异构数据泛化能力差的问题；此外，多流结构复杂、序列长度与帧率变化导致模型不稳定。

Method: SV-GCN为单流多特征融合框架：1）引入帧内相对运动特征以消除帧率敏感性；2）在浅层嵌入异构线索，实现早期融合，避免多流复杂度；3）针对变长序列设计全局掩码引导的有效帧时空图卷积模块，首次在该领域引入帧率不敏感性。

Result: 在E-Gait数据集上性能与当前最优方法相当，并在不同序列长度与帧率下表现出强泛化能力，为在大规模骨架动作识别数据集上预训练提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: SV-GCN以单流早期融合与有效帧时空图卷积实现帧率不敏感与异构泛化，验证了向大规模骨架动作识别预训练迁移的可行性。

Abstract: 3D skeleton-based gait emotion recognition faces high annotation costs, data scarcity, and poor generalization on heterogeneous data. This paper proposes SV-GCN, a single-stream multi-feature fusion framework with temporal invariance. We introduce intra-frame relative motion features to eliminate frame-rate sensitivity and embed heterogeneous cues at shallow layers, enabling early fusion without multi-stream complexity. For variable-length sequences, we design a global mask-guided valid-frame spatio-temporal graph convolution module, introducing frame-rate insensitivity for the first time in this domain. On the E-Gait dataset, our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art while demonstrating strong generalization across varying sequence lengths and frame rates, offering a viable pathway for pre-training on large-scale skeleton-based action recognition datasets.

</details>


### [43] [Language-Augmented Semantic Priors for B-Spline Surface Fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11708)
*Yunzhong Lou,Yusheng Luo,Jiahao Li,Yu Song,Xiangdong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LASP框架，用LLM从建模历史中推断结构化B样条先验，作为语义推理层指导传统CAD曲面拟合，实验表明语言驱动的推理可作为几何求解的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAD几何内核在曲面拟合与参数化中高度依赖预设的启发式初始化，且建模历史中蕴含的过程语义与设计意图在几何生成时被大量忽略，导致高层设计意图与求解器可执行的几何构型之间存在鸿沟，拟合结果往往次优且语义不一致。

Method: LASP将过程建模历史翻译为包含设计意图、几何上下文与功能关系的文本描述，再用微调后的LLM预测结构化的B样条先验参数；采用两阶段训练方案，融合局部几何规律与长程上下文依赖，输出可解释且语义连贯的先验。该框架不修改几何内核，而是作为现有求解器之上的语义推理层。

Result: 实验显示，相比传统机器学习方案，语言驱动的推理能够为几何求解提供强有力的归纳偏置，引导B样条拟合获得更契合设计目标、语义一致性更高的解。

Conclusion: 语言驱动的推理可作为几何求解的有效归纳偏置，LASP确立了现代CAD系统中语言引导几何优化的新范式。

Abstract: The use of B-splines and Non-Uniform Rational B-Splines surfaces constitutes the mathematical foundation of contemporary computer-aided design (CAD) systems. Despite long-term progress, geometric kernels in traditional CAD still rely heavily on predetermined heuristic initialization for surface fitting and parameterization. Meanwhile, the procedural semantics and design intent encoded in modeling histories are largely ignored during geometry generation. This disconnect creates a gap between high-level design intent and solver-executable geometric configuration, often leading to suboptimal and semantically inconsistent fitting results. To bridge this gap, we introduce LASP, a Language-Augmented Semantic Priors framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to infer structured, solver-usable B-spline priors from procedural modeling histories. Rather than modifying the geometric kernel itself, LASP operates as a semantic reasoning layer above existing solvers. It first translates modeling histories into rich textual descriptions that capture design intent, geometric context, and functional relationships, and then uses a fine-tuned LLM to predict structured B-spline prior parameters. LASP is trained through a two-stage scheme that combines local geometric regularities with long-range contextual dependencies, producing priors that are both interpretable and semantically coherent. This approach furnishes inductive signals that direct the conventional B-spline fitting process toward solutions that more accurately encapsulate the intended design objectives and demonstrate heightened semantic coherence. Compared to traditional machine learning schemes, the experiments demonstrate that language-driven reasoning can serve as a powerful inductive bias for geometric solving, establishing a new paradigm of language-guided geometric optimization in modern CAD systems.

</details>


### [44] [Revisiting Avatar-As-Image: High-Fidelity Registration is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11722)
*Margaret Kostyrko,Yuxuan Xue,Garvita Tiwari,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AvaImg，一个多阶段优化管线，首次实现从任意着装人体扫描中生成高保真SMPL(-X)+D配准及UV纹理与位移贴图，并验证其Avatar-as-Image表示与图像基础模型兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3D着装人体表示为标准化2D UV纹理和位移贴图极具吸引力，因其可复用预训练图像网络进行3D虚拟人的处理、生成与编辑。但该表示依赖通过高保真配准将扫描准确对齐并建立对应关系，这一前提从未被满足，被认为是此前基于UV方法质量受限的根因。目前尚无公开方法能从任意着装扫描中生成带UV纹理的高保真SMPL(-X)+D配准。

Method: AvaImg为多阶段优化管线：通过有符号绕数（signed winding numbers）强制"身体在衣物内"约束，并借助三级效率级联使其可行（运行时间约减少10倍，存储约节省95%）；采用由粗到细的位移优化恢复精细表面细节，最终输出带UV纹理与位移贴图的SMPL(-X)+D配准。

Result: 在六个数据集上，AvaImg在身体拟合、形状估计和表面配准方面均超越所有基线；生成的带纹理配准与扫描几乎难以区分（PSNR=34.48dB）。为验证Avatar-as-Image表示与图像基础模型的兼容性，作者用冻结的FLUX VAE自编码UV贴图，相对扫描仅增加0.76mm Chamfer误差，并表明所得贴图处于自然图像分布内。

Conclusion: AvaImg填补了高保真着装人体UV配准的空白，验证了Avatar-as-Image表示可兼容图像基础模型，支持利用2D生成先验进行3D虚拟人生成。代码、数据与Singularity容器将公开于 https://yuxuan-xue.com/avaimg。

Abstract: The representation of 3D clothed humans as standardized 2D UV texture and displacement maps over an underlying body model has long been studied. This compact representation is enticing as it enables pretrained image networks to process, generate, and edit 3D avatars, but is only useful if scans are accurately aligned and brought into correspondence via high-fidelity registration. This prerequisite has never been met, which we argue explains the limited quality of prior UV-based methods for clothed humans. Despite its significance, no public method produces high-fidelity SMPL(-X)+D registrations with UV texture from arbitrary clothed scans. We present AvaImg, a multi-stage optimization pipeline, to close this gap: it enforces body-inside-clothing constraint via signed winding numbers, made viable by a three-level efficiency cascade (~10x runtime reduced, ~95% storage saved), and recovers fine surface detail using coarse-to-fine displacement optimization. AvaImg outperforms all baselines in body fitting, shape estimation, and surface registration across six datasets, yielding textured registrations near-indistinguishable from scans (PSNR=34.48dB). For validation of AvaImg's Avatar-as-Image representation as imminently compatible with image foundation models, we auto-encode our UV maps via the frozen FLUX VAE. This achieves only 0.76mm added Chamfer error relative to scan and shows that the resulting maps lie within natural-image distributions, supporting the use of 2D generative priors for 3D avatar generation. Code, data, and Singularity containers will be at https://yuxuan-xue.com/avaimg.

</details>


### [45] [Logit Refiner: Improving Visual Autoregressive Models via Intra-Scale Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11804)
*Meimingwei Li,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Felix Krause,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出VAR的并行尺度内解码等价于平均场近似，丢弃了同尺度token的空间依赖，导致局部不连贯；提出轻量自回归模块Logit Refiner，恢复尺度内依赖，仅增~10%参数与<5%训练算力即可提升多种预训练VAR主干与文生图模型的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Visual Autoregressive Models（VAR）通过next-scale prediction在每个尺度内并行生成所有token。作者认为这种并行解码是一种mean-field式近似，忽略了同一尺度内token之间的空间依赖，因而无论主干容量多大，都会产生局部不连贯的样本——这是解码规则本身的局限，而非模型能力不足。因此需要一种方法来恢复尺度内依赖，且不依赖重新训练或增大模型容量。

Method: 提出Logit Refiner：一个轻量自回归模块，以冻结的预训练VAR主干特征为条件，对同一尺度内的token进行顺序采样，从而恢复intra-scale依赖。该模块仅增加约10%参数、消耗不到基座模型5%的训练算力，可直接插入任意预训练VAR检查点而无需重训。通过受控消融实验隔离出“联合尺度内采样”这一关键因素，排除额外容量或额外训练的贡献。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256类别条件生成任务上，覆盖310M至2B参数的多种主干，Refiner均一致提升生成质量；使1.1B参数模型超越规模两倍于它的模型。方法进一步泛化到text-to-image生成，证实mean-field瓶颈在VAR变体中普遍存在，并可被有效缓解。

Conclusion: VAR的并行尺度内解码存在mean-field瓶颈，是其解码规则的固有缺陷；通过轻量的Logit Refiner恢复尺度内联合采样，可在几乎不增加成本的情况下显著提升生成质量，并在不同主干规模与文生图任务上验证了有效性和泛化性。项目页面：https://compvis.github.io/logit-refiner/

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive Models (VAR) generate images through next-scale prediction, producing all tokens within each scale in parallel. We show that this parallel decoding constitutes a mean-field-style approximation that discards spatial dependencies among same-scale tokens, causing locally incoherent samples regardless of backbone capacity -- a limitation of the decoding rule. Addressing this limitation, we introduce the Logit Refiner, a lightweight autoregressive module that restores intra-scale dependencies by sequentially sampling tokens conditioned on frozen backbone features. Adding only ~10% parameters and less than 5% of the base model's training compute, it plugs into any pretrained VAR checkpoint without retraining. Controlled ablations isolate joint intra-scale sampling -- rather than additional capacity or training -- as the critical ingredient. Across backbones from 310M to 2B parameters on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256, the refiner consistently improves generation quality, enabling a 1.1B-parameter model to surpass one twice its size. The approach further generalizes to text-to-image generation, confirming that the mean-field bottleneck persists across VAR variants and is effectively alleviated by our method.
  Project page: https://compvis.github.io/logit-refiner/

</details>


### [46] [Guided Super-Resolution of Digital Elevation Models with Diffusion-Based Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11886)
*Armand Mihai Nicolicioiu,Dominik Narnhofer,Nando Metzger,Daniel Panangian,Ksenia Bittner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于去噪扩散模型的引导式超分辨率方法，利用高分辨率光谱影像将5米DSM增强至0.5米分辨率，在中欧多城市实验中实现了结构细节更丰富、地表几何更精确的高质量DSM重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率数字地表模型（DSM）在城市分析、三维建筑重建和基础设施监测中具有重要价值，但数据获取成本高、流程复杂，导致其可获得性受限。与此同时，商业卫星提供的粗分辨率DSM广泛可得，高分辨率光学影像也日益丰富。这种分辨率上的不匹配（粗DSM vs 高分辨率影像）为低分辨率DSM的超分辨率重建提供了契机，而传统插值或滤波方法难以恢复真实的地表细节。

Method: 采用去噪扩散（denoising diffusion）框架进行引导式超分辨率：以5米DSM为输入、高分辨率光谱影像为引导，将仅存在于影像中的信息（如清晰轮廓和详细屋顶结构）迁移到高程图中，输出0.5米分辨率DSM。该方法区别于传统插值或滤波，属于guided super-resolution，并借助基础图像先验（foundational image priors）完成重建。

Result: 在中欧多个城市的实验表明，该方法生成的DSM质量高，结构细节更丰富，地表几何更准确，优于传统插值或滤波技术。

Conclusion: 引导式超分辨率结合基础图像先验，是重建高分辨率地表模型的有效途径，展示了利用广泛可得的粗DSM与高分辨率影像恢复精细地表结构的潜力。

Abstract: High-resolution digital surface models (DSMs) play an important role in urban analysis, 3D building reconstruction, and infrastructure monitoring, yet their availability remains limited due to the high cost and complexity of data acquisition. In contrast, coarse DSMs from commercial satellite missions are widely accessible, and high-resolution optical imagery is increasingly available from aerial and satellite platforms. We address the resulting mismatch in spatial resolution and propose a DSM superresolution approach that enhances 5 m DSMs to 0.5 m resolution, using guidance from high-resolution spectral images. Our method employs denoising diffusion to transfer information that is visible only in the image, like crisp outlines and detailed roof structures, into the elevation maps. In this way, surface details are reconstructed more accurately than with conventional interpolation or filtering techniques. Experiments on several cities in Central Europe demonstrate that the proposed approach produces high-quality DSMs with improved structural detail and accurate surface geometry. Our results highlight the potential of guided super-resolution with foundational image priors as a means of reconstructing high-resolution surface models.

</details>


### [47] [3D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11894)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DPS：首个直接由雷达方程立体角形式推导的可微点渲染器，在ColoRadar六场景上RA图像Pearson相关达0.587，为三个光学NVS基线的1.7–5.2倍，单张RTX 4090每场景约3分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达新视角合成需要渲染器同时满足物理保真、复数输出、多视角可优化三个条件，但尚无方法同时具备。可微蒙特卡洛光线追踪直接实现雷达前向模型、支持显式材质与复数输出，却无法扩展到多视角NVS优化；NeRF、哈希网格、3D高斯等光学NVS移植方案训练快，但丢弃相位、以不透明学习特征替代显式材质建模，仅能输出功率型距离-方位（RA）幅度。

Method: 3DPS从雷达方程标准立体角形式直接推导。每个定向3D点携带ITU-R P.2040材质模型并以闭式求值，所得复相量通过预计算点扩散函数（PSF）splat到距离门中。复数输出使渲染器与产品无关：同一优化场景经标准FFT流程可得到ADC、复数距离像（CRP）和RA输出，无需按格式重训练。

Result: 在ColoRadar六个室外场景的留出RA图像上，3DPS平均Pearson相关为0.587，是RadarSplat、Radar Fields、DART三个光学NVS基线的1.7至5.2倍。单张RTX 4090上每场景训练约3分钟。

Conclusion: 3DPS首次将物理保真、复数输出与多视角可优化统一于雷达可微点渲染，兼顾精度与训练效率，并通过复数输出统一支持多种雷达数据格式。

Abstract: Solving novel view synthesis (NVS) for millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires a renderer that is physically faithful, complex-valued, and multi-viewpoint-tractable. No prior method achieves these three properties simultaneously. Differentiable Monte Carlo (MC) ray tracers implement the radar forward model directly with explicit material modeling and complex outputs, but do not scale to the multi-view optimization NVS demands. Optical-NVS ports of NeRF, hash grids, and 3D Gaussians train fast but discard phase and replace explicit material modeling with opaque learned features, restricting them to power-only range-azimuth (RA) magnitudes. We propose 3D Point Splatting (3DPS), the first differentiable point renderer for radar, derived directly from the standard solid-angle form of the radar equation. Each oriented 3D point carries an ITU-R P.2040 material model, evaluated in closed form, with the resulting complex phasor splatted into range bins through a precomputed point spread function (PSF). The complex-valued output makes the renderer product-agnostic. The same optimized scene yields analog-to-digital converter (ADC), complex range profile (CRP), and RA outputs through standard fast Fourier transform (FFT) pipelines without retraining for each format. On six outdoor ColoRadar scenes, 3DPS reaches 0.587 mean Pearson correlation on held-out RA images. This is between 1.7x and 5.2x the three optical-NVS baselines (RadarSplat, Radar Fields, DART). Training takes approximately 3 minutes per scene on a single RTX 4090.

</details>


### [48] [Caption-once, Frames-on-Demand: Visual-Need Routing for Budget-Aware Agentic Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11899)
*Weitong Cai,Hang Zhang,Yukai Huang,Yiqiao Xie,Shan Gao,Jiankang Deng,Songcen Xu,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CFD（"标题一次，按需帧"）——一个预算感知的边缘-云智能体框架：边缘端离线单次生成双轨叙事索引，云端在查询时以"故事优先"循环推理并借助Visual-Need Router按需触发有限关键帧检索，从而在长视频理解中实现精度与效率的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的长视频理解需在严苛算力与带宽预算下推理数小时内容。视觉token下采样会破坏时序结构，纯文本视频记忆又会丢失细粒度视觉属性。作者观察到视觉-文本对偶性：语言记忆在长程时序结构上优于稠密帧，而像素对属性级感知仍具决定性。

Method: CFD框架：1）边缘端单次离线字幕生成，构建双轨叙事索引——事件级故事骨架 + 片段级微日志，缓存并跨查询复用，无需重复字幕生成；2）查询时云端MLLM在故事优先循环中推理索引，核心为轻量Visual-Need Router——逐查询门控模块，仅对感知类问题（外观、屏幕文字、属性消歧）触发有界关键帧检索，时序结构类问题保留在语言空间；3）该路由器将视觉访问变为一等、查询条件化的成本，无论视频多长都限制每查询帧消耗。

Result: 在长视频基准上展现了较强的准确率-效率权衡，同时大幅减少在线视觉处理量。

Conclusion: 利用视觉-文本对偶性，将长程时序推理置于语言空间、视觉感知按需检索，CFD在受限预算下实现了长视频理解的高效与准确，验证了"标题一次、帧按需"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Long-video understanding on edge devices must reason over hours of content under tight compute and bandwidth budgets. Subsampling visual tokens loses temporal structure, while text-only video memories lose fine-grained visual attributes. We observe a visual-textual duality: language memories carry long-range temporal structure better than dense frames, while pixels remain decisive for attribute-level perception. Building on this insight, we propose Caption-once, Frames-onDemand (CFD), a budget-aware edge-cloud agentic framework. The edge runs a single offline captioning pass that builds a dual-track narrative index, an event-level story skeleton plus a clip-level micro-log, cached and reused across queries without re-captioning. At query time, a cloud-side MLLM reasons over the index in a story-first loop centered on a lightweight Visual-Need Router: a per-query gating module that triggers bounded keyframe retrieval only for perceptual questions (appearance, on-screen text, attribute disambiguation) and keeps temporal-structural questions in language space. The router turns visual access into a first-class, query-conditioned cost, capping per-query frame consumption regardless of video length. Experiments on long-video benchmarks demonstrate strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs while substantially reducing online visual processing.

</details>


### [49] [SenseNova-U1.5: Towards Native Unified Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11929)
*Haiwen Diao,Jiahao Wang,Chenjing Ding,Hanming Deng,Jiangnan Chen,Ruixi Zhang,Ruohui Wang,Wenwen Tong,Xiangyu Fan,Yubo Wang,Yue Zhu,Yuwei Niu,Zhengqi Bai,Zhiqian Lin,Zhitao Yang,Zhongang Cai,Bo Yang,Chen Feng,Chengguang Lv,Guangjia Liu,Guanlin Wang,Hanyu Zhang,Haojia Yu,Hongcan Xiao,Hongli Wang,Huan Wu,Huaping Zhong,Jian Fang,Jianan Fan,Jiaqi Li,Jiefan Lu,Jing Zuo,Jingcheng Ni,Junxiang Xu,Linjun Dai,Mutian Xu,Peishen Yan,Penghao Wu,Ruijie Mao,Ruisi Wang,Shihao Bai,Shuang Yang,Shuya Yang,Shuyan Zheng,Silei Wu,Siying Li,Tao Chu,Tianbo Zhong,Tongxi Zhou,Weichao Luo,Weichen Fan,Wenhao Jia,Wenjie Gao,Xiangli Kong,Yan Li,Yang Yong,Zimo Wen,Zixuan Qian,Wenxiu Sun,Ruihao Gong,Quan Wang,Lewei Lu,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SenseNova-U1.5，一个 8B-MoT 原生统一多模态模型，采用无编码器、无 VAE 架构，在图像保真度、文本渲染、复杂构图、多参考编辑与交错生成等任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型多依赖编码器或 VAE 作为视觉接口，难以在完全端到端框架中兼顾理解与生成；作者希望验证原生统一建模能否让多模态理解能力迁移到视觉规划与创作。

Method: 构建 8B-MoT 的原生统一多模态模型，去除编码器与 VAE，通过空间一致的 patch 重建强化视觉接口；使用精挑细选的生成与编辑数据、改进任务表述、结构化提示增强，支持最高 4K 原生分辨率扩展训练；后训练阶段针对视觉美学、双语文本渲染、信息图生成与图像编辑训练专用专家，并通过多专家 on-policy 蒸馏整合能力。

Result: 在广泛评测中，SenseNova-U1.5 大幅提升图像保真度、文本渲染、复杂构图、多参考编辑与交错生成性能，同时改善指令遵循并保持主体身份、几何结构与未修改区域；尽管生成数据中结构化格式暴露有限，模型仍能有效泛化到长而复杂的结构化视觉指令。

Conclusion: 结果表明原生统一建模是通向在完全端到端框架内实现感知、推理与创作系统的可行路径。作者将开源训练代码，包括监督微调、强化学习与 on-policy 蒸馏。

Abstract: We launch SenseNova-U1.5, an 8B-MoT native unified multimodal model that understands, reasons about, and generates visual content within an encoder-free and VAE-free architecture. We strengthen its visual interface through spatially coherent patch reconstruction and scale its training with carefully curated generation and editing data, improved task formulation, structural prompt enhancement, and native resolutions of up to 4K. For post-training, we optimize specialized experts for visual aesthetics, bilingual text rendering, infographic generation, and image editing, and consolidate their capabilities through multi-expert on-policy distillation. Across extensive evaluations, SenseNova-U1.5 largely advances image fidelity, text rendering, complex composition, multi-reference editing, and interleaved generation, while improving instruction following and preserving subject identity, geometry, and unmodified regions. Despite limited exposure to structured formats in its generation data, SenseNova-U1.5 generalizes effectively to long, complex, and structured visual instructions, further proving that multimodal understanding can transfer to visual planning and creation. Together, these findings position native unified modelling as a promising path towards systems that perceive, reason and create within a fully end-to-end framework. We will open-source training code, including supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation.

</details>

### [50] [NCP-ArchPreview Technical Report: Moving towards Latent Space Language Models through Next Concept Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10715)
*The Intern-NCP Team,:,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Shuang Cheng,Xu Cheng,Beiya Dai,Yufan Feng,Kewen Ge,Ruijun Ge,Jiayi Huang,Yang Jiao,Dahua Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Yifan Liu,Yuliang Liu,Biqing Qi,Mowen Ruan,Junzhe Shen,Yunchong Song,Hao Sun,Zhongbo Tian,Yixuan Wang,Rubin Wei,Jiaxin Xiong,Kangyu Yang,Qian Yao,Qi Zhang,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NCP-ArchPreview，一种潜空间语言模型，在NTP基础上增加"下一概念预测"(NCP)目标，预测跨多个token的离散概念；规模达8.9B参数、5.73T token训练，性能优于OLMo-3-7B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归预训练仅依赖token级下一词预测(NTP)，缺乏显式的、更抽象的概念级学习目标；作者希望在保留标准token级自回归生成能力的同时，引入跨token的概念级预测任务，以提升训练效率与下游表现。

Method: 在NTP之外并行引入NCP目标：由模型隐状态通过乘积量化(product quantization)直接构建概念词表形成潜空间，并用专门的Concept Module预测未来概念；预测的概念再反馈到token级以引导后续生成；NTP与NCP端到端联合训练。模型扩展至8.9B参数，在Dolma-3的5.73T token上训练，并设置参数对齐的8.9B基线及消融实验。

Result: 仅消耗51.3%的训练token即达到OLMo-3-7B的最终预训练损失；完整训练后下游宏平均高出OLMo-3-7B 2.45分，GSM8K提升5.99分；消融实验显示潜空间架构与NCP目标各自带来递进增益；仅用85%标准计算量即接近参数量对齐的8.9B基线训练损失。潜空间在预训练后仍具价值：仅更新17M参数的VQ模块即可实现轻量域适配；将概念表示注入DFlash2 drafter使平均接受长度提升4.17%，开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 概念级潜空间预测可作为token级NTP的有效补充，显著提升预训练效率与下游性能，且所学潜空间在域适配与投机解码等后训练场景中仍具复用价值，是目前最大规模的潜空间语言模型验证。

Abstract: We introduce NCP-ArchPreview, a latent-space language model that pushes autoregressive pretraining beyond standard next-token prediction (NTP). Alongside NTP, the model learns through Next Concept Prediction (NCP) to predict discrete concepts that span multiple tokens, introducing an explicit and more challenging concept-level objective while preserving standard token-level autoregressive generation. NCP-ArchPreview builds a latent space by constructing a product-quantized concept vocabulary directly from its hidden states, and subsequently learns to predict future concepts via a dedicated Concept Module. These predicted concepts are then fed back to the token level to guide subsequent generation, with NTP and NCP trained jointly end-to-end. We scale this architecture to 8.9B parameters and train it on 5.73T tokens from the Dolma-3 dataset, marking the largest demonstration of a latent-space language model to date. Remarkably, by consuming only 51.3% of the total training tokens, NCP-ArchPreview achieves the final pretraining loss of OLMo-3-7B. Following full pretraining, it outperforms OLMo-3-7B by 2.45 points on the downstream macro-average, including a notable 5.99-point gain on GSM8K. Controlled experiments isolate a clear progression of performance gains stemming from both the latent architecture and the NCP objective. Furthermore, utilizing only 85% of the standard computation, NCP-ArchPreview approaches the training loss of a strictly parameter-aligned 8.9B baseline. The learned latent space remains highly valuable after the pretraining stage: updating just the 17M-parameter VQ module yields a novel, lightweight interface for domain adaptation, while a simple injection of concept representations into a DFlash2 drafter improves the mean accepted length by 4.17% with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [51] [Think Before You Link: Rarity, Reasoning, and Retrieval in Multilingual Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10745)
*Parinthapat Pengpun,Simran Khanuja,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新定义多模态实体链接中的"稀有实体"，采用知识图谱结构指标（而非仅流行度指标）来识别稀有实体，并揭示SOTA模型在这些稀有切片上准确率下降15.4-39.9%。为此提出一种无需训练的框架，让具备推理能力的视觉语言模型迭代地检索并推理维基百科证据。实验表明推理与检索互补，二者结合效果最佳，在多语言基准MERLIN上整体提升6.9%、稀有实体切片最高提升23.3%，并发布MERLIN-Rare稀有实体测试切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态实体链接系统在稀有实体上性能退化，但先前工作主要通过基于流行度的指标（如页面浏览量）来衡量稀有性，这种单一视角可能遗漏许多真正稀有的实体。作者希望用知识图谱结构指标（刻画实体被文档化与连接的程度）来拓宽稀有性的定义，从而暴露不同稀有性定义下不同的失败模式。

Method: 1) 引入知识图谱结构指标来识别稀有实体，构建稀有实体切片；2) 提出一个简单、无需训练的框架，利用具备推理能力的视觉语言模型在维基百科上迭代检索并推理，动态收集证据；3) 通过受控实验分析推理与检索的互补性。

Result: 在多种稀有实体切片上，SOTA准确率下降15.4-39.9%，说明不同稀有性定义暴露不同失败模式。受控实验显示：仅推理对稀有实体准确率无显著提升；仅检索可提升稀有实体准确率但可能损害整体准确率；两者结合效果最佳。在五语言（印地语、印尼语、日语、泰米尔语、越南语）多模态实体链接基准MERLIN上，最佳系统整体较SOTA提升6.9%，稀有实体切片最高提升23.3%。

Conclusion: 知识图谱结构指标能识别出流行度指标遗漏的稀有实体，且不同稀有性定义揭示不同失败模式。推理与检索互补，二者结合在多语言多模态实体链接上取得显著提升，尤其在稀有实体上。作者发布MERLIN-Rare稀有实体测试切片及框架，以支持针对性评估。

Abstract: Multimodal entity linking grounds entity mentions in text and images to knowledge-base entries. These systems degrade on rare entities, but prior work measures rarity primarily through popularity-based metrics such as pageviews. We broaden this view using knowledge-graph structural metrics that capture how well an entity is documented and connected. These metrics identify many rare entities that popularity metrics miss. Across the resulting rare-entity slices, state-of-the-art accuracy drops by 15.4-39.9%, showing that different rarity definitions expose different failure modes. To address these failures, we introduce a simple, training-free framework in which a reasoning-capable vision-language model iteratively searches and reasons over Wikipedia, gathering evidence dynamically. Controlled experiments show that reasoning and retrieval are complementary. Reasoning alone does not significantly improve accuracy on rare entities. Retrieval without reasoning improves rare-entity accuracy but can hurt overall accuracy. Their combination performs best. On MERLIN, a multilingual multimodal entity linking benchmark over five languages (Hindi, Indonesian, Japanese, Tamil, Vietnamese), our best system improves over the state of the art by 6.9% overall and by up to 23.3% on rare-entity slices. We release MERLIN-Rare, rare-entity test slices for targeted evaluation, with our framework.

</details>


### [52] [Does Linguistic Structure Enrichment Enhance Coherence Assessment? Not With Current Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10893)
*Victor Mazzotti,Luiz Pereira,Marina Bitencourt dos Santos,Helena Maia,Carlos Caetano,Nádia Felix,Sandra Avila*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究加入句法与修辞信息能否提升文本不连贯性预测；实验发现纯文本反而准确率更高，因为附加信息与语言模型架构在结构和句法上不兼容。此外，在巴西虚假信息数据集上的零样本实验表明，文本连贯性可作为检测误导性内容的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽表达流畅，但常生成语法正确却语义不连贯、含矛盾或逻辑断裂的文本。作者希望探究通过引入句法与修辞信息来提升不连贯性预测效果的可行性，并验证连贯性评估的实际价值。

Method: 对比实验：在纯文本与增强（附加句法、修辞信息）文本两种输入条件下进行不连贯性预测；同时在巴西虚假信息数据集上开展零样本实验，检验连贯性作为误导性内容检测代理指标的能力。

Result: 纯文本取得更高准确率；引入的附加信息因在结构与句法上与语言模型架构不兼容，未能带来增益。零样本实验显示文本连贯性与误导性内容检测之间存在关联。

Conclusion: 增强句法和修辞信息在当前语言模型架构下并不能提升不连贯性预测，提示信息注入方式需与模型结构匹配；文本连贯性可作为检测误导性内容的实用代理指标。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have transformed human-computer interaction. Despite their fluency, these models often produce texts that are grammatically correct but semantically incoherent, containing contradictions or disruptions in logical flow. This work investigates whether enriching text with syntactic and rhetorical information can improve incoherence prediction. Our experiments and analysis show that plain texts achieved higher accuracy because the added information was structurally and syntactically incompatible with the language model's architecture. Additionally, to demonstrate the practical importance of coherence assessment, we performed zero-shot experiments on a Brazilian disinformation dataset, suggesting that textual coherence can serve as a proxy for detecting misleading content. Code and models are available at https://github.com/ittozzamV/cohereclassifier.

</details>


### [53] [LLM-Anchored Paralinguistic Enrichment for Alzheimer's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10896)
*Xiao Wei,Yuqin Lin,Yaru Cao,Jinyu Li,Bin Wen,Kai Li,Yueying Chen,Longbiao Wang,Jianwu Dang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LAPE框架，通过韵律事件文本化、词汇-韵律单元化与分块、以及文本锚定的副语言融合三项创新，将副语言线索（停顿、拖音）与LLM语言表征结合，用于阿尔茨海默病的语音自动检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语音的AD自动检测为非侵入、可扩展的早期认知筛查提供了途径。AD同时影响词汇语义组织与语音产生（如异常停顿与词拖长），但现有方法尚未充分将副语言线索与语言内容整合。

Method: 提出LLM-Anchored Paralinguistic Enrichment（LAPE），包含三项协同创新：(1) 韵律事件文本化，将停顿与拖音编码为带有时长感知重复的显式标记，使LLM能将其与词汇内容联合建模；(2) 词汇-韵律单元化与分块，仅对连续词单元进行池化，以保留两模态中的事件身份与幅度；(3) 文本锚定的副语言融合，使用NormGate对局部与话语级语音特征进行归一化并相对文本动态缩放，从而完成融合。

Result: 在ADReSS与ADReSSo数据集上采用参与者级交叉验证与留一被试评估，LAPE在全部四个主要设置上均取得最优性能。

Conclusion: LAPE有效整合了副语言线索与LLM语言表征，在AD语音检测任务上达到当前最优水平，代码将在论文录用后发布。

Abstract: Speech-based automatic detection of Alzheimer's disease (AD) provides a non-invasive and scalable approach to early cognitive screening. AD affects both lexical-semantic organization and speech production, including atypical pauses and word elongations. However, existing methods have yet to fully integrate these paralinguistic cues with linguistic content. We propose LLM-Anchored Paralinguistic Enrichment (LAPE), which enriches LLM-derived linguistic representations with paralinguistic cues through three coordinated innovations. The first is prosodic event textualization, which enables the LLM to model pauses and elongations jointly with lexical content by encoding them as explicit markers with bounded duration-aware repetition. The second is lexico-prosodic unitization and chunking, which preserves event identity and magnitude in both modalities by pooling only consecutive word units. The third is text-anchored paralinguistic fusion, which integrates local and utterance-level speech features by using NormGate to normalize and dynamically scale them relative to text. We evaluate LAPE on ADReSS and ADReSSo using participant-level cross-validation and leave-one-subject-out evaluation. LAPE achieves state-of-the-art performance across all four primary settings. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [54] [SearchAtlas: Analyzing Agentic Search Strategies via Evidential Query Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10901)
*Jiacheng Sang,Mengyuan Li,Sanxing Chen,Yukun Huang,Yu Feng,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SearchAtlas 框架，将 LLM 搜索智能体的原始搜索轨迹转化为结构化图，以分析其证据检索与传播过程，并揭示与错误答案强相关的过程性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 搜索智能体评测多依赖最终答案准确率，忽视搜索过程；而理解搜索策略需要分析可信证据如何被检索以满足问题约束，这些信息埋藏在冗长难解析的原始轨迹中，缺乏可解释性手段。

Method: SearchAtlas 通过自动化解析流水线将搜索轨迹转换为结构化图，图的边表示证据在推理轨迹中的传播路径（从检索该证据的查询到最终答案）。随后在 3 个基准上对 5 个搜索智能体进行分析，并与人工标注图及 LLM judge 进行对比。

Result: 自动解析流水线相对人工标注图达到 86.0% 的平均边 F1，且在重复运行中保持稳定。分析揭示了搜索规模与证据聚合的系统性差异，并暴露出碎片化的答案支撑、未到达答案的问题约束、未经验证的参数化知识进入回答等过程失败，这些失败与错误答案强相关，且相关性高于给定原始轨迹或有序查询列表的 LLM judge。

Conclusion: SearchAtlas 构建的图提供了有用的可解释性；过程诊断分数与最终答案正确性不一致的案例审计表明，该框架能捕捉无法归约为答案准确率的信息，为搜索智能体的过程评测提供了新视角。

Abstract: LLM search agents are often evaluated on final-answer accuracy, overlooking the process. Analyzing a search strategy requires understanding how credible evidence is retrieved to address question constraints. This valuable information is buried in raw search trajectories that are long and difficult to parse. We introduce SearchAtlas, a framework that converts search trajectories into structured graphs whose edges represent how evidence is propagated across the reasoning trace, from the query that retrieves it to the final answer. Our automated parsing pipeline achieves a mean edge F1 of 86.0% against human-annotated graphs and remains consistent across repeated runs. We analyze five search agents on three benchmarks, revealing systematic differences in search scale and evidence aggregation. SearchAtlas exposes fragmented answer support, question constraints that do not reach the answer, and unverified parametric knowledge entering the response. These process failures are strongly associated with incorrect answers, even more so than an LLM judge given either the raw trajectory or the ordered query list, suggesting that the constructed graphs provide useful interpretability. Moreover, an audit of cases in which process-diagnostic scores disagree with final-answer correctness shows that they capture information not reducible to answer accuracy.

</details>


### [55] [Auto-RecSys: Harnessing Autonomous Research Agents for Industry-Scale Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10922)
*Ming Li,Dai Li,Xuying Ning,Bo Sun,Rui Li,Yi Zhang,Silvia Gong,Xuan Cao,Rui Li,Cornelia Carapcea,Qunshu Zhang,Zhigang Wang,Yinglong Xia,Andy Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Auto-RecSys，一个面向工业级推荐模型长周期实验的自主研究系统，通过三项harness设计与双环自进化架构，解决长反馈回路与系统复杂性问题，显著减少单轮实验人力耗时并提升执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自动研究范式扩展到工业级推荐模型面临两大挑战：(1) 长反馈回路——模型训练耗时可达数天，串行迭代过慢，需跨多研究方向并行探索；(2) 系统复杂度——配置规模庞大、基础设施依赖脆弱、GPU任务持续多日，要求执行过程稳健且可恢复。

Method: Auto-RecSys提出三项harness设计：(1) 分布式异步执行，跨服务器并行运行多个实验；(2) 集中式跨服务器记忆，实现跨会话与故障的持久化、可恢复执行；(3) 认知—流程分离，由自然语言技能文件引导LLM推理，由确定性脚本保证操作正确性。同时采用双环自进化架构：执行进化环通过记录失败尝试、固化成功流水线积累模型专属操作知识；想法进化环利用实验结果指导后续构思。

Result: 在推荐模型上评估表明，Auto-RecSys显著降低每个实验周期所需的人力时间，并且随着playbook逐渐成熟，执行可靠性得到提升。

Conclusion: Auto-RecSys验证了自主研究系统在工业级推荐模型长周期实验中的可行性，其harness设计与双环自进化机制可有效应对长反馈回路与系统复杂度挑战。

Abstract: Auto-research agents have shown the potential to automate hypothesis generation, experiment execution, and iterative refinement. However, scaling this paradigm to industry-scale recommendation models introduces two challenges: (1) long feedback loops, where model training can take days, making serial iteration prohibitively slow and requiring parallel exploration across multiple research directions; and (2) system complexity, where large configurations, fragile infrastructure dependencies, and multi-day GPU jobs require robust and recoverable execution. We present Auto-RecSys, an autonomous research system for long-horizon experimentation on industry-scale recommendation models. Auto-RecSys addresses these challenges through three harness designs: (1) distributed asynchronous execution for running multiple experiments in parallel across servers, (2) centralized cross-server memory for persistent and recoverable execution across sessions and failures, and (3) cognitive-procedural separation, where natural-language skill files guide LLM reasoning while deterministic scripts enforce operational correctness. Auto-RecSys further employs a dual-loop self-evolving architecture: an Execution Evolution Loop in which model-specific playbooks accumulate operational knowledge by recording failed attempts and crystallizing successful pipelines, and an Idea Evolution Loop in which experimental outcomes inform subsequent ideation. Evaluated on recommendation models, Auto-RecSys significantly reduces the human time required per experiment cycle and improves execution reliability as its playbooks mature.

</details>


### [56] [Structurally Speaking: Motif-Oriented Graph Captioning through Bidirectional Graph-Text Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10923)
*Hsiao-Ying Lu,Dongyu Liu,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"Structurally Speaking"结构化提示协议，将图描述任务建模为双向图-文本翻译，引导LLM在显式连接与motif级抽象间转换，生成更短、motif更一致的图描述，同时保持可比的图恢复能力，无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图描述（graph captioning）方法多将邻接矩阵直接转化为冗长的边列表，可读性差。有用的图描述应将连接性抽象为可识别的motif（如hub、path、cycle、clique、bridge），这些motif是更紧凑、易读、易比较和易恢复的结构单元。直接提示GPT-5.1虽能生成可恢复的图描述，但冗长且存在motif解释不一致的问题。

Method: 将motif导向的图描述定义为双向图-文本翻译任务，描述需同时满足：保留足够拓扑以支持图恢复，并以简洁的motif级描述表达图结构。提出Structurally Speaking——一种轻量级结构化提示协议，引导模型在显式连接性与motif级抽象之间进行双向翻译，无需模型微调。

Result: 在基于motif的合成数据集上实验表明，结构化提示生成的描述更短、motif一致性更高，同时保持与直接提示相当（comparable）的图恢复性能。

Conclusion: 显式的拓扑到motif推理引导可提升LLM生成图描述的可解释性，且无需模型微调，说明结构化提示是改善motif导向图描述的有效途径。

Abstract: Graph captions should help readers understand graph structure, rather than simply translate adjacency matrices into long textual edge lists. A useful graph caption abstracts connectivity into recognizable motifs, such as hubs, paths, cycles, cliques, and bridges, because these motifs provide compact structural units that are easier to read, compare, and recover. In this paper, we study motif-oriented graph captioning as a bidirectional graph-text translation task, where captions must both preserve enough topology for graph recovery and express the graph through concise motif-level descriptions. We show that direct prompting of GPT-5.1 often produces graph-recoverable captions by enumerating node-to-node connections, but these captions are verbose and can contain inconsistent motif interpretations. To address this gap, we introduce Structurally Speaking, a lightweight structured prompting protocol that guides translation between explicit connectivity and motif-level abstraction. Experiments on a synthetic motif-based dataset show that structured prompting produces shorter and more motif-consistent captions while maintaining comparable graph recovery. These results suggest that explicit topology-to-motif reasoning guidance can make LLM-generated graph captions more interpretable without model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [57] [Using Semantic Uncertainty to Estimate Transition Relevance in Turn-taking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10934)
*Muhammad Umair,Jan P. de Ruiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究会话中的轮转机制，提出用语义不确定性（基于LLM采样续写句子的语义离散度变化）来建模不断演化的语义预期，从而在人机对话中预测话轮转换相关位置（TRP）。该方法在真实听众实时响应标注的数据集上优于基于提示和微调的纯文本基线，说明演化中的语义约束影响无脚本会话中的轮转机会感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语对话系统虽利用语言、声学和非言语线索，在无脚本互动中仍会产生时机不当的回应。核心难点在于预测轮转相关位置（TRP）——即听者可以（而非必须）接过话轮的时机。人类听者不等话轮结束，而是随着话语展开，利用对其发展中意义的预期来预测TRP并决定是否接话。作者希望验证这种不断演化的预期能否通过语义不确定性来建模。

Method: 提出语义不确定性度量：用LLM对正在进行的当前话轮采样可能的续写，通过语义离散度的变化来识别话轮内部的TRP。在带有TRP标注的数据集上评估，且该标注来自听众的实时响应，而非回溯式标注。与基于提示的方法及微调的纯文本基线进行对比。

Result: 该方法显著优于基于提示和微调的纯文本基线。

Conclusion: 结果为"不断演化的语义约束影响无脚本互动中被感知的轮转机会"这一观点提供了实证支持，表明语义不确定性可有效建模人类听者对TRP的预期机制。

Abstract: Turn-taking is a fundamental mechanism that governs when interlocutors speak and listen. Although Spoken Dialogue Systems (SDS) exploit a range of linguistic, acoustic, and non-verbal cues, they produce ill-timed responses in unscripted interaction. A central challenge is anticipating Transition Relevance Places (TRPs), or opportunities, not obligations, for a listener to take the floor. Human listeners do not wait for turn endings; as an utterance unfolds, they use expectations about its developing meaning to anticipate TRPs and decide whether to take the floor. We examine whether these evolving expectations can be modeled through semantic uncertainty -- an LLM-derived measure of how strongly a turn so far constrains what may plausibly come next. To do so, we sample possible continuations of an ongoing turn and use changes in semantic dispersion to identify TRPs within turns. We evaluate this account on a dataset with TRP labels derived from real-time listener responses, rather than retrospective annotation. Our approach substantially outperforms prompt-based and fine-tuned text-only baselines, providing empirical support for the view that evolving semantic constraints inform perceived turn-taking opportunities in unscripted interaction.

</details>


### [58] [Distribution-aware Language Neuron Identification in Multilingual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10993)
*Minjun Kim,Inho Won,Junghun Yuk,Dongyeon Kim,Jihyo Kim,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出分布感知语言神经元选择方法，通过激活分布重叠系数聚类语言，更有效地识别语言特定神经元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言大模型语言特定神经元识别基于激活值为正时的语言概率熵，忽略了语言表征的分布式与相互关联特性，可能无法充分刻画模型的多语言本质。

Method: 提出分布感知语言神经元选择：利用各语言在全激活范围（含负值）上的激活分布之间的成对关系，以语言间激活分布的成对重叠系数进行语言聚类，从而量化每个神经元的语言特异性。

Result: 在两个多语言大模型和两个留出语料上，该方法更有效地分离语言特定的因果效应，每个神经元对目标语言的损伤最高提升4.9倍，同时保持非目标语言性能。

Conclusion: 基于激活分布重叠的神经元识别优于基于熵的方法，能更精准定位语言特定神经元并保留跨语言通用能力。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (mLLMs) contain a small fraction of feed-forward neurons that are sensitive to particular languages, commonly termed language-specific neurons. Existing work measures language specificity using the entropy of each neuron's language-wise probabilities of being active, where a neuron is considered active when its activation value is positive. However, this approach may not fully capture the multilingual nature of mLLMs, where language representations are distributional and mutually related. We propose Distribution-aware Language Neuron selection, which leverages pairwise relationships between per-language activation distributions over the full activation range, including negative values. Specifically, we quantify each neuron's language specificity by clustering languages using pairwise overlap coefficients between their activation distributions. Across two mLLMs and two held-out corpora, our identifier more effectively isolates language-specific causal effects, yielding up to 4.9$\times$ higher on-target language damage per neuron while preserving off-target language performance.

</details>


### [59] [Rethinking Verbalized Confidence for LLM-as-a-Judge: A Compatibility Shift on Post-2025 Proprietary Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10996)
*Yu-Chung Hsiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现，在2025年后发布的顶级专有LLM上，言语化置信度（verbalized confidence）已取代对数概率（log-probabilities）成为更稳健的软评分机制，称之为"兼容性转变"，并引入"过度自信提示"与"自辩论"两项改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为言语化置信度存在过度自信、粗糙、倾向于整数聚集等缺陷，因此推荐使用对数概率作为LLM-as-a-Judge的软评分信号。作者质疑这一"标准建议"在新一代模型上是否仍然成立，并关注仅报告准确率会掩盖评分机制之间的差异。

Method: 在SummEval、AggreFact、HelpSteer2三个基准上，覆盖多达18个LLM进行评测。以标准言语化置信度为基线，叠加两个新组件：过度自信提示（overconfidence advisory）与自辩论（self-debate），并对比基于logprob的G-Eval，考察校准性、分数分布离散度及对任务主观性的鲁棒性。

Result: 在2025年后模型上，言语化置信度是优于logprob的信号，标准建议不再成立；两项新增组件共同提升了校准、分数分布离散度与主观性鲁棒性。同时存在"代际效应"：2025年后模型能以极小的平衡准确率代价容纳这两项改进，而2025年前模型则需付出可测量的性能惩罚。相比logprob-based G-Eval，言语化置信度在GPT家族顶级版本上是更具主观性鲁棒的软信号。

Conclusion: 这一转变在仅报告准确率的评估下不可见，因此建议在LLM-as-a-Judge中更广泛地采用软评分而非默认硬预测。更广义地说，言语化置信度已从logprob的较弱替代品转变为当代LLM评判者的实用软评分机制。

Abstract: Verbalized confidence, long dismissed as overconfident, coarse, and prone to round-number clustering, is now the more robust soft-scoring mechanism for LLM-as-a-Judge on top-tier proprietary models. Across SummEval, AggreFact, and HelpSteer2, spanning up to 18 LLMs, we show that the standard advice to prefer log-probabilities no longer holds on post-2025 models, where verbalized confidence is the better signal. We call this a compatibility shift. On top of a standard verbalized-confidence baseline, we introduce two new ingredients: an overconfidence advisory and self-debate. Together they improve calibration, score-distribution spread, and robustness to task subjectivity. We further observe a generation effect: post-2025 models accommodate these two additions with little balanced-accuracy cost, whereas pre-2025 models pay a measurable penalty. Compared with logprob-based G-Eval, verbalized confidence is the more subjectivity-robust soft signal on GPT-family top-tier releases. The shift is invisible under accuracy-only reporting. Rather than defaulting to hard predictions, we recommend broader use of soft scoring in LLM-as-a-Judge. More broadly, verbalized confidence has moved from a weaker substitute for logprobs to a practical soft-scoring mechanism for contemporary LLM judges.

</details>


### [60] [K/V-Cache Interventions Dissociate Representation Alignment from Persona Expression in Decoder-Only Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11020)
*Yu Sun,Mengyin Lu,Cong Feng,Guangming Lu,Huimin Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究将目标人设的K/V缓存轨迹移植到源人设生成中，发现表征层对齐与行为表达之间存在分离，且位置扰动导致一致的行为失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索K/V缓存干预作为解码器语言模型中人设控制的可控且结构化干预面，并检验表征级相似度能否预测下游人设表达。

Method: 在Llama-3.1-8B上对固定源-目标人设对实施13种K/V干预配置（早/中/晚期层带替换、全层与中层替换、位置扰动如滞后与打乱），并设置相同token序列对照以排除导入token历史的影响。

Result: 所有层带K/V替换均实现强局部V空间对齐（V-gap 0.91/0.89/0.84），但仅中层（9-20层）替换兼具显著目标标记表达与保留的词汇多样性；全层与中层替换对齐度相近（0.94 vs 0.89）却产生不同TTR（0.65 vs 0.77）；位置扰动一致抑制目标人设表达。相同token序列对照复现了L28表征偏移的符号与层定位。

Conclusion: 仅靠表征级相似度指标不足以预测下游人设表达；K/V缓存是可控但受结构约束的干预面。该研究刻画了高信号场景下的表征-行为分离，而非跨模型或人设对的普适性结论。

Abstract: We study K/V-cache interventions -- transplanting a target-conditioned K/V trajectory into a source-persona generation -- as a structured surface for persona control in decoder-only language models. Across 13 intervention configurations applied to Llama-3.1-8B for a fixed source-to-target persona pair, we report two consistent dissociations between representation-level alignment and behavioral expression, plus a common failure under position perturbations. First, all layer-band K/V replacements (early, mid, late) achieve strong local V-space alignment (V-gap 0.91, 0.89, 0.84), but only mid-layer replacement (layers 9-20) combines substantial target-marker expression with preserved lexical diversity. Second, full and mid-layer replacement induce comparable alignment (V-gap 0.94 vs. 0.89) yet produce different lexical-diversity profiles (TTR 0.65 vs. 0.77). Third, position perturbations (lag and shuffle) apply distinct operations yet uniformly suppress target-persona expression -- a common behavioral failure rather than a strict dissociation. Representation-level similarity metrics alone are thus not sufficient predictors of downstream persona expression in the regimes we study; the K/V cache emerges as a controllable but structurally constrained intervention surface. Because the transplanted trajectory carries the target's own generated token history, we characterize the intervention as trajectory-level transplantation rather than isolated persona-representation injection; a same-token-sequence control, decoding an identical token sequence under source vs. target conditioning, reproduces the sign and layer localization of the L28 representational shift, indicating the shift is not explained solely by imported token history. These findings characterize representation-behavior dissociation in a high-signal setting rather than establishing universality across models or persona pairs.

</details>


### [61] [When Noise Fabricates Bias: The Fragility of LLM-as-a-Judge Bias Measurement under Noisy Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11067)
*DongHyun Ryu,Jaehyeok Lee,YeongJun Hwang,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究对3,822条刻板印象相关回答施加5类真实噪声（多种强度），比较LLM评审的偏见判定。发现表层噪声不对称地推高偏见测量：中性被判为有偏的概率远高于反向，差距可达120倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多被用作评审来测量文本中的社会偏见，但其所评判的文本常含拼写错误、非正式拼写与标点断裂等噪声。这类表层噪声对偏见测量结果的影响尚不清楚，需要系统检验。

Method: 对3,822条刻板印象相关回答施加5种真实噪声条件，每种含多个强度等级；用4个LLM评审对加噪文本与原始文本分别做偏见判定并对比。

Result: 噪声导致的失真不对称：中性判定被翻转为有偏的可能性远高于有偏被翻转为中性，最高达120倍。两个非显然效应：（1）在最脆弱的评审中，当温和且真实的噪声水平下、消解（erasure）最少时，失真最纯粹；（2）随评审鲁棒性增强，失真趋近平权而非反转。

Conclusion: 基于含噪文本测得的偏见被系统性高估，且在对公平性最关键的类别中高估最严重；因此噪声是偏见测量中不可忽视的系统性偏差来源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as judges to measure social bias in text, yet the passages they judge are often noisy, containing typos, informal spelling, and broken punctuation. The consequences of such surface noise for social bias measurement remain unclear. To investigate this question, we apply five realistic noise conditions at multiple intensity levels to 3,822 stereotype-related responses and compare the resulting bias judgments with those on the original text. We find that such surface noise does not degrade bias measurement symmetrically: it is far more likely to turn neutral judgments into biased ones than biased judgments into neutral ones, by up to a 120x margin. We further observe two non-obvious effects across four LLM judges: in the most fragile judge the distortion is at its purest at mild, realistic noise levels, where erasure is scarcest, and as judges grow robust it attenuates toward parity rather than reversing. Bias measured on noisy text is therefore systematically overestimated, most in the categories that matter most for fairness.

</details>


### [62] [ProMediConv: Benchmarking Proactive Conversational Agents in Legal Dispute Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11101)
*Zesheng Wei,Mengfan Li,Wenhao Liu,Yixin Zhang,Zilei Wang,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出ProMediConv基准框架，将争议调解建模为主动式、多阶段、区分当事方的对话过程，并构建高保真数据集、提出细粒度指标MAD及基线ProMediAgent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 培养熟练调解员成本高、耗时长；现有基于LLM的调解研究存在任务设定不真实、数据集保真度低、评价指标粗糙等问题，难以刻画逐轮对话动态。

Method: 提出ProMediConv框架：将调解建模为主动、多阶段、当事方感知的对话过程，涵盖11种调解策略与4种当事方行为模式（BP）状态；基于972个真实完整案例构建带话语级策略与BP状态标注的高保真数据集；提出细粒度指标MAD（平均属性差异）衡量对话中的BP变化；并建立包含多模型与定制基线ProMediAgent的评测基准。

Result: 通过大量实证分析揭示了关键行为现象，并表明当前模型在动态多方调解中仍面临持续挑战。

Conclusion: ProMediConv为推进AI辅助冲突解决提供了严谨基础与重要量化标准，数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Dispute mediation is essential for maintaining social harmony and resilience, yet developing skilled mediators is costly and time-consuming. Existing LLM-based mediation research remains limited by unrealistic task formulations, low-fidelity datasets, and coarse evaluation metrics that obscure turn-by-turn dynamics. To address these gaps, we introduce ProMediConv, a novel benchmarking framework that models mediation as a proactive, multi-stage, and party-aware dialogue process incorporating 11 mediation strategies and four party behavior pattern (BP) states. Using 972 complete real-world cases, we construct a high-fidelity mediation dataset with utterance-level annotations of strategies and BP states. Furthermore, to better assess agent impact, we propose MAD (Mean Attribute Difference), a fine-grained metric that captures BP shifts throughout the dialogue. Leveraging this framework, we establish a comprehensive benchmark by evaluating diverse models alongside our tailored baseline ProMediAgent. Extensive empirical analyses reveal critical behavioral phenomena and underscore the persistent challenges current models face in dynamic, multi-party mediation. Ultimately, ProMediConv provides a rigorous foundation and a vital quantitative standard for advancing AI-assisted conflict resolution. Our dataset and codebase are accessible at https://github.com/ZsWei66/ProMediConv_repo.

</details>


### [63] [Overview of the NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 11: Agent-Based Experiment Reproduction from Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11117)
*Hanhua Hong,Yizhi Li,Luu Gia Huy,Jian Yang,Ming Zhou,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AgentActionBench，一个面向过程的智能体实验复现评测基准，覆盖ML与AI4Science共150篇论文，通过MCP动作记录器与论文定制化评分标准评估复现全过程，共含1万余条评分项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学复现对科研进展至关重要，但论文数量与复杂度增长使人工核验难以规模化。现有LLM智能体自动复现的评测多聚焦最终产物仓库且局限于机器学习领域，缺乏对复现过程的评估与跨领域覆盖。

Method: 构建AgentActionBench过程导向基准：采用基于MCP的Action Recorder全程捕获智能体复现行为并生成轨迹，配合论文专属评分标准进行评估；基准含150篇论文（120篇ML、30篇AI4Science），其中10%由人工标注作为验证集，并通过模型辅助扩充形成超10,000条评分项。

Result: 现有系统表现仍受限，执行环节是主要瓶颈；模型生成与人工标注评分标准之间呈较强Pearson与Spearman相关，验证了可扩展评分标准生成方法的可靠性。

Conclusion: AgentActionBench将复现评测从结果导向拓展至过程导向并延伸至AI4Science领域，揭示了当前智能体在实验执行能力上的不足，同时证明模型辅助评分标准构建具备可靠性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Reproducibility is essential to scientific progress, yet the growing volume and complexity of scientific publications make exhaustive manual verification increasingly impractical. Although recent advances in large language model (LLM) agents enable automated experiment reproduction, existing evaluations largely focus on final repositories and are typically limited to machine learning (ML). We introduce AgentActionBench, a process-oriented benchmark for evaluating agent-based experiment reproduction across ML and AI4Science domains. Our framework uses an MCP-based Action Recorder to capture agents' behaviour throughout the reproduction process and evaluates the resulting traces with paper-specific rubrics. AgentActionBench contains 150 papers, including 120 ML papers and 30 AI4Science papers. A human-annotated subset covering 10% of the benchmark provides validation data, while model-assisted augmentation expands the full benchmark to more than 10,000 rubric items. Experimental results show that current systems remain limited, with execution as the primary bottleneck. Meanwhile, the strong Pearson and Spearman correlations between model-generated and human-annotated rubrics validate the reliability of our scalable rubric-generation approach.

</details>


### [64] [From Repetition to Recognition: Inductive Discovery of Disinformation Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11128)
*Max Upravitelev,Veronika Solopova,Jing Yang,Charlott Jakob,Alexandra Tsiakalou,Neda Foroutan,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出三层评估框架（recovery、mining、discovery），用于无监督叙事标签生成，并在七个虚假信息数据集上比较聚类与图社区检测流程；结果显示两类方法互补，图方法在话题平衡与单例叙事发现上更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有叙事挖掘虽已形式化为从语料中归纳推断叙事标签，但其评估仍依赖预定义分类体系，属于封闭世界设定，无法捕捉参考标签之外的叙事，因此需要新的评估框架以支持开放世界下的叙事发现。

Method: 提出三层评估框架：recovery（对照语料自身分类体系）、mining（对照外部标签集）、discovery（无预定义标签）；在七个虚假信息数据集上比较基于聚类与基于图社区的两类流程，并对其中两个数据集进行人工验证的 discovery 评估。

Result: 自动化指标下两类方法互补；在含两个显著话题的语料中，聚类可将某一话题压缩至生成标签的2%，而图方法保持均衡；discovery验证发现大量单例（由单条主张衍生的叙事标签，占图输出的30-62%），聚类无法产生此类标签；标注者确认其中许多是可识别的虚假信息叙事。

Conclusion: 在开放世界发现中，叙事挖掘所假设的重复性可能是在语料之外被识别，而非在语料内部；作者发布了经人工验证的 Climate Obstruction 与 PolyNarrative 数据集叙事候选标签，以支持分类体系开发和数据集扩展。

Abstract: In disinformation datasets, narratives are often understood as recurring interpretive patterns that group texts under narrative labels. Recent work formalized narrative mining as inductively inferring narrative labels from corpora, but its evaluation stays tied to predefined taxonomies, a closed-world setting that cannot capture narratives absent from the reference labels. We introduce a three-tier evaluation framework for unsupervised narrative label generation: recovery (against a corpus's own taxonomy), mining (against external label sets), and discovery (without predefined labels). Applying it, we compare clustering-based and graph-community-based pipelines across seven disinformation datasets, with human validation of discovery on two. The two families are complementary under automated metrics, but in a corpus with two prominent topics, clustering can reduce one topic to 2% of generated labels while graph-based pipelines stay balanced. Discovery validation also reveals many singletons (narrative labels derived from single claims, 30-62% of graph outputs), which clustering cannot produce. Annotators confirm many as recognizable disinformation narratives, suggesting that in open-world discovery the repetition assumed by narrative mining may be recognized outside the corpus, not within it. We release human-validated narrative candidate labels for the Climate Obstruction and PolyNarrative datasets to support taxonomy development and dataset extension.

</details>


### [65] [Can LLMs Normalize Databases? A Benchmark and Multi-Agent Framework for Schema Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11141)
*Dong-Jae Koh,Huisu Kim,SeongHwan Yoon,Lasse M. Jantsch,Chun-Hee Lee,Seonghyeon Lee,Young-Kyoon Suh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出数据库规范化基准 DNBENCH（3,275 个样本，覆盖 1NF 到 BCNF），用三轴协议评估语义等价性、结构准确性与逻辑有效性，揭示 LLM 在依赖推断、模式分解和跨表约束重建上的系统性缺陷，并提出多智能体推理框架 MARS，使 DNB-SCORE 相比单提示基线提升 82.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多用于生成结构化输出，但当这些输出必须满足数据库级别的约束时，其可靠性尚不明确。作者以数据库规范化（涉及函数依赖推理、无损连接分解和跨表约束）为切入点研究这一问题。

Method: 构建 DNBENCH 基准，包含 3,275 个样本，用于评估从 1NF 到 BCNF 的 LLM 驱动数据库规范化；采用三轴协议衡量语义等价、结构准确性和逻辑有效性；设置 Single、Complex、Real World 三个难度层级。进而提出 MARS（Multi-Agent Reasoning for Schemas），将证据抽取、违规诊断、分解规划与模式生成、验证相分离。

Result: 在三个层级上，DNBENCH 揭示了依赖推断、模式分解与跨表约束重建中的反复失败；MARS 相较单提示基线将 DNB-SCORE 提升 82.0%。

Conclusion: 现有 LLM 在满足数据库级约束的结构化输出上仍不可靠，需要专门的评估基准与分解式多智能体推理方法；所有成果将在论文录用后发布。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate structured outputs, but their reliability remains unclear when those outputs must satisfy database-level constraints. We study this issue through database normalization, involving reasoning about functional dependencies, lossless join decompositions, and inter-table constraints. We introduce a Database Normalization Benchmark (DNBENCH), comprising 3,275 samples for evaluating LLM-driven database normalization from 1NF to BCNF. DNBENCH uses a three-axis protocol to measure semantic equivalence, structural accuracy, and logical validity. Across Single, Complex, and Real World levels, DNBENCH uncovers recurring failures in dependency inference, schema decomposition, and inter-table constraint reconstruction. We further propose Multi-Agent Reasoning for Schemas (MARS), which separates evidence extraction, violation diagnosis, and decomposition planning from schema generation and verification. MARS improves the DNB-SCORE by 82.0% over the single-prompt baseline. All artifacts will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [66] [Automated Identification of Competing Narratives in Political Discourse on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11202)
*Sergej Wildemann,Erick Elejalde*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无监督框架识别社交媒体政治话语中的竞争性叙事，基于德国政客推文，结合主题建模、事件检测与事件链接，通过两个极化议题案例验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体成为政治话语核心场域，叙事在其中形成演化并影响舆论；识别与分析不同意识形态间相互竞争的政治叙事，对理解现代政治传播动态至关重要。

Method: 无监督多阶段流水线框架，集成主题建模、事件检测、事件链接等自然语言处理技术，将数据组织为连贯故事并揭示不同用户群体的视角差异，从而检测关键竞争叙事及热点政治议题上的分歧框架与冲突。

Result: 两个极化政治议题的案例研究验证了方法的有效性，成功揭示并分析了分歧观点，能够识别围绕热门政治话题的竞争性叙事。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于理解数字公共领域中叙事的传播机制，并为政策制定者、社交媒体平台及关注政治话语监测的研究者提供洞见。

Abstract: Social media platforms have become central to shaping political discourse, serving as arenas where narratives form and evolve, influencing public opinion. Identifying and analyzing these narratives, particularly when they compete across different political ideologies, is crucial for understanding the dynamics of modern political communication. This paper presents an unsupervised framework for identifying and characterizing competing narratives in political discourse on social media, focusing on German politicians' tweets. The framework employs a multi-stage pipeline that integrates natural language processing techniques such as topic modeling, event detection, and event linking. By forming data into coherent stories and uncovering the distinct perspectives of user communities, the system is able to detect the key competing narratives, highlighting the divergent framings and conflicts surrounding trending political topics. Two case studies on polarizing political issues demonstrate the efficacy of the methodology, showcasing its ability to uncover and analyze divergent viewpoints. The findings contribute to the broader understanding of how narratives propagate within the digital public sphere and offer insights for policymakers, social media platforms, and researchers interested in monitoring political discourse.

</details>


### [67] [Assessing the Reusability of Public Speech Resources for Low-Resource Languages: A Central Kurdish Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11246)
*Hiwa Asadpour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该评论核查了一个库尔德语语音合成公开数据集（三个语音、35小时录音及论文）与论文描述的一致性，指出数据集存在配置文件记录未使用设备、测试录音未标注混入训练数据、代码缺陷导致长数字处理错误等问题，并批评下载页夸大了论文结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库尔德语使用者众多但语音技术匮乏，近期公开的免费语音资源对社区具有重要价值，因此有必要核查这些公开文件是否与论文相符。同时库尔德语缺乏英语、德语那样长期积累的词典与语言学描述支持，软件中的发音错误不易被发现，加之库尔德语存在显著的地域与书写变体，单一推荐用途可能掩盖代表性不足的问题。

Method: 作者对公开发布的三个库尔德语语音、35小时录音及相关论文进行比对审查，检查配置文件、数据划分、代码实现与下载页说明的一致性，并结合库尔德语的地域与书写变异、发音资源匮乏等背景，评估这些语音的代表性与可复用性。

Result: 审查发现多处不符：设置文件列出了从未使用的设备；测试录音未加标注地混入训练数据；代码缺陷导致长数字处理错误。下载页宣称的结果强于论文所报告的结果，并推荐某一语音用于通用场景，而该语音仅由三人朗读预制文本构建，剔除了大量日常与地域性口语，代表的是说话者的朗读风格而非整体库尔德语。

Conclusion: 多数问题可利用团队已有信息修复，且不改变已报告的结果；更完善的记录将主要便于他人尤其是社区语言学家核查与复用。许可证是主要例外：有声书所有者是否允许分享修正版本，将决定未来库尔德语语音能否基于此工作继续发展，还是必须从头开始。

Abstract: Kurdish is spoken by millions of people, but little technology can read it aloud. A recent study released three Kurdish voices, 35 hours of recorded speech, and a paper describing the work, all free to download. This review checks how well those public files match the paper. The research is careful about its limits, but the files contain several problems: a settings file lists equipment that was never used, test recordings are left unlabeled among training data, and a coding fault mishandles long numbers. The download page also claims a stronger result than the paper reports and recommends one voice for general use. That recommendation matters because Kurdish has major regional and written variation, while these voices were built from three people reading prepared texts. The process therefore removes much everyday and regional speech. English and German benefit from long traditions of dictionaries and linguistic description that help identify wrong pronunciations; Kurdish has far less such support, so software choices can go unchecked. The voices sound fluent, but they represent the reading styles of their speakers rather than Kurdish as a whole. Most of these issues can be fixed using information the team already has, without changing the reported results. Better records would mainly make the work easier for others, especially community linguists, to check and reuse. The license is the main exception: whether audiobook owners allow corrected versions to be shared will affect whether future Kurdish voices can build on this work or must start again.

</details>


### [68] [MultiHuSE: A Multimodal Dataset for Humour Styles and Emotions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11322)
*Mary Ogbuka Kenneth,Foaad Khosmood,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MultiHuSE，一个包含 2,407 段高清视频、50 位演员演绎 1,463 条文本的多模态幽默风格数据集，覆盖四种心理学幽默风格及中性内容，并部分标注情绪；基线实验显示多模态融合优于单模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算识别言语幽默具有挑战性，需综合语言、表达风格、情绪与文化语境；现有方法多局限于二分类，且缺乏同时刻画幽默心理学维度与表达变异的公开数据集。

Method: 构建 MultiHuSE 多模态数据集：50 位人口统计特征多样的演员对 1,463 条文本样本进行表演，形成 2,407 段高清视频，覆盖亲社会（affiliative）、攻击性（aggressive）、自我增强（self-enhancing）、自我贬损（self-deprecating）四种心理学幽默风格及中性内容，部分子集额外标注底层情绪；数据集支持同一文本的多种演员演绎，并开展单模态与多模态融合的基线分类实验。

Result: 多模态融合在幽默风格分类上优于单模态（准确率 80.1% vs. 77.4%），其中对亲社会幽默提升显著（66% 提升至 74%）；文本为最强的单一模态信号，融合模型仍带来有意义的性能提升。

Conclusion: MultiHuSE 为幽默与情绪关联的心理学理论提供实证支持，并为人类传播、幸福感及 AI 驱动交互研究开辟新方向；数据集可在终端用户许可协议下用于学术研究。

Abstract: Computational recognition of verbal humour remains a challenging task, requiring an understanding of language, delivery style, emotions, and cultural context. Most existing approaches focus on binary classification and lack datasets that capture psychological dimensions of humour alongside variations in expression. We introduce MultiHuSE, a multimodal dataset comprising 2,407 high-definition videos of 50 demographically diverse actors performing 1,463 text samples across four psychological humour styles (affiliative, aggressive, self-enhancing, and self-deprecating), as well as neutral content. A subset is additionally annotated for underlying emotions. The dataset uniquely captures multiple actor interpretations of the same texts, enabling systematic analysis of expressive diversity. Baseline experiments show that multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal approaches (80.1% vs. 77.4% accuracy) in humour style classification, with particularly strong gains for affiliative humour (66% to 74%). While text provides the strongest individual signal, fusion models deliver meaningful improvements. We hope that MultiHuSE provides empirical support for psychological theories linking humour and emotion, while also opening new avenues for research in human communication, well-being, and AI-driven interaction. The dataset is available for academic use under an End-User Licence Agreement.

</details>


### [69] [On the Impact of Anonymization on the Performance of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11335)
*Tobias Deußer,Max Hahnbück,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Tobias Uelwer,Christian Bauckhage,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对匿名化与模型效用间的权衡进行系统性实证研究：评估5个语言模型在11个基准上原始与匿名化输入的对比，发现性能下降高度依赖于模型能力与任务类型，可逆匿名化优于不可逆手段，且提示模型匿名化无收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益部署于敏感领域，匿名化输入以保护个人可识别信息成为关键实践，但匿名化对模型效用的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统的隐私-性能权衡研究。

Method: 系统性实证研究：选取5个主流语言模型，覆盖11个多样化基准，对比原始输入与伪名化（pseudonymized）输入下的表现；并开展额外实验，比较可逆匿名化（保留实体唯一性）、不可逆脱敏（如redaction）以及显式提示模型匿名化的效果。

Result: 匿名化总体导致性能下降但影响高度细分：能力更强的模型（如Qwen2.5-72B、GPT-4o mini）性能下降最大，表明更依赖具体实体信息；任务依赖性明显，TruthfulQA经匿名化后提升，而RGB等检索类任务出现灾难性下降；保留实体唯一性的可逆匿名化显著优于redaction等不可逆方法；显式提示模型关于匿名化无可见收益。

Conclusion: 匿名化并非一刀切方案，须与模型及任务协同设计以有效平衡隐私与效用；研究为构建更鲁棒、隐私感知的AI系统提供了重要基线。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains, anonymizing input data to protect personally identifiable information has become a critical practice. However, the impact of this anonymization on model utility is not well understood. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of the trade-off between privacy and performance. We evaluate five prominent language models across eleven diverse benchmarks, comparing their performance on original versus pseudonymized inputs. Our results reveal that while anonymization generally degrades performance, the effect is highly nuanced. We find that more capable models, such as Qwen2.5-72B and GPT-4o mini, suffer the largest performance drops, suggesting a stronger reliance on specific entity information. The impact is also task-dependent: performance on TruthfulQA improves with anonymization, while retrieval-focused tasks like RGB experience a catastrophic decline. Further experiments show that reversible anonymization techniques that preserve entity uniqueness significantly outperform irreversible ones like redaction, and that explicitly prompting models about anonymization offers no discernible benefit. We conclude that anonymization is not a one-size-fits-all solution and must be co-designed with the model and task in mind to balance privacy and utility effectively. Our findings provide a crucial baseline for developing more robust, privacy-aware AI systems.

</details>


### [70] [SEAR: Segment-Evidence-Aware Routing for Weak-to-Strong Multilingual Speech MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11355)
*Huy Hoang Le,Long-Bao Nguyen,Minh Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向MLC-SLM Challenge Task 2的系统，基于Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct，采用分段证据感知的数据与后训练流程，最终在官方评测集上取得90.92%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言对话语音语言模型任务需要模型同时理解语音声学信息与语义内容，而现有数据构建与训练流程难以保证音频证据的有效利用，易出现仅凭文本即可作答的“伪音频依赖”问题。

Method: 方法包含三部分：1）用语言模型将带时间戳的ASR转为连贯事件片段，按边界裕度扩展并从原始录音中裁剪；2）分别用Qwen3.6-27B合成语义MCQ、Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite合成声学MCQ，经结构、接地、答案一致性和目标模型可训练性校验，得到21种语言/口音变体的359,825道验证MCQ；3）以纯文本探针将数据划分为弱（文本可答）与强（依赖音频）两类，前者做监督微调，后者用GSPO强化学习，并辅以去偏优势、序列级重要性校正和动态过滤。

Result: 构建了覆盖21种语言和口音变体的359,825道经验证的MCQ数据，系统在最终官方评测集上达到90.92%的准确率。

Conclusion: 分段证据感知的数据构建结合文本探针分区与SFT+GSPO后训练，可有效提升多语言对话语音理解性能，验证了音频依赖型数据在强化学习阶段的关键作用。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for Task~2 of the second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge. We adapt Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct with a segment-evidence-aware data and post-training pipeline. A language model converts timestamped ASR into coherent event spans, which are expanded by a boundary margin and cropped from the original recording.
