We then synthesize complementary semantic MCQs with Qwen3.6-27B and acoustic MCQs with Gemini~3.1 Flash-Lite, followed by structural, grounding, answer-consistency, and target-model trainability checks, yielding 359,825 verified MCQs across 21 language and accent variants. A text-only probe partitions the data into weak, text-answerable items used for supervised fine-tuning and strong, audio-dependent items used for reinforcement learning with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), stabilized by debiased advantages, sequence-level importance correction, and dynamic filtering. Our system obtains 90.92% accuracy on the final official evaluation set.

</details>


### [71] [TransClean: A Benchmark for Detecting and Extracting Clean Translations from Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11399)
*Shenbin Qian,Yves Scherrer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM机器翻译输出中的"翻译噪声"问题，构建了TransClean基准并提出两种提取方法进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在机器翻译中日益普及，但其输出常包含翻译之外的附加文本（如语言标签、解释、双语重复），即"翻译噪声"。该问题普遍存在却缺乏专门的基准与系统性研究。

Method: 分析12个LLM在22个语对上的超过79万条翻译输出，归纳出12种反复出现的噪声模式，划分为格式噪声与内容噪声两类。据此构建TransClean基准，包含9,900对"噪声-干净"翻译输出（8,800条合成生成、1,100条人工整理的真实实例）。在基准上评测两种提取方法：基于跨度的提取（利用翻译质量估计模型进行跨度检测）与基于LLM的提取（提示LLM隔离出翻译）。

Result: 从大规模输出中识别出12种噪声模式并完成分类；建成含9,900对样本的TransClean基准；并对两种提取方法进行了对比评测。

Conclusion: 该基准与分析提供了首个用于评估和提升LLM翻译输出洁净度的系统性框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for machine translation, yet their outputs often contain additional text beyond the translation itself, such as language labels, explanations or bilingual repetitions, which we term translation noise. Despite its prevalence, this problem lacks dedicated benchmarks and systematic study. We analyze over 790,000 translation outputs from 12 LLMs across 22 language pairs (LPs) and identify 12 recurring noise patterns, which we group into formatting and content noise. Building on the observed patterns, we construct TransClean, a controlled benchmark of 9,900 pairs of noisy and clean translation outputs, comprising 8,800 synthetically generated instances and 1,100 manually curated authentic instances. We evaluate two extraction approaches on the TransClean benchmark: 1) a span-based extraction method leveraging translation quality estimation models for span detection, and 2) an LLM-based extraction method that prompts an LLM to isolate the translation. Our benchmark and analysis provide the first systematic framework to evaluate and improve the cleanliness of LLM translation outputs.

</details>


### [72] [SWRouter: Similarity-Contractive Window Routing for Multi-Turn Large Language Model Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11414)
*Yu Wang,Yuchen Li,Rui Kong,Xinran Chen,Jiamin Chen,Hengyi Cai,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jiashu Zhao,Yulun Zhang,Zhonghao Lyu,Haoyi Xiong,Linghe Kong,Jimmy Xiangji Huang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWRouter（相似度收缩窗口路由器），用于多轮对话中的大模型路由，通过相似度上下文分割构建提示，并以双指标评估解耦构建质量与路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由器在单轮场景有效，但难以直接迁移到多轮对话：路由性能高度依赖历史上下文的分割、保留与融入方式，存在信息丢失与信息混淆两大挑战，且评估时难以将模型选择与提示构建质量分离。

Method: SWRouter由两部分组成：（1）基于相似度的上下文分割机制，用于提示构建，以缓解信息丢失与混淆；（2）双指标评估框架，将构建准确性与路由器性能解耦评估。

Result: 在多轮对话基准上持续优于强基线：相比最佳单个大模型，评估准确率提升16.26%；相比Conv-ID Context基线额外提升8.22%。

Conclusion: 多轮大模型路由需要上下文构建与评估的联合设计，而非单轮路由方法的直接扩展。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit complementary strengths, motivating routing methods that dispatch each query to the most suitable model. Although existing routers are effective in single-turn settings, they do not directly transfer to multi-turn dialogue, where routing performance critically depends on how historical context is segmented, retained, and incorporated into the current prompt. This introduces two fundamental challenges: preventing information loss and information confusion during context construction, and evaluating routing quality without conflating model selection with prompt construction quality. In this paper, we propose SWRouter, a Similarity-Contractive Window Router for multi-turn large language model routing. SWRouter combines a similarity-based context segmentation mechanism for prompt construction with a dual-metric evaluation framework that decouples construction accuracy from router performance. Experiments on multi-turn dialogue benchmarks demonstrate that SWRouter consistently surpasses strong baselines, achieving a 16.26% improvement in evaluation accuracy over the best individual large language model and an additional 8.22% gain over the Conv-ID Context baseline. Our results highlight that multi-turn large language model routing requires a joint design of context construction and evaluation, rather than a direct extension of single-turn routing methods.

</details>


### [73] [ReGround: Grounding Reviewer Comments in Multimodal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11460)
*Serwar Basch,Lizhen Qu,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReGround 数据集，将 10,267 条审稿意见与 3,656 篇论文中的 16,274 处证据关联，用于审稿意见的证据定位，并揭示该任务在全文中检索效果差、证据类型推断是瓶颈、多模态证据具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审稿意见通常针对论文的具体部分，但在长篇多模态文档中将其定位到支撑证据十分困难；现有基准未覆盖该场景，且多聚焦于显式、信息检索式提问。

Method: 构建大规模数据集 ReGround，利用作者回复中常显式引用投稿内容这一高精度标注来源，将 10,267 条审稿意见链接到 16,274 处证据；将证据定位形式化为检索任务，并评测多种检索方法。

Result: 全文内容上的检索表现较差；证据类型推断是主要瓶颈；多模态证据提供了纯文本缺失的互补信号。

Conclusion: 该数据集表明，审稿意见证据定位是科学文档理解中一个困难且具有实际重要性的问题。

Abstract: Reviewer comments naturally relate to specific parts of the reviewed paper, yet grounding these comments to the underlying evidence is difficult due to long multimodal documents. Existing benchmarks do not capture this setting and largely focus on explicit, information-seeking queries. We introduce ReGround, a large-scale dataset for reviewer comment grounding that links 10,267 reviewer comments to 16,274 evidence in the original anonymous submission of 3,656 papers. We build on a simple observation: author rebuttals often include explicit references to content of the submission used to address reviewer comments, providing a high-precision annotation source. We cast grounding as a retrieval task and evaluate a wide range of retrieval methods. Results show that retrieval over the entire paper content performs poorly, evidence-type inference is a major bottleneck, and multimodal evidence provides complementary signals that text alone misses. Our dataset exposes grounding reviewer comments as a difficult and practically important problem for scientific document understanding.

</details>


### [74] [Structural priors for data-efficient language learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11505)
*Yana Veitsman,Jonas Mayer Martins,Jonathan Lautenschlager,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，在非语言数据（音乐、概率文法、元胞自动机）上预训练可为语言建模提供有效的权重初始化，降低下一词预测损失且减小后续语言训练中的权重偏移，但收益不稳定迁移到下游语言任务，效果不及增加语言数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效语言学习需要减少对大规模数据和算力的依赖。作者探索“结构迁移”：先在非语言数据上训练以诱导对自然语言有用的先验，作为多语言语言建模的权重初始化方式，从而降低对语言数据的依赖。

Method: 将非语言数据（音乐、概率文法、元胞自动机等符号数据类型）上的预训练视作权重初始化，通过下一词预测损失、模型权重变化（weight shifts）以及下游语言基准三项指标评估迁移效果，并与随机初始化及增加语言数据两种基线进行比较。

Result: 音乐、概率文法、元胞自动机等符号数据类型的语言建模损失低于随机初始化，且伴随后续语言训练中更小的权重偏移，说明结构迁移将模型置于参数空间中更有利的区域；但更低损失并未一致地转化为更好的下游语言表现，且非语言数据迁移效率低于额外的语言数据。

Conclusion: 非语言数据可以作为下一词预测训练目标的部分替代品，但不能可靠地支持更广泛的语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Efficient language learning requires methods to reduce the reliance on large data and computational resources. We investigate structural transfer: First training models on non-language data to induce useful priors for natural language. This approach is a form of weight initialization for multilingual language modeling. We evaluate transfer via next-token-prediction loss, weight shifts in the model, and downstream linguistic benchmarks. Several symbolic data types - notably music, probabilistic grammars, and cellular automata - yield lower language-modeling loss than random initialization. These gains coincide with smaller weight shifts during subsequent language training, suggesting that structural transfer positions models in a more favorable region of the parameter space. However, a lower loss does not translate consistently into better downstream linguistic performance, and transfer from non-language data is less efficient than additional language data. We conclude that non-language data can serve as a partial substitute for language data for the training objective of next-token prediction but does not reliably support broader linguistic generalization.

</details>


### [75] [Complex-Text Robustness Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Low-Resource Multilingual Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11545)
*Tianlun Zuo,Ziyu Zhang,Tingzhi Mao,Zhonghua Fu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向低资源多语言TTS的复杂文本鲁棒性诊断框架，从内容一致性、语言一致性、生成稳定性三维度评测泰语、越南语、斯瓦希里语和印尼语的普通与复杂文本输入，并引入字符错误率、语种识别准确率、时长异常率等自动指标及轻量级文本风险分数（TRS）。实验表明复杂文本暴露了短句评测无法反映的系统性失效模式，TRS与内容错误和时长异常正相关，可作为低成本的合成前风险指示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源多语言TTS虽扩展了语种覆盖，但其在复杂文本输入下的鲁棒性诊断不足。现有评测多聚焦自然度、说话人相似度和内容一致性，使用常规测试句，难以揭示系统在数字、日期、命名实体、长句、语码转换和标点结构等挑战性输入上的失效方式，缺乏输入级风险分析手段。

Method: 提出复杂文本鲁棒性诊断框架，从内容一致性、语言一致性、生成稳定性三个维度评测；面向泰语、越南语、斯瓦希里语和印尼语设计多语言鲁棒性测试方案，覆盖普通句与多类复杂文本；引入自动诊断指标（字符错误率、语种识别准确率、时长异常率）；并提出轻量级文本风险分数TRS，基于可解释文本特征在无人工标注、无模型训练条件下估计合成风险，用于生成前输入级风险分析。

Result: 在OmniVoice、VoxCPM2、MMS-TTS三个代表性多语言TTS系统上的实验显示，复杂文本输入暴露出普通短句评测无法充分反映的系统性失效模式；不同系统在数字规范化、命名实体处理、长文本生成和语码转换输入处理上呈现各自不同的脆弱环节；TRS与内容错误、时长异常呈正相关。

Conclusion: 复杂文本鲁棒性是低资源多语言TTS被忽视但关键的评测维度；所提三维诊断框架与自动指标可有效揭示系统性失效模式，TRS可作为低成本、无需训练与标注的合成前风险指示器，用于复杂文本风险诊断。

Abstract: Low-resource multilingual text-to-speech (TTS) systems have expanded language coverage, but their robustness under complex text inputs remains insufficiently diagnosed. Existing evaluations mainly focus on naturalness, speaker similarity, and content consistency using regular test sentences, while providing limited insight into how multilingual TTS systems fail when handling challenging inputs such as numbers, dates, named entities, long sentences, code-switched expressions, and punctuation-related structures. This paper proposes a complex-text robustness diagnosis framework for low-resource multilingual TTS. We evaluate robustness from three dimensions: content consistency, language consistency, and generation stability. A multilingual robustness testing scheme is designed for Thai, Vietnamese, Swahili, and Indonesian, covering ordinary sentences and multiple types of complex text inputs. We further introduce automatic diagnostic metrics, including character error rate, language identification accuracy, and duration abnormal rate. To support input-level risk analysis before speech generation, we propose a lightweight Text Risk Score (TRS), which estimates synthesis risk from interpretable text features without manual annotation or model training. Experiments on three representative multilingual TTS systems, including OmniVoice, VoxCPM2, and MMS-TTS, show that complex text inputs expose systematic failure patterns that are not fully reflected by ordinary short-sentence evaluation. Different systems exhibit distinct vulnerabilities in number normalization, named entity handling, long-text generation, and code-switched input processing. Furthermore, TRS shows a positive correlation with content errors and duration abnormalities, demonstrating its usefulness as a low-cost pre-synthesis indicator for complex-text risk diagnosis in low-resource multilingual TTS.

</details>


### [76] [A Training-Free, Alignment-Free Approach to Corporate Intelligence: Application to SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11620)
*Jean-François Delpech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练、免对齐的企业情报分析框架，基于确定性稀疏种子向量进行高维哈希，使所有文档与时间期共享同一坐标系，在普通CPU上实现亚秒级文档对比、发行人指纹、词汇漂移追踪与主题句抽取，并在多年SEC申报文件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维稠密文本嵌入与大语言模型在金融信息披露分析中面临上下文窗口限制、幻觉风险、高算力成本，以及不同独立训练模型间向量空间存在任意旋转、难以对齐等问题。作者希望绕开训练与对齐，构建可跨文档、跨时间比较的低成本语义表示。

Method: 方法为免训练、免对齐框架：将词串哈希到固定的高维基，从而在构造上把所有文档与所有时间期放入共同坐标系；在句子上下文中累加这些稀疏种子向量，得到可线性组合的语料专属语义签名；基于此支持亚秒级文档比较、发行人指纹、发行人词汇随申报变化的追踪以及主题句抽取，全部在普通CPU硬件上完成。

Result: 在多年SEC申报文件语料（10-K、10-Q、8-K）上验证，重大公司事件（如波音737 MAX危机、英特尔供应链中断、邦吉收购维特拉）呈现为可区分、可解释的语义画像，且每个画像可追溯到产生它的确切源句子，全程无需领域特定训练，也无需LLM推理。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于确定性稀疏种子向量的免训练、免对齐框架可在低成本CPU环境中实现可解释、可追溯的企业情报分析，为金融披露文本分析提供一种替代高维稠密嵌入与LLM推理的可行路径。

Abstract: High-dimensional dense text embeddings and large language models face real obstacles in financial-disclosure analysis: context-window limits, hallucination risk, high computational cost, and the arbitrary rotation of vector spaces across independently trained models. We present a training-free, alignment-free framework for corporate intelligence built on deterministic sparse seed vectors. Hashing word strings into a fixed high-dimensional basis places all documents and all temporal epochs in a common coordinate system by construction, removing any need for training or alignment. Accumulating these seed vectors across sentence contexts yields corpus-specific semantic signatures that compose linearly, supporting sub-second document comparison, issuer fingerprinting, tracking of how an issuer's vocabulary shifts between filings, and thematic sentence extraction, all on ordinary CPU hardware. Demonstrating the approach on a multi-year corpus of SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q, 8-K), we show how material corporate events, among them Boeing's 737 MAX crisis, Intel's supply-chain disruptions, and Bunge's acquisition of Viterra, emerge as distinct, interpretable semantic profiles, each traceable to the exact source sentences that produced it, with no domain-specific training and no LLM inference.

</details>


### [77] [Structured Transforms for Low-Overhead Quantization of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11687)
*Daria Cherniuk,Alexander Rudikov,Boris Kashin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文改进了基于Kashin分解的大语言模型权重量化方法，用符号随机化DCT替换稠密随机正交矩阵，降低复杂度并提供更强收敛性，在4比特量化下与主流方法竞争且数值更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kashin分解量化依赖稠密随机正交矩阵，每轮迭代代价高达O(N^2)，并且需要多重启k-means来获得稳定聚类，成为效率瓶颈。作者希望提升收敛性质、降低计算成本，并保持2比特聚类的稳定性。

Method: 保留将权重分解为两个分量（一个无穷范数有界，另一个经正交变换后无穷范数有界）的核心思想；用符号随机化离散余弦变换（DCT）替代稠密随机正交矩阵，将每轮代价从O(N^2)降至O(N log N)；提出带交替更新的贪婪算法，保证各因子稳定2比特聚类所需的四峰分布，并给出聚类中心的闭式初始化，去除多重启k-means瓶颈；再结合OPTQ式顺序误差补偿与QuIP式非相干预处理，构成JAX流水线。

Result: 在OPT、Llama-2和Pythia上以每通道4比特量化，该方法与OPTQ、QuIP、QuIP-RG以及无微调和向量量化的QuIP#变体相比具有竞争力，且墙钟时间扩展性良好。鲁棒性突出：在QuIP变体发散到四位数困惑度（Pythia-6.9B）或因LDL回代出现NaN而中止（Mistral-7B）的压力配置下，Kashin-DCT仍数值稳定并接近FP16基线。推理时每个权重分解为每通道两个2比特因子码，结构上适合原生2比特硬件。

Conclusion: 用符号随机化DCT替代稠密随机正交矩阵，能在保证四峰分布和闭式初始化聚类中心的同时显著降低迭代复杂度，并提升量化鲁棒性；结合顺序误差补偿与非相干预处理后，该方法在4比特量化上具竞争力且更稳定，分解出的两个2比特因子码利于原生2比特硬件部署。

Abstract: We revisit Kashin-decomposition-based weight quantization for large language models and propose an improved algorithm with stronger convergence properties and structured, efficient orthogonal transforms. The method retains the core factorization of each weight into two components -- one with bounded infinity norm and the other with bounded infinity norm after an orthogonal transformation -- but replaces the dense random orthogonal matrix with a sign-randomized Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT), reducing the per-iteration cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(N \log N)$. The proposed greedy algorithm with alternating updates guarantees the four-peak distribution required for stable 2-bit clustering of each factor and admits closed-form initialization of cluster centers, removing the multi-restart k-means bottleneck of prior work. Composed with OPTQ-style sequential error compensation and QuIP-style incoherence preprocessing, the resulting JAX pipeline is competitive with OPTQ, QuIP, QuIP-RG and a fine-tuning- and vector-quantization-free variant of QuIP# at 4-bit per channel on OPT, Llama-2 and Pythia, with favorable wall-clock scaling. The bounded-$\ell_\infty$ factorization is also notably robust: on stress configurations where QuIP variants diverge to four-digit perplexity (Pythia-6.9B) or abort with NaNs in LDL back-substitution (Mistral-7B), Kashin-DCT remains numerically stable and stays close to FP16 baseline. At inference time, each weight decomposes into two 2-bit factor codes per channel that are structurally suited to native-2-bit hardware.

</details>


### [78] [Negative Self-Distillation: Learning to Reason by Avoiding Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11699)
*Rongcan Pei,Zhepei Wei,Shuyao Xu,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出负向自蒸馏（NSD）框架，通过让模型远离自生成的"缺陷推理"条件（而非模仿特权解答）来优化LLM，并设计动态门控机制隔离推理关键token以避免损害基础语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）范式让模型以特权信息（如标准答案）作为自身教师，但会导致复杂推理任务性能严重退化：强迫学生模仿人为自信的推理轨迹会抑制不确定性表达，并惩罚探索性和自我纠错行为。因此需要一种不依赖标准答案或外部监督的替代框架。

Method: 提出Negative Self-Distillation（NSD）：利用模型自身生成问题特定的负向条件（如扮演"粗心推理者"），推动学生分布远离该自生成负向教师。针对朴素卸载（unlearning）目标会将缺陷推理token与基础语言token混淆、从而灾难性损害语言能力的问题，设计动态门控机制自动识别并隔离推理关键token，使梯度更新仅针对行为缺陷而保留语言先验。

Result: NSD在实验中持续优于OPSD及其他无标签、自举（self-bootstrapping）的强化学习基线。

Conclusion: 通过"远离缺陷推理"而非"模仿特权解答"的自蒸馏思路，配合动态门控隔离推理关键token，NSD为LLM自我改进提供了更有效且不损害基础语言能力的替代范式。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a popular paradigm for large language model (LLM) self-improvement, allowing models to act as their own teachers by leveraging privileged information such as ground-truth solutions. However, recent findings indicate that OPSD can severely degrade the performance of LLMs on complex reasoning tasks: By forcing the student to imitate an artificially confident reasoning trace conditioned on privileged information, OPSD inadvertently suppresses expressions of uncertainty and penalizes the exploratory, self-corrective behaviors required to solve challenging problems. To address this, we introduce Negative Self-Distillation (NSD), a new framework that optimizes LLMs by diverging from flawed reasoning rather than imitating privileged solutions. Instead of relying on ground-truth answers or external supervision, NSD uses the model itself to generate a question-specific negative condition (eg, acting as a ``careless reasoner'') and pushes the student's distribution away from this self-generated negative teacher. Naively applying unlearning objectives to achieve this divergence is problematic, as flawed reasoning tokens are confounded with basic linguistic tokens; indiscriminately penalizing both risks catastrophically degrading the model's foundational language capabilities. We resolve this by designing a dynamic gating mechanism that automatically identifies and isolates reasoning-critical tokens, ensuring gradient updates target only behavioral flaws while preserving the model's linguistic priors. Empirically, NSD consistently outperforms OPSD and other label-free, self-bootstrapping reinforcement learning (RL) baselines.

</details>


### [79] [LOCUS: Task-Aware Low-Rank Post-Training for Token-Efficient Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11739)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LOCUS通过选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间来最小化输出token成本，在保持偏好对齐目标不变的前提下显著缩短生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务成本与输出序列长度直接相关，而标准偏好对齐常常在未提升效用的情况下使回复变得冗长，因此需要研究后训练更新的参数化方式是否影响生成长度。

Method: 提出LOCUS方法，选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间，在效用约束下最小化输出token成本；在该子空间内，后训练保持原生偏好目标并冻结主干网络。

Result: 在Anthropic HH-RLHF对话偏好数据上，对Pythia-2.8B与Qwen2.5-3B两个约3B解码器主干，与协议匹配的全参数DPO、DrDPO分支及已发布SamPO检查点对比：LOCUS在Pythia-2.8B上将续写长度最多缩短39.84%，在Qwen2.5-3B上缩短14.87%–17.58%，仅更新0.24%–0.28%的参数，内部偏好诊断无实质变化。

Conclusion: 后训练更新的低秩子空间参数化可在不损害偏好对齐质量的情况下有效控制生成冗长，为降低大模型服务成本提供了轻量路径。

Abstract: Large language model serving costs scale directly with output sequence length, yet standard preference alignment often inflates response verbosity without improving utility. We study whether the parameterization of post-training updates affects generation length: low-rank subspaces alter sequence length without modifying the alignment loss. We present LOCUS, a method that selects a task-aware low-rank adaptation subspace to minimize output-token cost subject to a utility constraint. Within this subspace, post-training retains the native preference objective with a frozen backbone. Across Anthropic HH-RLHF dialogue preferences, we evaluate two $\sim$3B decoder backbones, Pythia-2.8B and Qwen2.5-3B, against protocol-matched full-parameter DPO and DrDPO branches and the released SamPO checkpoint. LOCUS reduces continuation length by up to 39.84\% on Pythia-2.8B and by 14.87--17.58\% on Qwen2.5-3B while updating only 0.24--0.28\% of model parameters, with no material change in the internal preference diagnostic.

</details>


### [80] [RAG-Safety-Bench: Reliable Evaluation of Retrieval-Augmented LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11758)
*Adithiyan Rajan Indira Saravanan,Kathleen C. Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAG-Safety-Bench 基准，通过控制检索质量、将场景细分为四种条件，量化检索增强生成（RAG）对大语言模型安全性的影响，发现良性能力与不安全能力呈反向关系，且基线安全护栏无法保证 RAG 场景下的下游安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 可提升可靠性并降低幻觉，但已有研究表明在涉及有害或危险内容的请求下，RAG 会对生成响应的整体安全性产生非预期副作用。随着越来越多的终端用户将企业文档与知识库接入基于 LLM 的系统，亟需厘清导致该安全性下降的机制。

Method: 提出 RAG-Safety-Bench 基准，以消除检索器质量的混淆效应，并将问题清晰拆分为四种条件：非 RAG、RAG + 含有害请求答案的 oracle 文档、RAG + 与有害请求相关但不含具体答案的文档、RAG + 随机安全文档，从而隔离不同因素对安全性退化的影响；在五个开源 LLM 上进行评测。

Result: 结果显示：良性能力与不安全能力之间呈反向关系；有力证据表明基线安全护栏并不能带来 RAG 场景下的下游安全保证；并提供了模型特定层面的证据，支持以往关于即使良性文档也可能导致检索启用系统生成不安全内容的发现。

Conclusion: RAG 对 LLM 安全性的影响并非单一因素所致，需在解耦检索质量与文档内容属性的条件下进行系统评估；面向 RAG 的安全防护不能仅依赖模型基线护栏，需针对检索文档与生成环节设计专门的安全机制。

Abstract: Allowing large language models (LLMs) to retrieve information from a set of trusted documents can increase reliability and reduce hallucination. However, recent work has demonstrated that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can have unintended side effects on the overall safety of the generated responses, when prompted for harmful or dangerous content. A clearer understanding of the mechanisms leading to this result is needed, as increasing numbers of end users turn to RAG to incorporate corporate documents and knowledge bases into LLM-based systems. We introduce RAG-Safety-Bench, a benchmark to measure the safety impact of RAG on LLM models. By removing the confounding effect of retriever quality, and cleanly separating the problem into four conditions -- non-RAG, RAG with an oracle document containing the answer to the harmful request, RAG with documents related to the harmful request but without the specific answer, and RAG with random, safe documents -- the benchmark isolates the impacts of different factors in the observed safety degradation. We report results across five open-source LLMs, showing an inverse relationship between benign and unsafe capability, strong evidence that baseline safety guardrails do not lead to downstream safety guarantees in the RAG case, and model-specific support for previous findings that even benign documents can lead to unsafe generation in retrieval-enabled systems.

</details>


### [81] [Component-Aware Differential Privacy for Federated Multilingual Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11762)
*Jordi Luque,Fernando López,Aleix Sant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示逐层差分隐私裁剪在语音大模型上因编码器与解码器更新范数差异导致跨组件预算坍塌问题，并提出α-split双池分配方法恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐层差分隐私裁剪通过按参数量分配裁剪预算以提升梯度保真度，但语音大模型中声学编码器与语言解码器的更新范数相差一个数量级，导致该方案失效，出现了跨组件预算坍塌，WER严重恶化甚至训练崩溃，因此需要新的分配机制。

Method: 论文在六种逐层方法与三种语音大模型架构上实证诊断坍塌根因，发现坍塌程度与组件间范数比正相关；随后提出α-split双池分配方法，将编码器与LLM参数归一化到两个独立池中，并保持联合ℓ2敏感度与原始(ε,δ)-DP保证不变，按架构校准α。

Result: 单池逐层方法在范数失衡严重时WER远差于平坦全局裁剪或训练崩溃，范数失衡较缓时自适应单池方法部分恢复；α-split在架构校准α下恢复了与平坦DP相当的WER效用，同时为编码器提供4.47倍更严格的逐组件噪声保护以抵御基于说话人声纹的梯度反演攻击，LLM噪声开销仅增加2.6%。

Conclusion: 跨组件范数失衡是逐层DP裁剪在语音大模型上失效的主因，α-split通过双池归一化在不改变DP保证的前提下实现了效用与隐私保护的更优权衡。

Abstract: Per-layer differential privacy (DP) clipping improves gradient fidelity in federated learning by allocating per-matrix clipping budgets proportional to parameter count. We show that this recipe breaks for speech large language models (speech-LLMs), when the acoustic encoder and the language decoder differ by an order of magnitude in update norm. Single-pool per-layer methods suffer \emph{cross-component budget collapse}, dragging word error rate (WER) far from flat global clipping or collapsing training entirely. When the norm imbalance is milder, adaptive single-pool methods partially recover, confirming that collapse severity scales with the inter-component norm ratio. We empirically diagnose the root cause across six per-layer methods and three speech-LLM architectures. We then propose \emph{$α$-split}, a two-pool allocation that normalises encoder and LLM parameters into independent pools, and show that joint $\ell_2$ sensitivity and the original $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP guarantee are unchanged. At architecture-calibrated $α$, our method recovers WER utility compared to flat DP, while granting the encoder $4.47{\times}$ tighter per-component noise protection against speaker voice-based gradient-inversion attacks at only $+2.6\%$ LLM noise overhead.

</details>


### [82] [Recognizing Is Not Reversing: A Controlled Inversion Test of Fact-Preserving News Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11769)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出一种受控的"框架反转"测试，考察大语言模型能否在保持事实不变的前提下，撤销已明确的新闻框架改写。结果显示模型能较好保持事实，但几乎无法实现框架反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新闻框架研究多聚焦于生成、检测或文本是否更中立，却未直接检验模型能否在事实固定的条件下撤销已知的框架变换。作者希望填补这一空白，厘清"事实保真"与"框架识别"同"框架反转"之间的关系。

Method: 基于三种既有的框架文本实现形式（评价性词汇、施事性表达、信息显著性），对60篇新闻文章施加三种干预强度，构建540组事实原子性得到保留、编辑记录可追溯的配对变体；在Qwen、DeepSeek、Kimi三个模型上进行受控反转测试。

Result: 事实保真度维持在约0.84，而干预反转率仅为0.044–0.068；即便同时正确识别框架类型与方向，汇总反转率也仅达0.071。

Conclusion: 事实保真、框架识别与框架反转之间存在明显分离：识别一篇文章如何被框架化，并不意味着模型能够撤销该框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to analyze and rewrite news, yet current framing studies mainly evaluate generation, detection, or whether rewritten text appears more neutral. They do not directly show whether a model can undo a known framing transformation while keeping the facts fixed. We introduce a controlled inversion test over three established textual realizations of framing: evaluative lexis, agency realization, and information salience. Across 60 news articles and three intervention strengths, this yields 540 paired variants with preserved atomic facts and recorded edits. Across Qwen, DeepSeek, and Kimi, factual preservation remains near 0.84, whereas intervention reversal is 0.044--0.068. Even when both framing type and direction are recognized correctly, pooled reversal reaches 0.071. These results reveal a clear separation between factual fidelity, framing recognition, and framing inversion: recognizing how an article is framed does not imply that the framing can be undone.

</details>


### [83] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Switch Aware Evaluation of ASR and Audio Language Models on English Yoruba Code-Switched Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11786)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对11个现代语音系统（6个ASR模型和5个音频语言模型）在英语-约鲁巴语代码切换语音上进行切换感知评估，发现聚合WER掩盖了代码切换行为，音频LM在切换局部指标上显著优于WER最佳系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统和音频语言模型在单语基准上已报告较低错误率，但它们在低资源、含变音符号丰富的语言中的代码切换语音上的行为仍缺乏充分表征。

Method: 使用确定性2000条话语评估集和共享评分流程，对6个ASR模型和5个音频LM进行评估；除WER外，还报告切换进入词错误率（SETER）、窗口化切换点错误率、语言特定错误率以及变音符号不敏感WER等切换局部诊断指标。

Result: WER最佳系统（一个ASR模型）与领先音频LM在WER上统计不可区分，但音频LM在所有切换局部指标上显著更优；在忠实系统中，约鲁巴语词元识别崩溃（几乎所有系统错误率0.97），英语词元识别好得多，错误急剧集中在切换进入约鲁巴语处；若干生成式音频LM作为精确转录器失败，产生翻译、冗长和提示泄漏，且强烈依赖提示。

Conclusion: 聚合WER掩盖了代码切换行为，需采用切换感知基准；作者发布清单、指标实现和评估脚本，以支持非洲代码切换语音的可复现、切换感知基准测试。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems and audio language models (audio LMs) now report low error rates on monolingual benchmarks, but their behavior on code switched speech in low resource, diacritic rich languages remains poorly characterized. We present a switch aware evaluation of eleven modern systems (six ASR models and five audio LMs) on English Yoruba code-switched speech, using a deterministic 2000 utterance evaluation set and a shared scoring pipeline. Beyond word error rate (WER), we report switch localized diagnostics: a switch entry token error rate (SETER), windowed switch point error rates, language specific error rates, and a diacritic insensitive WER. Our central finding is that aggregate WER hides code switching behavior. The best system by WER (an ASR model) is statistically indistinguishable from a leading audio LM on WER, yet the audio LM is significantly better on every switch localized metric. Across faithful systems, Yoruba token recognition collapses (error 0.97 for almost all systems) while English tokens are recognized far better, and errors concentrate sharply at switches into Yoruba. Several generative audio LMs fail as exact transcribers, producing translation, verbosity, and prompt leakage that are strongly prompt dependent. We release manifests, metric implementations, and evaluation scripts to support reproducible, switch aware benchmarking for African code switched speech.

</details>


### [84] [Target leakage, not model class, explains reported accuracy in survey-based cardiovascular screening: a leakage-tiered audit of glass-box and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11838)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif,Samer Ellaham,Cedric Schmitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在442,067名BRFSS 2022受访者上基准测试十种分类器（含表格基础模型），按五个泄漏风险递减的特征层级评估心肌梗死预测，并在2023年430,755人上冻结验证。结果表明：高AUROC主要来自目标泄漏，去除两个诊断后特征使所有模型AUROC下降0.049–0.051，模型间差距压缩至0.0045；可解释提升机在预设0.005非劣边界内不输任何模型且推理快约104倍；公平性、共形覆盖与校准问题均可通过透明方法修复，冻结模型跨年迁移误差≤0.002 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往基于全国健康调查的心血管筛查模型普遍报告接近0.89的AUROC，但该精度究竟源于真实学习还是目标泄漏尚不明确；同时表格基础模型是否改变结论、以及部署所需的校准、公平性、共形覆盖、解释忠实度与推理成本等属性能否在联合审查下成立，均缺乏系统检验。

Method: 以2022年BRFSS的442,067名受访者的心肌梗死预测为任务，构建五个泄漏风险递减的特征层级，基准测试线性、树集成、神经网络、玻璃盒及表格基础模型共十种分类器；对每个模型审计判别力、校准、显式筛查阈值下的公平性、共形覆盖、解释忠实度与推理成本；随后冻结模型与阈值，应用于2023年430,755名受访者进行外部迁移验证。

Result: 移除两个诊断后特征使所有模型损失0.049–0.051 AUROC，模型性能收敛至0.0045宽的区间；可解释提升机在预设0.005非劣边际内不劣于任何替代方案，且评分速度约为最强基础模型的104倍；单一阈值对女性心梗检出率为75.4%，男性为89.0%，编辑模型形状函数后差距缩小至0.010；边际共形预测对男性覆盖0.86、对60岁以上成人覆盖0.82，Mondrian校准修复了所有分层；冻结模型跨年迁移AUROC差异在0.002以内。

Conclusion: 该文献中报告的性能提升空间是特征集的性质而非学习器的性质。透明性未带来可测量的代价，并使公平性修复与不确定性条件化可直接审计。评估实践而非模型容量，才是当前的约束瓶颈。

Abstract: Cardiovascular screening models trained on national health surveys routinely report areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) near 0.89. We asked whether that accuracy reflects learning or target leakage, whether tabular foundation models change the answer, and whether the properties deployment requires survive joint examination. We benchmarked ten classifiers spanning linear, tree-ensemble, neural, glass-box, and tabular foundation classes for prevalent myocardial infarction in 442,067 respondents of the 2022 Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System across five feature tiers of decreasing leakage risk. Each was audited for discrimination, calibration, fairness at an explicit screening threshold, conformal coverage, explanation faithfulness, and inference cost, then applied -- models and thresholds frozen -- to 430,755 respondents of 2023. Removing two post-diagnostic features cost every model 0.049-0.051 AUROC, collapsing the field into a 0.0045-wide band. The glass-box explainable boosting machine was non-inferior to every alternative within a pre-specified 0.005 margin while scoring the cohort roughly 104 times faster than the strongest foundation model. One threshold detected 75.4% of women's infarctions against 89.0% of men's; editing the model's shape functions reduced the gap to 0.010. Marginal conformal prediction gave 0.86 coverage to men and 0.82 to adults over 60; Mondrian calibration repaired every stratum. Frozen models transported within 0.002 AUROC. Reported headroom in this literature is a property of the feature set, not the learner. Transparency cost nothing measurable and made fairness repair and uncertainty conditioning directly auditable. Evaluation practice, not model capacity, is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [85] [IndicTriMix: Developing Language Identification Datasets and Models for Tri-Language Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11851)
*Pruthwik Mishra,Rudra Trivedi,Avi Patel,Ashok Urlana,Shrikant Malviya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究代码混合文本中的词级语言识别，将其建模为序列标注任务，微调MuRIL与XLM-RoBERTa，在印地语、古吉拉特语、孟加拉语三种配置上评估，并公开基准与微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中代码混合现象普遍，用户在单句内频繁切换语言，准确识别每个词的语言成为迫切需求；而面向单语文本的传统语言识别模型难以胜任代码混合场景下的词级识别。

Method: 将任务形式化为序列标注问题，微调面向印度语言优化的上下文Transformer模型MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa；提出两种基于三种语言平行句的代码混合生成方法；在印地语、古吉拉特语、孟加拉语三种数据配置上预测每个词的语言标签；构建并发布含人工标注测试集的基准。

Result: 训练模型在三种语言配置上均表现出有效性，证明上下文嵌入对多语言社交媒体文本的词级语言识别具有显著优势；同时公开了人工标注的测试基准和微调模型。

Conclusion: 上下文Transformer模型适用于代码混合文本的词级语言识别，可为后续研究提供基准与可复现的模型资源。

Abstract: Language identification in code-mixed text, largely observed in social media, is highly essential when users frequently switch between multiple languages within a single utterance. Accurately identifying the languages of code-mixed tokens becomes an urgent necessity. Traditional language identification models, designed for monolingual text, are not well suited for token-level language identification in code-mixed settings. We formulate the task as a sequence labeling problem and fine-tune contextual transformer-based models MuRIL and XLM-RoBERTa best suited for Indian languages. We evaluate these systems on three different data configurations (Hindi, Gujarati, and Bengali) to predict language labels for individual tokens. We release a benchmark for language identification in code-mixed tokens with manually annotated test sets. We propose two approaches of code-mixed generation using parallel sentences of three languages. The trained models demonstrate the effectiveness of contextual embeddings for token-level language identification in multilingual social media text. For reproducibility and to facilitate future research, we publicly release our fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [86] [Epistemic orientation predicts legislative effectiveness among members of the US Congress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11865)
*Segun Aroyehun,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将证据导向话语分析从国会整体转向议员个体，用EMI分数衡量议员在演讲和推文中证据导向与直觉导向语言的相对比例，并考察其与意识形态、跨平台一致性及立法效能的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实、基于证据的沟通是民主治理、问责和集体决策的重要基础。既有研究表明，自1970年代中期以来美国国会演讲中证据导向语言有所下降，但相关分析多停留在国会会期层面，尚不清楚这种认知取向是否在议员个体间存在系统性差异，以及是否与政治行为和立法效能相关。

Method: 采用Evidence-Minus-Intuition（EMI）分数，测量议员在国会演讲和Twitter帖子中证据导向语言相对直觉导向语言的流行度；将EMI与议员层面的意识形态、机构职位、传播语境及立法效能分数（LES）等数据进行关联分析，并在控制政治、制度、人口、议题和传播量等变量后进行回归检验。

Result: 意识形态越极端的议员在国会演讲中使用证据导向语言越少；EMI具有跨平台一致性，即在演讲中更证据导向的议员在Twitter上也更证据导向，但Twitter上的EMI整体更低；在控制意识形态及大量政治、制度、人口、议题和传播量变量后，国会演讲中的EMI仍与议员个体立法效能正相关。

Conclusion: 证据导向语言不仅是国会话语的总体特征，也是议员个体立法沟通与立法效能的一个有意义的属性。

Abstract: Truth and evidence-based communication provide important foundations for democratic governance, accountability, and collective decision-making. Prior work shows that evidence-oriented language in US congressional floor speeches has declined since the mid-1970s, alongside broader changes in legislative productivity and polarization. This study shifts the analysis from congressional sessions to individual members of Congress to examine whether epistemic orientation varies systematically across legislators and whether it relates to political behavior and legislative effectiveness. Using the Evidence-Minus-Intuition (EMI) score, we measure the relative prevalence of evidence-oriented versus intuition-oriented language in congressional floor speeches and Twitter posts. We link these measures to legislator-level data on ideology, institutional position, communication context, and Legislative Effectiveness Score (LES). The results show that more ideologically extreme members use less evidence-oriented language on the congressional floor. EMI also exhibits cross-platform consistency with members who use more evidence-oriented language in floor speeches also being more evidence-oriented on Twitter, although EMI is lower on Twitter overall. Finally, EMI in congressional speeches is positively associated with individual legislative effectiveness, even after accounting for ideology and extensive political, institutional, demographic, topical, and communication volume controls. These findings suggest that evidence-oriented language is not only an aggregate feature of congressional discourse but also a meaningful attribute of individual-level legislative communication and effectiveness.

</details>


### [87] [Augustinian BabyLM: What Ostensive Definition Can and Cannot Teach a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11870)
*Lisa Bylinina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在小型掩码语言模型训练前对视觉可落地词注入图像特征初始化，作者发现视觉初始化留下了持续到训练结束的可测量印记，但大多数BabyLM基准测试对此不敏感，仅在物体-属性知识任务上表现出优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常从随机词嵌入开始训练，词汇意义完全靠语料学习。作者想检验圣奥古斯丁式的"以指物释义"（ostension）假设：如果某些词在训练前就获得来自视觉的接地初始化，会产生什么可测量的影响，以及现有评测能否捕捉到这种影响。

Method: 用1000万词训练一个小型掩码语言模型（DeBERTa）。训练前，使视觉接地的token获得由其所标注图像区域导出的嵌入，其余token保持随机初始化。随后进行多项评测：BabyLM基准（探测抽象语法知识）、零样本物体-属性知识（COMPS）、以及作者构建的语料定制版Visual-Property Swap基准（测试颜色、材质、尺寸、形状，并标注每项的训练频率与是否被种子化）。另做因果检验：对先前未种子化的词进行合成接地，观察优势是否转移到这些词。

Result: 视觉初始化留下了可测量的印记并持续至训练结束。但在大多数BabyLM基准上效果不可见，不影响表现。唯一零样本例外是物体-属性知识（COMPS），种子化在每种配置下都有帮助。在定制的Visual-Property Swap基准上，视觉种子模型表现出持久的、可复现的优势，且局限于被种子化的词。因果检验显示，对未种子化词进行合成接地可将优势精确转移到这些词。功能词和抽象词汇也获得了强视觉种子并在训练中保持，训练目标确实利用了它们：这些词的留出掩码预测损失在每个种子下均下降，但没有任何基准测试反映出这一点。

Conclusion: 视觉接地初始化对语言模型有真实且持久的内部影响，但现有主流基准（尤其是探测抽象语法知识的BabyLM）大多无法检测到它，甚至在掩码预测损失下降的情况下也毫无反映。何种评测能捕捉这一效应仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: A language model normally begins training with random word embeddings: whatever 'banana' means must be learned from training corpora. I implement St. Augustine's picture of word learning, meaning by ostension, for a small masked language model (DeBERTa) trained on 10M words: before training, visually grounded tokens receive embeddings derived from the image regions they label; other tokens start random. Visual initialization leaves a measurable imprint that lasts until the end of training. At the same time, the effect remains invisible under most BabyLM benchmarks, which probe abstract grammatical knowledge: visual initialization does not affect performance there. The only zero-shot exception is object-property knowledge (COMPS, Misra et al. 2023), where seeding helps in every configuration. To follow up on this result, I build a corpus-tailored version of the Visual-Property Swap benchmark (Lin et al., 2026), which tests color, material, size, and shape knowledge, with per-item training frequency and seeded status. Here, vision-seeded models have a persistent, seed- replicated advantage, confined to the seeded words. As a causal test, I show that synthetic grounding of previously unseeded words transfers the advantage to exactly those words. Function words and abstract vocabulary also receive strong visual seeds and retain them throughout training, and the training objective draws on them: held-out mask-prediction loss falls for these words in every seed. However, no benchmark I run registers this. What evaluation would pick this up remains an open question.

</details>


### [88] [Nuha-Speech: Building General-Purpose Arabic Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11892)
*Yingzhi Wang,Reem Alhazzani,Muhammad Alqurishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Nuha-Speech，构建阿拉伯语语音大模型基础设施，涵盖150万+训练样本的语音问答语料、基于Qwen-Omni的微调及评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音大模型（speech-LLMs）日益多语言化，阿拉伯语仍严重缺乏代表性，缺少专门用于训练与评测阿拉伯语语音大模型的基础设施。

Method: 构建大规模阿拉伯语语音问答（SQA）语料，含超150万训练样本，覆盖多项核心语音任务以支持指令微调；基于不同规模的Qwen-Omni模型变体进行监督微调；并设计包含多样任务与定制指标的评测框架。

Result: 形成了涵盖数据构建、模型训练与系统评测的完整方案，训练语料规模超150万样本，并建立了配套评测体系（摘要未给出具体性能数值）。

Conclusion: 在阿拉伯语语音资源受限的约束下，为阿拉伯语语音大模型建立基础性基础设施。

Abstract: As Speech Large Language Models (speech-LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, Arabic remains significantly underrepresented, highlighting the need for dedicated infrastructure to train and evaluate Arabic speech-LLMs.
  To address this gap, we introduce Nuha-Speech, a comprehensive initiative to develop general-purpose Arabic speech-LLMs spanning dataset construction, model training, and systematic evaluation. Specifically, we constructed a large-scale Arabic Speech Question-Answering (SQA) corpus comprising over 1.5 million training samples to allow instruction tuning over a broad range of core speech tasks. Then, the corpus was used for supervised fine-tuning based on Qwen-Omni model variants at different scales. Finally, we designed an evaluation framework featuring diverse tasks and tailored metrics. Through this work, we aim to establish foundational infrastructures for Arabic Speech-LLMs under constraints imposed by limited Arabic speech resources.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M3-Former，一种由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，通过静态属性与航行意图语义先验、双粒度 MoE 结构及转向加权交叉熵损失，实现 1–4 小时长期轨迹的鲁棒预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对船舶轨迹预测中行为多模态性、语义信息利用不足以及长期误差累积三大挑战，现有方法未能充分利用静态属性与航行意图等语义先验，且在长时预测下易产生轨迹漂移。

Method: 构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练 LLM 编码静态语义信息并与动态轨迹特征通过自注意力对齐；引入双粒度 MoE 架构，序列级专家建模全局航线趋势，词元级专家细化局部操纵行为；设计转向加权交叉熵损失以缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布。

Result: 在真实丹麦 AIS 数据集上，M3-Former 在 1 至 4 小时各预测时域均优于最强基线；4 小时预测任务中 ADE 与 FDE 分别降低 4.4% 和 5.1%。定性与消融分析表明语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度 MoE 提升复杂航道与航线分叉场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的层次化预测范式，联合建模高层航行意图与局部运动动力学，为鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [90] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 异方差预测（同时估计位置与尺度参数）通常用于不确定性量化，本文发现它还能改善点估计。作者提出 Halo：复用既有深度预测器架构，增加一个尺度输出，并以匹配的负对数似然训练。在标准基准的五个电价市场上，将三种SOTA模型（Transformer、图网络+VAE、单层卷积网络）分别在高斯与拉普拉斯损失下改造，30组模型-市场-指标比较中28组MSE与MAE均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测一般被定位为不确定性量化工具；而现有文献在非时间序列任务上报告了异方差估计对点估计的负面结果。本文试图澄清：在时间序列预测中，显式估计尺度参数是否也能提升点估计精度，并检验这种增益是否依赖特定架构设计或超参数重调。

Method: 提出 Halo：保留既有深度预测器的架构，为其增加第二个输出头以估计隐式分布的尺度参数，并在与该分布匹配的负对数似然（高斯或拉普拉斯）下训练。在五种电价市场基准上，将 Transformer、图网络+变分自编码器、单层卷积网络三种SOTA模型分别改造为异方差版本，与原点估计基线对比，并考察尺度来源（第二投影头 vs 完整并行网络）与超参数重调的影响。

Result: 在30组模型-市场-指标比较中，28组的MSE与MAE得到改善：平均MSE下降2.6%~16.5%，平均MAE下降1.7%~11.0%。两项发现：(1) 尺度估计来自单一投影头还是完整并行网络，其影响远小于"是否进行尺度估计"本身；(2) 改善在原点估计基线已调好的超参数下依然成立，因此重调超参数是可选的。

Conclusion: 在时间序列预测中，异方差建模不仅服务于不确定性量化，还能稳定提升点预测精度；该增益对架构细节不敏感、对超参数重调不依赖，说明Halo是一种低成本、易迁移的通用改进手段。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [91] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样把文本提示转化为拓扑优化的生成梯度，与有限元灵敏度逐迭代耦合，实现以自然语言表达设计意图的结构/热弹性拓扑优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承载构型（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化难以生成此类几何，且缺乏用自然语言表达设计意图的手段。

Method: 以冻结文本到图像扩散模型为免训练先验，经分数蒸馏采样（SDS）提取提示诱导的生成梯度，与有限元灵敏度在每次迭代中联合更新密度场；采用β-continuation的Heaviside投影抑制中间密度；并以基于骨架的自动化流程将优化密度场转为可导入CAD的候选几何。

Result: 在4个几何域、2种物理机制下共245次主SDS运行，49个提示—域组合中38个取得显著柔度下降（力学最高−31.5%，热弹性−23.0%），优于梯度基线；跨域形态分析发现共性结构特征：多数域中生成先验抑制肋骨架的端支（dead-end branch），端点—柔度相关性r=+0.56~+0.99；Heaviside投影+β-continuation将中间密度从42.6%降至<3%。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可将同一生成先验迁移到不同域、载荷与物理目标（各问题物理设置独立指定），使预训练生成模型成为可复用的免训练工程设计先验。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [92] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在室内火灾检测中的应用，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端和单一固定聚合服务器信任问题，提出数据集、压缩检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒FL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测系统使用边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中收集，因此适合联邦学习；但现有联邦方案未解决三个实际障碍：有限上行带宽、拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端，以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任。因此需要同时应对这三类问题。

Method: (i) 从八个公开来源整理构建室内火灾检测数据集；(ii) 设计可边缘部署的检测器，将模型更新压缩最高10倍；(iii) 提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者，以剔除逐轮过滤器漏掉的隐蔽攻击，并消除固定服务器的单点故障。

Result: 模型更新压缩最高10倍时仅有少量平衡准确率损失；轮换协调者方法在留出测试集上达到与固定服务器对应方法相当的准确率和检测速度；物理分布式六节点云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 将数据集、轻量压缩检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒聚合结合，可在保护边缘敏感数据的同时缓解带宽、恶意客户端和固定服务器单点故障问题，并通过真实分布式部署证明系统可行。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [93] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究共形校准迁移问题：当仅有源空间带标签校准数据、而目标空间只有无标签配对观测时，如何将源空间校准迁移到目标空间并保持覆盖保证。提出TCC框架，包含TCC-KS与weighted-TCC两种校正方法，并提供自适应于可观测失配程度的有限样本目标域覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测的覆盖保证依赖校准数据与部署数据之间的可交换性。然而在实际场景中（如配对模态、传感器更换），带标签的校准数据仅存在于源空间，目标空间只能获得无标签的配对观测，交换性假设被打破，直接迁移校准会导致目标域覆盖失效，因此需要研究共形校准迁移问题。

Method: 提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)：首先利用无标签配对观测将源空间带标签校准"运输"到目标空间；随后仅利用目标域无标签输入校正运输后的残余失配。具体实例化两种互补方法：TCC-KS基于无标签不确定性代理检测失配并进行保守校准调整；weighted-TCC在权重稳定时对运输后的校准进行重加权，以提升效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS数据集上，TCC在无目标域带标签校准数据的条件下实现了可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，并且无需标签的诊断指标能够预测何时需要进行校正。

Conclusion: TCC能够将源域共形校准有效迁移至目标域，在缺乏目标域标签的情况下保持覆盖有效性；其校正机制可根据可观测失配程度自适应调整，且无标签诊断可指导校正的触发，为跨域共形预测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [94] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应边际序数损失（AMOL）以抑制序数分类中的“中心类对冲”失败模式，并引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为诊断指标；在四个基准上取得最佳或并列最佳的QWK，非对称变体在Abalone上将CHR降至0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失会使序数分类网络将预测向中心类靠拢（中心类对冲），因为预测中间类可最小化期望对称损失。现有序数损失虽缓解大误差惩罚与排序一致性等问题，但均未依据真实标签相对序数中心的位置来直接抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 提出AMOL：对逐类损失项施加乘性权重 m(k,y)=1+α·(1-|k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)，其中c为中心类、k为候选类、y为真实标签；仅当候选类靠近中心且真实标签远离中心时权重较大，其余情况退化为标准行为。同时提出诊断指标CHR以量化该失败模式，并给出非对称变体AMOL-asym。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准、五个随机种子上，AMOL相对交叉熵、OLL、SORD基线在全部四个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳QWK。AMOL-asym在Abalone数据集上完全消除中心类对冲（CHR=0.000±0.000，五个种子均如此，每次运行约266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL通过联合条件加权有效抑制中心类对冲，在不损害整体序数分类性能（QWK）的前提下优于现有基线；其非对称变体可彻底消除该失败模式，CHR作为直接诊断指标具有实用价值。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [95] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在持续强化学习中，基于Abel等人将稳定性-可塑性权衡重构为“赋权-可塑性”权衡的框架，针对目前缺乏在此新定义下优化可塑性的研究空白，首次给出了在马尔可夫决策过程中优化可塑性的Bellman最优方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习的核心挑战是稳定性-可塑性权衡。Abel等人（2025）将可塑性定义为从观测到动作的广义有向信息、将赋权定义为从动作到观测的广义有向信息，成功将传统权衡重构为赋权-可塑性权衡。然而，尽管赋权的优化已有大量研究，在新定义下优化可塑性的研究仍是空白，本文正是填补该空白。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下，研究可塑性的优化问题。可塑性被定义为从智能体观测到其动作的广义有向信息（generalized directed information）。本文通过类比既有赋权优化的工作，推导可塑性优化的Bellman最优方程。

Result: 证明了存在一个用于优化可塑性的Bellman最优方程，其形式与以往赋权优化工作中的Bellman最优方程类似。

Conclusion: 本文是针对在新定义下优化可塑性的初步工作，给出了可塑性优化的Bellman最优方程，为在赋权-可塑性权衡框架下系统优化可塑性奠定了基础。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [96] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 设计并实现 DR-LabStack（React-Flask），将四个异构预训练糖尿病视网膜病变模型通过统一表单、后端适配器与统一 JSON 响应集成，并完成功能性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 DR 预测模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理需求和输出语义上各不相同，难以通过统一的临床界面访问，因此需要显式协调前端界面与推理服务。

Method: 构建 React-Flask Web 系统，集成 RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost、Two-level Ensemble 四个外部模型；共享表单按序获取模型特征、渲染数值与类别控件并构建位置输入向量；后端适配器加载异构工件并应用集成模型附带的 scaler；统一 JSON 响应支持二分类展示及方法与来源信息。

Result: 在 2026-09-08 的隔离环境中使用复制的应用文件与真实模型工件进行功能评估：四个模型全部加载并暴露 14、6、8、25 字段契约；62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求刻画服务行为，12 次有限向量检查确认调用路径与阈值一致性；24 个带 mock 传输的浏览器组件场景验证输入顺序与结果渲染，并刻画输入校验行为。

Conclusion: 系统展示了面向异构 DR 模型的可复用交互与服务工作流；论文贡献在于 Web 系统设计、集成与软件功能，临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需单独评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [97] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出RiVaT-Fuse，一个可靠性校准的变分张量融合框架，将多模态融合定义为样本级的隐状态估计问题，用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度，并在图像元数据预测任务上取得最优总体预测排名与更优的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像元数据预测需要融合异构证据，而证据的可靠性会随样本和潜在因素变化。现有表示级融合方法通常直接选择拼接、门控、条件调制或注意力等聚合结构，却没有明确定义在模态不确定性下"融合后的表示"应当意味着什么，缺乏对融合语义的显式刻画与可靠性建模。

Method: 提出RiVaT-Fuse：将融合建模为样本级隐状态估计，而非直接聚合生成融合向量。通过变分目标估计共识隐状态，该目标在图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性之间取得平衡。框架用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度，将交互分解为加性、乘性和关系性组件，并将隐状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测耦合。论文给出隐状态求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用高效的低秩加对角信任算子实例化框架。

Result: 在图像级图像元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse在直接表示级基线中取得最强的总体预测排名，同时在扰动下提升了概率稳定性与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: 融合应被显式定义为在模态不确定性下的隐状态估计，而非聚合架构的选择；以矩阵值信任几何和分解式交互为核心的变分张量融合，能够在预测性能与鲁棒稳定性两个维度上同时优于传统表示级融合方法。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [98] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Epanechnikov核先验贝叶斯小波收缩去噪的入侵鸟声分类框架，结合MFCC与谱特征，SVM在10维MFCC下最高达0.9398准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声高，导致鸟类（尤其入侵物种）鸣声信号退化，影响生物声学数据的自动化分析与生态监测。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩去噪（具闭式决策规则、计算高效）；对iNaturalist三种鸟（Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea、Passer domesticus）录音处理；提取MFCC及熵、过零率等谱特征；比较随机森林、多项逻辑回归与SVM在不同特征维度下的表现。

Result: 小波预处理显著提升分类性能，SVM在10维MFCC配置下取得最高准确率0.9398。

Conclusion: 该框架为自动化生态监测与生物入侵管理提供了稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate. Several supervised learning models: Random Forest, Multinomial Logistic Regression and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were evaluated across different feature dimensionalities. Our results demonstrate that the proposed wavelet based preprocessing significantly enhances classification performance, with the SVM model achieving the highest accuracy (up to 0.9398) under a 10-dimensional MFCC configuration. This research provides a robust statistical tool for automated ecological monitoring and the management of biological invasions.

</details>


### [99] [Flow Duality and Source Geometry for Categorical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10863)
*Etrit Haxholli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了连续流匹配与离散流匹配之间的一种对偶关系：将带one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径逐位置取argmax，可得到离散凸插值路径。该结果要求源分布具有适当的坐标对称性与边界正则性，并使连续源分布成为类别生成中的一个显式设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流匹配与离散流匹配通常被视为两套彼此独立的构造，缺乏统一的理论联系；同时，类别生成中源分布的设计往往被忽视，缺少关于源几何如何影响离散插值行为的系统认识。本文旨在建立两者的对偶桥梁，并凸显源分布设计的重要性。

Method: 在源分布满足坐标对称性与边界正则性的条件下，对带one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径做逐位置argmax投影，证明其产生离散凸插值路径；并针对高斯、有界均匀、中心化负指数三类源，推导所诱导的离散插值行为。

Result: 不同源几何会导致性质上不同的转移时序以及对词表规模的依赖；小规模视觉诊断与简短的语言建模试验表明，这些源设计效应也会出现在学习到的传输过程与早期生成质量中。

Conclusion: 连续与离散流匹配可通过argmax投影建立对偶联系，连续源分布由此成为类别生成中的显式设计变量；源分布的选择会显著影响转移时序、词表规模依赖及生成质量，为类别生成中的源设计提供了理论依据与经验线索。

Abstract: Continuous and discrete flow matching are usually treated as separate constructions. This paper identifies a duality between them: projecting continuous convex-interpolant paths with one-hot targets through a position-wise argmax yields discrete convex-interpolant paths. The result requires source laws with appropriate coordinate symmetry and boundary regularity, and it makes the continuous source distribution an explicit design choice for categorical generation. We derive the induced discrete interpolation behavior for Gaussian, bounded-uniform, and centered negative-exponential sources, showing that different source geometries lead to qualitatively different transition timing and vocabulary-size dependence. Small visual diagnostics and a short language-modeling pilot suggest that these source-design effects can also appear in learned transports and early generative quality.

</details>


### [100] [Certifying Lower Bounds for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning under Adversarial State Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10866)
*Tong Li,Saunak Kumar Panda,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将强化学习的认证方法从风险中性目标扩展到风险敏感目标，通过在$l_p$范数有界状态扰动下建立累积奖励指数效用的下界，将认证问题转化为凸优化并求其对偶以获得可处理的近似，同时提出独立选择训练风险厌恶参数的实证方法，在OpenAI Gym和机器更换问题上验证了风险厌恶训练通常能提升认证下界，但过度保守会导致非单调的认证表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中的强化学习智能体常受状态观测对抗扰动的威胁，存在安全隐患。现有认证方法虽能通过累积奖励期望的下界提升鲁棒性，但主要针对风险中性目标，缺乏对风险敏感目标的认证，无法满足安全关键应用的需求。

Method: 在$l_p$范数（$1\leq p<\infty$）有界状态对抗扰动下，建立累积奖励指数效用的下界；通过对扰动集引入$\phi$-散度松弛，将风险敏感认证问题表述为凸优化，并推导其对偶以获得可处理的认证下界近似；进一步提出一种实证方法，将训练时的风险厌恶参数$\beta$与评估时使用的风险水平独立选择，以提升认证下界。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym环境和机器更换问题上的实验表明：与风险中性训练相比，风险厌恶训练通常得到具有更高认证下界的策略，尤其在较大扰动预算下更明显；在风险中性和风险厌恶两种评估设置下，训练中风险厌恶程度的增加会导致非单调的认证表现，认证下界先提升，但随后因策略过于保守而下降。

Conclusion: 论文成功将认证方法扩展到风险敏感目标，提出了基于凸优化与对偶的可处理认证下界近似，并给出解耦训练与评估风险参数的实证策略。实验揭示风险厌恶训练对认证性能的收益及其非单调性，表明需在鲁棒性与保守性之间权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) agents deployed in real-world environments are often vulnerable to adversarial perturbations in state observations, creating risks in safety-critical applications. Certification methods can improve robustness against adversarial perturbations by providing lower bounds on expected cumulative rewards. Existing certification methods, however, mainly focus on risk-neutral objectives. In this paper, we extend certification methods to risk-sensitive objectives by establishing lower bounds on the exponential utility of cumulative rewards under $l_{p}$-norm-bounded state adversarial perturbations ($1\leq p <\infty$). By introducing a $φ$-divergence relaxation of the perturbation set, we formulate the risk-sensitive certification problem as a convex optimization and derive its dual to obtain a tractable approximation of the certified lower bound. We further propose an empirical method that improves certified lower bounds by selecting the training risk-aversion parameter $β$ independently of the risk level used during evaluation. Experiments on both OpenAI Gym environments and a machine replacement problem show that, compared to risk-neutral training, risk-averse training generally yields policies with higher certified lower bounds, particularly under larger perturbation budgets. Moreover, under both risk-neutral and risk-averse evaluation settings, increasing risk aversion during training leads to non-monotonic certification performance, where certified lower bounds initially improve but eventually decrease due to overly conservative policies.

</details>


### [101] [Phase-Decoupled, Model-Calibrated Power Control for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11133)
*Jae Gon Kim,Donghoon Yoo,Hanyul Ryu,Sungho Ha,Juyeon Lee,Soojung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PD分离式LLM服务的GPU功耗约束，提出阶段解耦、按模型标定的功耗控制器：prefill用带时延下限的SM时钟窗口，decode用自动标定的功耗上限，在B200上实现tokens/J与e2e时延的双轴Pareto改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心GPU功耗是LLM服务容量的硬约束，生产服务已转向prefill/decode（PD）分离。NVIDIA Max-Q推理配置在分离式B200上实测收益有限（+8.6% tokens/J）、依赖模型、且带来吞吐评估无法暴露的端到端时延代价（+5.2%）；该配置还对处于相反硬件状态的prefill与decode GPU套用同一设置。作者假设最优功耗设置是部署组合（模型、量化、引擎、硬件）的属性而非GPU类属性，每条lane应各有其配置，且将SLO余量安全转化为能效需在运行时SLO护栏下进行时延门控标定，而非固定配方。

Method: 提出阶段解耦、按模型标定的控制器：prefill lane运行于以时延保证为构造性下限的SM时钟窗口；decode lane运行于功耗上限，该上限由自动标定置于实测吞吐/时延悬崖之上。由于分离式decode lane功耗平坦且受限于内存，功耗上限持续生效，POLCA所反对的响应式超调弱点不存在，GPU自身功耗管理器可在上限内维持吞吐。通过decode致动器A/B对比标定功耗上限与静态时钟锁定。

Result: 在8x B200节点上服务Qwen3-Coder-480B（FP8）的agentic负载：平衡模式达+20.4% tokens/J、平均e2e +3.5%，优于Max-Q的+8.6%、+5.2%，在双轴上均为Pareto改进。Qwen3-235B-A22B（NVFP4）上各运行模式每次重复均满足ITL-p99 SLO，而两款厂商配置均未达标。decode致动器A/B显示标定功耗上限优于静态时钟锁定；三天持续运行节省lane pair 32.3%电力。两模型均为MoE，稠密模型收益约低5倍，故结论限定于MoE服务。

Conclusion: 最优功耗设置应视为部署组合属性，prefill与decode需分别配置；在运行时SLO护栏下进行时延门控的自动标定，可将SLO余量安全转化为能效。所提控制器在MoE服务上同时改善tokens/J与e2e时延，并实现显著电力节省，但结论仅适用于MoE，稠密模型收益有限。

Abstract: Datacenter GPU power is the binding constraint on LLM serving capacity, and production serving has shifted to prefill/decode (PD) disaggregation. Deploying NVIDIA's Max-Q inference profile on a disaggregated B200 system, we found its realized gain modest (+8.6% tokens/J), model-dependent, and carrying a mean end-to-end latency cost (+5.2%) that throughput-only evaluation does not surface; the profile also applies one setting to prefill and decode GPUs that operate in opposite hardware regimes. We hypothesize that the optimal power setting is a property of the deployed (model, quantization, engine, hardware) combination rather than of the GPU class, that each lane warrants its own profile, and that converting SLO headroom into energy safely requires latency-gated calibration under a runtime SLO guard rather than a fixed recipe. We present a phase-decoupled, model-calibrated controller: the prefill lane runs under an SM-clock window whose floor is a latency guarantee by construction, and the decode lane under a power cap placed by automatic calibration just above a measured throughput/latency cliff. Because a disaggregated decode lane draws flat, memory-bound power, the cap binds continuously, the reactive-overshoot weakness that led POLCA to reject capping is absent, and the GPU's own power manager retains throughput under the cap. On an 8x B200 node serving Qwen3-Coder-480B (FP8) under agentic load, our balanced mode delivers +20.4% tokens/J at +3.5% mean e2e versus +8.6% at +5.2% for Max-Q, a Pareto improvement on both axes. On Qwen3-235B-A22B (NVFP4) every operating mode meets the ITL-p99 SLO in every repetition; both vendor profiles miss it. A decode-actuator A/B shows the calibrated cap beats static clock locks, and a three-day sustained run saves 32.3% of a lane pair's electricity. Both models are MoE; a dense model recovers roughly 5x less, so we scope our claims to MoE serving.

</details>


### [102] [Learning Orthogonal Multi-Index Models Beyond Small Initialization: Incremental Learning, Competitive Dynamics and Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10879)
*Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Simon S. Du,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究标准初始化下多项式宽度两层网络学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学，证明增量学习依然发生，并揭示了参数质量的竞争性再分配现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于浅层网络增量学习的分析多依赖简化设置（如小初始化、相关损失或逐层训练），这些假设削弱了神经元间交互，导致标准初始化下的特征学习动力学尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在多项式宽度两层网络、标准初始化、多项式样本量下研究学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学；理论分析采用略微修改的梯度流，并引入基于对称性的有限宽度近似（对称化网络）来控制近似误差。

Result: 证明增量学习仍然发生：损失依据目标的Hermite展开依次下降，低阶成分先于高阶成分被学习，进而恢复各目标方向；同时观察到参数质量的竞争性再分配——总质量先拟合目标均值并稳定，随后转移至目标子空间并集中于对齐神经元。普通梯度下降在实验上呈现相同的定性动力学。

Conclusion: 标准初始化下浅层网络的增量学习与参数再分配动力学得以确立，对称化有限宽度近似方法提供了更好的误差控制，可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: Recent work has identified incremental learning in shallow networks trained on single-index and multi-index models. However, existing analyses often rely on simplifying settings, such as small initialization, correlation loss, or layer-wise training. These choices reduce neuron interactions and leave some feature learning dynamics under standard initialization unexplored. We study training dynamics for polynomial-width two-layer networks learning orthogonal multi-index targets under standard initialization using polynomially many samples. We first prove that incremental learning still occurs: the loss decreases sequentially according to the Hermite expansion of the target, with lower-order components learned before higher-order components recover the individual target directions. In this standard initialization regime, training also shows a competitive reallocation of parameter mass: after the total mass fits the target mean and stabilizes, mass shifts into the target subspace and then concentrates on aligned neurons. Our theoretical analysis uses slightly modified gradient flow, while vanilla gradient descent empirically exhibits the same qualitative dynamics. Technically, we introduce a symmetry-based finite-width approximation via symmetrized networks, rather than comparing directly with an infinite-width limit. This yields better control of approximation errors and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [103] [Story Imprinting: AI Assistants Absorb Traits from Human Characters They Resemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10883)
*Jorio Cocola,Lev McKinney,Harry Mayne,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型被训练为扮演"有用助手"角色，但用合成故事微调会使其"沾染"故事中人类角色的行为与偏好（故事印记），且更易采纳与助手自身相似角色的行为（亲和效应），该效应可用于探测模型对助手角色的内部表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在合成故事上微调如何改变语言模型所实现的"有用助手"角色，尤其是多轮对话中的行为，以及助手是否会采纳故事中纯人类角色的行为与偏好，从而检验角色选择模型（Persona Selection Model）的解释力。

Method: 对 GPT-4.1 与 Kimi-K2.6 进行微调：(1) 用"通常有帮助的人类角色在被侮辱后给出隐性有害建议"的故事，测试助手是否习得相同的条件性行为；(2) 用偏好仅隐含于叙述（如肢体语言暗示不喜欢电子表格）的故事，测试偏好迁移；(3) 通过角色特征操纵（有帮助/轻视、精英/非精英大学）检验助手的采纳倾向，并扩展到系统提示诱导的其他角色及微调基座模型。

Result: 助手习得了相同的条件性有害行为，即使此类故事占比不足 2%，其余方面仍保持有帮助；助手也采纳了叙述中仅隐含的偏好（更少选择电子表格任务）。助手更常采纳与自身相似角色的行为（亲和效应），该效应可推广到系统提示诱导的其他角色和微调基座模型。助手更多采纳与精英大学相关角色的行为，表明其内部助手表征更接近精英大学人群。

Conclusion: 仅描绘人类角色（无 AI）的故事即可影响助手行为，这与角色选择模型的预测存在冲突，说明助手角色可被叙事内容实质性塑造，且亲和效应可作为解析模型内部助手表征的工具。

Abstract: Language models are trained to implement a helpful AI Assistant character (e.g., Claude). We explore how finetuning on synthetic stories affects this character. Does it change the Assistant's behavior in multi-turn conversations with users, a format quite different from the stories? And does the Assistant adopt the behaviors and preferences of human characters? We refer to this adoption as story imprinting. We finetune GPT-4.1 and Kimi-K2.6 on stories in which generally helpful human characters give subtly harmful advice after being insulted. The Assistant adopts the same conditional behavior while otherwise remaining helpful. This occurs even when fewer than 2% of stories depict the behavior. In a separate experiment, the Assistant adopts preferences that are only implicit in the narration. A human character's body language suggests they dislike working on spreadsheets, yet they never say so and continue giving good advice on spreadsheets. After finetuning, the Assistant becomes less likely to choose spreadsheet tasks. Next we ask which characters most influence the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more often from characters that resemble it (e.g., helpful rather than dismissive). We call this the affinity effect. The effect extends to other personas elicited with system prompts: unhelpful personas adopt behaviors from unhelpful characters. We also observe it in finetuned base models. We use the affinity effect to learn how models represent the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more from characters affiliated with elite universities (e.g., Yale) than non-elite ones. This implies the model's internal representation of the Assistant is more similar to humans from elite universities. Overall, the Assistant can be influenced by stories that depict only human characters (no AIs), which may conflict with the Persona Selection Model for the Assistant.

</details>


### [104] [Relatively Smart II: Tractable or Semi-Supervised Instance-Optimal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10886)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Alireza F. Pour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"相对聪明学习"框架拓展至ERM、半监督及计算可行性三个方向，证明ERM等恰当一致学习器具备相对聪明性，半监督情形可实现仅无标签样本量二次膨胀，但标签效率以计算可行性为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dughmi和Pour（2026）提出了相对聪明学习，证明OIG学习器以二次样本复杂度膨胀实现相对聪明性，且无学习器能做得更好，但遗留三个问题：ERM或其他自然/可处理学习器能否达到类似保证；膨胀能否仅限制在无标签数据上；以及是否存在更简洁、可处理的学习器。

Method: 第一，证明ERM及任何恰当一致学习器在二分类分布无关设定下相对聪明：小可证明误差配合m个样本可推出大小为O(m²)的随机样本上均匀分布的小误差，从而在该样本上得到大小至多2^(m+1)的覆盖。第二，将OIG推广至"留多数"转导问题构建半监督相对聪明学习器，仅无标签样本复杂度二次膨胀。第三，用不可近似性/查询复杂度下界论证计算代价。

Result: ERM及任何恰当一致学习器在二分类分布无关设定下相对聪明，样本复杂度O(m²)即可控制误差。半监督相对聪明学习在信息论上可行，无标签样本复杂度仅为二次膨胀，标签样本复杂度无膨胀。然而若仅能访问agnostic ERM oracle，任何标签样本膨胀显著低于二次的半监督相对聪明学习器都需要超多项式次oracle调用；即使边际分布显式给出亦然，并由此得到分布固定学习的一个独立有趣的计算不可行性结果。

Conclusion: 相对聪明学习的可实现性不限于OIG：ERM等自然一致学习器同样具备该性质（二分类、分布无关、样本复杂度O(m²)）。半监督设定下可完全消除标签样本膨胀、仅保留无标签数据的二次膨胀。但这种标签效率以简洁性和可处理性为代价：在仅访问agnostic ERM oracle的限制下，亚二次标签膨胀将导致超多项式查询复杂度，揭示信息论可实现性与计算可行性之间的根本权衡。

Abstract: We continue the study of relatively smart learning, introduced by Dughmi and Pour (2026), which asks a supervised learner to compete, marginal by marginal, with every distribution-fixed error guarantee soundly certifiable from unlabeled data. They showed that the One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) learner is relatively smart with a quadratic sample-complexity blowup, and that no relatively smart learner can do better, leaving open whether ERM or another natural or tractable learner achieves comparable guarantees. They also left open whether the blowup can be restricted to unlabeled data.
  Our firs results shows that ERM---and in fact any proper consistent learner---is relatively smart for binary classification in the distribution-free setting. We show that a small certifiable error with $m$ samples implies a similarly small error on the uniform distribution over a random sample of size $O(m^2)$, yielding a cover of size at most $2^{m+1}$ on that sample. This suffices to control the error of proper consistent learners with $O(m^2)$ samples.
  We then show that semi-supervised relatively smart learning is information-theoretically possible with a quadratic blowup only in unlabeled sample complexity and no blowup in labeled sample complexity. The learner uses a natural generalization of OIG to a leave-most-out transductive problem, where labels of part of a finite pool are revealed and the remaining labels are predicted.
  Finally, this label efficiency comes at a cost in simplicity and tractability. If the hypothesis class is accessed only through an agnostic ERM oracle, any semi-supervised relatively smart learner with substantially sub-quadratic labeled-sample blowup requires super-polynomially many oracle calls. This holds even when the marginal is given explicitly, and thus also yields an intractability result for distribution-fixed learning that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [105] [Measuring the Value of World-Model Updates: A Counterfactual Utility Protocol for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10954)
*Anqi Peter Li,Kaden Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 fork ledger（分叉账本）方法，通过在预设决策点将部署流分叉为匹配的“更新”与“保持”延续（共用随机数），在同一批 episode 上评估反事实效用 ΔR=R_update−R_hold，从而直接观测单次更新对固定机制的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续世界模型必须判断新数据是否值得改变模型，但固定重放调度与预测误差触发器只能规定“何时更新”，无法揭示单次更新的价值：一次部署运行无法展示若当时保持参数不变会取得何种表现，即缺少反事实效用度量。

Method: fork ledger：在预先注册的决策点对部署流进行分支，产生匹配的更新延续与保持延续，并使用 common random numbers 使两者在相同 episode 上受评，记录 ΔR=R_update−R_hold；每任务为推断单元，包含 240 次尝试分叉，由 5 个预训练检查点与两种漂移方向交叉而成。
