Result: 始终应用同一固定更新机制在三个模拟控制任务上均降低回报：CartPole −144.0（checkpoint-bootstrap 95% CI [−185.4,−116.1]，收敛回报约 650）、Walker −82.8（[−101.1,−61.7]）、Cheetah −18.6（[−29.0,−6.6]）。由于发散是应用更新的结果，估计量计入所有尝试分叉；仅限 720 次中未崩溃的 693 次时，CartPole 与 Walker 符号不变（−113.4、−82.1），Cheetah 变为未决（−3.9；[−17.5,+13.0]）。

Conclusion: 该账本使固定机制下的反事实效用可观测，使触发器可依据其所选更新的价值来评判，而非仅依赖惊讶度检测。

Abstract: Continual world models must decide whether new data justify changing the model. Fixed replay schedules and prediction-error triggers specify when to update, but neither reveals the value of an individual update: one deployment run cannot show how the same model would have performed at that moment had it held its parameters. We introduce the fork ledger, which branches a deployment stream at pre-registered decision points into matched update and hold continuations under common random numbers. It evaluates both continuations on the same episodes and records $ΔR = R_{\mathrm{update}} - R_{\mathrm{hold}}$. Always applying one fixed update mechanism lowers return on all three simulated control tasks: CartPole ($-144.0$; checkpoint-bootstrap $95\%$ CI $[-185.4,-116.1]$, against a converged return near $650$), Walker ($-82.8$; $[-101.1,-61.7]$) and Cheetah ($-18.6$; $[-29.0,-6.6]$). Divergence is an outcome of applying the update, so the estimand counts every attempted fork; restricted to the $693$ of $720$ that did not collapse, CartPole and Walker are unchanged in sign ($-113.4$ and $-82.1$) and Cheetah becomes unresolved ($-3.9$; $[-17.5,+13.0]$). The task is the unit of inference: each contributes $240$ attempted forks over five pretrained checkpoints crossed with two drift directions. The ledger makes counterfactual utility observable for a fixed mechanism, allowing triggers to be judged by the updates they select rather than by surprise detection alone.

</details>


### [106] [When More Is Not Better: Component Anti-Synergy in a P300 Speller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10961)
*Lucas Yang,Rui Liu,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过四因素全因子实验检验P300拼写器"组件越多越好"的假设，发现各组件价值是条件性的而非可加性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代P300脑机接口流水线常将多个各自表现良好的组件组合，并默认"more-is-better"，但这一假设缺乏系统验证。作者希望明确各组件（EA、xDAWN、被试校准、语言模型先验）的独立贡献及其交互效应。

Method: 在公开P300数据集上进行四因素全因子实验，变量为是否纳入欧氏对齐（EA）、xDAWN空间滤波、被试校准和语言模型先验；用准确率、重复次数和信息传输率（ITR）为指标，并以混合效应模型进行分析。

Result: 组件价值是条件性的而非加性的：校准是最强的单一贡献因素，EA可在零校准场景下补偿其缺失；叠加各自有效的组件反而可能降低性能，出现组件反协同；语言模型支持并非普遍有益，其作用强烈依赖底层EEG流水线的强度，更大语言模型呈现类似模式。

Conclusion: 结果挑战"全部开启"的最大化流水线设计，强调应根据可用EEG证据的质量来选择空间与语言支持组件。

Abstract: P300 brain-computer interface (BCI) spellers can provide hands-free communication for people with severe motor impairments. Modern pipelines combine multiple individually promising components, often assuming that 'more-is-better'. We tested this assumption using a four-component full-factorial experiment varying the inclusion of Euclidean Alignment (EA), xDAWN spatial filtering, subject calibration, and language model priors on a public P300 dataset. Performance was evaluated using accuracy, repetitions, and information transfer rate (ITR) with mixed-effects models. Results show that the value of components is conditional rather than additive. Calibration was the strongest singular contributor, while EA compensated for its absence in zero-calibration settings. Adding independently useful components could also reduce performance, revealing component anti-synergy. Contrary to conventional wisdom, LM support was not universally beneficial: its effect depends strongly on the strength of the underlying EEG pipeline, while results from a larger LM showed a similar pattern. Together, these findings challenge maximal 'all-on' pipeline design and highlight the value of selecting spatial and language-support components according to the quality of available EEG evidence.

</details>


### [107] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Krotov-Hopfield二分架构（类H）作为联想记忆模型，用拉格朗日量统一分析多项式与指数存储机制，揭示可见层与隐藏层分别控制稳定性与存储规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield网络的联想记忆是高维无序系统的吸引子动力学，高阶与指数扩展将其检索更新变为softmax注意力。但多项式与指数两种机制此前用不同方法分析，缺乏统一架构来回答"是什么决定了存储规模"这一问题。

Method: 采用类H二分架构，每一层由一个拉格朗日量固定，以隐藏神经元作为检索序参量。多项式负载下使用replica方法得到复本对称相图与闭式容量；带softmax隐藏层时负载为指数级，通过副本表示将热力学映射到随机能量模型计数；并分析加热对检索稳定性的影响。

Result: 多项式负载下，交叉干扰矩对Ising和球面可见神经元是共同的，两者差异仅来自可见熵，并给出闭式容量。softmax隐藏层使负载指数增长，系统呈现顺磁、凝聚与冻结相。加热通过注意力量化重分配破坏检索，典型高斯模式在任意负载下保持亚稳。两种机制在交叉干扰统计上不同：多项式负载为中心极限，指数负载为大偏差。

Conclusion: 类H将检索分为两个角色：可见拉格朗日量决定稳定性，隐藏拉格朗日量决定存储规模。该双轴框架不仅阐明了多项式与指数机制的统一结构，也可指导新拉格朗日量的设计。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>


### [108] [EGGROLL, Unrolled: Understanding and Improving Low-Rank Evolution Strategies at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10980)
*Ege C. Kaya,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析EGGROLL（用低秩高斯积替代密集高斯扰动的进化策略）的有限秩几何与估计误差，提出LOO-ROLL估计器，在保持有限秩总体场的同时将每次方向的两次评估减为一次，显著降低估计MSE并提升LLM后训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGGROLL以低秩（常为秩一）高斯乘积替代密集高斯权重扰动，使进化策略对LLM可行且计算高效，但秩一扰动位于环境矩阵空间的零体积子集，几何性质严重，其有限秩、非零扰动半径下的更新场性质与估计误差缺乏理论刻画，需要系统分析并改进估计效率。

Method: 刻画有限秩、非零扰动半径下EGGROLL的平均更新场，并分析有限种群估计器的误差：总体场由对扰动平滑后目标梯度施加显式预解（resolvent）得到；证明预解可引入非保守分量并可反转最优点的局部稳定性；证明其在任意秩、任意半径下对二次目标精确；对光滑目标，首次局部有限秩修正为O(σ²/r)，并在光滑性假设下给出非渐近误差界。在局部仿射模型下推导秩一扰动相对密集高斯ES的梯度估计方差比2(m+n+1)/(mn+1)。进而提出LOO-ROLL留一估计器，保持有限秩总体场并将每方向两次对偶评估减为一次。

Result: 秩一扰动仅使梯度估计方差相对密集高斯ES增加2(m+n+1)/(mn+1)，对4096×4096矩阵为0.098%。在等评估成本下，LOO-ROLL将transformer块中估计器MSE减半；在匹配墙钟时间、十个后训练设置、最大8B参数模型上，LOO-ROLL在个体配对检验中改善七项结果且无显著损失。GSM8K测试集上，0.6B模型准确率由38.1%升至63.0%，8B模型由65.9%升至80.0%。Transformer实测复现了预测的有限秩方差，秩比较未显示秩八存在可复现的基于奖励的优势。

Conclusion: EGGROLL的有限秩总体场可由对平滑目标梯度的显式预解刻画，虽可能非保守并改变局部稳定性，但对二次目标精确，光滑目标下误差受控且秩一方差代价极小；LOO-ROLL在保持该总体场的前提下将评估次数减半，显著降低MSE并在多种LLM后训练任务中一致提升性能（GSM8K最高从65.9%提升至80.0%），且未发现秩八的可复现优势。

Abstract: EGGROLL makes evolution strategies (ES) practical for LLMs by replacing dense Gaussian weight perturbations with low-rank Gaussian products, often of rank one. This choice is computationally attractive but geometrically severe: each rank-one perturbation lies in a zero-volume subset of the ambient matrix space, despite having identity covariance. We characterize the mean EGGROLL update field at finite rank and nonzero perturbation radii, then analyze the error of its finite-population estimator. The population field is obtained by applying an explicit resolvent to the gradient of the objective smoothed by the perturbations. We show that the resolvent can introduce a nonconservative component and can reverse the local stability of an optimum. EGGROLL is nevertheless exact on every quadratic objective at every rank and radius. For smooth objectives, its first local finite-rank correction is $O(σ^2/r)$, and nonasymptotic bounds control the resulting field error under smoothness assumptions. Under a local affine model, rank-one perturbations increase the variance of the gradient estimator by only $\frac{2(m+n+1)}{mn+1}$ relative to dense Gaussian ES, or $0.098\%$ for a $4096\times4096$ matrix. We then introduce LOO-ROLL, a leave-one-out estimator that preserves the finite-rank population field while replacing EGGROLL's two antithetic evaluations per direction by one. At equal evaluation cost, LOO-ROLL halves estimator MSE in transformer blocks. At matched wall time across ten post-training settings and models up to 8B parameters, LOO-ROLL improves seven outcomes in individual paired tests, with no significant loss. On the GSM8K test set, accuracy increases from $38.1\%$ to $63.0\%$ at 0.6B and from $65.9\%$ to $80.0\%$ at 8B. Transformer measurements recover the predicted finite-rank variance, while the rank comparisons show no reproducible reward-based advantage for rank eight.

</details>


### [109] [Thompson Sampling for Non-Monotone Convex Ridge Bandits: Monotonicity Is Not Needed for Polynomial Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10981)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文否定了TS贝叶斯遗憾上界对单调链接函数必要性的猜想，证明对任意凸（可能非单调）链接的脊损失，精确后验TS的贝叶斯遗憾为$\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$，并给出十二点反例、$O(d^2)$基数上界及紧性构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bakhtiari等（COLT 2025）证明了在凸单调脊损失下Thompson采样具有$\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$的贝叶斯遗憾，并提问链接函数的单调性是否必要。本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 通过显式十二点构造说明单次移除John椭球二分法在非单调链接下失效；转而建立"无信息"配置的$O(d^2)$基数上界，其核心是Boolean舍入论证：与秩$r$矩阵最大范数距离不超过$1/(4r)$的$0$-$1$矩阵秩至多$2r-1$；并给出对固定可测选择一致的信息比-遗憾转换。

Result: 对任意先验及任意固定可测极小值选择，精确后验TS的贝叶斯遗憾为$O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\log(e+nd\max\{1,\diam K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$；构造$d(d+1)$个无信息损失证明基数上界在大直径-间隔区间内是紧的（仅差常数）。

Conclusion: 单调性对TS的贝叶斯遗憾保证并非必要，非单调链接下仍可得到多项式遗憾上界，但指数由$d^{5/2}$退化至$d^{9/2}$；能否保持单调情形的$d^{5/2}$依赖仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Bakhtiari, Lattimore and Szepesvári (COLT 2025) proved that Thompson sampling (TS) has Bayesian regret $\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$ for bandit convex optimisation with convex \emph{monotone} ridge losses $f(x)=\ell(\ip{x}θ)$, and asked whether monotonicity of the link is necessary. We give a qualitative negative answer. For every prior on $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz convex ridge losses with an arbitrary convex, possibly non-monotone, link, and for any fixed measurable selection of minimisers, exact-posterior TS has Bayesian regret $O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\,\log(e+nd\max\{1,\diam K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$. The monotone proof relies on a single-removal John-ellipsoid dichotomy; we show by an explicit twelve-point configuration that this dichotomy fails for non-monotone links, and replace it by an $O(d^2)$ cardinality bound for ``uninformative'' configurations. The bound uses a Boolean rounding argument: a $0$-$1$ matrix within $1/(4r)$ in max-norm of a rank-$r$ matrix has rank at most $2r-1$. We construct $d(d+1)$ uninformative losses, showing that the cardinality bound is tight up to constants in the large-diameter-to-gap regime, and give a self-contained information-ratio-to-regret transfer that is uniform over fixed measurable selections. Whether the $d^{5/2}$ dependence of the monotone case can be retained remains open.

</details>


### [110] [Importance Weighting for Unlabeled-unlabeled Learning under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10994)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无标签-无标签(UU)学习的分布偏移自适应方法，基于重要性加权，用训练分布中的UU数据和少量测试分布中的UU数据最小化测试风险，可统一处理PU、噪声标签等多种问题且在真实数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UU学习假设测试分布与训练分布具有相同的类条件密度，但在实践中因分布偏移该假设往往不成立，因此需要一种能适应分布偏移的UU学习方法。

Method: 基于重要性加权的方法：利用训练分布中的UU数据与测试分布中的少量UU数据，通过估计的重要性权重对训练数据加权以最小化测试风险。作者证明在UU数据上可以原则性地完成这一过程，且不依赖协变量偏移等具体偏移类型假设。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 借助UU学习的通用性，该方法可在单一框架内处理分布偏移下的PU学习、噪声标签学习等多种问题，而现有方法通常仅针对特定问题定制，且该方法无需对偏移类型作额外假设。

Abstract: Unlabeled-unlabeled (UU) learning allows us to learn a binary classifier from two sets of unlabeled data with different class-priors. It is a general framework because it includes a wide variety of supervised learning such as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, noisy label learning, and similarity-based learning. Existing UU learning assumes that the test and training distributions have the same class-conditional densities. However, this assumption rarely holds in practice due to distribution shifts. This paper proposes a distribution shift adaptation method for UU learning that uses UU data in the training distribution and a few UU data in the test distribution. The proposed method is based on the importance weighting, which minimizes the test risk by using training data with estimated importance weights. Although existing importance weighting methods cannot handle UU data, we show that it can be done in a principled manner. Thanks to the generality of UU learning, our method can handle various learning problems such as PU and noisy label learning under distribution shift within a single framework while existing methods are usually tailored to a specific problem. Moreover, it does not require any assumption of the shift types such as covariate shift. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with real-world datasets.

</details>


### [111] [Topological Necessities: Mechanism-Invariant Strategic Subgoals for Cross-Embodiment Goal-Conditioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11014)
*Hao Shi,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出“拓扑必要性（topological necessities）”这一新对象：由成功轨迹构建的传输加权载体上，用0维与1维同调刻画必经阶段（分离集）与环路路径选择，得到可枚举、带壳层证书的“门”集合，并以递归拓扑门层级形式接入决策循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程目标条件强化学习中，高层模块提出的子目标多是价值函数或隐动作的隐式副产物，与产生它的执行器强绑定，缺乏可迁移性和可解释的证书。作者希望找到一种独立于具体执行器、可从离线轨迹恢复的、描述“所有成功执行器都必须经历的阶段顺序”的对象。

Method: 将必经阶段定义为拓扑概念：不可跳过的阶段是对所有可行路径都必须穿越的分离集，自由空间中的环则强制产生路径选择。作者在由成功轨迹构建的传输加权载体上，用同调方法在0维和1维读取这两类结构，得到可枚举的门的集合及其壳层级证书，即“拓扑必要性”；这些带证书的门以递归拓扑门层级的形式进入决策循环。

Result: 在固定且同构的自由空间下，该对象可跨执行器存活：在PointMaze数据上冻结的门无需重训练即可迁移到Ant和Humanoid，在统一接口下取得最高Humanoid综合成绩96.1，在多路径任务上比使用地图特权信息的参考方法高36.0（p=1.4e-5）；规划器在PointMaze上饱和（100±0），在AntMaze（giant +22.9）和Kitchen（+15.8/+12.6）上达到或超过最强基线。

Conclusion: 拓扑必要性构成一类可枚举、带证书且与执行器解耦的子目标对象，能够在执行器替换时保持有效并支持免重训练迁移，在多个长时程导航与操作基准上优于或持平强基线，表明拓扑结构为长时程目标条件RL提供了可迁移的高层抽象。

Abstract: Long-horizon goal-conditioned reinforcement learning delegates control to a high-level module that proposes subgoals, but existing subgoals are implicit byproducts of value functions or latent actions, tied to the executor that produced them. We study a different object: a route-conditioned order of unavoidable stages that every successful executor must traverse, recoverable from offline trajectories and belonging to none of them. Its defining properties are topological: an unskippable stage is a separating set that every admissible path must cross, and a loop in free space forces a route choice. We read the two by homology in dimensions 0 and 1 over a transport-weighted carrier built from successful trajectories, yielding an enumerable gate set with shell-level certificates; the certified gates are what we call topological necessities. Certified gates enter the decision loop as a recursive topological gate hierarchy. Under a fixed, isomorphic free space, the object survives executor replacement: gates frozen on PointMaze data transfer without retraining to Ant and Humanoid, attaining the highest Humanoid aggregate under a unified interface (96.1), with +36.0 over a map-privileged reference on the multi-route task (p=1.4e-5); the planner saturates PointMaze (100+/-0) and matches or exceeds the strongest baselines on AntMaze (giant +22.9) and Kitchen (+15.8/+12.6).

</details>


### [112] [T1: Terminal Agent Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11042)
*Junyao Yang,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Zongxia Li,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T1：122B MoE 强化学习智能体，真实 shell 环境 300+ 轮工具调用，Terminal-Bench 2.1 达 64.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体应用正转向长时域任务（编程、科学发现），其中终端任务尤为关键，但现有模型在长轮次工具调用下的训练稳定性与能力迁移仍面临挑战。

Method: 采用 122B 总参数的 MoE 模型，在云沙箱真实 shell 中执行任务，每任务最多 300+ 轮工具调用，以各任务自带验证器执行结果作为奖励。训练配方包含三部分：(1) 激进热启动稳定 actor-critic，并以通过验证器绝对数量构造稠密过程奖励；(2) 通过 TITO 构造、在轮次边界进行漂移修复的精确采样 token 训练，以及 rollout routing replay（记录并回放采样器在每个 MoE 层的逐 token 专家选择）实现稳定优化；(3) 全分布外语料，使用与 Terminal-Bench 2.1 不相交的隔离种子与合成任务。

Result: TITO 与 R3 将训练-推理对数概率差从 0.021 降至 0.013，损失区域 token 漂移精确为零。Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，后训练流水线将基座模型从 43.8% 提升至 T1 的 64.0% resolved；Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上 T1 达 27.9%，超越 GPT-5.4 与 GLM-5.1。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了在长时域终端任务中，结合稠密过程奖励、TITO/R3 稳定化优化与分布外训练语料，可显著提升智能体的真实能力并实现有效迁移，而非基准过拟合。

Abstract: Agent usage is shifting toward long-horizon tasks such as coding and scientific discovery, among which terminal tasks are especially important. We introduce T1, a Mixture-of-Experts model of 122B total trained with reinforcement learning, operating a real shell in a cloud sandbox for up to 300+ tool-call turns per task, rewarded by executing each task's own verifier. We provide a comprehensive recipe: First, an aggressively warm-started to stabilize actor-critic training, with a dense process reward scoring trajectories by the absolute number of passing verifiers. Second, stable optimization through TITO construction, training on the exact sampled token identifiers with drift repair at turn boundaries, and rollout routing replay, recording the sampler's per-token expert choices at every MoE layer and replaying them during training. Third, fully out-of-distribution training corpus: isolated seeds and synthesized tasks disjoint from Terminal-Bench 2.1 ensures gains reflect genuine capability transfer over benchmark overfitting. Together, TITO and R3 cut the training-to-inference log-probability difference from 0.021 to 0.013, with exactly aligned zero token drift in the loss region. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, our post-train pipeline raises initial base model from 43.8% to T1 with 64.0% resolved. On Long-Horizon Terminal Bench, T1 reaches 27.9% and surpasses GPT-5.4 and GLM-5.1.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond Solver Verdicts: Generative Reward Models for Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11085)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Aman Goel,Ali Torkamani,Xiaoxue Han,Joseph Lilien,Ferhat Erata,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经符号系统中形式翻译与指定形式化之间的“判定保持不忠实”（VPU）问题形式化，证明仅凭结构的判定级启发式检测上限仅为随机水平，并提出生成式验证（GenV）将离线Z3等价性预言机蒸馏为无参考的连续等价性分数，实现0.961 AUROC并带来下游11.3个百分点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统依赖数学求解器保证推理正确性，但求解器无法判断形式翻译是否与指定形式化保持严格的参考等价性。这种盲区导致一种隐蔽失效模式：错误编码仍能成功执行并给出预期判定。作者将此形式化为VPU（Verdict-Preserving-Unfaithfulness），并指出结构化的、仅看判定的验证启发式在数学上被限制在随机检测水平，因此需要新的验证范式。

Method: 作者提出生成式验证（GenV）：先构建离线Z3等价性预言机，再将其蒸馏为无参考（reference-free）的连续参考等价性分数，具体做法是复用语言模型原生词表空间进行读出。通过决策投影logit透镜与稀疏自编码器（SAE）进行机制分析，验证该生成式读出能够无需显式定位训练即自然提取精确的空间错误坐标。此外提出预言机挖掘的验证器GenV+HN。

Result: GenV+HN在参考等价性验证任务上取得0.961 AUROC；可零样本泛化到未见翻译器与差异较大的形式风格；在智能体测试时计算分配（agentic test-time compute allocation）中带来11.3个百分点的下游准确率增益。理论层面证明了结构化的判定级启发式检测能力被数学上限制在随机水平。

Conclusion: 仅依赖判定结果的结构化验证本质上无法可靠检测参考等价性违例，而生成式验证通过蒸馏求解器等价性信号、利用语言模型词表空间读出连续分数，可有效识别并定位不忠实编码，且具备强跨分布泛化能力，可直接提升下游推理系统的计算分配效率。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems rely on mathematical solvers to guarantee reasoning correctness, yet solvers are fundamentally blind to whether a formal translation maintains strict reference-equivalence to a designated formalization. We formalize this vulnerability as Verdict-Preserving-Unfaithfulness (VPU): a failure mode where an incorrect encoding executes successfully and matches the expected verdict. We theoretically prove that structural, verdict-only verification heuristics are mathematically bounded to chance-level detection on these deceptively valid traces. To resolve this, we introduce Generative Verification (GenV), which distills an offline Z3-equivalence oracle into a reference-free, continuous reference-equivalence score by repurposing the language model's native vocabulary space. Mechanistic analysis via decision-projected logit lenses and sparse autoencoders shows this generative readout natively extracts precise spatial error coordinates without explicit localization training. Empirically, our oracle-mined verifier (GenV+HN) achieves 0.961 AUROC in reference-equivalence verification, generalizes zero-shot across unseen translators and divergent formal styles, and yields an 11.3-point downstream accuracy gain in agentic test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [114] [How Wrong Can a Good Predictor Be? Diverging Updates with Vanishing Predictive KL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11132)
*Qifu Wen,Shuaijun Liu,Zihan Zhou,Xi Zeng,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明：在平稳对称高斯HMM中，即使贝叶斯更新映射之间存在无界差距，预测KL仍可趋于零，说明内部更新差距本身不足以判定预测失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界常以内部更新映射（belief更新）的近似误差来推断预测质量的退化。作者希望检验这一直觉是否成立：即更新映射之间的差距是否能作为预测失败的充分证据。

Method: 构造平稳对称高斯HMM，固定K≥2。比较精确贝叶斯混合与一个显式确定性径向滤波（radial filter），二者作用于相同的K-1个belief坐标。分析中心logits中的差距随q→0+的增长，以及分类KL散度D_KL(exact‖radial)的消失；并沿平稳HMM轨迹分析终端KL的收敛，借助无切换典型block导致的共同置信锥与softmax曲率抑制分歧，以及单个高斯极大事件控制自适应噪声。

Result: 当q→0+时，最坏情况下中心logits中的分离至少按自然置信尺度L_K(q)线性增长，而D_KL(exact‖radial)在同一显式见证下趋于零。沿平稳轨迹，滤波后验间的期望终端KL以H(q)=⌈-log(q)/c⌉+1收敛到零。对K∈{2,4,8}的等间距高斯扫描展示了相反趋势，长时程二元对照比较了饱和与非饱和递推。

Conclusion: 内部更新差距与预测代价之间存在两个缺失环节：分离状态对期望损失的贡献，以及解码器敏感性。因此无界的内部更新差距本身并不证明预测失败。该构造固定K，不提供压缩何时无害的普适判据，也未刻画内部差距何时必然导致任务损失。

Abstract: Accurate posterior prediction need not require accurate approximation of Bayesian updates. We prove that an unbounded gap between the update maps can coexist with vanishing predictive KL for every fixed finite $K\ge2$ in a stationary symmetric Gaussian HMM. Exact Bayesian mixing and an explicit deterministic radial filter act on the same $K-1$ belief coordinates. As $q\to0^+$, their separation in centered logits in the worst case grows at least linearly in the natural confidence scale $L_K(q)$, while their categorical $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$ vanishes at the same explicit witness. Along stationary HMM trajectories, the expected terminal KL between filtered posteriors also converges to zero at $H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$. Typical blocks without switches drive both filters into a common confidence cone, where softmax curvature suppresses their disagreement; a single Gaussian maximal event controls adaptive noise. A sweep with equally spaced Gaussians over $K\in\{2,4,8\}$ illustrates the opposing trends, and binary controls at long horizons compare saturating and nonsaturating recurrences. The result isolates two missing links between internal update gaps and predictive cost: the contribution of separating states to expected loss and decoder sensitivity. Thus even an unbounded internal update gap does not by itself certify predictive failure. The construction is fixed in $K$ and does not provide a universal criterion for when compression is harmless or characterize when internal gaps must incur task loss.

</details>


### [115] [Bidirectional Multimodal Fusion of Sky Images and Time-Series for Solar Forecasting with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11135)
*Ken Chen,Maneesha Perera,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SolCloudLLM，一种将天空图像与数值时间序列对齐融合的多模态LLM光伏功率/GHI短期预测框架，在多个数据集上取得最高25.4%的MSE相对下降，并在少样本场景中保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期光伏功率与全球水平辐照度预测对调度、备用计划和电网运行至关重要；该尺度误差主要来自云致爬坡，仅依赖历史数值数据难以预判云到达，地面天空图像是关键互补物理信号。同时预测性能高度依赖站点与局部观测条件，站点特定数据稀缺。现有LLM时序预测方法多为单模态，如何有效引入天空图像尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出SolCloudLLM：将天空图像块与时间序列块进行对齐，并通过双向多模态融合整合二者表征，得到统一表示后映射到LLM的嵌入空间进行预测。

Result: 在SIRTA与SKIPP'D数据集上，SolCloudLLM在所有预测时域上MSE均优于最佳基线，最大相对MSE降低25.4%；分层分析显示多模态融合收益主要集中在多云条件下；在几乎所有少样本设置下取得最佳性能，而其他深度学习基线显著退化，常被非学习的物理方法超越。

Conclusion: 将天空图像与数值时序在LLM框架内进行对齐与双向融合，可有效提升短期光伏功率与GHI预测精度、鲁棒性与数据效率，尤其在多云与少样本场景下优势明显。

Abstract: Short-term photovoltaic (PV) power and global horizontal irradiance (GHI) forecasts are essential for effective dispatch, reserve scheduling, and grid operations. At these forecasting horizons, errors are predominantly driven by cloud induced ramps: relying solely on historical numerical data may struggle to anticipate an incoming cloud, making ground-based sky images a crucial complementary physical signal. Furthermore, forecast performance is highly sensitive to location and local observing conditions, creating a strong need for site-specific data that are often scarce. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated competitive performance and high data efficiency in time-series forecasting. Despite their success, existing LLM-based forecasting methods remain predominantly unimodal, relying primarily on historical numerical time-series data. Effectively incorporating sky imagery into an LLM-based forecasting framework remains under-explored and an open challenge. In this paper, we propose SolCloudLLM, an LLM-based multimodal forecasting framework. SolCloudLLM aligns sky-image patches with time-series patches and fuses their corresponding representations through bidirectional multimodal fusion, yielding a unified representation that is subsequently mapped into the embedding space of an LLM. Extensive experiments on the SIRTA and SKIPP'D datasets demonstrate that SolCloudLLM consistently outperforms the best baseline methods in MSE across all forecasting horizons, achieving a maximum relative MSE reduction of 25.4%. Stratified analysis further indicates that the benefits of multimodal fusion are concentrated primarily under cloudy conditions. Notably, SolCloudLLM achieves the best performance in nearly all few-shot settings, whereas other deep learning baselines experience substantial performance degradation and are frequently outperformed by the non-learning physical method.

</details>


### [116] [LILA: Calibration-Free Structured Pruning of Large Language Models via Latent Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11163)
*Sankar Behera,Dhruv Singh,Anshika Agnihotri,Raj Kumar Choudhary,Satyadev Ahlawat,Yamuna Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LILA提出一种无需校准数据、梯度或辅助网络的闭式谱剪枝准则，基于KS距离度量神经元重要性，在LLaMA-2-7B与Phi-2上以零校准数据达到或超越校准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法在剪枝阶段依赖校准数据、梯度计算或大型辅助策略网络，带来额外开销与部署复杂度。本文旨在提出一种无需训练与校准数据的硬件友好压缩方法。

Method: LILA通过比较完整FFN权重矩阵与神经元消融后权重矩阵的经验奇异值分布之间的Kolmogorov–Smirnov距离来评分神经元重要性，形成闭式谱规则；无需微调、校准数据或辅助网络。进一步将LILA扩展为基于KS分数动态分配稀疏预算，并辅以Neural Tangent Kernel分析提供理论支撑。

Result: 在25%稀疏度下，无需微调的LILA在LLaMA-2-7B零样本准确率上超过PruneNet（45M参数RL策略）1.57个百分点；在所有稀疏度水平上优于WikiText-2校准的SliceGPT最多6.0个百分点，且保持原始架构。经一轮LoRA恢复微调后，在LLaMA-2-7B和Phi-2上以零校准数据将强校准基线SliceGPT的差距缩小至0.48个百分点以内。NTK分析证实相比随机剪枝功能失真降低22倍。动态稀疏预算扩展在中度压缩下取得最优生成保持，并揭示高压缩下的单层架构瓶颈。

Conclusion: LILA以闭式谱准则实现无校准、无梯度、无辅助网络的结构化剪枝，性能优于或媲美强校准基线，并具备NTK理论支撑；动态稀疏分配在中等压缩下表现优异，但高压缩受限于单层架构瓶颈。

Abstract: Structured pruning of large language models (LLMs) offers hardware-efficient compression, yet existing methods require calibration data, gradient computation, or large auxiliary policy networks at pruning time. LILA (\emph{Latent-Informed Layer Analysis}) scores neuron importance via the Kolmogorov--Smirnov (KS) distance between empirical singular value distributions of the full and neuron-ablated feed-forward network (FFN) weight matrix, providing a closed-form spectral rule requiring no training, calibration data, or auxiliary network. Without any fine-tuning, LILA surpasses PruneNet (45M-parameter RL policy) by 1.57~pp in zero-shot accuracy on LLaMA-2-7B at 25\% sparsity, and outperforms WikiText-2-calibrated SliceGPT by up to 6.0~pp across all sparsity levels, while preserving the original architecture. After one epoch of LoRA recovery fine-tuning, LILA achieves highly competitive performance, matching the heavily calibrated SliceGPT baseline to within a 0.48~pp margin across LLaMA-2-7B and Phi-2, despite using zero calibration data. A Neural Tangent Kernel analysis confirms a 22$\times$ reduction in functional distortion versus random pruning, providing theoretical grounding for the spectral importance criterion. Finally, extending LILA to dynamically allocate sparsity budgets via KS-scores yields state-of-the-art generative preservation at moderate compression, while uncovering fundamental single-layer architectural bottlenecks at higher compression regimes.

</details>


### [117] [When does a spectral prior help graph learning? Connectivity-loss estimation under road-network disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11166)
*Van-Truong Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用图神经网络学习对一阶Fiedler灵敏度的有界修正，以估计多边删除后相对代数连通度损失，并在合成图与13个OSM区域上验证其相对解析基线的改进与域敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时发生的大量路网链路中断需要快速评估，而精确谱重算代价过高、局部近似精度不足，因此需要一种兼顾效率与精度的折中方法。

Method: 以图神经网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、边感知MPNN）学习一阶Fiedler灵敏度的有界残差修正；设置独立、空间聚类与按边介数定向三类失效模式；采用图不相交的合成划分，并零样本迁移至六国13个OSM区域，与解析基线及二阶扰动对比，同时分析稀疏扩展与摊销筛选成本。

Result: 扩展OSM测试中，残差GCN将空间失效MAE降低0.0391（95%分层区间0.0151–0.0662），残差GraphSAGE将定向失效MAE降低0.0257（0.0095–0.0446）；二阶扰动仅使一阶MAE改善0.0028–0.0053。定向迁移下修正斜率下降，表明残差围绕系统先验误差收缩。留一国OSM互迁结果不一：残差GCN使定向失效MAE改善0.0622（0.0169–0.1153），但空间点估计变差。稀疏扩展可支持至20,000节点，并分离一次性谱设置与摊销筛选成本。

Conclusion: 谱残差是一种有用但对域敏感的归纳偏置，适用于结构连通性筛选；代码、缓存网络与可复现性材料已存档于doi:10.5281/zenodo.22307723。

Abstract: Rapid evaluation of many simultaneous road-link disruptions requires a practical compromise between exact spectral recomputation and local approximation. We estimate relative algebraic-connectivity loss after multi-edge deletion using graph neural networks (GNNs) that learn a bounded correction to a first-order Fiedler sensitivity. The study considers independent, spatially clustered, and edge-betweenness-targeted failures, with graph-disjoint synthetic splits and zero-shot transfer to 13 OpenStreetMap (OSM) areas in six countries. GCN, GraphSAGE, and edge-aware MPNN backbones are compared with analytical baselines. In expanded OSM tests, residual GCN improves spatial-failure MAE by 0.0391 (95% hierarchical interval 0.0151-0.0662), while residual GraphSAGE improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0257 (0.0095-0.0446). Second-order perturbation improves first-order MAE by only 0.0028-0.0053. Correction slopes decrease under targeted transfer, indicating residual shrinkage around systematic prior error. Leave-one-country-out OSM-to-OSM transfer is mixed: residual GCN improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0622 (0.0169-0.1153) but worsens the spatial point estimate. Sparse scaling extends to 20,000 nodes and separates one-time spectral setup from amortized screening cost. These results characterize the spectral residual as a useful but domain-sensitive inductive bias for structural connectivity screening. Code, cached networks, and reproducibility artifacts are archived at doi:10.5281/zenodo.22307723.

</details>


### [118] [Hierarchical Clustering Can Jointly Satisfy Richness, Consistency, and Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11173)
*Daichi Kuroda,Maximilien Dreveton,Matthias Grossglauser,Patrick Thiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Kleinberg不可能定理的三大公理推广到层次聚类，证明层次版本可同时满足，且存在不可数多个满足公理的"可容许"方法，并刻画其偏序结构与共同骨架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦聚类缺乏公认的"簇"定义，Kleinberg不可能定理表明没有任何平坦聚类方法能同时满足尺度不变性、丰富性和一致性三条自然公理。作者追问：当输出由单一划分变为层次结构时，这种不可能性是否依然存在。

Method: 将三条公理作层次化推广，构造并验证多个可容许层次聚类方法，包括基于良分离簇的方法以及非二元的单连接（single linkage）变体；进而用"细化"关系在可容许方法类上定义偏序，分析其极元结构与共同骨架。

Result: 层次化公理可联合满足，可容许方法有不可数多个；某对可容许方法之间存在恒定的细化关系；该偏序集无极元，且含不可数多个两两不可比的极大元，体现出可容许方法的巨大多样性。

Conclusion: 层次聚类中Kleinberg式不可能性不再成立，但多样性仍受约束：每种可容许方法都包含一个由充分良分离簇构成的层次，任意有限个可容许方法都共享这样一个非平凡共同骨架。

Abstract: Despite its ubiquity, clustering lacks a universally accepted definition of what is a cluster. Kleinberg's Impossibility Theorem formalizes this difficulty by showing that no flat clustering method can simultaneously satisfy three natural axioms: scale invariance, richness, and consistency. In this paper, we ask whether this impossibility persists when the output is a hierarchy rather than a single partition. We show that, in contrast to the flat clustering setting, the hierarchical analog of these axioms are jointly satisfiable. In fact, there exist uncountably many hierarchical clustering methods satisfying these axioms, which we call admissible. We explicitly construct several admissible methods, including methods based on well-separated clusters and a non-binary version of single linkage. For certain pairs of admissible methods, the hierarchy produced by one always refines that produced by the other. This refinement relation defines a partial order on the class of admissible methods. This partially ordered set has no greatest element and contains uncountably many pairwise incompatible maximal elements, revealing substantial diversity among admissible methods. Nevertheless, this diversity is constrained: every admissible method contains a hierarchy of sufficiently well-separated clusters, and every finite collection of admissible methods shares such a nontrivial common backbone.

</details>


### [119] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES) under Time-Varying Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11207)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 CONES 框架推广至损失函数随时间变化的情形：凸损失下投影近端算法同时达到 O(T^{1-β}) 遗憾与 O(T^β) 移动代价，强凸下达到 O(1) 遗憾与 O(log T) 移动代价，并给出匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 CONES 模型仅允许目标函数固定、可行域嵌套收缩，而实际在线场景中损失函数往往也随时间变化。本文旨在将 CONES 推广到损失函数时变的情形，并刻画遗憾与移动代价之间的权衡关系。

Method: 采用投影近端算法（projected proximal algorithm）求解时变损失下的嵌套演化可行集问题；分别针对凸损失与强凸损失进行分析，并通过对抗性下界证明（限制弱自适应算法与次线性 anytime 遗憾算法）刻画移动代价下界。

Result: 凸损失时，投影近端算法对任意 β∈[0,1) 同时实现 O(T^{1-β}) 遗憾与 O(T^β) 移动代价；任意弱自适应算法若遗憾为 O(T^β)，则移动代价下界为 Ω(T^{(1-β)/2})。强凸损失时，同时实现 O(1) 遗憾与 O(log T) 移动代价；任意具次线性 anytime 遗憾的算法移动代价下界为 Ω(log T)。

Conclusion: 时变损失下 CONES 的遗憾—移动代价权衡由投影近端算法上界与匹配（或近似匹配）下界共同刻画，强凸情形尤为严格：O(log T) 移动代价不可进一步改进。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} was introduced in \cite{CONESVaze} where the objective function \(f\) remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence \(S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T\). The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost $M_\cA(T)$ while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known \emph{nested convex body chasing} (NCBC). In this paper, we extend CONES to allow for loss functions $f_t'$s to also change over time. When all loss functions are convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost, respectively, for any $β\in [0,1)$, over a time horizon of $T$. We also show that any {\it weakly adaptive} online algorithm with $O(T^β)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(T^{\frac{1-β}{2}}\right)$ for any $β\in [0,1)$. When all loss functions are strongly convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm simultaneously achieves $O(1)$ regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with sublinear {\it anytime} regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(\log T\right)$.

</details>


### [120] [REVA: Reusable Evidence View Aggregation for Context-Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11209)
*Tuan Nguyen,Qiran Hu,Banruo Liu,Khoa D. Doan,Kok-Seng Wong,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REVA框架，通过挖掘历史查询-文档-模型交互中的注意力痕迹构建可复用的证据视图，实现低成本、高效的RAG检索后压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG依赖长上下文导致延迟、KV缓存显存和token成本上升；现有检索后压缩方法逐查询独立运行，依赖辅助模型或重写，且在线开销可能抵消短提示带来的收益。作者从数据挖掘视角重新审视该问题，希望将历史交互聚合为可复用资源。

Method: 首先验证现代压缩器相对简单截断的增益不稳定且增加推理延迟。继而提出REVA框架：挖掘目标生成器的历史注意力轨迹，构建以文档为键、与预算无关的分数存储；将token级注意力映射到可读词单元，跨重复文档访问聚合重要性，并渲染保留文档顺序与标准RAG接口的、针对特定预算的纯文本视图。

Result: 在四个代表性基准和现代LLM上，REVA相比现有先进方法在生成质量上提升1.0-5.8分，同时将压缩开销降低5.3至15.6倍，额外延迟低于40毫秒。

Conclusion: 将RAG压缩从逐查询在线操作转变为可复用的离线证据聚合是可行且高效的，REVA在质量与效率上同时优于现有方案，且保持标准RAG接口，便于落地。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive large language model (LLM) applications by conditioning generation on retrieved documents, but longer contexts increase latency, key-value (KV) cache memory, and token cost. Post-retrieval compression can reduce this cost, yet existing compressors often operate independently for each query, rely on auxiliary models or rewriting, and introduce online overhead that can offset the benefit of shorter prompts. We revisit RAG compression from a data-mining perspective by aggregating historical query--document--model interactions into reusable evidence views. We first show that modern compressors have unstable gains over simple truncation and can add substantial inference-time latency. We then propose Reusable Evidence View Aggregation (REVA), a framework that mines the target generator's historical attention traces into a document-keyed, budget-agnostic score store. REVA maps token-level attention to readable word units, aggregates importance across repeated document accesses, and renders budget-specific plain-text views that preserve document order and the standard RAG interface. Across four representative benchmarks and modern LLMs, REVA improves generation quality by 1.0--5.8 points over existing advances, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 5.3 to 15.6, adding less than 40 ms of latency.

</details>


### [121] [Legible Failures: Detecting and Repairing In-Context Binding Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11216)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha,Abhinav M. Hari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在实体-义务绑定任务上，模型答错时其冻结隐状态中常仍保留正确答案：线性探针在错误样本上的准确率显著高于1/K基线，且探针可改进错误检测并用于引导残差流提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误答案无法区分模型是"缺乏所需信息"还是"持有信息但未能使用"。作者希望检验后者是否存在，即在模型输出错误绑定时，其内部表示是否仍编码了正确绑定，并进一步判断这种探针信号是否可操作。

Method: 在实体-义务绑定任务上，对16个公开模型检查点（每个3个随机种子）进行评估。将数据分为训练/验证/测试折：在训练折拟合线性探针，在验证折选择层，在独立测试折报告结果。采用查询-实体反事实排除token存在性与近因性解释，并构造基于探针输出分歧符号的评分用于错误检测，同时尝试用探针解码的绑定引导残差流（不使用金标签）。

Result: 在模型答错的样本上，探针准确率超过严格当前义务基线1/K=0.125，提升+0.196（95% CI [+0.101, +0.296]，按模型自助法）。基于探针-输出分歧符号的评分使失败检测较模型自身置信度提升+0.079 AUROC（95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]）；而原始探针置信度相比模型置信度无显著提升。将残差流朝探针解码绑定引导，在全部八个受测模型上平均提升准确率+0.168（95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]）。

Conclusion: 与近期研究认为探针检测到的错误难以干预不同，本文发现在上下文绑定这一设定中，探针不仅可解码被模型"知道但未用"的信息，而且具有可操作性，可用于改进错误检测与直接提升模型准确率。

Abstract: A wrong answer does not show whether the model lacked the needed information or held it and failed to use it. On an entity-obligation binding task, a language model can emit an incorrect prompt-supplied binding while a linear probe can recover the correct one from its frozen hidden state. We measure how often this occurs across 16 public checkpoints, each evaluated with three seeds. We fit a probe on a training fold, select its layer on a validation fold, and report results on a disjoint test fold. On the trials each model gets wrong, probe accuracy exceeds the strict present-obligation baseline, 1/K = 0.125, by +0.196 (95% CI [+0.101, +0.296], bootstrapped over models). A query-entity counterfactual rules out token presence and recency. A score built from the sign of probe-output disagreement improves failure detection over the model's own confidence by +0.079 AUROC (95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]). Raw probe confidence gives no measurable improvement over model confidence. Steering the residual stream toward the probe-decoded binding, with no gold label, raises accuracy on all eight models tested by a mean of +0.168 (95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]). Where recent studies report that probe-detected errors are resistant to interventions, we find that in-context binding is a setting in which probes are actionable.

</details>


### [122] [Predicting Privacy Leakage from Weight Spectral Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11780)
*Richard J. Preen,Jim Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究WeightWatcher谱指标能否作为成员推断攻击(MIA)脆弱性的廉价代理，发现稳定秩与MIA成功率强正相关，Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区间与MIA脆弱性持续负相关，且这些关联强于泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的MIA需要训练计算昂贵的影子模型，使大规模隐私评估不切实际，因此亟需低成本的隐私泄露代理指标。作者希望利用重尾自正则化框架导出的廉价谱指标来替代影子模型。

Method: 在图像与表格分类任务上评估多个WeightWatcher谱指标，将其与MIA隐私泄露的关系同传统泛化度量进行对比。

Result: 跨数据集中，稳定秩与整体MIA成功率呈强正相关；Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区间与MIA脆弱性呈一致负相关；这些关联均强于泛化差距所给出的关联。

Conclusion: 神经网络谱可能包含传统过拟合度量未能完全刻画的隐私泄露信息，谱分析是面向可扩展隐私审计的有前景方向。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are widely used to audit the privacy disclosure risk of machine learning models, however current state-of-the-art attacks require training computationally expensive shadow models, making large-scale privacy evaluation impractical. In this work, we investigate whether inexpensive spectral metrics derived from the heavy-tailed self-regularisation framework can serve as proxies for MIA vulnerability. We evaluate several WeightWatcher spectral metrics on image and tabular classification tasks and compare their relationship with MIA privacy leakage against conventional measures of generalisation. Across datasets, stable rank exhibits a strong positive correlation with overall MIA success, while Log alpha-Norm shows a consistent negative correlation with MIA vulnerability at the low false-positive regime. These associations are observed to be stronger than those obtained using the generalisation gap. The results indicate that neural network spectra may contain information about privacy leakage that is not fully captured by conventional measures of overfitting, motivating spectral analysis as a promising direction for scalable privacy auditing.

</details>


### [123] [Polyhedral Geometry of Time-to-First-Spike Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11227)
*Manjot Singh,Guido Montúfar,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究脉冲神经网络在time-to-first-spike模型下的表达能力，证明其神经元发放时间可表示为具有指数级约束仿射分片的maxout形式，并给出因果区域数量的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络适合异步、事件驱动计算，是常规前馈网络的补充，但其表达能力（尤其输入空间划分的丰富程度）尚需理论刻画，需与ReLU网络进行对比。

Method: 采用time-to-first-spike模型，在输入输出映射连续且分片线性、仿射分片由因果可行性约束（决定哪些突触前脉冲先于神经元发放）确定的设定下，将每个神经元的发放时间表示为受高度约束的maxout形式；并把因果区域形式化为具有固定因果集的多面体区域。

Result: 推导出浅层与多层前馈脉冲网络中因果区域最大数量的上界与下界，理论与实验均表明脉冲网络能产生比常规前馈ReLU网络更丰富的输入空间划分。

Conclusion: 脉冲神经网络在time-to-first-spike下具有更强的空间划分表达能力，因果区域分析为其表达性提供了理论工具，并显示其相对ReLU网络的优势。

Abstract: We study the expressivity of spiking neural networks, which provide a natural framework for asynchronous, event-driven computation complementary to conventional feedforward neural networks. We consider the time-to-first-spike model in a setting for which the input-output map is continuous and piecewise linear, with affine pieces governed by causal feasibility constraints that determine which presynaptic spikes occur before a neuron fires. We first show that each neuron's firing time admits a maxout-like representation with exponentially many, highly constrained affine pieces. We then formalize causal regions as polyhedral regions with fixed causal sets and derive upper and lower bounds on the maximal number of causal regions in both shallow and multilayer feedforward spiking networks. Our theoretical and experimental results show that spiking networks can generate richer partitions of the input space than conventional feedforward ReLU networks.

</details>


### [124] [Solving Few-Shot Multiobjective Multitask Optimization via Iterative Sequential Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11228)
*Tingyang Wei,Haofeng Wu,Ananda Phan Iman,Zhao Wei,Jiao Liu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代序列迁移（IST）方法，将多任务优化（MTO）建模为一系列序列迁移优化问题，并利用似然信息驱动的任务优先级机制，在紧评估预算下提升多目标多任务优化的知识迁移效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务优化依赖对齐各任务的精英解分布来实现有效知识迁移，但在少样本优化场景中评估预算受限，难以识别精英解分布，形成关键瓶颈；多目标多任务问题中每个优化器还需逼近连续Pareto流形而非单一最优点，进一步加剧该挑战。

Method: 提出迭代序列迁移（IST）方法：将MTO建模为一序列顺序迁移优化问题，每次迭代仅聚焦单个目标任务进行评估；并设计似然信息驱动的任务优先级机制，识别最有可能具备知识整合条件的任务，以最大化迁移效用。

Result: 在基准问题和真实世界问题上的实证结果验证了所提方法在紧预算条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: IST通过序列迁移与任务优先级机制绕开精英解分布对齐瓶颈，为紧评估预算下的多目标多任务优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Applying knowledge transfer across multiple optimization tasks, multitask optimization (MTO) emerges as a promising approach to solving synergistic optimization tasks simultaneously. However, the development of effective knowledge transfer mechanisms in MTO fundamentally relies on aligning elite solution distributions across tasks. This dependency creates a critical bottleneck in few-shot optimization regimes, as restricted evaluation budgets impede the identification of elite solution distributions required for beneficial transfer. This challenge is exacerbated in multiobjective multitask problems, where each optimizer must approximate a continuous Pareto manifold rather than a single optimal point. This paper introduces Iterative Sequential Transfer (IST) to circumvent this bottleneck. We model MTO as a sequence of sequential transfer optimization problems, concentrating evaluations on a single target per iteration. We propose a likelihood-informed task prioritization mechanism to maximize transfer utility by identifying the task most likely ready for knowledge integration. Empirical results on benchmark and real-world problems verify the effectiveness of the proposed method under tight budgets.

</details>


### [125] [MUtE: A Dual Framework for Concept Erasure and Counterfactual Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11253)
*Antoine Saillenfest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视概念擦除的最优界，推导出一类可自然诱导确定性对偶反事实映射的擦除函数，并给出带平移偏置的实现，统一了概念擦除与反事实生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从表示中擦除特定概念信息有助于缓解偏见或解释模型决策，其联合目标是在使目标概念不可预测的同时最大程度保留与概念无关的信息。但理论最优性与实际表示学习之间存在差距。

Method: 重新分析概念擦除的最优界，推导出一类新的擦除函数，该类函数天然产生确定性、对偶的反事实映射；并设计一种在反事实轨迹上施加平移偏置（translational bias）的实现，该约束与现代语言模型中许多概念的几何表现方式一致。

Result: 框架支持在概念擦除与反事实生成之间无缝切换；实验证明其在提升下游算法公平性和生成反事实文本方面有效。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了概念擦除的理论最优性与实际表示学习之间的差距，统一了概念擦除与反事实生成，并在公平性与反事实文本生成任务上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Erasing concept-specific information from representations has been proven useful for mitigating bias or interpreting model decisions. The joint objective is to transform the original representations such that the target concept becomes unpredictable, while maximally preserving concept-unrelated information. In this work, we revisit the optimal bounds of concept erasure to derive a novel class of erasure functions that naturally induce a deterministic, dual counterfactual mapping. Bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical representation learning, we design an implementation that imposes a translational bias on counterfactual trajectories - a constraint that aligns with how many concepts geometrically manifest in modern language models. Our framework enables seamless navigation between concept erasure and counterfactual generation. We empirically demonstrate its efficacy in improving downstream algorithmic fairness and generating counterfactual texts.

</details>


### [126] [A Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11314)
*Xue Qiu,Jianli Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出动态融合大语言模型DF-LLM用于交通流预测，通过时空嵌入、时空融合与LLM主干三大模块协同提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流预测是智能交通系统的核心支撑技术。传统神经网络受限于单一特征建模，难以突破精度瓶颈；大语言模型则对空间拓扑信息捕获不足，且时空相关性挖掘不充分。

Method: DF-LLM包含三个核心组件：时空嵌入模块实现多尺度时空特征的协同表示；时空融合模块通过图卷积整合空间拓扑与动态依赖；LLM主干采用差异化参数适配策略平衡训练效率与交通数据适应性，并引入上下文聚合注意力模块增强全局依赖，同时利用残差连接缓解深层网络梯度消失。

Result: 在全部四个数据集上的指标对比中，DF-LLM均取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: DF-LLM通过时空特征协同建模与LLM参数适配的结合，有效提升了交通流预测性能。

Abstract: Traffic flow prediction is a core supporting technology for intelligent transportation systems. It uses historical data to infer future traffic dynamics in specific areas, thereby helping to alleviate congestion and improve resource allocation efficiency. Traditional neural networks struggle to break through accuracy limits due to their reliance on singular feature modeling, while large language models (LLMs) suffer from insufficient capture of spatial topological information and mining spatiotemporal correlation. This study proposes a Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model (DF-LLM) for traffic flow prediction. The model incorporates three core components: spatiotemporal embedding module, spatiotemporal fusion module, and LLM backbone. The spatiotemporal embedding module enables synergistic representation of multi-scale spatiotemporal features. The spatiotemporal fusion module integrates spatial topology and dynamic dependencies via graph convolution. The LLM backbone adopts a differentiated parameter adaptation strategy to balance training efficiency and traffic data adaptability. Additionally, it introduces a context aggregation attention module to strengthens global dependencies. More importantly, the LLM backbone takes the residual connections to mitigate the gradient vanishing in deep networks. Experiments show that DF-LLM has achieved better performance by comparing the metrics on all the four datasets.

</details>


### [127] [Estimating Inconsistency Response Surfaces under Uncertainty in Cyber-Physical System Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11331)
*Johannes Mäkelburg,Tim Schwabe,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将CPS多模型一致性问题重构为"干预-响应"建模问题，用Saltelli采样与多保真蒙特卡洛生成数据集，训练代理模型直接从不确定性几何预测不一致性，并基于响应面做敏感性分析与梯度式一致性补救，实现微秒级评估与最小不确定性干预修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS通常由多个相互关联的模型表示，共享模型元素需在这些模型间保持兼容。不确定性（如传感器噪声、模型抽象）会改变模型元素的可行取值域，从而引发不一致。现有方法只能对给定不确定性配置判定一致性，难以系统性地探索、分析和解释大规模不确定性空间中的不一致，缺少可扩展的手段。

Method: 1) 将不一致性重构为干预-响应建模问题；2) 采用Saltelli采样结合多保真蒙特卡洛估计生成干预-响应数据集；3) 训练代理模型，从传播后的不确定性几何直接预测不一致性；4) 基于所学响应面开展敏感性分析，识别主导不确定性驱动因素；5) 提出基于梯度的一致性补救方法，求解恢复一致性的最小不确定性干预。

Result: 在48个场景、10个CPS领域上的实验表明，代理模型的预测与蒙特卡洛估计相当，但评估时间从毫秒级降至微秒级，从而在固定计算预算下可实现数量级更多的响应面评估；敏感性分析能够定位主导不确定性来源，梯度式补救方法可确定恢复一致性的最小不确定性干预。

Conclusion: 不确定性下的不一致性可以通过响应面建模被有效学习、分析和修复，为CPS开发中面向不确定性的可扩展一致性管理提供了基础。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) are commonly represented through multiple interconnected models. During development, CPS consistency requires that shared model elements remain compatible across these models. Uncertainty, for example, due to sensor noise or model abstraction, changes the admissible values of model elements and can introduce inconsistencies, i.e., situations in which models can no longer be jointly satisfied. While existing approaches can determine consistency for a given uncertainty configuration, they provide limited support for systematically exploring, analyzing, and explaining inconsistency across large uncertainty spaces. We address this challenge by reformulating inconsistency as an intervention response modeling problem. Using Saltelli sampling and multi-fidelity Monte Carlo estimation, we generate intervention-response datasets and train a surrogate model that directly predicts inconsistency from the propagated uncertainty geometry. Experiments on 48 scenarios and 10 CPS domains show that the surrogate matches Monte Carlo estimates while reducing evaluation time from milliseconds to microseconds, enabling orders-of-magnitude more response-surface evaluations within fixed computational budgets. Building on the learned response surfaces, we perform sensitivity analysis to identify dominant uncertainty drivers and introduce a gradient-based consistency recourse method to determine minimal uncertainty interventions that restore consistency. The results show that inconsistency under uncertainty can be effectively learned, analyzed, and repaired through response-surface modeling, providing a scalable foundation for uncertainty-aware consistency management in CPS development.

</details>


### [128] [Reification as a Transferable Vocabulary: Zero-Shot Link Prediction with Vanilla GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11347)
*Camille Pradel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将迁移机制从架构中移出、嵌入到图表示里：通过"物化"(reifying)输入图（每条事实变节点，用6个元关系连接主客实体与关系类型），使现成GNN在单图30分钟训练后即可零样本迁移到40个归纳链接预测基准，最佳GAT可媲美专门的基础模型ULTRA，并可扩展至关系数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱基础模型（如ULTRA）依赖专门设计的架构硬编码迁移机制，以实现在未见图上的零样本链接预测。作者希望把该迁移机制从架构中解耦出来，转移到图表示层面，从而用通用GNN即可获得同等迁移能力，并探索该表示能否推广到关系数据库等其他关系型结构。

Method: 提出"物化"（reification）表示：将每个事实变为一个节点，通过固定的六元关系词汇表连接到其主语、宾语和关系类型；关系类型作为匿名共享节点而非模型参数。在此表示上使用五种教科书级GNN（GAT、GINE(求和)、GINE(均值+最大)、GraphSAGE、R-GCN），每个模型仅在单个含4,245条三元组的知识图谱上、用单张NVIDIA A100训练30分钟。同一固定词汇表扩展至关系数据库：行作为实体、外键列作为关系类型。

Result: 五种GNN均零样本迁移至40个归纳链接预测基准；其中最佳的现成GAT在ULTRA自身评测套件上与之持平（ULTRA为在三张图上预训练的专用基础模型）。在关系数据库上的初步探测（两个未见数据库，不使用单元格值、schema文本或上下文标签）显示，该家族中一个在三张知识图谱上预训练的模型，其外键目标排名远高于随机初始化和度数控制基线。

Conclusion: 通过物化表示把迁移机制从架构搬到表示层，可用通用现成GNN实现与专用基础模型相当的零样本链接预测迁移，并展现出跨知识图谱与关系数据库的通用性。作者公开全部40个基准的代码、检查点和评测流水线，为可复现研究提供支持。

Abstract: Knowledge graph foundation models such as ULTRA achieve zero-shot link prediction on unseen graphs through dedicated architectures that hard-code a transfer mechanism. In this work we move that mechanism out of the architecture and into the representation, by \emph{reifying} the input graph: every fact becomes a node, connected to its subject, object, and relation type through a fixed vocabulary of six meta-relations, with relation types as anonymous shared nodes rather than model parameters. On this representation, five textbook GNNs (GAT, GINE with sum and with mean+max aggregation, GraphSAGE, R-GCN), each trained on a single knowledge graph of 4,245 triples for 30 minutes on one NVIDIA A100, transfer zero-shot to 40 inductive link-prediction benchmarks. The best of them, an off-the-shelf GAT, matches ULTRA, a dedicated foundation model pretrained on three graphs, across ULTRA's own evaluation suite. The same fixed vocabulary extends to relational databases, a row becoming an entity and a foreign-key column a relation type; a preliminary probe on two unseen databases, with no cell values, schema text or in-context labels, shows a model of this family pretrained on three knowledge graphs ranking foreign-key targets far above random-initialization and degree controls. We release the code, the checkpoints, and the evaluation pipeline for all 40 benchmarks.

</details>


### [129] [Prevalence Determines Precision:Silent Contamination in Detector-Defined Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11449)
*Jia Huang,Yankai Wan,Yangjun Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 数据集精度由每个候选池中的真阳性流行率决定而非检测器质量；三个池的虚假率差异巨大，跨池迁移精度会产生+422%误差，贝叶斯表达式可在3.3%内预测；污染是继承检测器形状的第二种信号，其方向依赖估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多ML数据集通过检测器/启发式/模型在候选池上运行构建，被接受的样本成为标签，因此数据集精度实际上受各池真阳性流行率支配，而非仅由检测器质量决定。作者希望检验这一贝叶斯机制，并说明忽略流行率差异会带来多大偏差，同时厘清污染对估计量的影响。

Method: 使用单一检测器与单一时间段，构建一个由检测器定义的事件数据集，并以独立官方索引对每个检测项标注为真实或幻影。在三个候选池中比较虚假率，并检验两种预测方式：(1) 将两个高虚假率池的精度迁移到低虚假率池；(2) 使用贝叶斯表达式进行预测。进一步对检测响应曲线做真实事件分量与幻影分量的凸组合分解，并考察同一窗口下不同统计量因分母被污染而表现出的稀释或膨胀，以及归一化后估计量变为比值均值所导致的期望不存在问题。

Result: 三个池的幻影率分别为81.7%、9.0%和0.0%。将两个高虚假率池的精度迁移到低虚假率池，预测为0.955而实测为0.183，误差达+422%；贝叶斯表达式对三个池的预测误差均在3.3%以内。检测响应曲线可精确表示为真实事件分量与幻影分量的凸组合，残差为1.1e-16，其中幻影473例多于真实事件308例。相同窗口下，一个统计量被稀释而另一个被膨胀，因其分母同样受到污染。常见的归一化将估计量变为比值均值，其期望未必存在；在同样的335个事件上该估计量给出0.40，而定义良好的估计量给出0.10。

Conclusion: 数据集精度受候选池真阳性流行率支配，跨池迁移精度会严重失真，而贝叶斯表达可准确预测。污染并非可加噪声，而是继承检测器形状的第二信号，其对估计量的影响方向（稀释或膨胀）取决于分母是否被污染。归一化引发的比值均值问题会使估计量失去良好定义，因此需谨慎选择估计量并显式建模流行率与污染。

Abstract: Many ML datasets are constructed by running a detector, heuristic, or model over candidate pools; accepted items become labels. Dataset precision is then governed by true-positive prevalence in each pool via Bayes, not solely by detector quality. Using one instrument and period, we hold a detector-defined event dataset plus an independent official index labeling every detected item as real or phantom. One detector, three pools yield phantom rates 81.7%, 9.0%, and 0.0%. Transferring precision from the two high-rate pools to the low-rate pool predicts 0.955 versus measured 0.183, a +422% error; the Bayes expression predicts all three within 3.3%. The detected response curve is an exact convex combination of a true-event and a phantom component (residual 1.1e-16), with phantoms outnumbering true events 473 to 308, so contamination is a second signal with detector-inherited shape, not additive noise. Contamination direction depends on the estimator: on identical windows one statistic is diluted and another inflated because its denominator is also contaminated. A common normalization turns the estimator into a mean of ratios whose expectation need not exist; on the same 335 events it returns 0.40 where the well-defined estimator returns 0.10.

</details>


### [130] [DeFiFlowBench: Benchmarking and Improving Safe Executability in Natural-Language DeFi Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11504)
*Abhinav Rajeev Kumar,Harshit Arora,Varun Singh,Manikandan Nanjappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeFiFlowBench：自然语言DeFi工作流合成基准（207条团队撰写提示），从图覆盖、配置完整性、声明式安全谓词三方面评估，并在本地EVM上执行受支持的交易配置；提出Koan-Safe（意图解析器+可替换生成器+结构修复+默认安全参数），静态安全代理0.67（最好基线0.33），保存输出上无不安全执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构上合法的DeFi工作流仍可能授权一笔代价高昂的交易（如高价格冲击）；现有评估多停留在声明层面，缺乏基于执行的安全评测，且缺少衡量自然语言合成DeFi工作流质量与安全性的统一基准。

Method: 构建DeFiFlowBench：207条团队撰写的提示，用于自然语言DeFi工作流合成；度量图覆盖、配置完整性与声明式安全谓词，并在本地EVM上测试受支持的交易配置。对比直接、约束、少样本提示（固定5%价格冲击上限）。提出Koan-Safe：仅提示的意图解析器、可替换生成器、带默认安全参数的结构修复；并做匹配候选消融（关闭强制约束）与额外策略上限诊断（36例网格）。

Result: 直接/约束/少样本提示在每种配置下产生14–19次不安全留出执行；由报价推导的滑点边界无法阻止订单自身价格冲击。Koan-Safe混合变体在75条留出工作流提示上静态安全代理得分0.67（最优基线0.33），在保存的基准输出上无不安全执行；关闭强制约束的消融产生14–17次不安全执行。默认注入存在局限：原本宽松的阈值仍可授权不安全交易，单独评估的策略上限在36例诊断网格上解决该失效。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式交易保护与基于执行的评估范式，同时需区分"声明安全"与"一般性保证"：声明式安全不能等同于系统安全。

Abstract: A structurally valid DeFi workflow can still authorize a costly trade. We introduce DeFiFlowBench, a benchmark of 207 team-authored prompts for natural-language DeFi workflow synthesis. It measures graph coverage, configuration completeness, and declared safety predicates, then tests supported trade configurations on a local EVM. Direct, constrained, and few-shot prompting produce 14-19 unsafe held-out executions per configuration under a fixed 5% price-impact cap. A slippage bound derived from a quote does not prevent the price impact of the order itself. We propose Koan-Safe, which combines a prompt-only intent parser, a replaceable generator, and structural repair with default safety parameters. On 75 held-out workflow prompts, its hybrid variant scores 0.67 on the static safety proxy, compared with 0.33 for the best baseline. Koan-Safe records no unsafe executions on the saved benchmark outputs. A matched-candidate ablation produces 14-17 unsafe executions when enforcement is disabled. Additional tests expose the limits of default injection: permissive existing thresholds can still authorize unsafe trades. A separately evaluated policy cap addresses this failure on a 36-case diagnostic grid. These results support explicit trade protections and execution-based evaluation, while distinguishing declared safety from a general guarantee.

</details>


### [131] [Generalized Score Matching for Parameter Estimation on Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11521)
*Nishanth Shetty,Saisuchith Mahajan,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小概率流（MPF）学习出发，构造性地推导了定义在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 凸子集上的广义分数匹配目标，证明其为二阶真局部评分规则，并在指数族模型下给出凸性与有限样本估计的相合性，实验验证了在分区函数不可解析求解的约束域上的参数估计与生成建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然估计对未归一化模型需要计算并对分区函数求导，往往不可处理；分数匹配通过消除对归一化常数的依赖提供了可行替代方案。作者希望厘清广义分数匹配的来源、适用范围及其在约束域问题中的理论保证。

Method: 以 Minimum Probability Flow（MPF）学习为起点，构造性推导 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 凸子集上的广义分数匹配目标，并将经典分数匹配与非负数据的域适应变体纳入统一框架；随后从评分规则理论证明该目标为二阶真局部评分规则，并在指数族模型下分析目标的凸性与有限样本估计量的相合性。

Result: 所推导目标被证明为二阶真局部评分规则，保证最小化该目标可恢复真实密度；对指数族模型证明了目标函数的凸性，并在标准正则条件下建立了有限样本估计量的相合性。实验在凸子集上定义的指数族模型密度参数估计以及一个生成建模用例中对比了基于广义分数匹配的估计器。

Conclusion: 该推导揭示了广义分数匹配的范围与适用性，统一了经典及域适应分数匹配，并为分区函数解析不可处理的约束域学习提供了有理论保证的估计框架。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) estimation is a principled and statistically efficient approach for learning probabilistic models. However, for unnormalized models, ML estimation requires evaluating the partition function and differentiating through it, which may not always be tractable. Score matching provides a practically viable alternative that circumvents this obstacle by fitting the score in a way that eliminates dependence on the normalizing constant. We derive the generalized score matching objective on a convex subset of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ constructively starting from Minimum Probability Flow (MPF) learning, and show how classical score matching as well as domain-adapted variants for non-negative data arise naturally within the proposed framework. We show that the resulting objective is a {\it proper local scoring rule} of second-order, which provides the theoretical guarantee that the true density is recovered when the objective is minimized. Furthermore, for a model belonging to the exponential family, we establish convexity of the objective together with consistency of the finite-sample estimator under standard regularity conditions. Our derivation sheds new light on the scope and applicability of generalized score matching in various problem settings. We compare generalized score matching-based estimators on constrained domains, where the partition function is analytically intractable. We provide experimental results on parameter estimation for model densities belonging to the exponential family defined over convex subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, and a generative modeling use-case to demonstrate broader applicability of the proposed generalized score matching framework.

</details>


### [132] [A Dataset and Model for Imputing Water Surface Elevation on a Large and Extremely Sparse Spatiotemporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11580)
*Ruben Cartuyvels,Karim Douch,Gabriele Bertoli,Mounia El Baz,Artemis Vrettou,Sébastien Lefèvre,Diego Fernandez Prieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AmazonSWE数据集（含SWOT等卫星测高数据，覆盖亚马逊流域19K河段、2016-2026年），用于大规模时空图插补，并提出双向选择性状态空间模型，利用连通子图采样与拓扑感知位置编码，在实测站点上较现有方法RMSE降低18-39%，且可预测全部河段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 河流水位连续监测对洪水预报、水资源管理和全球水循环研究至关重要，但全球多数地区实测站点稀少，制约可靠建模框架的发展。卫星测高有潜力缓解该问题，却因时间覆盖稀疏而受限。现有插补基准在稀疏度、图规模与拓扑结构上均与真实河流网络差异较大，已有时空图插补方法难以适用。

Method: 1）构建AmazonSWE数据集：整合多来源预处理卫星测高数据（含宽刈幅SWOT传感器），覆盖亚马逊流域超19K河段、10年（2016-2026），保留实测站点用于评估；2）提出采样连通子图、并将空间与时间展平为单一token序列、配合拓扑感知位置编码的方法；3）采用简单的双向选择性状态空间模型进行插补。

Result: 数据集每日观测河段不足1%，远稀疏于现有插补基准；其有向无环河流拓扑在结构上不同于且规模大于现有数据集图。实验表明先前时空图插补方法不适应此拓扑、规模与稀疏度；所提模型相比已发表的最先进SWOT水位加密方法（统计+物理建模），在实测站点上RMSE降低18-39%，并可对每个河段给出预测，而非仅限邻近卫星覆盖充分的河段。

Conclusion: AmazonSWE提供了具有社会影响的真实应用场景及显著技术挑战，推动了大规模稀疏时空图插补研究；所提双向选择性状态空间模型在精度与覆盖完整性上均优于现有方法，适用于河流网络水位密集化。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of water surface elevation across river networks is critical for flood forecasting, water resource management, and understanding the global water cycle. Yet, the scarcity of in situ gauges across much of the globe constrains the development of reliable modeling frameworks. Satellite altimetry has the potential to alleviate this problem but its use is currently hindered by sparse temporal coverage. To this end, we introduce AmazonSWE, a dataset for training and evaluating large-scale spatiotemporal graph imputation methods that integrates processed satellite altimetry measurements from a range of sources, including the recent wide-swath SWOT sensor. The dataset covers over 19K river sections and 10 years (2016-2026) in the Amazon river basin, with in situ gauges held out for evaluation. Besides contributing a novel real-world use case with the potential for societal impact, AmazonSWE introduces significant technical challenges: with fewer than 1% of sections observed per day, the dataset is far sparser than existing imputation benchmarks, and its directed acyclic river topology is both structurally different from and larger than graphs in existing datasets. We show that prior spatiotemporal graph imputation methods are not adapted to this topology, scale and sparsity, and propose a simple bidirectional selective state space model that outperforms them by sampling connected subgraphs and flattening space and time into a single token sequence with topology-aware positional encodings. Compared to the state-of-the-art published method for SWOT-based WSE densification, which integrates statistics with physical modeling, our model reduces RMSE against in situ gauges by 18-39%, while producing predictions for every river section rather than only those with sufficient nearby satellite coverage.

</details>


### [133] [LoaDiff: Conditional Generation of Electricity Consumption Time Series for Energy Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11639)
*Mariia Baranova,Adrien Petralia,Etienne Le Naour,Nathan Etourneau,Guillaume Hofmann,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LoaDiff，一种基于扩散模型的合成智能电表负荷曲线生成方法，可生成全年、亚小时级负荷曲线，并支持静态家庭属性与动态上下文变量条件控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源转型推动分布式发电、电气化电器和需求响应普及，使居民用电行为发生变化；负荷预测、电器识别、需求侧灵活性分析等应用需要细粒度智能电表数据，但这类数据受严格访问限制与数据保护法规约束，因此需要真实感强的合成数据替代方案。

Method: 提出 LoaDiff——面向全年、亚小时级智能电表负荷曲线的扩散生成模型，支持对静态家庭属性（如电器拥有情况）和动态上下文变量（如日历信息、室外温度）进行灵活条件控制；在三个居民用电数据集上与多种生成基线进行对比。

Result: 实验从四个互补维度评估：保真度与多样性、训练记录记忆风险、对负荷预测和电器识别的下游效用、以及替代温度条件下的条件可控性。结果显示 LoaDiff 能生成真实且多样的负荷曲线，在生成质量与有限的记忆证据之间取得良好权衡，保留对下游能源应用有用的信息，并对条件变量变化作出连贯响应。

Conclusion: LoaDiff 可作为受访问限制条件下智能电表数据的现实合成替代方案，在生成质量、隐私风险、下游效用和条件可控性方面表现均衡。

Abstract: The energy transition is reshaping residential electricity consumption through the increasing adoption of distributed generation, electrified appliances, and demand-response programs. Understanding these evolving behaviors requires access to granular smart-meter data for applications such as load forecasting, appliance detection, and demand-side flexibility analysis. However, such data are subject to strict access restrictions and data-protection regulations. Thus, realistic synthetic alternatives are necessary. In this paper, we introduce LoaDiff, a diffusion-based generative model for year-long, sub-hourly smart-meter load curves. LoaDiff supports flexible conditioning on static household attributes, such as appliance ownership, and dynamic contextual variables, including calendar information and outdoor temperature. We evaluate the model against multiple generative baselines on three residential electricity-consumption datasets. Our experiments assess four complementary dimensions: fidelity and diversity, training-record memorization risk, downstream utility for load forecasting and appliance detection, and conditional controllability under alternative temperature conditions. The results show that LoaDiff generates realistic and diverse load profiles, achieves a favorable trade-off between generation quality and limited evidence of memorization, preserves information useful for downstream energy applications, and responds coherently to changes in conditioning variables.

</details>


### [134] [Learnware and AI Model Management System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11656)
*Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出从"AI模型存储"向"AI模型管理"的范式跃迁，通过将管理基本单元由模型升级为"学件（Learnware = 模型 + 规格）"，并以不泄露训练数据、具有数据保持性质的规格生成机制及学件坞系统（LDS）为实现路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型池本质是"AI模型存储系统"，仅做保存与索引，无法满足未来需求。真正的目标是构建"AI模型管理系统"，使不同开发者、不同任务、不同数据与目标下训练的模型可被识别、复用乃至组装以应对未来用户任务。核心矛盾在于：模型开发者普遍不愿共享训练数据，系统须在不访问开发者训练数据（理想情况下也不访问用户原始数据）的前提下运作；而现代AI模型的功能即使开发者本人也未必完全理解，因此识别"哪个模型对某任务有用"乃至组装异构模型显得近乎不可能。

Method: 关键在于把管理的基本单元从机器学习模型升级为"学件"：学件 = 模型 + 规格。规格借助机器学习过程生成，生成过程中不披露开发者的训练数据，并具有理论上已证明的数据保持（data-preservation）性质；该规格的赋予过程即把训练好的模型转化为学件。由于规格按公开参考标准生成、跨模型可比，因此可作为AI模型的协作协议。以此为基础构建学件坞系统（LDS）。

Result: 规格的生成无需暴露开发者训练数据且具备可证明的数据保持性质；规格因依公开参考生成而具有跨模型可比性，从而支持模型的识别、复用与组装，并可充当独立开发模型（含智能体）之间的协作协议。LDS据此提供了通往强大AI模型管理系统的实现路径。

Conclusion: 将管理单元由模型升级为学件，使原本看似不可达的"无数据访问的模型识别与组装"目标成为可能；学件坞系统是通向AI模型管理系统的可行路径，且学件规格可扩展为AI模型协作协议。

Abstract: The transition from file storage to database management systems transformed stored data into managed resources. AI now faces an analogous transition from AI model storage to AI model management. Existing model pools essentially serve as \textit{AI model storage systems}. What is needed instead are \textit{AI model management systems} that enable models trained by different developers, for different tasks, with different data, and under different objectives to be identified, reused, and even assembled to address future user tasks. Because AI model developers are generally unwilling to share their training data, such systems should operate without accessing the training data of model developers and, ideally, without accessing raw data of future users. This requirement poses a fundamental challenge: the functionality of a modern AI model may not be fully understood even by the developer who trained it. How, then, can a system identify which models are useful for a given user task, let alone assemble models developed independently for different purposes? At first glance, this objective may appear unattainable. It becomes possible, however, by upgrading the basic unit of management from a machine learning model to a \textit{learnware}. \textit{Learnware = Model + Specification}. The specification, whose assignment transforms a trained model into a learnware, is generated with the help of a machine learning process without disclosing the training data of the developer and has a theoretically established data-preservation property. The \textit{Learnware Dock System (LDS)} provides a path toward powerful AI model management systems. Because specifications are generated according to a published reference and are comparable across models, they can also serve as an AI model \textit{collaboration protocol} through which independently developed models, including intelligent agents, can collaborate.

</details>


### [135] [Dynamic language model representations for multi-objective reaction optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11790)
*Joshua W. Sin,David Ming Segura,Bojana Ranković,Siu Lun Chau,Marius D. R. Lutz,Andrea Anelli,Ryan P. Burwood,Kurt Püntener,Maximilian J. Notheis,Raphael Bigler,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出用文本描述反应条件、由微调语言模型动态学习反应表征，并与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，嵌入多目标贝叶斯优化闭环；在镍/钯交叉偶联中收敛更快，并前瞻性应用于钯催化氰化与三目标不对称氢化，两轮高通量实验即获得可克级放大的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学合成常需同时优化产率、选择性、安全性等多个目标，而模型驱动方法的关键在于反应组分如何表征。现有一热编码缺乏化学信息，分子描述符又难以跨化学异构组分通用；对结构功能多样的组分，共享表征应包含什么并不清楚，且为每个新反应体系重新构建表征本身就是一项艰难的研究任务。

Method: 绕过人工构建表征的步骤，直接从文本中动态学习反应表征：将反应条件的文本描述用微调语言模型编码，该模型与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，从而在多目标贝叶斯优化循环内生成任务自适应表征。在镍催化与钯催化交叉偶联、序贯与并行实验模式下进行评估，并前瞻性应用于钯催化氰化（混合配体齿合度与非均相添加剂）及三目标不对称氢化（涵盖手性铱与钌催化剂家族）。

Result: 相比描述符库或一热编码，该方法以更少实验达到优化收敛。前瞻性应用中，两轮高通量实验（192个反应，不到各自设计空间的3%）即获得可直接转化为克级的条件：钯催化氰化为94%分离收率，不对称氢化达84%分离收率且对映体过量99.6%。

Conclusion: 以文本驱动的任务自适应表征可替代人工设计表征，避免为新反应体系重建表征，并在多目标贝叶斯优化中显著提升实验效率，所得条件具备直接克级放大能力。

Abstract: Optimising chemical reactions across multiple objectives, such as yield, selectivity, and safety, is central to chemical synthesis, and model-driven approaches depend critically on how reaction components are represented. Established featurisations are either chemically uninformative, as with one-hot encodings, or, as with molecular descriptors, do not readily extend across chemically distinct components. For structurally and functionally diverse components, it is therefore unclear what a shared representation should contain. Constructing such a representation is itself a challenging research undertaking that must be revisited for each new reaction system. Here we bypass this step by learning the reaction representation dynamically from text. Textual descriptions of reaction conditions are encoded by a fine-tuned language model trained jointly with Gaussian process surrogates, yielding task-adaptive representations within a multi-objective Bayesian optimisation loop. Across nickel- and palladium-catalysed cross-couplings in both sequential and parallel experimentation regimes, this approach reaches optimisation convergence in fewer experiments than descriptor libraries or one-hot encoding. Applied prospectively to a palladium-catalysed cyanation spanning mixed ligand denticity and heterogeneous additives, and to a three-objective asymmetric hydrogenation across chiral iridium and ruthenium catalyst families, two rounds of high-throughput experimentation (192 reactions, under 3% of each design space) delivered conditions translating directly to gram scale in 94% and 84% isolated yield, the latter at 99.6% enantiomeric excess.

</details>


### [136] [Thinking with Looped Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11801)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Chanhyuk Lee,Floor Eijkelboom,Nicholas M. Boffi,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"循环流"(looped flows),用局部去噪目标训练循环模型的递归更新,使梯度仅覆盖少数更新时仍能学到可跨时间传递计算量的循环状态;推理时对学习到的概率流速度场做积分,通过更细的时间网格与多样初始噪声样本提升困难任务性能,在六个推理基准上总体超越此前最好的循环模型(ARC-AGI-1 58.8%,ARC-AGI-2 12.2%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类和机器常通过花费更多计算时间来求解更难的问题,深度学习中循环模型在推理阶段通过反复更新隐状态实现这一思想。但实际训练时反向传播仅穿过一次或少数几次更新,导致早期的更新难以被训练来支撑后续更新,即长时程的递归训练困难。作者希望绕开这一"梯度只覆盖少量更新"的瓶颈。

Method: 提出 looped flows:用局部去噪目标(local denoising objectives)训练递归过程,而非依赖长程反向传播。通过在去噪目标之间施加时间关联——采用渐进降低的噪声水平与共享噪声——激励模型学习能够随时间迁移有用计算量的循环状态,即使梯度只覆盖少数几步更新。推理阶段形式化为对由所学去噪器参数化的概率流速度场进行积分,并与循环状态耦合;这样可以通过更细的时间网格投入更多计算,并可从不同初始噪声样本得到多个有效预测。

Result: 在六个推理基准(含两个多解基准)上,looped flows 总体上优于此前的 SOTA 循环模型,取得 ARC-AGI-1 测试准确率 58.8%、ARC-AGI-2 12.2%。

Conclusion: 循环模型的递归训练可被重构为带时间关联的局部去噪问题,从而在梯度仅覆盖少数更新时仍学到可迁移的循环状态;推理时以概率流积分的方式可控地增加计算量并产生多解,在困难推理任务上显著超越先前循环模型。

Abstract: Humans and machines often solve harder problems by spending more time on computation. In deep learning, looped models implement this idea during inference by recurrently updating a hidden state. In practice, however, their training backpropagates through only one or a few updates, making it hard to train early updates to support future ones. We propose looped flows, an approach that sidesteps this issue by training the recurrence with local denoising objectives. By imposing temporal association across denoising objectives through progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise, the model is incentivized to learn recurrent states that transfer useful computation over time, even when gradients cover only a few updates. We then formulate inference as integrating the velocity of a probability flow parameterized by the learned denoiser, coupled with recurrent states. This allows solving harder problems by spending more computation through a finer temporal grid and enables multiple valid predictions from different initial noise samples. Across six reasoning benchmarks including two multi-solution benchmarks, looped flows outperform prior state-of-the-art looped models overall, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

</details>


### [137] [Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11842)
*Luyi Jia,Boyan Zhang,Yilun Liu,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于纤维化最优传输（fiberwise OT）的模型感知调度构建方法，将预测风险与系数路径动能作用结合，得到闭式最优时间分配，在DDPM与flow matching上稳定超越强基线，并观察到跨模型设置的归一化风险曲线具有经验普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与flow matching的调度（信号/噪声系数插值路径）显著影响生成质量。已有的基于动能作用最小化的调度虽能解释强基线，但是model-agnostic，忽略了预测器自身的误差，因此无法针对具体模型优化时间分配。

Method: 在概率路径上固定时间与状态时，相容的信号/噪声分解构成仿射纤维；在纤维内以真实分解与预测器诱导分解之间的最优传输代价的均值定义fiberwise prediction risk。将该项与系数路径的动能作用组合，在给定系数曲线上得到闭式最优时间分配；方法推广到一般线性预测目标，风险曲线可由早期baseline checkpoint估计。

Result: 在DDPM与flow matching上跨预测目标、训练配置、风险估计checkpoint、数据集与架构评估，模型感知调度持续优于强基线：flow matching在CIFAR-10、16次函数评估下取得38.6%的相对FID下降。各model-agnostic动能基线有各自的动能参考坐标，在该坐标下不同设置、独立训练的模型所得的fiberwise risk曲线归一化到单位面积后高度一致，相应的训练调度形变也一致，显示经验普适性；预训练checkpoint诊断将该归一化风险一致性扩展到更大的条件潜空间扩散模型与2-RF模型。冻结的解析分配模板可在不再估计风险、不做模型特定拟合的情况下保留大部分模型感知增益。

Conclusion: 将预测误差以纤维化OT风险形式引入调度设计，可在闭式解下显著提升生成效率与质量；且风险profile与调度形变在模型/设置间具有归一化普适性，使冻结解析模板可作为通用即用调度方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. We define a fiberwise prediction risk by averaging optimal-transport costs between the true and predictor-induced decompositions within these fibers. On a fixed coefficient curve, combining this risk with coefficient-path kinetic action yields a closed-form optimal time allocation. This construction extends to general linear prediction targets, and the risk profile can be estimated from an early baseline checkpoint. We evaluate DDPMs and flow matching across prediction targets, training configurations, risk-estimation checkpoints, datasets, and architectures. Our model-aware schedules consistently outperform strong baselines, including a 38.6% relative FID reduction for flow matching on CIFAR-10 at 16 function evaluations. Each model-agnostic kinetic baseline determines its own kinetic reference coordinate. In these coordinates, fiberwise-risk profiles from independently trained models in different settings align closely after normalization to unit area. The resulting schedule deformations used in training also align, suggesting empirical universality across the evaluated models and settings. Pretrained-checkpoint diagnostics extend this normalized-risk agreement to larger conditional latent diffusion and 2-RF models. A frozen analytic allocation template retains most of the model-aware improvement without further risk estimation or model-specific fitting.

</details>


### [138] [AdamX: Cosine similarity meets gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11867)
*Francisco Caldas,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一阶优化器AdamX，利用余弦相似度自适应控制更新幅度，并引入方差校正机制以平滑早期训练，在多个基准数据集和架构上展现竞争性收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器在控制更新幅度方面存在局限，作者希望设计一种可扩展、模型无关且易于集成的一阶优化方法，同时改善训练早期的不稳定性。

Method: 提出AdamX优化器，将余弦相似度作为控制更新幅度的自适应机制；并引入方差校正方案（variance rectification）以促进训练早期更平滑的优化过程。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和架构上进行了实证评估，结果表明AdamX取得了具有竞争力的收敛速度。评价指标为固定超参数预算下达到预设性能阈值所需的epoch数。

Conclusion: AdamX是一种可扩展、模型无关、易于集成的优化器，在收敛效率上具有竞争力，代码与实验已开源。

Abstract: We introduce AdamX, a first-order optimizer that incorporates cosine similarity as an adaptive mechanism for controlling update magnitudes. The proposed method is scalable, model-agnostic, and straightforward to integrate into existing training pipelines. We further introduce a variance rectification scheme that promotes smoother optimization during the early stages of training. Overall, we provide empirical evidence that AdamX achieves competitive convergence rates across a range of benchmark datasets and architectures. Performance is evaluated in terms of the number of epochs required to reach predefined performance thresholds under a fixed hyperparameter budget. Code and Experiments available at: https://github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX.

</details>


### [139] [The Last AI Built by Humans: Toward Genuine Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11873)
*Yi Duan,Ying Liu,Zirui Tang,Haodong Chen,Jun Zhou,Yumou Liu,Bangrui Xu,Yukai Wu,Sidi Chen,Yuhan Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyou Yu,Shaokun Han,Xuzhou Zhu,Le Zhou,Bolin Lu,Wei Zhou,Jiachen Liu,Nuozhou Fang,Jiaxin Tian,Ruoyu Chen,Yuxuan Li,Kai Zuo,Kaiyan Zhang,Jiantao Qiu,Conghui He,Guoliang Li,Bowen Zhou,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhoufutu Wen,Jihua Kang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出递归自我改进（RSI）概念，用Headroom-Closed Index（HCI）揭示现有LLM的局限，并给出从执行自主到递归元改进的发展路线图，结合多场景与产业实践分析实现真正RSI的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽能利用经验与反馈，但难以将经验转化为持久性改进，缺乏对自身改进过程的改进能力，因此需要引入并系统化研究递归自我改进（RSI）。

Method: 先以Headroom-Closed Index（HCI）诊断现有LLM的问题；随后界定RSI概念与路线图（改进-执行自主、改进策略自主、经验获取自主、环境适应自主、递归元改进）；再跨场景（科学发现、具身智能、软件工程等）比较需求与演进速度；并结合产业实践与初步实证证据，将RSI研究与实际系统对接。
