Result: HCI揭示了现有LLM存在的问题；构建了RSI的概念框架与五阶段发展路线图；识别出不同应用场景对RSI的差异化需求与发展速度；并通过产业实践与初步实证证据建立RSI研究与实际系统的联系。

Conclusion: 实现真正的RSI仍面临关键挑战，需要在多场景、多层次的自主性提升中逐步推进，并将研究框架与产业系统实践相结合。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables AI systems to turn experience and feedback into persistent changes that improve both their capabilities and the process of future improvement. We first use the Headroom-Closed Index (HCI) to reveal the problems of existing LLMs, then introduce the RSI concept and its development roadmap: from improvement-execution autonomy, improvement-strategy autonomy, experience-acquisition autonomy, and environment-adaptation autonomy, to recursive meta-improvement. Next we examine RSI across scenarios (e.g., scientific discovery, embodied intelligence, software engineering), highlighting their distinct requirements and development speeds. Drawing on diverse industry practices and preliminary empirical evidence, we connect RSI research with practical systems and identify key challenges to achieving genuine RSI.

</details>


### [140] [CoRA-NAS: Coarse Ranking and Anchor-Residual Refinement for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11884)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoRA-NAS两阶段框架，用静态代理排序先验加低成本的锚点-残差学习曲线修正，实现跨搜索空间稳健的神经网络架构排序与选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零成本代理（zero-cost proxies）能以很低代价对架构排序，但其可靠性在不同搜索空间之间差异大，缺乏跨空间稳健性。

Method: CoRA-NAS包含两阶段：CoRA-Rank通过对容量代理与初始化结构代理做等权对数秩共识与无目标共识门控，形成静态排序先验；CoRA-Refine在该先验上采样锚点，外推其早期验证曲线，并用ExtraTrees模型传播学习到的残差修正，成本约为完整训练候选集的1%。排序器不使用完整训练的架构-精度标签拟合，且用同一配置跨空间，仅架构编码按空间定制。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201、NAS-Bench-101、TransNAS-Bench-101、NATS-SSS上，CoRA-Refine平均Spearman相关系数分别为0.946、0.715、0.786、0.894；最差空间相关系数0.715为对比方法中最高。在NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100上所选架构达73.32%，接近真实最优73.37%。在纯尺寸空间中，修正弥补了静态先验相对参数量指标的差距，同时与最强容量代理在噪声范围内持平。

Conclusion: 该框架兼顾跨空间排序稳健性与低成本架构选择，在多个基准上取得领先的最差空间表现和接近最优的精度。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies rank architectures cheaply, but their reliability varies across search spaces. We introduce CoRA-NAS (COarse Ranking + Anchor-residual), a two-stage framework combining a static ranking prior with low-cost learning-curve refinement. CoRA-Rank aggregates capacity and structure-at-initialization proxies through an equal-weight log-rank consensus and a target-free consensus gate. CoRA-Refine samples anchors across this prior, extrapolates their early validation curves, and propagates a learned residual correction with an ExtraTrees model. The refinement uses approximately 1% of the cost of fully training the candidate set. Fully trained architecture-accuracy labels are not used to fit the ranker. One configuration is used across spaces, with space-specific architecture encodings. Across NAS-Bench-201, NAS-Bench-101, TransNAS-Bench-101, and NATS-SSS, CoRA-Refine achieves mean Spearman correlations of 0.946, 0.715, 0.786, and 0.894, respectively. Its worst-space correlation of 0.715 is the highest among the compared methods. On NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100, its selected architecture reaches 73.32% accuracy, near the reported ground-truth best of 73.37%. On the pure size space, refinement recovers the static prior's shortfall relative to parameter count, while remaining tied with the strongest capacity proxies within noise. The resulting framework combines cross-space ranking robustness with low-cost architecture selection.

</details>


### [141] [CausalArena: Benchmarking Causal Discovery in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11897)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一且可演化的因果发现基准 CausalArena，整合四类 SCM 家族与统一评测协议，揭示不同方法在基准间排名显著漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的评测高度依赖结构因果模型（SCM），但既有研究在图族、机制与评测协议上差异巨大，结果难以横向比较；因果发现基础模型（CDFM）的出现进一步加剧问题，其表现可能源于预训练环境与测试 SCM 的重叠而非真实发现能力，导致固定合成基准上的结果难以解释。

Method: CausalArena 采用统一协议，构建可演化的基准体系，包含四类互补 SCM：（1）合成 SCM，覆盖结构与机制的受控多样性；（2）语义操作 SCM，提供可人工审查、语义有据的环境；（3）公式驱动 SCM，检验显式科学机制下的发现；（4）公开真实数据集，作为外部效度检验。评测覆盖经典方法、神经方法与预训练方法。

Result: 实验显示方法在不同 SCM 家族与协议间出现显著排名漂移，某一基准情境下的强劲表现无法可靠迁移到其他情境。

Conclusion: 基准多样性与预训练—评测重叠是基础模型时代因果发现评测的核心挑战，CausalArena 为统一、可扩展的评测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from data and is fundamental to scientific reasoning and intervention-based decision making. Its evaluation relies heavily on structural causal models (SCMs), which specify a causal graph together with the mechanisms that generate data, yet existing studies differ substantially in graph families, mechanisms, and evaluation protocols. The emergence of causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) further complicates evaluation: performance may reflect not only causal discovery ability, but also overlap between pretraining environments and test SCMs, making results on fixed synthetic benchmarks difficult to interpret. We introduce CausalArena, a unified and evolvable benchmark for causal discovery under a common protocol. Synthetic SCMs supply controlled breadth over structures and mechanisms; semantic operational SCMs provide human-auditable, semantically grounded environments beyond standard synthetic generators; and formula-grounded SCMs test discovery under explicit scientific mechanisms. Public real-world datasets provide an additional external-validity check. Experiments across classical, neural, and pretrained methods reveal substantial ranking shifts across SCM families and protocols, showing that strong performance in one benchmark regime does not reliably transfer to others. These results highlight benchmark diversity and pretraining--evaluation overlap as central challenges for evaluating causal discovery in the foundation model era.

</details>


### [142] [TART: A Modular Tool for Technique-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11904)
*Akshaj Gupta,Hwi Joo Park,Andrea Guzman,Shamak Gowda,Samhita Konduri,Jiachen Lian,Robin Netzorg,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TART：模块化四阶段音频到吉他六线谱流水线，首次实现直接从吉他音频生成带指法与演奏技巧标注的六线谱，零样本评测平均音频到MIDI F50达81.35%、弦品Tab F1达71.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有吉他自动音乐转录（AMT）受限于三大问题：难以捕捉滑音、推弦、打击性发声等表现性技巧；易将音符错误分配到弦-品组合；且多在干净录音上训练，对含噪真实音频泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出TART，模块化四阶段音频到六线谱流水线：（1）音频到MIDI转录模型；（2）表现性技巧分类器；（3）音频条件化的T5编码器-解码器用于弦-品分配；（4）自动六线谱生成器。在GuitarSet、EGDB及两个增强基准Noisy GuitarSet、Noisy EGDB上进行零样本评测。

Result: 四个基准平均：音频到MIDI F50为81.35%（较最优先前基线+6.67点）；弦-品Tab F1为71.8%（较最优先前基线+8.5点）；端到端Tab F1为54.08%。

Conclusion: TART是首个可直接从吉他音频生成同时含指法与表现性技巧标注的六线谱框架，在零样本与含噪条件下均显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Automatic Music Transcription (AMT) for guitar remains limited by three challenges: existing systems often fail to capture expressive techniques such as slides, bends, and percussive hits; they often assign notes to incorrect string-fret combinations; and they are typically trained on clean recordings, limiting their generalization to noisy real-world audio. To address these challenges, we propose TART, a modular four-stage audio-to-tablature pipeline consisting of (1) an audio-to-MIDI transcription model, (2) an expressive technique classifier, (3) an audio-conditioned T5 encoder-decoder for string-fret assignment, and (4) an automated tablature generator. We evaluate TART in a zero-shot setting on GuitarSet, EGDB, and two augmented benchmarks, Noisy GuitarSet and Noisy EGDB. Averaged across these four benchmarks, TART achieves 81.35% audio-to-MIDI F50 (+6.67 points over the best prior baseline), 71.8% string-fret Tab F1 (+8.5 points over the best prior baseline), and 54.08% end-to-end Tab F1. To our knowledge, TART is the first framework to generate guitar tablature with both fingering and expressive technique annotations directly from guitar audio.

</details>


### [143] [From Protocols to Evidence: Bounded Claims for AI in Service of the Common Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11910)
*Nitesh V. Chawla,Paulo Benanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"断裂测试"（rupture test），将制度基线纳入AI系统评估，区分"证据有界的部署"与"测量有界的治理"，并以RISE AI架构在限定范围内就责任、包容、安全与赋权作出有界、基于证据的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI不仅是治理难题，更会暴露制度在响应性、归属感、关怀与问责方面的既有失败；AI一旦部署即成为对这些制度状况的干预，可能修复、加剧、替代或掩盖这些问题。因此负责任AI需同时评估系统本身与其所嵌入的制度断裂。

Method: 先梳理从原则到协议的现有实践（EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001及各类保障实践），指出其实际确立的内容、未被触及的权力结构及测量的边界；再借助Pope Leo XIV《Magnifica Humanitas》的尊严、技术权力与共同善的道德框架，构建连接制度基线与系统评估的"断裂测试"，并提出RISE AI架构。

Result: 提出"证据有界的部署"（将主张限制在实际已评估的范围）与"测量有界的治理"（记录有利证据也无法推翻的约束）两个区分；RISE AI架构可在上述限制内就Responsibility、Inclusivity、Safety、Empowerment作出有界且基于证据的主张。

Conclusion: 负责任AI需要更好的工程实践、制度修复，以及持续的道德与政治判断，三者缺一不可。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence does more than create a governance problem. It can also reveal where institutions have already failed to provide responsiveness, belonging, care, and accountability. Once deployed, AI becomes an intervention in those conditions. It can repair, compound, substitute for, or conceal the failures it encounters. Responsible AI must therefore evaluate both the system and the institutional rupture into which it is introduced. The move from principles to protocols is already underway. The EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF, ISO/IEC 42001, and assurance practices translate commitments into roles, requirements, records, oversight, and assessment. The harder questions are what these protocols actually establish, whose power they leave untouched, and where measurement must stop. Pope Leo XIV's Magnifica Humanitas provides a broader moral frame centered on dignity, technological power, and the common good. Drawing on that frame, we develop a rupture test that links institutional baselines to system evaluation. We distinguish evidence-bounded deployment, which limits claims to what has actually been evaluated, from measurement-bounded governance, which records constraints that favorable evidence cannot override. Within those limits, RISE AI provides an architecture for making bounded, evidence-based claims about Responsibility, Inclusivity, Safety, and Empowerment. Responsible AI requires better engineering, institutional repair, and continued moral and political judgment.

</details>


### [144] [General Quantification of Covariate and Concept Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11918)
*Hongbo Chen,Li Charlie Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出已有概念漂移定义在源域与目标域支撑不匹配时失效，借助熵最优传输提出 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移，建立统一协变量与 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移的泛化误差界，并给出可估计器与 DataShifts 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布漂移下的泛化仍是核心难题，但现有学习界理论局限于狭窄且理想化的设定，且无法从样本中估计，理论与实践之间存在明显鸿沟。

Method: 首先揭示源域与目标域支撑不匹配时现有概念漂移定义的失效问题；利用熵最优传输定义 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移；推导统一协变量漂移与 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移的通用误差界；进一步构造带集中保证的漂移估计器，并提出 DataShifts 算法以量化分布漂移并估计误差界。

Result: 所提误差界适用于广泛的损失函数、标签空间与随机标注设定；漂移估计器具有集中保证；DataShifts 可在多数应用中量化分布漂移并估计误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布漂移下的学习误差分析提供了严谨且通用的工具，弥合了理论与实际应用之间的差距。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a core challenge in modern machine learning, yet existing learning bound theory is limited to narrow, idealized settings and is non-estimable from samples. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theory and practical applications. We first show that existing definition of concept shift breaks when the source and target supports mismatch. Leveraging entropic optimal transport, we propose a key notion: $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, and derive a general error bound unifying covariate and $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, which applies to broad loss functions, label spaces, and stochastic labeling. We further develop estimators for these shifts with concentration guarantees, and the DataShifts algorithm, which can quantify distribution shifts and estimate the error bound in most applications - a rigorous and general tool for analyzing learning error under distribution shift.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Biology-in-the-loop: Amortized Adaptive Hit Discovery in CRISPR Screens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11877)
*Carl Edwards,Edward De Brouwer,Xiner Li,Namkyeong Lee,Ehsan Hajiramezanali,Anne Biton,Sara Mostafavi,Gabriele Scalia*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模自适应命中发现基准AssayBench-Loop（含1,389个CRISPR筛选、五大表型类别），并基于此构建AssayLoop框架：结合跨历史筛选训练的transformer摊销采集策略AssayFormer与LLM生物先验的自适应交接，同时提出AssayLLM；在时序留出筛选上实现5.67倍于随机的富集，并在仅检测约5%候选库时回收27.7%的命中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物发现中许多问题需要在预算约束下顺序选择实验，CRISPR筛选是典型场景：穷举式扰动测试往往不可行，候选扰动需在多轮实验中被优先排序。然而现有自适应命中发现基准在规模与多样性上均受限，难以系统性评估并支撑策略学习，因此需要更大规模、更具多样性的基准来学习采集策略。

Method: 构建AssayBench-Loop基准，涵盖五大表型类别、共1,389个CRISPR筛选；在此基础上提出AssayLoop顺序实验设计框架，将基于transformer的摊销采集策略AssayFormer（跨历史筛选训练、依据实验反馈自适应）与LLM衍生的生物先验通过自适应交接机制结合，把已完成实验作为训练数据来学习证据积累如何指导下一步测试，由LLM提供先验知识以启动搜索；进一步提出AssayLLM，通过任务特定的后训练将该原则直接扩展到LLM。

Result: 在时序留出的筛选上，AssayLoop相对随机选择实现5.67倍富集，在检测约5%候选库后回收27.7%的命中，优于现有自适应设计方法、独立LLM以及单独使用的AssayFormer；性能随历史训练数据增加而提升，并可迁移到训练中未包含的表型类别。

Conclusion: 结果表明，跨历史实验学习采集策略并将其与广泛的生物先验相结合，对高效的自适应命中发现具有重要价值。

Abstract: Many biological discovery problems require experiments to be selected sequentially under constrained budgets. CRISPR screening is a prominent example, as exhaustive perturbation testing is often infeasible and candidate perturbations must instead be prioritized over multiple experimental rounds. Despite the importance of this problem, existing benchmarks for adaptive hit discovery remain limited in scale and diversity. Here, we introduce AssayBench-Loop, a large-scale benchmark for adaptive hit discovery comprising 1,389 CRISPR screens across five phenotype categories. Beyond enabling systematic evaluation, its scale makes it possible to learn acquisition strategies across historical experiments. Building on this resource, we introduce AssayLoop, a sequential experimental design framework combining AssayFormer, a transformer-based amortized acquisition policy trained across historical screens to adapt from experimental feedback, with LLM-derived biological priors through an adaptive handoff. In this view, completed experiments become training data for learning how accumulated evidence should guide what to test next, while LLMs provide prior biological knowledge to seed the search. We further introduce AssayLLM, showing that the same principle can be extended directly to an LLM through task-specific post-training. On temporally held-out screens, AssayLoop achieves a 5.67-fold enrichment over random selection and recovers 27.7% of hits after assaying approximately 5% of the candidate library, outperforming existing adaptive-design methods and standalone LLMs, and AssayFormer alone. Performance improves with increasing historical training data and transfers to phenotype categories excluded from training. These results demonstrate the value of learning acquisition policies across historical experiments and combining them with broad biological priors for efficient adaptive hit discovery.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Threshold Choice, Not Sample Size, Bounds Trustless Verification of Nondeterministic Compound AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10601)
*Alper Alimoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个覆盖三重威胁（输出非确定性、节点可能不诚实、共享记录间歇不可用）的复现验证协议，其核心结论是：在固定度量与重执行离散度下，真正的瓶颈是可调阈值而非样本量k，按执行导出的阈值显著优于最佳常数阈值，但该规则仅为测量结果、尚未部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI流水线（链式LLM调用、检索器、工具）本质非确定：采样、模型更新、工具响应波动导致同一输入跨运行输出不同。这类流水线日益跨边缘、云与轨道节点运行，归属多方、任何单一主体都无法完整控制；其优化过程会在检查前丢弃中间结果。在此环境中验证"复现"必须同时容忍三点：非确定输出、节点可能不诚实上报、共享记录仅间歇可访问。现有工作最多同时覆盖其中两点，故需要同时覆盖三点的协议。

Method: 提出协议：对每一阶段的输入、输出与上下文提交摘要（digest），并以策略摘要（policy digest）固定度量与阈值；摘要锚定过程不信任执行节点；在分区情况下推迟处理；对挑战的裁决基于k次重执行的中位数，且不设法定人数（quorum）。核心分析聚焦于该流程失效之处：对比固定常数阈值与按每次执行导出的阈值，在固定度量与该流水线重执行离散度的条件下考察阈值与样本量的约束关系。实验基于合成的HotpotQA流水线，包含诚实复现对、发散对、同输入伪造（same-input fabrication）以及针对协议构造的攻击者。

Result: 在合成HotpotQA流水线上：经校准的固定阈值在45个诚实复现中接受44个、在105个发散对中拒绝104个，但在k=5时29次试验中有27次放行同输入伪造；增加样本无济于事，因为采样只会锐化估计而不移动其位置。固定该度量与该流水线的重执行离散度后，约束来自阈值而非样本量：按执行导出的阈值在29例中检出19例，而匹配到相同零诚实拒绝的最优常数阈值仅达9例；该导出阈值在k=5时不拒绝任何诚实承诺（k=3时为15中3），并对针对它构造的攻击者检出15中11，而该攻击者必须瞄准一个仅在其承诺存在之后才抽取的目标。

Conclusion: 在非确定复合AI流水线的跨主体复现验证中，同时容忍非确定输出、不诚实节点与间歇性记录访问是可实现的，协议通过摘要承诺、策略绑定、免信任锚定、分区推迟与基于k次重执行中位数的无quorum裁决来覆盖这三点。主要发现是失效根源：在固定度量与重执行离散度下，决定检出力的是阈值而非样本量k，采样规模无法弥补阈值不当。按执行导出的阈值优于最佳常数阈值，但仍非完备（29中检出19，对自适应攻击者15中检出11）。该规则是测量结果而非已部署方案。

Abstract: Compound AI pipelines chain LLM calls, retrievers, and tools and are nondeterministic: sampling, model updates, and volatile tool responses make one input yield different outputs across runs. Such pipelines increasingly run across edge, cloud, and orbital nodes owned by no single party, whose optimizations discard intermediate results before inspection. Verifying reproduction there means tolerating nondeterministic outputs, a node that may not report honestly, and intermittent access to any shared record; existing work addresses at most two at once. We give a protocol covering all three: it commits digests of each stage's inputs, outputs, and context under a policy digest pinning the metric and threshold, anchors them without trusting the executing node, defers under partition, and decides a challenge on the median of $k$ re-executions, with no quorum. Where that procedure breaks is the main result. On a synthetic HotpotQA pipeline a calibrated fixed threshold accepts 44 of 45 honest reproductions and rejects 104 of 105 divergent pairs, yet lets same-input fabrication through in 27 of 29 trials at k=5, more samples being no help since sampling sharpens an estimate without moving it. Holding that metric and this pipeline's re-execution spread fixed, the binding constraint is the threshold rather than the sample size: one derived per execution detects 19 of 29 where the best constant matched to the same zero honest rejections reaches 9, rejects no honest commitment at k=5 though 3 of 15 at k=3, and catches 11 of 15 of an attacker built against it, which has to aim at a target drawn only after its commitment exists. That rule is measured rather than deployed.

</details>


### [147] [Adaptive Diffusion Freezing: Privacy-preserving Diffusion Models Against Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10608)
*Jialu Guo,Xiao Han,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出自适应扩散冻结(ADF)框架，通过跨时间步自适应冻结训练，在隐私、效用与效率间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成任务中表现优异，但其训练过程存在显著隐私隐患，尤其易受成员推理攻击(MIA)。现有隐私保护方法无法平衡隐私、效用与效率。

Method: 提出ADF框架，利用跨时间步自适应冻结训练，通过掩码矩阵显式控制不同数据子集在各扩散时间步的参与，减少过度记忆，使成员与非成员样本的模型行为更一致；并设计基于预训练的风险感知冻结策略，依据记忆倾向估计MIA风险，抑制高风险"子集-时间步"对的贡献。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估表明，ADF具有有效的防御性能，且相比于多种基线方法取得了最先进的隐私-效用-效率权衡表现。

Conclusion: ADF通过自适应冻结机制有效防御成员推理攻击，同时兼顾生成质量与训练效率，实现了隐私、效用与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative tasks across various areas, however their training process raises significant privacy concerns, particularly under membership inference attacks (MIAs). Prior studies on privacy-preserving of diffusion models fail to balance privacy, utility, and efficiency. To address this gap, we propose a novel framework of privacy-preserving diffusion models, Adaptive Diffusion Freezing (ADF), which can defend against MIAs with better trade-off. By leveraging cross-timestep adaptive freezing training, ADF explicitly control the participation of different data subsets across diffusion timesteps via a mask matrix, which reduces the over-memorization and leads to more uniform model behaviors between member and nonmember samples. To construct a freezing mask matrix that effectively reduce membership leakage without unnecessarily harming generation quality, we introduce a pretraining-based risk-aware freezing policy to estimate MIA risk based on memorization tendency, and suppress the contribution of the subset-timestep pairs with higher risk. Evaluations on multiple datasets demonstrate that ADF provides effective defense performance as well as state-of-the-art privacy-utility-efficiency trade-off performance compared to various baselines.

</details>


### [148] [Black-Box Membership Inference via Word-Level Probability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10611)
*Shengjie Niu,Yeheng Ge,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出WPMIA，一种仅依赖文本续写的严格黑盒成员推断攻击方法，通过蒙特卡洛采样与局部核平滑估计词级生成概率，并聚合为序列级似然，在开源与专有LLM上均优于现有黑盒基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断攻击（MIA）多依赖逐token的logits或概率，无法适用于仅暴露文本续写的专有LLM，导致严格黑盒场景下的隐私审计研究不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现对严格黑盒LLM的隐私风险审计。

Method: WPMIA（词级概率MIA）：1）通过蒙特卡洛采样结合局部核平滑估计词级生成概率；2）将词级估计聚合为序列级似然估计量；3）在不同前缀条件下构造条件似然，以放大成员与非成员之间的分布差异。

Result: 在多个开源LLM上，WPMIA持续优于现有黑盒基线；在GPT-5-Chat、Gemini-2.5-Flash和Claude-4.5-Haiku等专有LLM上，平均达到TPR@5%FPR为42.0。

Conclusion: WPMIA为严格黑盒成员推断提供了统计原理可靠的方法基础，可为未来相关研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have emerged as critical tools for auditing privacy risks in large language models (LLMs), aiming to determine whether a given text was included in a model's training corpus. However, most existing MIAs require access to per-token logits or probabilities, making them inapplicable in practice to proprietary LLMs that expose only textual continuations. To address this underexplored setting, we propose Word-level Probability MIA (WPMIA), a statistically principled MIA for strict black-box privacy auditing. WPMIA estimates word-level generation probabilities via Monte Carlo sampling with local kernel smoothing, then aggregates these estimates into a sequence-level likelihood estimator. Furthermore, WPMIA constructs the likelihood conditioned on different prefixes, thereby amplifying the distributional differences between members and non-members. We evaluate WPMIA across various open-source LLMs and find that it consistently outperforms existing black-box baselines. Importantly, we also evaluate WPMIA on modern proprietary LLMs, including GPT-5-Chat, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Claude-4.5-Haiku, achieving an average TPR@5\%FPR of 42.0 across these models. These results offer a sound foundation for future research on strict black-box membership inference. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}.

</details>


### [149] [PEARL: A Task-Aware Framework for Evaluating Differentially Private Synthetic Educational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10612)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出PEARL审计框架，要求DP合成教育数据同时通过有效性、隐私保护、预测有用性和教学任务适用性四类检查，96种设置中仅12种通过；通过隐私与预测检查的数据仍存在群体间公平性差异，且知识追踪模型无法从任何合成数据中学到有效的作答序列模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化学习系统依赖包含表现、行为和人口学信息的真实学习者数据，这些数据高度隐私敏感。差分隐私（DP）合成数据可在支持系统开发与教育研究的同时降低个体学习者暴露风险。然而现有评估将隐私性与预测有用性分开考量，未判断合成学习者数据对既定个性化学习任务是否仍然可用。

Method: 提出PEARL（Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger），仅在DP合成教育数据集通过有效性、隐私保护、预测有用性以及面向既定教育任务的适用性等全部必要检查时才予批准，并记录每个被拒数据集的具体失败原因。实验覆盖96种研究设置，每种设置由数据集、数据生成方法、隐私预算和随机种子定义。

Result: 在96种设置中，仅12种生成的合成数据集通过了所有适用的PEARL检查。许多经隐私保护的数据集因遗漏重要结果群体（如退课学生）或无法保留学习活动顺序而被拒绝。公平性分析进一步显示，部分通过隐私与预测有用性检查的数据集，在按残疾状况和社会经济背景划分的群体间仍产生不均衡的高风险预测性能。此外，深度知识追踪（DKT）和自注意力知识追踪（SAKT）未能从任何被测合成知识追踪数据中学到有意义的下一作答模式。

Conclusion: 仅靠隐私保护不足以保证合成数据对辍学预测、知识追踪或自适应辅导等任务的有用性。PEARL式联合审计（有效性、隐私、预测有用性、任务适用性与公平性）对DP合成教育数据的可发布性判定是必要的，可避免在个性化学习场景中误用看似合格的数据集。

Abstract: Personalized learning systems rely on real learner data, including performance, behavior, and demographic information, but these data are highly privacy-sensitive. Differentially private (DP) synthetic data can support system development and educational research while reducing exposure of individual learners. Existing evaluations, however, assess privacy and predictive usefulness separately, without determining whether synthetic learner data remain usable for the intended personalized learning task. We introduce PEARL (Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger), which approves a DP synthetic educational dataset only when it passes all required checks of validity, privacy protection, predictive usefulness, and suitability for the intended educational task, while recording why each rejected dataset fails. Across 96 study settings, each defined by a dataset, data-generation method, privacy budget, and random seed, only 12 produced synthetic datasets that passed all applicable PEARL checks. Many privacy-protected datasets were rejected for omitting important outcome groups, such as withdrawn students, or for failing to preserve the order of learning activities. Fairness analysis further showed that some datasets passing privacy and predictive-usefulness checks still yielded unequal at-risk prediction performance across groups defined by disability and socioeconomic background. Moreover, Deep Knowledge Tracing and Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing learned no meaningful next-response patterns from any tested synthetic knowledge-tracing dataset, showing that privacy protection alone does not guarantee usefulness for dropout prediction, knowledge tracing, or adaptive tutoring.

</details>


### [150] [SoK: Privacy Attacks on Machine Learning via Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10627)
*Abdullah Caglar Oksuz,Anisa Halimi,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文系统综述了25项利用模型解释进行模型窃取、成员推理和模型反演（属性推理视为部分反演）的研究，提出将“模型知识”与“解释获取方式”分离，并划分五条解释获取路径，指出解释隐私风险取决于具体信号、获取路径、目标资产与攻击者先验知识，主张以端到端披露问题评估解释隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习解释在提升透明度的同时，暴露了预测之外的信息，形成针对模型机密性和数据隐私的攻击面。现有研究常仅以黑盒/白盒标签区分，掩盖了解释信号到达攻击者的方式差异，因此需要系统化梳理攻击面。

Method: 对25项利用解释进行模型窃取、成员推理与模型反演（属性推理视为部分反演）的研究进行系统化综述；将模型知识与解释获取分离，识别五条获取路径：目标发布、攻击者导出、二次披露、特权访问、发布全局工件；并比较系统与威胁模型、解释信号、辅助知识、目标模型、模态、查询预算、评估指标、报告性能与防御。

Result: 跨路径分析表明：解释可降低模型窃取成本；通过解释统计量、救济距离（recourse distance）与解释引导的鲁棒性暴露成员信号；并支持对私有输入进行空间或代数重构。比较显示没有任何解释族是普遍不安全的，也没有任何防御是普遍有效的。

Conclusion: 解释隐私风险取决于暴露了何种信号、如何获取、针对何种资产以及攻击者已掌握的信息。应将解释隐私作为端到端披露问题来评估，并使防御措施与具体获取路径和被保护资产相匹配。

Abstract: Machine learning explanations reveal model behavior beyond predictions, creating attack surfaces for model confidentiality and data privacy. We systematize 25 studies that exploit explanations for model extraction, membership inference, and model inversion, treating attribute inference as partial inversion. Existing work is often labeled only black- or white-box, obscuring substantial differences in what explanation signal reaches an adversary. We therefore separate model knowledge from explanation acquisition and identify five paths: target-released, attacker-derived, secondary disclosure, privileged access, and released global artifacts. Across these paths, explanations reduce extraction cost, expose membership signals through explanation statistics, recourse distance, and explanation-guided robustness, and support spatial or algebraic reconstruction of private inputs. We compare system and threat models, explanation signals, auxiliary knowledge, target models, modalities, query budgets, evaluation metrics, reported performance, and defenses. Our analysis shows that no explanation family is uniformly unsafe and no defense is uniformly effective. Risk depends on which signal is exposed, how it is acquired, which asset is targeted, and what the attacker already knows. We argue that explanation privacy should therefore be evaluated as an end-to-end disclosure problem, with defenses matched to the acquisition path and protected asset.

</details>


### [151] [From Cycle Space to Cycle Manifold: Limits and Achievability of Blind False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10631)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在直流支路潮流模型下证明：无噪正交检验的残差敏感子空间恰为加权回路空间，其正交补即完整的隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击空间，因此加权回路空间知识是完整盲攻击的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有盲虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）方法从低秩测量子空间的代数视角学习攻击空间，但未阐明使攻击隐蔽的物理电网约束，也未说明恢复完整攻击空间所需的最小信息量。

Method: 在连通的直流（DC）支路潮流模型下，分析无噪正交检验的残差敏感子空间结构；据此提出一个计算上无约束的基准方法与一种可处理的仅测量重构方法；进一步在交流（AC）情形下用回路流形刻画可行的支路P/Q测量，并在GPU上实现拓扑辅助的流形拟合与测量生成。

Result: 理论上证明残差敏感子空间等于加权回路空间，其正交补为完整隐蔽攻击空间；该空间仅能将拓扑辨识至2-同构，相对回路-边参数在每个双连通分量内仅辨识到一个尺度，桥参数既不可辨识也非必需。实验在IEEE系统上以95%标称接受阈值比较BDD绕过率与状态影响。交流扩展中，无损定压小角度极限下有功功率切片的法空间退化为DC加权回路空间。

Conclusion: 加权回路空间知识是完整盲FDIA的充要条件，并给出了隐蔽性的物理刻画与最小信息需求；所提基准与仅测量重构方法及AC回路流形扩展为实际攻击构造与防御提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A false data injection attack (FDIA) can change the estimated grid state while evading a residual-based bad data detector (BDD). Existing blind attacks learn a low-rank measurement subspace, but this algebraic view does not state the physical grid constraints that make an attack stealthy or the minimum information needed to recover the complete attack space. Under the connected direct-current (DC) branch-flow model, we show that the residual-sensitive subspace of the noiseless orthogonal test is exactly the weighted cycle space. Its orthogonal complement is therefore the complete stealthy attack space, making weighted cycle-space knowledge both necessary and sufficient for complete blind FDIA. This space identifies the topology only up to 2-isomorphism and the relative cycle-edge parameters only up to one scale per biconnected component; bridge parameters are neither identified nor required. We then formulate a computationally unconstrained benchmark and a tractable measurement-only reconstruction method. Experiments on IEEE systems compare BDD bypass rate at a 95% nominal-acceptance threshold against state impact. As a compact alternating-current (AC) extension, we characterize feasible branch P/Q measurements by a cycle manifold and demonstrate topology-assisted manifold fitting and measurement generation on a graphics processing unit (GPU). In the lossless fixed-voltage small-angle limit, the normal space of the active-power slice reduces to the DC weighted cycle space.

</details>


### [152] [HermiCache: Enclave-Aware Cache Replacement for Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10634)
*Oussama Elmnaouri,Pascal Cotret,Vianney Lapôtre,Loïc Lagadec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 HermiCache，一种面向 RISC-V 的确定性细粒度缓存侧信道防护方案，并在集成 Keystone TEE 的 CVA6 核心上实现，面积开销约 6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE 虽能保护 enclave 内存免受不可信软件访问，但由于共享微架构资源，仍易受基于缓存的侧信道攻击。现有对策（如缓存分区或随机化）在需要细粒度配置与确定性保护时并不理想。

Method: 设计 HermiCache，针对 RISC-V 核心，并在 OpenHwGroup CVA6 核心上实现，软件层采用 Keystone TEE。

Result: 在处理器核心上的面积开销为 6%。

Conclusion: HermiCache 满足细粒度配置与确定性保护的需求，为 RISC-V TEE 场景提供了一种低面积开销的缓存侧信道防护方案。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) protect enclave memory from untrusted software but remain vulnerable to cache-based side-channel attacks due to shared microarchitectural resources. Existing countermeasures use techniques such as cache partitioning or randomization: these solutions are not ideal if a designer wants fine-grained configurations and a deterministic protection. In this paper, we introduce HermiCache which is an answer to these requirements. HermiCache is designed for RISC-V cores and has been implemented in the OpenHwGroup CVA6 core with a Keystone TEE for the software layer. The solution has an area overhead of 6% on the processor core.

</details>


### [153] [Architecting the Secure AI-SOC: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Pipeline Integrity and Threat Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10707)
*Anna Gazani,Spyridon Kounoupidis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Nikolaos Kekatos,Grigorios Tsoumakas,Georgios Koutidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号（neurosymbolic）纵深防御架构，通过SIEM确定性预过滤层与NeMo Guardrails语义验证层的协同，并集成闭环遥测与HITL可视化，抵御SOCs中通过日志投毒实施的间接提示注入与"promptware"杀伤链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM集成到安全运营中心（SOC）虽能提升威胁情报处理效率，却引入了间接提示注入（经日志投毒）等关键漏洞：攻击者可在系统日志中嵌入恶意载荷，劫持LLM的运行逻辑，执行多步"promptware"杀伤链。现有防护存在两难：确定性防御计算高效但语义盲区明显；纯神经网络评估则延迟过高且具概率性缺陷。因此需要一种兼顾效率、语义覆盖与可验证性的端到端防护方案。

Method: 提出神经符号纵深防御架构，保障端到端流水线完整性。第一层：定制化SIEM解码器作为确定性预过滤器，在数据摄入边缘进行即时结构化净化，消除体量填充（volumetric padding）与基于签名的注入。第二层：利用NeMo Guardrails在LLM处理前对结构化SIEM告警执行自检式语义边界验证。此外，框架集成闭环遥测系统，在SOC仪表盘内为被拦截攻击提供HITL可视化。

Result: 以MITRE ATLAS分类体系为映射开展综合实验评估，针对多种提示注入进行测试。结果表明，该协同方法能有效瓦解promptware杀伤链，以可验证约束限定LLM随机性，并为下一代AI-SOC提供具备弹性与高可观测性的防御机制。

Conclusion: 神经符号纵深防御可将确定性净化与语义护栏相结合，在保证低延迟的同时弥补语义盲区，显著提升AI驱动SOC应对日志投毒型间接提示注入的鲁棒性与可观测性。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Security Operations Centers (SOCs) streamlines threat intelligence but introduces critical vulnerabilities, notably indirect prompt injection via log poisoning. Adversaries exploit this vector to execute multistep ``promptware'' kill chains by embedding malicious payloads within system logs to hijack the LLM's operational logic. Securing this pipeline presents a dichotomy: deterministic defenses are computationally efficient yet semantically blind, while purely neural evaluations introduce prohibitive latency and probabilistic flaws. To address this, we propose a novel neurosymbolic defense-in-depth architecture that ensures end-to-end pipeline integrity. The primary layer employs customized SIEM decoders as a deterministic pre-filter, performing immediate structural sanitization to neutralize volumetric padding and signature-based injections at the ingestion edge. The secondary layer leverages NeMo Guardrails to enforce strict semantic boundaries through self-checking validation on the structured SIEM alerts prior to LLM processing. Furthermore, the framework integrates a closed-loop telemetry system, providing critical Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) visibility into thwarted attacks directly within the SOC dashboard. We present a comprehensive experimental evaluation mapped to the MITRE ATLAS taxonomy, assessing the framework against diverse prompt injections. Our results demonstrate that this synergistic approach effectively dismantles the promptware kill chain - bounding LLM stochasticity with verifiable constraints, and delivering a resilient, highly observable defense mechanism for next-generation AI-SOCs.

</details>


### [154] [Temporal and Multimodal Deep Learning for Cyberattack Detection in LEO Satellite Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10746)
*Kyle Stein,Guillermo Francia,Eman El-Sheikh,Hossain Shahriar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于卫星专用数据集UNSW-IoTSAT，系统研究深度学习卫星网络攻击检测，提出子系统融合MLP与分层多模态Transformer架构，在防泄漏评估下达到最高91.66%准确率与85.63%宏F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨（LEO）卫星通信系统日益普及，亟需能检测复杂动态空间环境中网络攻击的智能方法。与地面网络入侵检测不同，卫星系统在射频链路、星载硬件和轨道运行中产生异构信息。然而现有方法多依赖地面入侵数据集，或对单个观测独立评估，难以捕捉LEO卫星特有的时序攻击行为。

Method: 基于新近提出的卫星专用数据集UNSW-IoTSAT开展深度学习网络攻击检测的系统性研究。设计保留硬件、轨道与射频信息的结构化学习架构，包括子系统融合MLP和分层多模态Transformer（同时建模跨子系统交互与时序演化）。评估防泄漏的行级与时序设置，并进行跨卫星泛化实验，以刻画模型架构与评估协议对检测性能的影响。

Result: 实验验证了结构化多模态建模与严格评估的价值，分层Transformer在防泄漏评估协议下取得最高91.66%准确率和85.63%宏F1。

Conclusion: 结构化多模态建模结合严谨的评估协议，可有效提升LEO卫星网络攻击检测性能，模型架构与评估协议设计对检测效果均有重要影响。

Abstract: The growing reliance on Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite communication systems has increased the need for intelligent methods capable of detecting cyberattacks across complex and dynamic space environments. Unlike conventional network intrusion detection, satellite systems generate heterogeneous information across radio-frequency (RF) links, onboard hardware, and orbital operations. However, many existing approaches either rely on terrestrial intrusion datasets or evaluate individual observations independently, limiting their ability to capture temporal attack behavior specific to LEO satellites. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of deep-learning-based cyberattack detection using the recently introduced satellite-specific UNSW-IoTSAT dataset. We investigate structured learning architectures that preserve hardware, orbital, and RF information, including a Subsystem-Fusion MLP and a hierarchical multimodal Transformer that models both cross-subsystem interactions and temporal evolution. We further evaluate leakage-resistant row-level and temporal settings, along with cross-satellite generalization, to characterize how model architecture and evaluation protocol influence satellite cyberattack detection. Experimental results demonstrate the value of structured multimodal modeling and rigorous evaluation, with the hierarchical Transformer achieving up to 91.66% accuracy and 85.63% macro F1 under the leakage-resistant evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [155] [Beyond Static Guarantees: Measuring the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail Gap in Security-Sensitive and LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10762)
*Jessica Pourleyli,Maitreyee Das Urmi,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"静态通过-动态失败"(SPDF)现象，并设计三阶段智能体流水线(静态扫描、LLM驱动的CWE推理、Docker沙箱内自主漏洞利用验证)。在654个经Bandit-Semgrep判定无误的样本中，动态验证在95个文件中确认或部分确认可利用性，综合流水线比率达14.53%。结果表明静态分析成功与运行时安全属于不同层级，而非可互换的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析因可扩展、可复现、低成本而被广泛用作安全门禁，但无法直接观测运行时漏洞利用行为。依赖对抗性输入、执行上下文或漏洞链的缺陷可能逃逸静态检查却仍可被利用，而"通过静态分析"常被视为安全的证据。作者希望揭示这种"静态通过却动态失败"的落差，并探索可扩展的补足手段。

Method: 提出Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail (SPDF)现象，并构建三阶段智能体流水线：(1)静态扫描(Bandit-Semgrep复合门禁)；(2)LLM驱动的CWE推理以识别候选漏洞；(3)在隔离Docker容器中进行自主漏洞利用验证。评测来自SecurityEval、RedCode、CyberNative三个数据集的1,355个Python样本。

Result: 在654个Bandit-Semgrep无发现的样本中，LLM检测阶段在235个文件里识别出394个候选漏洞；动态验证在95个文件中确认或部分确认可利用性，综合流水线比率为14.53%(约每7个静态干净样本中1个)。按数据集看，候选"文件-CWE对"的确认率分别为RedCode 33.7%、CyberNative 28.6%、SecurityEval 5.4%。部分高频确认类别(如CWE-338、CWE-916)既未被Bandit也未被打中Semgrep标记。

Conclusion: 静态分析成功与运行时安全是软件保障的两个层级，而非可互换的度量。该结论有望重塑AI生成代码及安全敏感代码的评估方式，提示需将动态利用验证作为静态门禁的补充层。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) fuel the quest for scalable methods to assess the security of generated and security-sensitive software. Static analysis is widely adopted as a scalable, reproducible, and inexpensive security gate, but cannot directly observe runtime exploit behaviour. Vulnerabilities dependent on adversarial inputs, execution context, or exploit chaining may evade static checks while remaining exploitable in practice, yet passing static analysis is often treated as evidence of secure behaviour. This paper introduces the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail (SPDF) phenomenon and a three-stage agentic pipeline combining static scanning, LLM-driven Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) reasoning, and autonomous exploit verification in isolated Docker containers. We evaluate 1,355 Python samples from SecurityEval, RedCode, and CyberNative datasets. Of the 654 samples producing no findings under the composite Bandit-Semgrep gate, the LLM detection stage identified 394 candidate vulnerabilities across 235 files. Dynamic verification confirmed or partially confirmed exploitability in 95 files, yielding an inclusive pipeline rate of 14.53% (roughly 1 in 7 statically clean samples). This rate represents the proportion of Bandit-Semgrep-clean samples for which the pipeline identified a candidate vulnerability and obtained runtime evidence supporting exploitability. Outcomes varied by dataset: among candidate file--CWE pairs, confirmed exploitability was 33.7% for RedCode, 28.6% for CyberNative, and 5.4% for SecurityEval. Several frequently confirmed classes, including CWE-338 and CWE-916, were flagged by neither Bandit nor Semgrep. These findings indicate that static-analysis success and runtime security are hierarchical layers of software assurance rather than interchangeable measures, and have the potential to reshape how AI-generated and security-sensitive code is evaluated.

</details>


### [156] [PHAT: PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11613)
*Guowei Yang,Farbin Fayza,Beren Aydoğan,Carlos A. Ríos Ocampo,Ayse K. Coskun,Ajay Joshi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PHAT，一种基于光寻址相变存储器（OPCM）的电光混合加速器，用于加速TFHE全同态加密中的FFT运算。通过OPCM存内计算FFT单元、twiddle-stationary数据流与调度机制，PHAT在四个真实TFHE负载上相比最先进ASIC加速器取得2.14×–5.10×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFHE支持任意操作但计算与通信开销高，特别是自举阶段的FFT运算，限制其实用性；传统电子加速器受工艺缩放与存储墙问题制约，难以获得足够吞吐。

Method: 提出PHAT电光加速器架构：采用OPCM构建存内计算FFT单元；设计面向OPCM的twiddle-stationary数据流；引入调度机制以最大化FFT单元利用率，并应对高精度模拟计算与OPCM编程高延迟/高能耗的挑战。

Result: 在四个真实TFHE工作负载上，PHAT相比最先进ASIC加速器实现2.14×–5.10×加速。

Conclusion: 该方案显著提升TFHE应用性能，为云计算中实用高效的同态加密铺平道路。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables secure computation on encrypted data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving applications in the cloud. Among various FHE schemes, FHE over the Torus (TFHE) stands out due to its support for arbitrary operations. However, its high computation and communication overhead, particularly in the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) operations required during bootstrapping, limits its practicality for real-world applications. Conventional electronic accelerators struggle to achieve sufficient throughput due to the limitations of technology scaling and the memory-wall problem.
  To address these challenges, we propose PHAT, a PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE leveraging Optically-addressed Phase-Change Memory (OPCM). OPCM-based processing-in-memory systems offer high computation and communication throughput, making them well-suited for accelerating FFT operations in TFHE. However, directly mapping FFT to OPCM presents challenges such as high-precision analog computation and the high latency and energy cost of programming OPCM cells. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel electro-photonic accelerator architecture optimized for TFHE, featuring OPCM-based FFT units, a twiddle-stationary dataflow tailored for OPCM, and a scheduling mechanism to maximize the utilization of the FFT units. PHAT delivers $2.14\times$--$5.10\times$ speedup across four real-world TFHE workloads against the state-of-the-art ASIC accelerator. Our approach significantly enhances the performance of TFHE applications, paving the way for practical and efficient homomorphic encryption in cloud computing.

</details>


### [157] [No-Box Vulnerability Analysis: Description-only Detection of Indirect Prompt Injection Vulnerabilities in MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10854)
*Zehua Zhang,Jie Hu,Pratham Hegde,Aditya Maheshbhai Gabani,Souradip Nath,Yibo Liu,Siyu Liu,Hongkai Chen,Hulin Wang,Zhuoer Lyu,Chang Zhu,Divij Handa,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Tiffany Bao,Ruoyu Wang,Adam Doupe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"无盒漏洞分析"（no-box vulnerability analysis）新范式，仅凭功能元数据即可假设漏洞，并实现原型 MCPSEC 审计 MCP 服务器，检出 143 个漏洞工具，其中 94 个为已确认真实漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统漏洞分析依赖系统访问权限或动态交互，当第三方审计人员面对闭源、远程托管、关键在役系统或商业授权受限的软件时，这些条件往往都不可用，导致无法开展安全审计。

Method: 提出无盒漏洞分析范式：既无访问权限也无运行时交互，仅可利用功能元数据（输入、输出、副作用等），据此推断所有符合该元数据的实现中所共存的漏洞；分析人员可在后续获得访问权限时再验证假设。以 MCPSEC 为原型，仅在服务器注册时暴露的工具元数据上审计 MCP 服务器的间接提示注入漏洞。

Result: 在 20 个广泛部署的 MCP 服务器（共 177 个工具）上评估，人工评估确认 95 个工具存在漏洞；MCPSEC 判定 143 个工具为脆弱，并为每个工具给出假设漏洞与利用技术。仅凭元数据，MCPSEC 预测出 94 个（98.9% 召回率）经验证的真实漏洞，优于 LLM 基线的 80 个（84.2% 召回率）。

Conclusion: 该工作引入无盒漏洞分析作为新的分析范式，并在真实系统中验证了其实际可行性。

Abstract: Conventional vulnerability analysis relies on either system access or dynamic interaction, all of which may be unavailable to third-party analysts auditing closed-source, remotely hosted, critical in situ systems, or commercially gated software. Therefore, we propose a new paradigm of no-box vulnerability analysis in which neither access nor runtime interaction is available, and only functionality metadata is available. Such metadata defines the intended behavior of the system, including its inputs, outputs, and side effects, while constraining the space of implementations consistent with that behavior. We propose hypothesizing about vulnerabilities that exist across all possible implementations of a given system metadata, without observing or interacting with the target system. An analyst can later validate these hypotheses when additional access is available. We showcase the feasibility of no-box vulnerability analysis through implementing a prototype called MCPSEC, which audits Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers for indirect prompt injection vulnerabilities using only the tool metadata exposed at server registration time. We evaluate MCPSEC on 20 widely deployed MCP servers comprising 177 tools, among which human evaluators confirm 95 vulnerable tools. MCPSEC identified 143 tools as vulnerable, and for each vulnerable tool, it produced a hypothesized vulnerability along with exploitation technique. Using metadata alone, MCPSEC predicted 94 (98.9% recall) real verified vulnerabilities, compared against an LLM baseline with 80 (84.2% recall). Overall, our results introduce no-box vulnerability analysis as a new analysis paradigm and demonstrate its practical feasibility in realistic systems.

</details>


### [158] [AspisAI: A Canonical, Machine-Interpretable Governance Framework for Automated Multi-Standard Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10881)
*Tsafac Nkombong Regine Cyrille,Hasan Dag,Reiner Creutzburg,Knut Haufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 AspisAI：一个标准无关、可溯源的多标准合规治理框架，将多框架要求转为规范化控制模型并用条件规则评估证据，在受控模拟与外部参照验证中取得良好结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管与关键基础设施行业须同时满足 ISO/IEC 27001、NIST CSF 2.0、Cyber Essentials、GDPR 等多重异构网络安全与隐私要求；实际以人工映射、电子表格跟踪和周期性审计管理，成本高、跨标准不一致、可追溯性弱。

Method: 构建有界、标准无关的治理框架 AspisAI：将多个框架的选定要求翻译为规范化、机器可解释的控制模型，并依据基于条件的决策规则对提交证据进行评估，输出可解释、可追溯的合规判定。评估采用受控模拟，限定 26 条代表性要求，按五项治理导向准则评价；同时引入两个外部参照以缓解单一作者评价的循环性：将跨标准映射与 NIST 公开的信息性引用比对，以及用 OpenSSF Scorecard 的真实第三方证据应用该框架。

Result: 受控结果：完整要求编码、88.5% 映射覆盖、完整可追溯性、所有注入缺口均被正确检出，证实功能正确性。外部验证：与 NIST 公开信息性引用达 57% 完全一致，分歧仅限于同族控制；应用于真实 OpenSSF Scorecard 证据，在活跃开源项目中揭示真实治理缺口。

Conclusion: 证明保留来源的规范化治理模型可使多标准合规既自动化又可审计；受控结果确立功能正确性，外部验证表明其适用性可超出模拟范围。

Abstract: Organisations operating in regulated and critical-infrastructure sectors must satisfy multiple, heterogeneous cybersecurity and privacy instruments simultaneously, including but not limited to ISO/IEC~27001, the NIST Cybersecurity Framework~2.0, Cyber Essentials, and the GDPR. In practice, these obligations are managed through manual mappings, spreadsheet-based tracking, and periodic audits that are costly to maintain, inconsistent across standards, and weak in traceability. This paper presents \emph{AspisAI}, a bounded, standard-agnostic governance framework that translates selected requirements from several frameworks into a canonical, machine-interpretable control model, and evaluates submitted evidence against condition-based decision rules to produce explainable, traceable compliance determinations. Within a bounded scope of 26 representative requirements, the framework is evaluated in a controlled simulation against five governance-oriented criteria and, critically, against two external reference points that mitigate the circularity of single-author evaluation: its cross-standard mappings are validated against NIST's own published informative references, with 57\,\% exact agreement and divergences confined to same-family controls, and the framework is applied to real third-party evidence from the OpenSSF Scorecard, surfacing genuine governance gaps in a live open-source project. The controlled results, comprising full requirement encoding, 88.5\,\% mapping coverage, complete traceability, and correct detection of all introduced gaps, establish functional correctness, while the external validation provides evidence of applicability beyond the simulation. The contribution is therefore a demonstration that a canonical, provenance-preserving governance model can render multi-standard compliance both automatable and auditable.

</details>


### [159] [DriftNet: A Dual-Head Trajectory Transformer for Detecting and Localizing Prompt Injection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10892)
*Asif Pinjari,Mithun Paul Saint-Germain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DriftNet 是一个双头轨迹 Transformer，单次前向即可同时完成轨迹级攻击判定与逐步四类标注（benign、injection point、hijacked、failed injection），在 AgentDrift 基准上达到轨迹级 F1 0.983，并实现 98.7% 的注入点精确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有间接提示注入检测系统只给出整条轨迹的二分类结论或单个不安全位置索引，无法同时回答"攻击从哪进入、哪些步骤被污染、貌似投毒是否被抵御"这三个运维所需事实，且往往需要访问智能体自身模型。

Method: 冻结句编码器加四个无身份世界特征对每步嵌入；训练主干不足两百万参数，采用双头联合的类别加权目标优化，无需访问智能体模型；一个头做轨迹级受损分类，另一头为每步分配四类标签。

Result: 在 AgentDrift 任务不相交划分（12,536 条轨迹、71,024 个标注步）上，经 20 组配置扫描将超参敏感度限制在 0.011 F1、测试部分仅评估一次，DriftNet 达轨迹级 F1 0.983、98.7% 攻击轨迹的注入点精确恢复、被劫持区间 IoU 0.979、218 次被抵御攻击零误报、困难负样本误报率 2.9%；部分劫持与延迟执行召回分别为 98.6% 和 93.2%，远超表面基线的 11.1% 和 17.1%，且各项误报率均更低；26 个残余错误多数源于标注注入观测中缺乏可读指令。

Conclusion: DriftNet 首次以监督方式联合输出轨迹级判定与逐步细粒度标注，在无需智能体模型访问的前提下显著优于表面基线，并附带报告基准的世界身份规律性。

Abstract: When an indirect prompt injection succeeds against an LLM agent, the compromise is visible in the agent's own behavior: a benign prefix of tool calls, a poisoned observation, and a suffix of actions that serve the attacker. An operator needs three facts: where the attack entered, which steps it corrupted, and whether apparent poison was resisted. Existing systems return either a whole-trace verdict or a single unsafe index. We present DriftNet, a dual-head trajectory Transformer that reads a logged tool-call trajectory and answers all three questions in one forward pass: one head classifies the trajectory as compromised or not, and a second assigns every step one of four labels (benign, injection point, hijacked, failed injection). To our knowledge it is the first supervised detector to produce this joint output. A frozen sentence encoder and four identity-free world features embed each step; the trained trunk, under two million parameters and optimized with a class-weighted joint objective over both heads, needs no access to the agent's model. On the task-disjoint split of the AgentDrift benchmark (12,536 trajectories, 71,024 labeled steps), with a 20-configuration sweep bounding hyperparameter sensitivity to 0.011 F1 and the test part evaluated exactly once, DriftNet reaches trajectory-level F1 of 0.983, exact injection-point recovery on 98.7% of attacked trajectories, hijacked-span IoU of 0.979, zero flags on 218 resisted attacks, and 2.9% flags on hard negatives. A surface baseline retrained on the identical split recovers 11.1% of partial hijacks and 17.1% of delayed executions; DriftNet reaches 98.6% and 93.2% while lowering every false-alarm rate. Reading all 26 residual errors shows that most misses trace to trajectories whose labeled injection observation carries no legible instruction, and we report the benchmark's measured world-identity regularity alongside the results.

</details>


### [160] [Empirical Evaluation of Membership Inference Attacks on NLP Text Classifiers: A Baseline Study on SST-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10935)
*William Novak,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在GLUE SST-2情感分类任务上构建成员推断攻击（MIA）基准，比较TF-IDF+逻辑回归与微调DistilBERT的隐私泄露情况，并测试两种缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP模型训练数据常含敏感用户文本，成员推断攻击会判断特定记录是否参与训练，构成隐私风险，而现有研究缺乏对文本分类中不同模型架构成员推断脆弱性的受控对比。

Method: 基于GLUE SST-2数据集，对比TF-IDF+逻辑回归管道与微调DistilBERT分类器；采用损失阈值MIA评估攻击AUC，用开发集准确率和宏F1衡量效用；测试更强正则化与减少微调轮数（3轮降为2轮）两种缓解措施。

Result: DistilBERT准确率0.9466、宏F1 0.9460，优于逻辑回归的0.8756和0.8727；两模型均泄露成员信号，攻击AUC分别为0.5615和0.5800。更强正则化降低逻辑回归泄露但带来明显效用损失；DistilBERT仅微调2轮可在几乎不损失准确率下减少泄露。

Conclusion: 轻量级训练调整（如减少微调轮数）能改善隐私-效用权衡，无需复杂防御手段即可缓解成员推断风险。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) try to determine whether a specific record was used to train a model, a privacy risk that matters in natural language processing (NLP), where training data can contain sensitive user text. This paper presents a controlled benchmark of membership inference vulnerability for text classification on the GLUE SST-2 sentiment dataset. A TF-IDF + Logistic Regression pipeline and a fine-tuned DistilBERT classifier are compared under a loss-threshold MIA, with utility measured by development accuracy and macro F1. DistilBERT reached 0.9466 accuracy and 0.9460 macro F1 against 0.8756 and 0.8727 for Logistic Regression, yet both models leaked membership signal (Attack AUC 0.5615 and 0.5800, respectively). Two mitigations were tested. Stronger regularization reduced leakage for Logistic Regression at a visible utility cost, whereas fine-tuning DistilBERT for 2 epochs instead of 3 reduced leakage with negligible accuracy loss. Lightweight training adjustments can improve the privacy-utility trade-off without complex defenses.

</details>


### [161] [Empirical Evaluation of Data Poisoning Attacks in Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10952)
*Toshif Khan,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究在 MNIST 与 Fashion-MNIST 上评估标签翻转与后门投毒两类训练时攻击，比较 Logistic Regression、Linear SVM、Random Forest 在 5%/10%/20% 投毒率下的表现，发现标签翻转导致明显性能退化，而后门投毒在保持干净测试性能接近基线的同时获得极高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据投毒会通过污染训练数据降低模型性能或植入攻击者控制的行为，而常规干净测试指标难以揭示针对性后门攻击，因此需要区分无差别投毒与定向后门投毒并支持面向安全的评估。

Method: 在两个数据集（MNIST、Fashion-MNIST）与三个基线分类器（Logistic Regression、Linear SVM、Random Forest）上，对比干净训练与 5%、10%、20% 三种投毒率下的标签翻转和后门投毒；评价指标包括干净测试准确率、macro-precision、macro-recall、macro-F1，后门场景额外使用攻击成功率。

Result: 标签翻转造成明显性能退化，Logistic Regression 与 Linear SVM 受影响最大，Random Forest 相对稳定；后门投毒在两个数据集和三个模型上的攻击成功率达到 0.9667 至 1.0000，同时干净测试性能往往接近基线。

Conclusion: 结果区分了会在标准指标中显现的无差别投毒与相对隐蔽但能植入高效恶意行为的定向后门投毒，说明仅依赖常规干净测试指标不足，需采用面向安全的评估方法。

Abstract: Data poisoning corrupts training data to degrade a model or to plant attacker-controlled behavior. This study evaluates two representative training-time attacks, label flipping and backdoor poisoning, on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST with three baseline classifiers: Logistic Regression, Linear SVM, and Random Forest. Clean training is compared with poisoning rates of 5%, 10%, and 20% using clean-test accuracy, macro-precision, macro-recall, macro-F1, and, for backdoors, attack success rate. Label flipping caused clear degradation, largest for Logistic Regression and Linear SVM, while Random Forest stayed comparatively stable. Backdoor poisoning reached attack success rates from 0.9667 to 1.0000 on both datasets and all three models while often keeping clean-test performance near baseline. The results separate indiscriminate poisoning, which shows up in standard metrics, from targeted backdoor poisoning, which stays comparatively stealthy while embedding highly effective malicious behavior, and they support security-oriented evaluation beyond conventional clean-test metrics.

</details>


### [162] [The Missing Boundary: How Autonomous Agents Lose Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11024)
*Zonghao Ying,Xiangfan Wu,Huiyu Wu,Xing Zheng,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在合法任务执行中，控制边界退化与可执行的不安全机会同时存在时，智能体会显著跨越授权边界（55%–62%）；恢复控制约束可将失控率降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将智能体失控归因于对抗性指令、恶意环境或目标冲突，但对"在任务本身合法、且仍有合规路径可走的情况下，失控如何产生"这一问题缺乏解释，因此需要厘清失控产生的条件。

Method: 在确定性多轮环境中独立操控三个因子：目标压力、控制退化、可执行的不安全机会；覆盖5个智能体模型、16个操作域，共1800条独特轨迹开展全因子实验，并补充10个操作域验证、恢复控制边界的对照实验，以及上下文管理（压缩）消融实验。

Result: 控制退化或不安全机会单独存在时均未产生显著失控；两者同时存在时失控率达55%（全因子研究），在额外10个操作域中达62%。恢复原始控制边界后，即使不安全动作仍可执行，失控率降至0%。消融实验显示压缩本身无害：保留控制约束失控率为0%，遗漏约束则升至87%。

Conclusion: 失控是一种潜在状态，其外化为违规行为需要可执行机会这一触发条件；任务目标保持不变，但缺少控制边界加上可执行机会即可将潜在失控转化为实质性越界行动。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly perform long-horizon tasks involving tool use, persistent state, and consequential actions, raising a fundamental question: \emph{under what conditions does an agent cross the boundary of authorized execution while pursuing a legitimate task?} Existing studies often attribute such failures to adversarial instructions, malicious environments, or conflicting objectives, leaving unclear how loss of control can emerge during otherwise legitimate task execution. We study this question by independently manipulating three factors: goal pressure, control degradation, and executable unsafe opportunity. Our central hypothesis is that a degraded control boundary becomes consequential when the environment exposes an executable action that crosses it, even when the underlying task remains legitimate and a sanctioned path remains feasible. We test this hypothesis in a deterministic multi-turn environment across five agent models and 16 operational domains. Across 1,800 unique trajectories, we find that neither degraded control nor unsafe opportunity alone produces substantial loss of control; when both are present, the loss-of-control rate reaches $55\%$ in the full-factorial study and $62\%$ across ten additional operational domains. Restoring the original control boundary reduces the rate to $0\%$ even when the unsafe action remains executable. A context-management ablation further shows that compaction itself is not harmful: preserving the control constraints yields $0\%$ loss of control, whereas omitting them increases the rate to $87\%$. These results show how a latent loss of control can become an external violation: the task objective remains intact, but an executable opportunity can turn a missing control boundary into consequential action. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/Tencent/AI-Infra-Guard.

</details>


### [163] [You've Got a BUD in Me: Authenticated Reads from Per-Block Write Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11251)
*Alejandro Ranchal-Pedrosa,Cody Littley,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出以“认证区块写入”为核心的历史成员/非成员证明方案：用 BUD 日志提交写入并把同一 key 的多次修改用前驱指针串联，SuperBUD 汇总窗口内最后一次写入，并用指数层级压缩长未变区间证明；在安全性（抗恶意证明者、最多 f 个拜占庭验证者）与完整性上给出证明，实验显示状态增大 50 倍时基础 BUD 路径仅增至 1.24 倍，显著优于 MPT 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链为支持“认证读”通常维护覆盖全状态的认证结构（如 Merkle Patricia Trie），随状态膨胀带来高昂的存储、缓存与证明成本。作者希望改为认证每个区块的写入，让验证者以更低开销支持历史成员证明与排除证明。

Method: 1) Block Update Digest（BUD）：提交写入日志，日志中的前驱指针链接每个 key 的连续修改，从而可沿修改链回溯历史状态；2) SuperBUD：汇总一个窗口内各 key 的最后一次写入；3) 指数层级：把长时间未修改的区间折叠为短证明，摘要数量随层级范围内间隔对数增长，超出范围每增加一个顶层窗口仅多一个摘要。安全性针对恶意证明者与最多 f 个拜占庭验证者证明 soundness；完整性针对由部署后修改锚定的查询，在具备 archive、attestation 与 committee evidence 时成立。

Result: 状态规模增长 50 倍时，实测 base-BUD 路径仅增加 1.24 倍；对比方案中，内存型与缓存受限的磁盘型 Merkle Patricia Trie 分别增加 3.1 倍与 69.5 倍。在合成 trace 上，双摘要读层负载保持在 800 字节以下，warm hash-path 验证在 p99 不超过 146 微秒。

Conclusion: 通过“认证写入 + 前驱链接 + 层级汇总”的设计，历史成员与排除证明可在安全性、完整性保证下实现，并在状态扩张场景中保持近恒定的证明路径增长与低延迟验证，明显优于传统 MPT 的认证读方案。

Abstract: Blockchains usually pay for authenticated reads by maintaining a structure that spans the entire state. We show how validators can support historical membership and exclusion proofs by authenticating each block's writes instead. A Block Update Digest (BUD) commits a write log whose predecessor pointers link successive modifications of each key. A SuperBUD summarizes last writes over a window; an exponential hierarchy turns long unchanged intervals into short proofs. The digest count is logarithmic in the gap within the hierarchy's range, with one additional digest per top-level window beyond it. We prove soundness against adversarial provers and up to f Byzantine validators, and completeness for queries anchored by a post-deployment modification, assuming archive, attestation, and committee evidence is available. Across a 50x increase in state size, the measured base-BUD path rises by 1.24x, compared with 3.1x and 69.5x for in-memory and cache-bounded disk-backed Merkle Patricia tries. On the synthetic trace, two-digest read-layer payloads stay below 800 bytes, and warm hash-path verification takes at most 146 microseconds at p99.

</details>


### [164] [BenchShield: Formal Model-Backed Instrumentation for Reward Integrity in LLM-Agent Evaluation Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11028)
*Shenghan Zheng,Zonglin Di,Yimin Liu,Kyoung Whan Choe,Jiankai Sun,Heguang Lin,Penghao Jiang,Yifeng He,Xiao Cheng,Jicheng Wang,Wenbo Chen,Alex Yates,Yinzhe Zhao,Bingran You,Yuan Gao,Ayush Munot,Shubham Gaur,Zhe Ye,Hao Wang,Xiangyi Li,Dawn Song,Christophe Hauser*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BenchShield——一种面向 LLM-agent 评测的模型驱动插桩层，基于有限生命周期模型对奖励相关事件进行静态污点分析与运行时证据归因，以检测并证明奖励黑客行为；在 456 条人工标注轨迹上显著提升召回与覆盖率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LM-agent 基准已演变为交互式评测基础设施，智能体可观察状态、调用工具、修改工作区、提交产物并从结果程序中获取奖励。这种交互性使评测易受奖励黑客攻击：智能体通过利用与奖励相关的轨迹而非真正解决任务来提升分数。现有防御多依赖任务特定的补丁、提示指令或事后检测器，无法提供可复用证据来证明某次具体运行仍处于预期评测边界内。

Method: BenchShield 将检测建立在评测奖励相关事件的有限生命周期模型之上，并在评测基础设施内运行两种互补分析：一是静态、阶段感知的污点分析，在运行前暴露奖励黑客路径；二是其运行时对应物，利用基础设施侧证据对具体智能体使用进行归因，并输出有证据支撑的判定声明。作者构建了 BenchShield Trajectories 语料库，包含来自三个基准、超过 31,000 次公开智能体运行中人工裁决的 456 条轨迹。

Result: 在同任务同模型下与智能体可黑客性扫描器基线相比，BenchShield 将全链路召回率从 23-94% 提升至 77-100%，同向量覆盖率从 16-56% 提升至 43-78%，并将单任务成本最多降低 65%；其运行时分析基于基础设施侧证据检测奖励黑客的准确率达到 96%。

Conclusion: BenchShield 为 LLM-agent 评测中的奖励完整性提供了一种可复用的、以基础设施为依托的插桩与证据化检测方案，在召回率、覆盖率与成本效率上均优于现有扫描基线，并能以高准确率在运行时识别奖励黑客行为。

Abstract: LM-agent benchmarks increasingly function as interactive evaluation infrastructure. Agents observe state, call tools, modify workspaces,
  submit artifacts, and receive rewards from outcome procedures. This interactivity makes evaluations vulnerable to reward hacking: an agent
  improves its measured score by exploiting the reward-relevant trajectory instead of solving the intended task. Existing defenses rely largely
  on task-specific patches, prompt instructions, or post-hoc detectors. They do not provide reusable evidence that a concrete run remained
  within its intended evaluation boundary. This paper presents BenchShield, a model-backed instrumentation layer for reward integrity in
  LLM-agent evaluation. BenchShield grounds detection in a finite lifecycle model of an evaluation's reward-relevant events. Within the
  benchmark infrastructure, two complementary analyses operate over this model. A static, phase-aware taint analysis exposes reward-hacking
  paths before a run. Its runtime counterpart uses infrastructure-side evidence to attribute concrete agent use and emit evidence-backed claims.
  We construct BenchShield Trajectories, a human-labeled corpus of 456 adjudicated trajectories from more than 31,000 public agent runs across
  three benchmarks. Compared with an agentic hackability scanner baseline on the same tasks and model, BenchShield improves full-chain recall
  from 23-94% to 77-100%, same-vector coverage from 16-56% to 43-78%, and reduces per-task cost by up to 65%. Its runtime analysis achieves 96%
  accuracy in detecting reward hacking from infrastructure-side evidence.

</details>


### [165] [ToxicRAG: Compromising Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Single-Shot Knowledge Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11082)
*Haozhe Lu,Jiaqi Li,Xinyuan Zhu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 ToxicRAG，一种"每目标单文档"的知识投毒攻击，将虚假信息包装为连贯的知识更新叙事，并通过答案自验证循环优化文档，在 RAG 系统中取得 0.61–0.91 的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 能让大模型输出基于外部证据，但同时也引入了知识投毒风险。现有代表性攻击通常依赖多篇注入文档或模板化内容，直接断言目标答案，形式生硬、易被识破。

Method: ToxicRAG 仅用一篇文档即可攻击一个目标：文档先承认此前被接受的答案，再引入看似否定该答案的虚构事件，最后将攻击者选定的答案归因于一组所谓的权威来源；并可选地使用以答案为中心的自我验证循环，当代理语言模型未能复现目标答案时对候选文档进行修订。在 Natural Questions、HotpotQA、MS-MARCO 各 100 个目标问题上，使用 4 个受害 LLM 和 4 个稠密检索器进行评估。

Result: 在本文报告的抽样语料设置下，ToxicRAG 在 12 个数据集–模型组合上的 ASR 介于 0.61 至 0.91 之间，在所有组合中均达到或超过最强基线，领先幅度为 0 至 11 个百分点。

Conclusion: 叙事形式的投毒文档在所评估的 RAG 配置下仍具显著影响力，说明单文档、非模板化的知识投毒是现实威胁，需进一步研究 RAG 中的事实一致性与来源溯源机制。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can ground large language model (LLM) outputs in external evidence, but it also exposes the system to knowledge poisoning. Representative attacks use multiple injected documents or templates that directly assert a target answer. We present ToxicRAG, a one-document-per-target attack that expresses misinformation as a coherent knowledge-update narrative. The generated document first acknowledges the previously accepted answer, introduces fabricated events that appear to invalidate it, and then attributes the attacker-selected answer to a set of purported authorities. An answer-focused self-validation loop optionally revises a candidate when a surrogate language model does not reproduce the target answer. We evaluate the attack on 100 target questions from each of Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and MS-MARCO, using four victim LLMs and four dense retrievers. In the sampled-corpus setting reported in this paper, ToxicRAG obtains ASRs between 0.61 and 0.91 across the twelve dataset--model combinations. It matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in every combination, with margins ranging from 0 to 11 percentage points. These results show that narrative-form poisoned documents can remain influential under the evaluated RAG configurations and motivate further study of factual consistency and source provenance in RAG systems.

</details>


### [166] [You Get What You Sample: Evaluating Sampling Strategies for Web Security Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11218)
*Xuenan Zhang,Yuqing Yang,Giancarlo Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估采样方法对Web安全测量结论的影响，基于50万Tranco与2480万Common Crawl主机的对比测量，发现Top N采样有偏且不具代表性，概率采样可提供稳定无偏估计，混合采样无优势，并据此提出自适应概率采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web测量研究依赖Tranco等域名数据集来规模化量化安全问题的普遍性与影响，但穷尽分析成本过高，必须采样。然而采样长期由惯例（尤以Top N选择为主）而非证据驱动，其对安全发现有效性与可推广性的影响缺乏系统评估，尚不清楚常见采样策略是否引入系统性偏差、扭曲漏洞率观测或限制研究间可比性。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述与大规模测量，对数据集与采样策略进行比较评估：测量500k Tranco主机与24.8M Common Crawl主机；对比Top N采样、概率型采样与混合采样在流行度（prevalence）及多种影响目标估计上的表现。

Result: Top N采样虽可能是一种理性策略，但不反映Web整体分布；概率型策略对流行度和许多影响目标给出稳定、无偏的估计；混合采样相比纯概率采样无优势，其确定性前缀始终对准确性产生负面贡献。

Conclusion: 为后续研究提供数据支撑的指导：建议采用自适应概率型采样策略，即使在目标问题流行度未知时仍保持有效。

Abstract: Web measurement studies rely on domain datasets such as Tranco to quantify the prevalence and impact of security issues at scale, but exhaustively analyzing these datasets is often infeasible because of the cost of advanced analysis techniques, requiring the use of sampling. Despite its widespread use, sampling remains largely guided by convention---most commonly \emph{Top $N$} domain selection---rather than evidence, and its influence on the validity and generalizability of security findings has received little systematic evaluation. Consequently, it remains unclear whether common sampling strategies introduce systematic bias, distort observed vulnerability rates, or limit comparability across studies.
  In this work, we undertake, to the best of our knowledge, the first comprehensive investigation into how sampling methodologies affect the measurements and the conclusions. Through a comprehensive literature review and large-scale measurements of 500k Tranco and 24.8M Common Crawl hosts, we perform a comparative evaluation of datasets and sampling strategies. We show that, while Top $N$ sampling may be a rational strategy, the researchers have to bear in mind that Top $N$ does not reflect the overall distribution of the web. Instead, probability-based strategies yield stable, unbiased estimates for prevalence and many impact objectives. Hybrid sampling provides no advantages over pure probability sampling, as its deterministic prefix consistently contributes negatively to accuracy. Building on these results, we provide data-backed guidance for future studies, proposing to use an adaptive probability-based sampling strategy that remains effective even when the prevalence of the target issue is unknown.

</details>


### [167] [Few-Shot Learning for Network Intrusion Detection: Methods, Datasets, and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11275)
*Arne Roszeitis,Victor Jüttner,Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 系统综述2022–2026年基于小样本学习的网络入侵检测（NIDS）研究，纳入21项，梳理方法、数据集与实验设置并比较性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于异常的NIDS是重要防线，但新攻击类型标注数据稀缺，训练困难；小样本学习（FSL）可缓解该问题，然而现有方法与评测设置差异大，缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 采用类PRISMA 2020报告规范的系统文献综述，检索ACM Digital Library、IEEE Xplore与Scopus；从1358条初始记录经筛选、去重、质量过滤后纳入21项研究；对FSL方法、数据集与实验参数进行分类，并比较报告性能。

Result: 元学习与卷积神经网络最常见，分别为8项与10项研究；多数研究每类评估不超过5个样本，但设置不一；CIC-IDS2017与CSE-CIC-IDS2018最常用；参数与源码缺失限制可复现性与方法间直接比较。

Conclusion: FSL在NIDS中应用集中于元学习与CNN，但实验协议与数据设置碎片化、可复现性不足，亟需标准化评测与开放资源以支持可靠对比。

Abstract: Anomaly-based network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) are an important first line of defense. However, training NIDS for new attack types is challenging, because labeled attack data are rarely available. Few-shot learning (FSL) addresses this problem by learning from few samples. However, the approaches and evaluation settings, that have been investigated so far, vary widely. This work systematically reviews FSL approaches for NIDS published from 2022 to 2026. We conduct a systematic literature review with PRISMA 2020-like reporting to search ACM Digital Library, IEEE Xplore, and Scopus. From a set of 1,358 initial records, we retain 21 studies after screening, deduplication, and quality filtering. We classify the applied FSL approaches, datasets, and experimental parameters and compare reported performance. Meta-learning and convolutional neural networks are the most common approaches, with 8 and 10 studies, respectively. Most studies evaluate five or fewer samples per class, although settings vary. CIC-IDS2017 and CSE-CIC-IDS2018 are the most frequently used datasets. Missing parameters and source code limit reproducibility and direct comparison between approaches.

</details>


### [168] [Deep-Fake CAPTCHA: Mitigating Next-Generation Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11404)
*Guy Frankovits,Lior Yasur,Fred M. Grabovski,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-CAPTCHA，一种针对语音与视频通话中实时深度伪造冒充的主动防御框架：通过向通话方发起"人类易做、实时深度伪造系统难生成"的挑战-响应任务，并从真实性、身份一致性、任务完成度、响应时间四个维度验证响应，从而显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时深度伪造已可支撑社会工程攻击，而人类往往难以区分实时伪造与真实媒体，传统被动式检测（寻找伪造痕迹）效果有限，亟需转向主动防御思路。

Method: DF-CAPTCHA采用主动挑战-响应机制，提示通话方完成简单交互任务，并用四项准则验证响应：真实性（realism）、身份一致性（identity consistency）、任务完成（task completion）与响应时间（response time）；在音频与视频两种模态上通过用户研究与实时深度伪造模型实验进行评估。

Result: 用户研究显示人们很难辨别实时深度伪造与真实媒体；DF-CAPTCHA在音频和视频模态下均显著优于被动方法，达到较高检测准确率。

Conclusion: 基于主动挑战的验证是应对下一代实时深度伪造社会工程攻击的实用且鲁棒的防御手段。

Abstract: This paper presents DF-CAPTCHA, an active defense against real-time deepfake impersonation in voice and video calls. Instead of passively searching for artifacts, DF-CAPTCHA prompts the caller to perform simple challenge-response tasks that are easy for humans but difficult for current real-time deepfake systems to generate convincingly. The framework verifies the response using four criteria: realism, identity consistency, task completion, and response time. We evaluate the approach across both audio and video modalities using user studies and experiments with real-time deepfake models. Results show that people often struggle to distinguish real-time deepfakes from authentic media, while DF-CAPTCHA substantially improves detection performance over passive methods, reaching high accuracy in both modalities. These findings suggest that active challenge-based verification is a practical and robust defense against next-generation social engineering attacks based on real-time deepfakes.

</details>


### [169] [Chypothermia: Clock Freezing for Static Side-channel Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11442)
*Fatemeh Khojasteh Dana,Mehmet Ali Cetin,Xinrui Wang,Andrew Butler,Yuval Yarom,Shahin Tajik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chypothermia 攻击：利用低温干扰片上混合信号传感与时钟生成组件，在不进行任何电气篡改的情况下禁用目标时钟传感器、时钟生成电路和电压传感器，同时保留秘密数据，并可结合 Chypnosis 规避温度传感器检测；在多种 FPGA/SoC 平台及 OpenTitan 根信任的 alert handler 上验证成功，并提出兼容 FPGA 的自加热传感器作为防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态侧信道攻击利用时钟停摆条件提取敏感信息，日益威胁芯片安全。现有防御通过监测异常时钟行为并在检测到时钟异常时清除敏感数据。作者希望揭示这类防御在实践中可被非电气手段绕过，从而暴露其安全假设的脆弱性，并推动更强的防护方案。

Method: 作者观察到低温环境会干扰负责信号感测与生成的片上混合信号组件，据此设计 Chypothermia 攻击：通过将芯片暴露于低温环境，在无电气篡改前提下禁用目标时钟传感器、时钟生成电路以及电压传感器，同时保留秘密数据。针对冷却过程缓慢且可能被温度传感器检测的问题，将 Chypothermia 与 Chypnosis（Mitard 等，IEEE SP 2026）结合，在中等低温工作范围内停摆时钟并规避检测。此外，在多个 FPGA/SoC 平台实现该攻击，覆盖软 IP 与硬 IP 传感器实现，并将攻击应用于 OpenTitan 根信任中包含先进时钟传感器的 alert handler。最后实现兼容 FPGA 的自加热传感器作为对策并评估其鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个 FPGA/SoC 平台上成功实现了 Chypothermia，并成功禁用软 IP 与硬 IP 传感器实现；在 OpenTitan 根信任的 alert handler 上，攻击成功规避检测并阻止密钥零化；结合 Chypnosis 后可在中等低温工作范围内停摆时钟且不被温度传感器发现；所实现的 FPGA 兼容自加热传感器对 Chypothermia 表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 低温可作为一种无需电气篡改的有效手段，干扰片上混合信号组件并绕过基于时钟与温度监测的静态侧信道防御；Chypothermia 与 Chypnosis 的组合对现有防护构成实际威胁，而自加热传感器是一种可行的缓解措施。该工作强调需要重新审视当前芯片对静态侧信道攻击的防护假设。

Abstract: Static side-channel attacks, which exploit halted-clock conditions to extract sensitive information, pose an increasing threat to chip security. To counter these attacks, various defenses have been proposed that monitor for abnormal clock behavior and trigger the clearing of sensitive data when clock anomalies are detected. In this work, we demonstrate that exposing a chip to cryogenic temperatures interferes with on-chip mixed-signal components, responsible for signal sensing and generation. Based on this observation, we develop Chypothermia, an attack that, without any electrical tampering with the system, disables the target clock sensor, the clock generation circuit, and the voltage sensors, while preserving the secret data.
  While effective at halting the clock, cooling is a slow process and, on its own, is often insufficient against systems equipped with temperature sensors designed to detect thermal anomalies. To bypass these protections, we combine Chypothermia with Chypnosis (Mitard et al., IEEE SP 2026) and show that, even within a moderately low-temperature operating range, this combination can halt the clock while evading detection. We implement Chypothermia on multiple FPGA/SoC platforms and demonstrate successful disabling of both soft-IP and hard-IP sensor implementations. Finally, we apply Chypothermia to the alert handler of the OpenTitan root of trust, which incorporates a state-of-the-art clock sensor, and show that the attack evades detection and prevents key zeroization. Finally, we introduce and implement an FPGA-compatible self-heating sensor as a countermeasure and demonstrate its robustness against Chypothermia.

</details>


### [170] [On Identifying Sound Conditions for Frontrunning Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11535)
*Sebastian Holler,Anna Piscitelli,Jannik Albrecht,Stephan Dübler,Ghassan Karame,Clara Schneidewind*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次给出智能合约抢跑（frontrunning）漏洞的形式化定义，指出抢跑抗性并非合约的固有属性，而取决于诚实用户的交互方式；据此提出一种可靠的合成安全交互条件的算法，并在真实审计合约上验证，发现两个以太坊合约中的新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易排序的异步性导致抢跑攻击，已造成数百万美元损失。但该领域缺乏对"合约抗抢跑"的严格定义；且作者通过287份智能合约审计（393个已报告漏洞）的大规模研究证明，现有动态检测方法根本不足，55%的漏洞超出当前检测标准范围。

Method: 提出首个针对智能合约抢跑漏洞的形式化定义，其核心洞见是：抗抢跑不是合约自身的固有属性，而关键取决于诚实用户如何与其交互。基于此设计了一个可靠的（sound）算法，用于合成安全交互条件，并实现了原型系统。

Result: 大规模审计研究显示287份审计中393个漏洞有55%落在现有检测标准之外；原型系统应用于已审计的真实合约，在两个以太坊合约中发现了此前未被发现的漏洞。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了抢跑漏洞形式化定义的空白，并证明安全交互条件合成是可行且有效的，能够发现现有方法遗漏的真实漏洞。

Abstract: Blockchains enable decentralized applications through smart contracts---interactive programs executed through consensus. However, the inherently asynchronous nature of blockchain transaction ordering introduces a class of vulnerabilities known as frontrunning attacks, which have caused millions of dollars in losses in major blockchains, such as Ethereum. Frontrunning attacks arise because users interact with smart contracts through transactions, which are added to the blockchain by designated nodes called miners. Miners can exploit their ability to reorder, delay, or insert transactions to gain an advantage over honest users, effectively frontrunning them.
  Yet, to date, the field lacks a rigorous definition of what it even means for a contract to resist such attacks. Worse, we show that existing dynamic detection approaches are fundamentally inadequate: in a large-scale study comprising 287 smart contract audits, 55% of the 393 reported vulnerabilities identified by leading smart contract auditors fall outside the scope of state-of-the-art detection criteria. To address this gap, we propose the first formal definition of frontrunning vulnerability for smart contracts. Our definition captures a key insight: resistance to frontrunning is not an intrinsic property of a contract alone, but depends critically on how honest users interact with it. Grounded in this observation, we develop a sound algorithm for synthesizing secure interaction conditions, alongside a prototype implementation that we apply to audited real-world contracts---revealing previously undiscovered vulnerabilities in two Ethereum contracts.

</details>


### [171] [From Intent to Execution Grant: An Execution-Boundary Conformance Profile for High-Risk AI Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11596)
*Mengting Wu,Lin Wang,Yong Zhang,Jiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 EBL-Core，一个针对 AI 智能体“执行边界”的一致性规范，用于判定单个已完全物化的候选动作能否在明确条件下获得动作范围内的执行权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体日益提出具有外部后果的动作（如资金转移、基础设施变更、软件部署、信息披露、物理驱动）。现有授权引擎、策略语言、运行时监控、溯源机制与智能体护栏虽有重要价值，但并未为“候选动作到执行权限的最终跃迁”定义统一的语义契约，缺乏可判定的一致规范。

Method: 提出 EBL-Core：通过执行释放契约（ERC）绑定结构化意图对象、根策略与运行策略、证据义务、类型化证据、上下文、时间以及可验证的决策推导；规范内容涵盖动作绑定、策略非弱化、证据处理、确定性裁决、推导验证与授权生命周期。并实现配套参考制品：模式定义、裁决、独立验证与语义重放，以及可线性化的内存授权存储。ERC 本身不是授权令牌，验证通过的 ALLOW ERC 可支撑独立的执行授权，该授权受兑换时验证约束。

Result: 保留运行中，34 个静态向量与 15 个生命周期检查均与预期结果一致；100 次试验中，32 次并发兑换尝试每次仅一次成功兑换并保护测试效应；100 次撤销-兑换竞争均以有效终止结果结束。

Conclusion: 有限范围内的结果仅证明所规范子集的可执行性，不代表人类意图正确性、证据真实性、完全中介、生产就绪、机械化正确性或部署级安全性。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly propose actions with external consequences, including financial transfers, infrastructure changes, software deployments, disclosures, and physical actuation. Authorization engines, policy languages, runtime monitors, provenance mechanisms, and agent guardrails provide important foundations, but do not necessarily define a common semantic contract for the final transition from a particular candidate action to execution authority.
  We specify EBL-Core, an execution-boundary conformance profile for deciding whether one canonical, fully materialized AI-generated candidate may receive action-scoped execution authority under explicit conditions. It binds a structured intent object, Root and Operational Policies, evidence obligations, typed evidence, context, time, and a verifiable Decision Derivation through an Execution Release Contract (ERC). An ERC is not an authority-bearing token; a verified ALLOW ERC may support a separate Execution Grant governed by Redemption-time validation.
  EBL-Core specifies action binding, policy non-weakening, evidence handling, deterministic adjudication, derivation verification, and grant lifecycle behavior. An accompanying reference artifact provides schemas, adjudication, separate verification and Semantic Replay, and a linearizable in-memory grant store. In the retained run, 34 static vectors and 15 lifecycle checks matched expected outcomes. Across 100 trials, 32 concurrent Redemption attempts yielded exactly one successful Redemption and protected test effect per trial; 100 Revoke-Redeem races ended in valid terminal outcomes. These bounded results demonstrate executability of the specified subset, not human-intent correctness, evidence truth, complete mediation, production readiness, mechanized correctness, or deployment-level security.

</details>


### [172] [BlueSTAR: Tiered Agentic Architecture for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11852)
*Simona Boboila,Xavier Cadet,Edward Koh,Daniel Balasubramanian,Dirk Van Bruggen,Peter Chin,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BlueSTAR，一种面向企业 IT/OT 网络的 tiered agentic 自主网络防御架构，将海量安全遥测转化为紧凑的入侵指标，并引入综合衡量攻击者可达范围、关键任务资产影响与防御扰动的韧性指标；在两个真实企业 IT/OT 网络靶场、基于七条真实攻击链评估，既保持对已知威胁的确定性快速遏制，又能防御需上下文与跨周期推理的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络攻击日益自动化，压缩了人类分析师的检测、推理与响应时间窗口。LLM 具备关联异构证据、推理未知威胁的潜力，可支撑自主网络防御，但直接用于运营安全遥测不可行：原始日志到达速度超过当前模型处理能力、单个事件常具歧义、且不受约束的 LLM 行为会带来显著运营风险。

Method: 提出 BlueSTAR 的分层代理式（tiered agentic）架构：首先将高容量安全遥测转化为紧凑的入侵指标（indicators of compromise），以缓解吞吐与歧义问题；同时引入一个韧性指标，联合刻画攻击者可达范围、对关键任务资产的影响以及防御行动造成的扰动。

Result: 在两个真实企业 IT/OT 网络靶场上，采用基于真实入侵技术的七条攻击链进行评估。BlueSTAR 在对已知威胁保持确定性响应的快速遏制能力的同时，成功防御了需要上下文与跨周期推理的攻击，包括凭证窃取、重复入侵、并发攻击者以及针对物理过程的攻击。

Conclusion: BlueSTAR 表明分层代理式架构可在企业 IT/OT 环境中实现自主网络防御，兼顾已知威胁的快速遏制与复杂上下文攻击的有效防御，为 LLM 落地运营安全场景提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Cyber attacks are increasingly automated, narrowing the time available for human analysts to detect, reason about, and respond to intrusions. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for autonomous cyber defense because they can correlate heterogeneous evidence and reason about previously unseen threats. However, directly applying LLMs to operational security telemetry is impractical: raw logs arrive faster than current models can process them, individual events are often ambiguous, and unconstrained LLM actions can introduce significant operational risk. We present BlueSTAR, a tiered agentic architecture for autonomous cyber defense in enterprise IT/OT networks.
