In addition to predictive accuracy, models are appreciated for their potential to provide interpretability, offering explicit equations that relate input variables to outcomes. However, achieving interpretability and plausibility remains challenging, as evolved models may be complex or scientifically inconsistent. In this study, we explore whether Large Language Models, can assist in improving the explainability of Symbolic Regression models generated by evolutionary computation methods. Building upon our previous work on estimating body fat percentage using grammar-based Genetic Programming , we investigate the use of LLMs as post-processing tools to analyze and rank evolved expressions according to their interpretability and medical plausibility. Four symbolic expressions are analysed by three LLMs over three repeated runs, and the resulting interpretations and rankings are assessed by a panel of three clinicians. Across the three LLMs, comparative model-ranking outputs received more favorable clinician assessments than isolated term-level interpretations. However, the LLMs also produced physiologically and mathematically questionable explanations, indicating that they are better suited to comparative auditing under expert oversight than to autonomous validation.\blfootnote{The present work is an extended version of a paper submitted into a journal.

</details>


### [207] [Calibration-Aware Uncertainty Cascades for Efficient Heterogeneous Model Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11446)
*Yilin Zhang,Han Jiang,Cai Xu,Ying Liu,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出校准感知不确定性级联（CAUC），通过独立校准各模型置信度建立统一可靠性尺度，实现与模型池和预算解耦的部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构模型协作旨在平衡预测性能与推理成本。现有方法存在局限：训练路由器将路由决策绑定到固定任务和模型池；原始置信度级联的阈值在异构模型间缺乏一致的可靠性语义。因此这些方法难以适应模型池和部署预算的变化。

Method: CAUC是一个简单的后处理框架，独立校准每个模型的置信度，并利用验证数据选择部署策略。校准后的置信度分数建立统一的可靠性尺度，用于决定接受早期预测、调用更强模型，或选择性组合模型输出。该统一决策准则将部署策略与特定模型池或运行预算解耦。理论上证明校准赋予置信度阈值明确的选择性风险解释，而未经校准的分数无此可靠性保证。

Result: 在六个语言基准上，CAUC相对仅用强模型推理平均提升1.9%准确率，同时避免约47%的强模型调用。在图像分类基准上，保持或提升预测性能的同时，测量GFLOPs降低最多57%。

Conclusion: 校准是实现异构模型协作中可靠、可迁移部署策略的关键，CAUC为置信度阈值提供了显式的选择性风险语义，并在多类任务上实现了性能与成本的良好权衡。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model collaboration seeks to exploit the complementary strengths of different models to balance predictive performance and inference cost. Existing approaches typically rely either on trained routers, which tie routing decisions to a fixed task and model pool, or on raw-confidence cascades, whose thresholds lack consistent reliability semantics across heterogeneous models. Consequently, these approaches adapt poorly to changing model pools and deployment budgets. We propose Calibration-Aware Uncertainty Cascades (CAUC), a simple post-hoc framework that independently calibrates each model's confidence and selects deployment policies using validation data. The resulting calibrated confidence scores establish a common reliability scale for accepting an early prediction, invoking a stronger model, or selectively combining model outputs. This unified decision criterion decouples deployment policies from any particular model pool or operating budget. We further show theoretically that calibration gives confidence thresholds an explicit selective-risk interpretation, whereas uncalibrated scores offer no comparable reliability guarantee. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, across six language benchmarks, CAUC achieves an average relative accuracy improvement of 1.9% over strong-model-only inference while avoiding approximately 47% of strong-model calls. On image classification benchmarks, it maintains or improves predictive performance while reducing measured GFLOPs by up to 57%.

</details>


### [208] [RouteRepair: Instance-Level Failure Diagnosis and Targeted Repair in LLM-Based Automated Heuristic Design for Routing Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11452)
*Binghao Ji,Di Huang,Jiahui Fang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RouteRepair框架，通过实例级表现诊断父代启发式的弱点，进行有界、证据约束的定向修复，在TSP与CVRP上显著提升路由启发式效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自动启发式设计虽能生成有效路由规则，但聚合评估会掩盖其在特定实例结构上的重复性失败，需要针对具体薄弱环节进行修复。

Method: 从实例级性能诊断父代启发式的特定弱点，对相应启发式组件做定向修改，同时保护已表现良好的行为；结合路由证据、求解器行为与程序上下文定义有界修复目标，并通过匹配的父子评估验证失败修复与附带退化。

Result: 在TSP与CVRP上覆盖构造式搜索、引导局部搜索与蚁群优化；RouteRepair-GLS将TSP平均最优性差距从1.7476%降至0.7587%；构造式CVRP启发式相对savings启发式平均路线成本降低1.91%；生成的ACO先验优于匹配的手工设计先验。

Conclusion: 失败感知、证据约束的精炼方法能改善困难实例上的路由启发式，同时保持已良好求解实例的性能。

Abstract: Efficient routing optimization is essential to freight transportation, urban logistics, and shared mobility, where high-quality heuristics are often required under limited computational budgets. Recent large language model (LLM)-based automated heuristic design methods can generate effective routing rules, but aggregate evaluation may mask recurrent failures on particular instance structures. To address this limitation, this study develops RouteRepair, which diagnoses parent-specific weaknesses from instance-level performance and applies targeted modifications to the corresponding heuristic components while protecting behavior that already performs well. Routing evidence, solver behavior, and program context are combined to define bounded repair objectives, and each intervention is validated through matched parent-child evaluation of failure recovery and collateral degradation. Experiments on the traveling salesman problem (TSP) and capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP) span constructive search, guided local search, and ant colony optimization. RouteRepair-GLS reduces the mean TSP optimality gap from 1.7476% to 0.7587%, while the constructive CVRP heuristic lowers average route cost by 1.91% relative to the savings heuristic; the generated ACO priors also outperform matched hand-designed priors. These results show that failure-aware, evidence-constrained refinement can improve routing heuristics on difficult instances while preserving performance on cases they already solve well.

</details>


### [209] [The Convention Gap: Towards Measuring Implicit Communication in Cooperative AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11489)
*Makoto Fukushima,Hua-Dong Xiong,Ehsan Moradi Pari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"约定差距"（convention gap）指标，用于量化人机/人际/AI间合作中隐含沟通约定的差异，并以Hanabi卡牌游戏为实验平台，重放约10.1万次动作，发现该指标能有效区分人类与AI的玩法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合作型AI代理主要以AI-AI基准评估，但人类合作依赖言外之意（隐含约定），这类信息在AI-AI基准中可能无法体现。因此需要一个能衡量隐含沟通的指标，来评估AI与人类合作的有效性。

Method: 提出"约定差距"，即由沟通字面内容预测的失败概率与实际观测失败率之差。利用Hanabi的有限牌堆和确定性提示约束，使该后验可精确计算。重放来自三个公开数据集（人际 hanab.live、AI-AI HOAD、人机 HanabiData）的约10.1万次游戏动作，并以OBL（Off-Belief Learning）代理作为已知答案检验，其约定内容由构造控制。

Result: 约定差距在人类配对中为+26.2个百分点，AI配对中为-0.7个百分点，人机配对中为+16.4个百分点，且集中于未受提示的牌（人类配对+46pp）。在人机对局中，人类可获得字面信息相似（预测失败率38-41%），但人类失败率为14.4%-34.4%，差距从+24.1到+6.2pp不等，差距最大的伙伴反而导致人类失败最少。游戏得分受各类样本构成影响，而约定差距在代理层面区分了人类与AI玩法。OBL代理在无约定水平差距为+1.6pp，单调上升至+21.7pp。

Conclusion: 结果表明，约定兼容性（而非AI-AI性能）可能更能预测AI与人类伙伴合作的有效性。

Abstract: Cooperative AI agents are evaluated against other AIs, yet human cooperation relies on implicit conventions---shared protocols for reading meaning beyond the literal message---which AI-AI benchmarks may not capture. We propose the \emph{convention gap}, the difference between the failure probability predicted from the literal content of communication and the observed failure rate, as a metric of implicit communication. In the card game Hanabi, the finite deck and deterministic hint constraints make this posterior exactly computable. We replayed about 101,000 play actions from three public datasets of human-human (hanab.live), AI-AI (HOAD), and human-AI (HanabiData) games. The gap was +26.2 percentage points (pp) in human pairs, $-$0.7~pp in AI pairs, and +16.4~pp in human-AI pairs, and was concentrated on plays of cards that had received no hints (+46~pp in human pairs). Within human-AI play, the literal information available to humans was similar across the three AI partners (mean predicted failure 38--41\%), but human failure rates ranged from 14.4\% to 34.4\% and the gap from +24.1 to +6.2~pp; the partner eliciting the largest gap produced the fewest human failures. Game score carried different information: it depended on each corpus's roster composition, whereas the gap separated human from AI play at the agent level. As a known-answer check, Off-Belief Learning agents, whose convention content is controlled by construction, gave a gap of +1.6~pp at the convention-free level, rising monotonically to +21.7~pp. These results suggest that convention compatibility, rather than AI-AI performance, may predict an AI's effectiveness with human partners.

</details>


### [210] [ActMap: Single-Pass Uncertainty Quantification from Generation-Time Activation Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11498)
*Jacopo Dardini,Roberta Calegari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ActMap，一种将生成期各层各 token 隐状态轨迹压缩为固定 12×32×128 张量的白盒表征，训练轻量分类器即可从单次生成估计答案正确性，性能优于采样、token 概率、注意力与嵌入基线，并可与大 67 倍的密集激活检测器媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的实际不确定性量化需在单次生成下判断某答案是否可信。现有方法或需多次采样，或仅用输出 token 概率，或把内部计算压缩为单一隐状态，信息利用不足且成本较高。

Method: ActMap 将生成期隐藏状态轨迹（每层、每生成 token）压缩为固定 12×32×128 的时序统计通道张量，保持跨 transformer 深度与池化隐藏坐标的结构；生成过程中捕获、无可测开销，体积 96 KiB。以紧凑 Vision Transformer 为轻量分类器，从每个 map 输出估计正确概率；并做容量匹配 MLP 对照与遮挡分析定位信号。

Result: 在短答案 QA、直接作答数学与摘要事实性任务上，用三个 7-8B 指令微调模型进行域内训练与评估：ActMap 一致优于采样、token 概率、注意力及嵌入基线；与在体积大 67 倍的密集激活张量上训练的 ACT-ViT 相比，平均 AUROC 基本持平，并在十二对中有十对校准误差更低。

Conclusion: ActMap 以紧凑、可审计、固定形状的表征支持单次生成的弃答、路由与选择性验证，是面向已部署模型可扩展监督的实用基础组件。

Abstract: Practical uncertainty quantification (UQ) for large language models must decide,
  from a single generation, whether a specific answer should be trusted. Existing
  methods either sample multiple generations, read only output-token probabilities,
  or reduce the model's internal computation to a single hidden state. We introduce
  ActMap, a white-box representation that compresses the generation-time hidden-
  state trajectory (every layer, every generated token) into a fixed $12 \times 32
  \times 128$ tensor of temporal-statistic channels that preserves structure across
  transformer depth and pooled hidden coordinates. The map is captured during the
  generation pass with no measurable overhead, has a fixed shape across model
  depths and hidden sizes, and occupies 96 KiB: a compact artifact that can be
  retained for audit-relevant generations and probed directly, with occlusion
  analysis localizing the classifier's signal to mid-depth regions of the map. A
  lightweight classifier, instantiated as a compact Vision Transformer, reads an
  estimated correctness probability from each map in a fraction of a millisecond;
  capacity-matched MLPs perform comparably, indicating the representation itself
  carries the result. Trained and evaluated in-domain on short-answer QA, direct-
  answer math, and summarization factuality with three instruction-tuned 7-8B
  models, ActMap consistently outperforms sampling, token-probability, attention,
  and embedding baselines, and matches ACT-ViT, a detector trained on dense
  activation tensors $67 \times$ larger, at essentially the same mean AUROC with
  lower calibration error on ten of twelve pairs. The resulting score supports
  abstention, routing, and selective verification from a single generation, making
  it a practical primitive for scalable oversight of deployed models.

</details>


### [211] [Lightweight LiDAR-Based Cone Detection Framework Using Random Forest for Formula Student Driverless](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11527)
*Márk Mező-Kerekes,Péter Praksz,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向CPU执行的轻量级纯LiDAR感知流水线，用于Formula Student Driverless赛车，结合地面去除、IMU运动补偿、DBSCAN聚类与基于几何特征的随机森林分类，通过特征重要性分析将输入特征由12个降至7个，在2,371个真实赛事聚类上取得98.33%的F1分数和3.13 ms端到端CPU运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠、低延迟的感知对Formula Student Driverless赛车至关重要，但现有流水线多依赖深度学习与多传感器融合，通常需要GPU加速，难以满足资源受限赛车队的实时性与部署需求。

Method: 构建仅使用LiDAR的轻量级感知流水线，包含地面去除、基于IMU的运动补偿、DBSCAN聚类和基于几何特征的随机森林分类；并通过特征重要性分析将模型输入特征从12个精简至7个。

Result: 在从真实FSD赛事采集的2,371个带标注聚类上评估，流水线取得98.33%的F1分数，端到端运行时间为3.13 ms，且仅在CPU硬件上运行。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限的自动驾驶赛车队提供了实用且可复现的基线，并公开了数据集、标注工具与训练模型。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency perception is crucial for Formula Student Driverless vehicles, yet many existing pipelines rely on deep learning and multi-sensor fusion, often requiring GPU acceleration. This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-only perception pipeline tailored for CPU execution, combining ground removal, IMU-based motion compensation, DBSCAN clustering, and geometric feature-based Random Forest classification. Feature importance analysis reduced the model input from 12 to 7 features while preserving performance. Evaluated on 2,371 labeled clusters collected from real FSD events, the pipeline achieves an F1-score of 98.33% and an end-to-end runtime of 3.13 ms on CPU-only hardware. The released dataset, labeling tool, and trained models provide a practical and reproducible baseline for other resource-constrained autonomous racing teams.

</details>


### [212] [Prompt Revision as a Source of Cultural Bias in Text-to-Image Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11532)
*Aleksandra Urman,Elsa Lichtenegger,Salima Jaoua,Azza Bouleimen,Robin Forsberg,Corinna Hertweck,Stefania Ionescu,Nicolò Pagan,Ancsa Hannak,Joachim Baumann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WORLDVIEW多语言基准（15种语言、31个语言-语境配对、8,960条提示词），首次将文生图系统的"提示词改写层"作为审计对象，发现改写层会过度标注非西方/非英语语境、将其压缩为狭窄且刻板的词汇，并证明该层本身是文化刻板印象的因果来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用文生图系统在生成前会静默改写用户提示词，用户既无法关闭也无法查看。既有文化偏见审计只分析最终图像、把生成过程视为单一流水线，因而无法定位偏见究竟产生于哪一环。作者希望找到文化偏见的真正来源，以便针对性地修复。

Method: 构建WORLDVIEW基准：8,960条提示词，覆盖15种语言、31个语言-语境配对。用该基准对DALL-E-3、Imagen-4、GPT-Image-1.5三个系统的改写层进行三步审计：（1）衡量改写层对每种文化语境的标注强度；（2）判断是否将该语境压缩为狭窄词汇；（3）判断该词汇是否具有刻板印象特征。以无语境英文提示为基线进行对比，并在无改写层的模型上比较原始提示与改写提示所生成的图像。

Result: 相对于无语境英文基线，美国是标注最弱的语境；非西方、非英语语境被显著更重地标注，被压缩为跨主题提示词通用的狭窄词汇，并被简化为可识别的文化刻板印象。通过在无改写层模型上对比原始提示与改写提示所生成的图像，确认改写层本身是这种刻板化的因果来源，且此前未被记录。

Conclusion: 要定位并修复文化偏见，必须审计"实际部署的系统"而非仅审计模型本身；提示词改写层是文化刻板印象的关键因果环节，应成为偏见审计与治理的直接对象。

Abstract: Commercial text-to-image systems silently revise user prompts before generating images, a step users typically cannot disable or even see. Yet, existing audits of cultural bias examine only the final images and treat generation as a single pipeline, so they cannot tell where the bias originates. We introduce WORLDVIEW, a multilingual benchmark of 8,960 prompts across 15 languages and 31 language-context pairings. Using it, we audit the revision layer in three systems (DALL-E-3, Imagen-4, GPT-Image-1.5) through a three-step analysis of how heavily it marks each cultural context, whether it flattens that context into a narrow vocabulary, and whether that vocabulary is stereotypical. Relative to a no-context English baseline, the US is the least-marked context, while non-Western and non-Anglophone contexts are marked far more heavily, flattened into narrow vocabularies applied across topically diverse prompts, and reduced to recognizable cultural stereotypes. Comparing images from original versus revised prompts on models without a revision layer, we identify the layer itself as a previously undocumented, causal source of this stereotyping. To locate cultural bias, and fix it, we must audit the system as deployed, not the model alone.

</details>


### [213] [Characterizing Job Power Elasticity for Power-Flexible AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11542)
*Philip Colangelo,Charles Dawson,Shayan Sengupta,Ayse Coskun,Varun Sivaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画了LLM训练任务的"功率弹性"，提出归一化指标PFI量化降功率的性能代价，并基于131次H200训练运行等数据证明PFI感知的功率分配可在功率约束下最大化吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练是现代数据中心增长最快的电力需求来源之一，电力供应成为AI基础设施扩展的主要瓶颈。若能使训练负载的功耗具备灵活性，可为AI增长释放额外电力、抑制电价上涨并提升现有电网利用率。但要实现这种灵活性，必须先理解GPU降功率时训练负载性能如何变化。

Method: 提出Power Flexibility Index（PFI），一种量化降功率性能代价的归一化指标，并作为SLA感知功率灵活性的控制原语。采集131次H200 LLM训练运行数据（另含24次H200验证运行与34次匹配的H100运行），覆盖稠密与MoE模型、预训练与微调任务、最多32块GPU；识别可在运行时预测PFI的遥测信号；并实现PFI感知的功率分配策略。

Result: LLM训练任务表现出显著但差异化的功率弹性；遥测信号可在运行时预测PFI；PFI感知的功率分配在功率约束下最大化总tokens/秒吞吐。在30%降功率下，每任务恢复约1.5k tokens/s，相当于等权分配与完全信息oracle之间性能差距的63%。

Conclusion: 功率弹性可作为训练任务的可测量属性，为功率感知、电网友好的AI基础设施奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training is among the fastest-growing sources of electricity demand in modern data centers, and power availability is a primary bottleneck to continued AI infrastructure growth. Making the power consumption of these workloads flexible could unlock additional power for AI growth, limit increases in electricity prices, and improve the utilization of existing grid infrastructure. However, to realize this flexibility, we must first understand how the performance of training workloads changes when GPU power is reduced.
  This paper presents the first systematic characterization of \emph{job power elasticity} (the sensitivity of throughput to power reductions) in LLM training. To quantify elasticity, we introduce the \emph{Power Flexibility Index (PFI)}, a normalized metric that quantifies the performance cost of power reductions and provides a control primitive for SLA-aware power flexibility.
  We collect data from 131 LLM training runs on H200 (plus 24 H200 validation runs and 34 matched H100 runs), including both dense and mixture-of-experts models, pretraining and fine-tuning tasks, and up to 32 GPUs. We find that LLM training jobs exhibit substantial but variable power elasticity, and we identify telemetry signals that predict PFI at runtime. Finally, we demonstrate that PFI-aware power allocation maximizes total tokens/second throughput under power constraints. Under a 30\% power reduction, PFI-aware power allocation recovers ~1.5k tokens/s per job, 63\% of the performance gap between an equal-weight allocation and an oracle with perfect information. Our results establish power elasticity as a measurable property of training jobs and provide a foundation for power-aware, grid-responsive AI infrastructure.

</details>


### [214] [Making Alternative Data Work: Context-Augmented LLMs for Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11607)
*Jihoon Kwon,Lawrence Liu,Daekyung Park,Sumin Kim,Haverty Jack,Hoyoung Lee,Katherine Bjorkman,Josh McKenney,Peter Laurelli,Nicole Kagan,Zach Golkhou,Thorsten Neumann,Edward Tong,Pete Petersen,Yoon Kim,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yongjae Lee,Chanyeol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双智能体LLM框架，通过上下文学习将替代数据与传统财务信息结合以预测企业营收，实验证明优于单一数据源和标准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替代数据（如消费交易、网络流量、预测市场）能为企业未来财务表现预测提供及时信号和传统公开来源未涵盖的互补信息。但企业级替代数据历史覆盖有限、仅适用于特定预测目标或企业子集，且分散于众多异构渠道，难以灵活融入传统预测方法。LLM具备解读指令、从上下文示例学习并整合异构信息的能力，无需任务特定参数更新，故研究其能否通过上下文学习整合替代数据预测企业表现。

Method: 提出双智能体框架：第一个智能体识别每个替代数据渠道可能具有信息价值的公司，第二个智能体利用公司及渠道特定的上下文预测营收。在四个商业替代数据渠道上评估该框架。

Result: 实验中，将替代数据与其他财务信息一并作为上下文输入，相较仅使用任一单一来源，提升了LLM的预测表现；且这些预测比标准预测基线更准确。

Conclusion: LLM为整合替代数据与异构财务信息提供了一种灵活且实用的方法。

Abstract: When forecasting a firm's future financial performance, alternative data - data collected from non-traditional sources such as consumer transactions, web traffic, and prediction markets - can provide timely signals about firms' operating activities and broader market conditions. These signals may reveal information that is not captured by traditional public sources and can therefore provide complementary information for forecasting firms' future financial performance. However, firm-level alternative data often have limited historical coverage, are relevant only to specific prediction targets or subsets of firms, and are distributed across numerous heterogeneous channels, making them difficult to incorporate flexibly into conventional forecasting approaches. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) can interpret instructions, learn from in-context examples, and generate predictions by combining heterogeneous information without task-specific parameter updates. Motivated by this potential flexibility, we investigate whether an LLM can forecast firm performance by integrating alternative data with other financial information through in-context learning. We propose a two-agent framework that first identifies the firms for which each alternative data channel is likely to be informative and then predicts revenue using firm- and channel-specific context. We evaluate the framework across four commercial alternative data channels. In our experiments, adding alternative data in context alongside other financial information improves the LLM's forecasting relative to either source alone, and these forecasts are more accurate than those of standard forecasting baselines. These findings suggest that LLMs provide a flexible and practical approach to integrating alternative data with heterogeneous financial information.

</details>


### [215] [Distributed Optimization of Modular Production Systems using Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Inverse Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11615)
*Andreas Schwung,Steve Yuwono,Sofiene Lassoued,Dorothea Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于近似逆过程模型的model-based强化学习框架，用于高柔性模块化制造系统的数据驱动自学习控制，将驱动动力学与状态空间动力学解耦，使RL训练仅在任务空间进行，并在模块化生产实验平台上验证了性能与训练速度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高柔性、模块化制造系统对数据驱动的自学习控制有需求，但传统强化学习在训练中同时学习驱动动力学与状态空间动力学，导致学习复杂度高、训练效率低，尤其在多样化异构模块场景下难以快速收敛。

Method: 采用一种新的model-based强化学习框架，在策略训练中引入近似逆过程模型，将驱动动力学学习与状态空间动力学解耦，从而使RL训练完全在任务空间内进行；并提出一种轻量级前馈结构用于近似逆模型，将其集成到标准RL算法的策略网络中；在包含异构生产模块的实验室模块化生产测试平台上进行验证，重点关注off-policy算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法提升了模块化制造单元的性能，并加快了训练速度，且对off-policy算法的改进尤为明显。

Conclusion: 在强化学习中引入近似逆过程模型并实现任务空间内的策略训练，可有效提升模块化制造系统的自学习控制效率与性能，为高柔性制造单元的数据驱动控制提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for data-driven self-learning control of highly flexible, modular manufacturing systems. Specifically, we employ a novel framework for model-based reinforcement learning which introduces approximate inverse process models within the training of reinforcement policies. This approach disentangles the learning of actuation dynamics and the dynamics in state space, resulting in RL-based training solely within the task space. We propose a lightweight feedforward architecture for approximate inverse models and integrate them within the policy network of standard RL algorithms. We apply the approach to a laboratory modular production testbed with heterogeneous production modules. The results underline the efficiency improvements for modular manufacturing units in terms of both performance and training speed, particularly for off-policy algorithms.

</details>


### [216] [MAPLE: Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11636)
*Kesheng Chen,Yamin Hu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAPLE 是一个记忆增强的 LLM 优化智能体，通过语言建模、数学规划与进化搜索相结合，在连续自然语言请求下维护优化问题，并在 NLDO 基准上取得高在线质量与帕累托超体积表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实运营是动态的：需求、资源与优先级不断变化，需要更新数据、约束和目标。领域从业者虽懂业务约束但常缺乏运筹学专业知识，而现有以孤立请求为中心的方法难以快速适配，且无法保留既有决策与复用有效搜索结果。

Method: 提出 MAPLE（Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution）。它将基于语言的问题构建与数学规划、进化搜索结合，并保留优化程序、已接受方案、历史更新与候选解，以支持后续连续的自然语言请求。同时构建 NLDO 基准，包含 15 条轨迹、180 次更新，覆盖选择、排程、排班、路径规划与云资源放置。

Result: 在主评估中，MAPLE 完成全部轨迹，在线标量质量达 0.951，帕累托超体积比为 0.875。受控对比进一步表明，维护可执行状态可提升更新有效性，并能在大幅修订中保留有用的搜索信息。

Conclusion: 记忆增强与可执行状态维护是让 LLM 优化智能体适应动态现实运营的关键，可在多轮自然语言更新中兼顾有效性、连续性与搜索信息复用。

Abstract: Domain practitioners understand their business constraints but may lack operations-research expertise or dedicated support. LLM-based optimization agents translate natural-language requirements into models or solver programs that established optimization tools can execute. This progress makes optimization more accessible, but real-world operations are dynamic: changing demand, resources, and priorities require updates to data, constraints, and objectives. Methods centered on isolated requests offer limited support for rapid adaptation that preserves earlier decisions and reuses useful search results. We introduce MAPLE (Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution), an agent for maintaining optimization problems through successive natural-language requests. MAPLE combines language-based problem construction with mathematical programming and evolutionary search. It retains the optimization program, accepted plans, earlier updates, and candidate solutions for subsequent requests. We introduce NLDO, a benchmark of 15 trajectories and 180 updates spanning selection, scheduling, rostering, routing, and cloud-resource placement. In the main evaluation, MAPLE completes all trajectories and achieves online scalar quality of 0.951 and a Pareto hypervolume ratio of 0.875. Controlled comparisons further show that maintaining executable state improves update validity and can preserve useful search information across substantial revisions.

</details>


### [217] [Autonomy, Social Norms, and Alignment: Towards a Developmental Framework for Autonomous Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11660)
*Marica Notte,Ludovica Marinucci,Vieri Giuliano Santucci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出应将AI对齐视为一个渐进的教育过程：智能体通过内在动机驱动的探索、经验学习与协作，在类似"监管沙盒"的教学环境中逐步建构伦理规范，从而在复杂动态情境中实现对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模型虽具备泛化与复杂输出能力，但迁移到具身智能体时存在明显局限：现有系统依赖预置数据集与人类反馈策略，在动态或未知环境中不足；智能体须通过与环境的直接交互获取知识。引入内在动机等高层机制可增强自主探索，但也使"确保智能体与人类目标对齐"更为困难。既定规则在非结构化、动态情境中失效，因此需要重新思考对齐的实现路径。

Method: 概念性与理论性论证（非实证研究）。核心思路：以认识论过程将规范根植于经验之中——从简单的、情境化的原则出发，经由经验、自主学习以及与其他道德主体的协作，逐步建构更复杂的规则；借鉴儿童通过探索环境与参与集体实践来习得社会规范的类比；并援引Dennett的立场，认为道德主体地位并非与生俱来，而是依据其负责任地管理日益增加的自由度之能力被逐步赋予。

Result: 将"监管沙盒"重新界定为AI的教学环境（pedagogical environments）：在其中的动态空间里，对齐作为一个形成性（formative）过程而发展，通过交互与协作，在复杂度递增的场景中逐步塑造自主行为。

Conclusion: AI对齐不应被视为一次性植入的静态规则集，而应被理解为渐进的、基于经验的教育过程；监管沙盒可作为实现这一过程的可操作框架，使智能体的自主性与人类规范的建构同步演进。

Abstract: In recent years, artificial intelligence has made extraordinary progress thanks to large-scale models capable of generalization and the generation of complex outputs. However, transferring this potential into embodied agents reveals a significant limitation: the most advanced systems rely on pre-existing datasets and human feedback strategies that are powerful but insufficient in dynamic or unknown contexts. To adapt, an agent must acquire knowledge through direct interaction with its environment. One strategy to address this challenge involves introducing higher-level mechanisms, such as intrinsic motivations, which leverage curiosity and competence, to guide exploration and learning in complex environments. While this flexibility expands autonomy, it complicates the task of ensuring agents remain aligned with human goals. Alignment, already a challenge for artificial systems in general, becomes even more complex in unstructured and dynamic contexts where predefined rules prove insufficient. To be effective and adaptable, norms must be rooted in experience through an epistemological process that starting from simple, situated principles allows for the gradual construction of more complex rules through experience, autonomous learning, and cooperation with other moral agents. Similarly to children learning social norms by exploring their environment and participating in collective practices, artificial agents must also be educated toward alignment. Following Dennett, the status of a moral agent is not innate but is attributed gradually based on the ability to responsibly manage increasing degrees of freedom. From this perspective, the regulatory sandboxes can be viewed as pedagogical environments for AI: dynamic spaces where alignment develops as a formative process, progressively shaping autonomous behaviors through interaction and cooperation in scenarios of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [218] [COBRA-Skills: Contextual Bandit-Guided Evolution for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11682)
*Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Xiang Li,Jie Mao,Zikun Qu,Junfeng Luo,Yao Shu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COBRA-Skills：将技能优化建模为预算约束下的序贯优化，用上下文赌博机引导候选评估并迭代进化技能，在六个基准上取得最优平均性能，成本较SkillOpt降低55–58%，且每基准仅需50个优化样例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可从既往任务经验中蒸馏可复用技能，但现有技能优化方法依赖昂贵的基于执行的评估与大量任务数据，成本高、数据需求大。

Method: 提出COBRA-Skills框架：把技能优化表述为动态演化候选空间上的预算化序贯优化；结合上下文赌博机引导的优先级排序与基于证据的技能进化，有选择地将评估预算分配给有前景或信息量大的候选，并利用执行反馈持续精炼技能种群。

Result: 在六个异构智能体基准和三个目标模型上，平均性能在对比方法中最强；相对SkillOpt优化成本降低55–58%；每个基准仅使用50个独特优化样例。进一步分析表明，该方法对智能体harness变化具有鲁棒性，且在目标模型自身用于技能生成与精炼时仍有效。

Conclusion: COBRA-Skills在有限预算与少量样例条件下高效实现技能优化，兼顾性能、成本与鲁棒性，并支持以目标模型进行技能生成与精炼。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can benefit from reusable skills distilled from prior task experience, yet existing skill optimization methods often rely on costly execution-based evaluation and substantial task data. We introduce \textbf{COBRA-Skills}, an efficient framework that formulates skill optimization as budgeted sequential optimization over a dynamically evolving candidate space. COBRA-Skills couples contextual-bandit-guided prioritization with evidence-grounded skill evolution, selectively allocating evaluations to promising or informative candidates while continually refining the skill population from execution feedback. Across six heterogeneous agent benchmarks and three target models, COBRA-Skills consistently achieves the strongest average performance among compared methods, while reducing optimization cost by 55--58\% relative to SkillOpt and using only 50 unique optimization examples per benchmark. Further analyses show that COBRA-Skills remains robust to changes in the agent harness and performs effectively when the target model itself is used for skill generation and refinement.

</details>


### [219] [When Agents Disagree: Bayesian Backward Reasoning as a Label-Free Anchor for Multi-Agent Collective Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11709)
*Ken Chen,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对多个LLM智能体答案冲突时的集体决策问题，提出通过贝叶斯反向推理构建反向后验，并利用前向与反向两种分解路径的Jensen-Shannon散度衡量跨路径一致性，进而设计三种聚合策略（MinJS、FwdJS、LogLin），在DDXPlus上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体决策方法（投票、选举规则、LLM评判）均依赖前向推理，即单向地将证据映射为标签，因此其聚合的估计共享同一"证据到标签"的因子分解方式，容易继承前向池内的相关误差。作者希望引入不同因子分解的估计以降低误差共现，从而提升智能体多样性带来的收益。

Method: 对每个实例，基于显式似然通过贝叶斯反向推理构造反向后验，与智能体的前向后验形成两种不同因子分解的近似。用Jensen-Shannon散度衡量前向与反向路径的一致性，据此对智能体排序，并衍生出三种策略：硬选择（MinJS）、软重加权（FwdJS）和对数线性融合（LogLin）。此外，在有标注数据时采用轻量级两阶段校准进一步精化反向锚点。

Result: 在DDXPlus数据集、五种LLM骨干上评估：MinJS在所有骨干上均优于随机选择；FwdJS总体优于最强基线；LogLin在所有评估方法中表现最佳，且在智能体意见分歧的子集上增益最大。反向后验虽单独准确率较低，但作为锚点比仅前向方案更有用。两阶段校准可进一步提升聚合性能。

Conclusion: 前向与反向两种因子分解的估计倾向于不共享相同误差，跨路径一致性是有效的聚合信号。反向后验作为互补锚点为集体决策提供额外信息，结合校准可进一步改善聚合效果。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents yield conflicting answers, the decision-making process dictates whether agent diversity improves performance or merely compounds shared errors. Existing collective decision-making methods, including voting, electoral rules, and LLM judges, rely on forward reasoning: they map evidence to labels in one direction. Although these methods can combine diverse forward traces, they still aggregate estimates that share this evidence-to-label factorization and can inherit correlated errors within the forward pool. We therefore construct a reverse posterior for each instance through Bayesian backward reasoning from an explicit likelihood. The forward and reverse posteriors provide differently factorized approximations of the underlying posterior. Because estimates from different factorizations may tend to share the same error less often, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence to rank agents by cross-path consistency. This cross-path consistency signal underlies three strategies: hard selection (MinJS), soft reweighting (FwdJS), and log-linear fusion (LogLin). Evaluated on DDXPlus across five LLM backbones, our proposed strategies show consistent improvements: MinJS outperforms random selection across all backbones, FwdJS generally improves over the strongest baseline, and LogLin achieves the best performance among the evaluated methods, with its largest gains on the subset where the agents disagree. Despite its weaker standalone accuracy, the reverse posterior serves as a more useful anchor than forward-only alternatives, providing complementary information for collective decision-making. When labeled data are available, a lightweight two-stage calibration can further refine the reverse anchor and improve aggregation performance.

</details>


### [220] [SIRF: A Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model for Industrial Content Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11752)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRF 通过将平台复杂风控策略内化到权重中，在高精度、秒级延迟、仅输出判定的部署约束下显著提升自动处置覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业内容风控的真实部署约束不是平均准确率，而是在高精度与秒级延迟下能自动处理多少风险，且无需额外人工标注。

Method: 利用 EntiGraph、MAGA 重写与账号级思维链（CoT）合成策略数据，通过继续预训练（CPT）将策略内化进模型权重；采用同源受控对比（Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT，策略注入与判定式输出一致，仅差策略接地 CPT）。

Result: SIRF-8B-SFT 达到 71.3% Black Recall@P95，较基线提升 15.1pp，仅用约 70M CPT token，且不损害通用能力；在该接口下与可获取 logprob 的模型中，匹配或超越更大规模系统；部署为树模型裁定层可多召回 20% 被误罚样本，并以低成本迁移至冻结场景，相对误罚减少约 70%。

Conclusion: 策略内化式 CPT 是在低延迟、高精度、判定式输出约束下提升风控自动处置能力的有效路径，并具备低成本跨场景迁移性。

Abstract: For industrial content risk control, the real deployment constraint is not average accuracy but how much risk can be auto-handled under high precision and second-level latency. We present SIRF (Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model), which internalizes a platform's complex policies, synthesized without additional human annotation via EntiGraph, MAGA rewriting and account-level chain-of-thought (CoT), into the weights via continued pretraining (CPT), so rules are applied at high precision under an ultra-low-latency, verdict-only deployment. A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [221] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过逐层隐状态干预，研究语言模型在回答问题时对"查询路由信息"与"目标知识"依赖的动态变化，发现路由依赖与内容依赖存在阶段性交接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在回答问题过程中，对查询路由信息（请求方向）与目标知识的依赖如何随层数变化，并厘清"可读性、自然强度、因果操控、晚期内容依赖"这几个通常被混淆的测量维度。

Method: 在问题末端的隐状态上进行逐层干预，跨Qwen、Llama、Gemma三个模型，比较国家-大洲问题（名词、形容词、代码答案）。构建三种测量：(1)对条件化请求方向（pair-conditioned，单国自然问题中查询哪个国家）；(2)全局请求方向（global，配对问题中第一国 vs 第二国）；(3)独立选择候选，测试对隐状态中已有内容的控制。并对冻结的Qwen自然问题状态做诊断性再分析。

Result: 条件化请求方向在干预开始改变后续拟合知识之前逐渐增强，该因果窗口在答案支持内容仍在形成时打开。三模型配对轨迹不一致：Gemma呈部分重叠的中层路由-内容轮廓，Llama在相同门控下无持续的路由效应窗口。配对协议中，对全局请求方向的依赖从较早固定层到较晚层集递减，而对拟合内容的依赖持续存在。匹配的Qwen对照显示条件化方向保留晚期效应。

Conclusion: 请求信息与内容依赖在层间存在操作性的"交接"，但该交接针对全局拟合方向而非全部请求信息；应将早期可读性、自然强度、因果操控与晚期内容依赖区分开来。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [222] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Explainability Assistant，一个基于现代 LLM 函数调用能力的开源对话式 XAI 系统，用于提升能耗预测中复杂 ML 模型的可解释性。系统意图解析准确率达 94%，支持灵活自然语言交互且无需任务特定微调；专家对比评估显示其可用性与任务准确率优于传统 XAI 仪表盘，所有专家均偏好该对话式界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能耗预测依赖日益复杂的 ML 模型（如基于遗传编程的符号回归器），其预测对设施管理者和楼宇运营人员而言难以解释。传统 XAI 仪表盘需要较高技术门槛且灵活性不足，难以支持动态、情境感知的探查；此前对话式方案（如 TalkToModel）受限于僵化自定义语法，意图解析准确率仅 76.8%。

Method: 提出 Explainability Assistant，一种开源对话式 XAI 系统，利用现代 LLM 的函数调用（function-calling）能力替代自定义语法，从而支持灵活的自然语言交互、适应不同 ML 问题类型，且无需任务特定微调。文中给出系统架构，并与传统 XAI 仪表盘开展面向能源领域专家的对比评估。

Result: 系统意图解析准确率达到 94%，显著高于 TalkToModel 的 76.8%；对比评估表明其可用性提升、任务准确率稳定，且所有专家均一致偏好对话式界面用于实际工作。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 函数调用的对话式 XAI 系统可有效克服传统仪表盘与僵化语法方案的局限，在能耗预测等专业场景中提供更易用、更准确且更灵活的可解释性支持，具备实际落地价值。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [223] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现主流基于深度学习的线性推荐算法本质等价于核范数或Frobenius范数正则化，并据此提出两种兼具低秩、闭式解优势的新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习推荐算法在标准基准上表现领先，但不同技术（如dropout、autoencoder）构建的模型性能相近、代价函数相似，作者希望探究这种性能可比性是巧合，还是可统一到单一框架下。

Method: 对多种线性表现领先的推荐模型进行理论分析，揭示其等价于核范数正则化或Frobenius范数正则化；进而对Frobenius范数正则化进行推广，推导出两种低秩闭式解。

Result: 所有线性领先模型仅添加核范数或Frobenius范数正则项；核范数类具有限制预测能力的刚性结构但解低秩且有闭式解；Frobenius范数类表达力更强、效率更高，但解为满秩或需难以调参的数值过程（如ADMM）。

Conclusion: 提出的两种低秩闭式解融合了核范数与Frobenius范数两类方法的优点，兼顾低秩结构与闭式求解优势。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [224] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MindTopo，一个基于认知科学与形式拓扑的多模态大模型拓扑直觉基准，涵盖连续性、分离性、序、包围与纽结五类性质，包含推理与规划两个认知层次、13 类程序化任务共 11,030 个实例，评测 14 个 MLLM 及生成增强的智能体配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不仅依赖距离、角度、形状等度量性质，还依赖在连续形变下保持不变的拓扑关系。认知科学认为拓扑关系是空间理解的基础，但现有基础模型评测大多聚焦度量或视角相关关系，缺乏对拓扑直觉的系统评估。

Method: 构建 MindTopo 基准：1）基于认知科学和形式拓扑定义五类拓扑性质——连续性、分离性、顺序、包围、纽结；2）每类性质分两个认知层次，Reasoning（识别拓扑关系或推断其变化）与 Planning（将基础模型实例化为闭环智能体，由策略选择环境动作）；3）13 种程序化生成任务类型、难度可控，共 11,030 个实例；4）评测 14 个 MLLM，并研究引入图像与视频生成增强的智能体配置，其中 3 个视频生成模型用于规划场景。

Result: 所有 MLLM 在推理任务上的表现均优于规划任务；表现最佳的模型仍远低于人类观测水平。在 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 上，监督微调与强化学习对推理的提升大于对规划的提升。生成观测能保留局部线索并到达看似合理的终点，但经审计的 rollout 无法可靠遵循环境动态，也无法在状态转移中保持拓扑关系。

Conclusion: 当前多模态基础模型虽具备一定拓扑推理能力，但在闭环规划与跨状态拓扑保持方面存在明显不足，且微调与生成增强难以弥补这一差距。MindTopo 为评估模型的拓扑直觉提供了系统化、可控的测试平台。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [225] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了2–8B参数、适合边缘部署的小型视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专用模型BioCLIP（300M）在96物种识别任务上的分类学知识，发现所有模型在相机陷阱野外图像上性能显著下降，BioCLIP虽体量小却大幅领先VLM，且领域鸿沟本身源于图像质量转变而非模型架构缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常部署于连接受限的边缘硬件，因此实际可部署的小型VLM（而非前沿大模型）才是物种识别的现实评估对象。作者希望检验这一2–8B部署相关区间内的模型是否具备真正的分类学知识，并与领域专用小模型进行对比，以厘清性能差异究竟源于模型规模还是专用训练数据。

Method: 在96物种任务上，将四个小型VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专用模型BioCLIP（300M参数）进行对比。评测涵盖两类数据：干净的iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science采集集的相机陷阱图像，并采用两个独立采样的评测集。此外，还在开放式提示条件下统计模型生成语法合法但分类学不存在的物种名比例，并进行跨评测集的复现性分析。

Result: 所有模型识别准确率均远高于随机水平，但所有模型——无论通用还是专用——在野外图像上都急剧退化，领域鸿沟为9.6–26.6个百分点，且在分类层级和两个评测集上表现一致。BioCLIP在扩大的200图像样本上大幅超越所有VLM，领先33.2–59.2个百分点，尽管其参数量远小，说明差距源于专用训练数据而非模型规模。然而BioCLIP自身的领域鸿沟（18.0个百分点）与最佳VLM（22.3个百分点）在统计上无显著差异。开放式提示下，5.9–9.6%的回复为语法合法但分类学不存在的物种名，其相对捏造率排序在两个评测集上完全复现。

Conclusion: 在部署相关的2–8B参数区间，小型VLM确实具备高于随机的分类学识别能力，但领域专用模型凭借训练数据的专门性而非规模优势取得显著领先。清洁照片到野外图像的退化是图像质量转变本身的属性，而非通用模型的固有弱点，因为BioCLIP的领域鸿沟与最佳VLM无统计差异。模型捏造物种名的相对比率排序具有跨评测集的稳健复现性，比任何单点估计更可靠，提示在开放式提示部署中需关注幻觉风险。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>

### [226] [Cortical information transfer reveals conserved hemispherical network dynamics across human handedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10870)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,José Garcia Vivas Miranda*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究结合排列传递熵（PTE）与网络指标，通过EEG映射左右利手者在手写任务中的有向皮层信息流，发现皮层通信具有严格的尺度依赖性，且左利手者在用同一解剖手执行任务时其半球间动力学与右利手者功能等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动与大脑偏侧化究竟源于根本不同的神经架构，还是来自保守的网络动力学，是网络科学与神经生物学的核心问题。传统皮层激活测量难以揭示复杂运动任务中有向信息交换如何随用手偏好而调整，因而无法判断左利手者是否具有非典型神经组织，抑或遵循共享的动力学原则。

Method: 采用排列传递熵（PTE）结合基于网络的指标，对右利手与左利手个体在高精度运动执行（手写）任务中的EEG数据进行有向皮层信号流映射。

Result: 皮层通信严格依赖尺度，且与利手无关：同侧优势主要出现在全局、网络层面的交互中，而半球间交互保持对侧特征。关键在于，左利手者在使用同一解剖手执行任务时，其半球间信息动力学与右利手者功能等价，否定了镜像式或非典型偏侧化的假设。

Conclusion: 人类皮层偏侧化依赖跨用手偏好保守的动力学原则，表明功能不对称反映的是由表观遗传或社会灵活性所驱动的适应性优化，而非固定的结构差异。

Abstract: Whether human motor and brain lateralization arises from fundamentally distinct neural architectures or emerges from conserved network dynamics remains a central question at the intersection of network science and neurobiology. Conventional measures of cortical activation often fail to resolve how directed information exchange adapts to manual preference during complex motor tasks. This ambiguity leaves it unclear whether left-handed individuals possess atypical neural organization or follow shared dynamical principles. Here, we combine Permutation Transfer Entropy (PTE) with network-based indices to map directed cortical signal flow using electroencephalography (EEG) during high-precision motor execution (handwriting) in both right- and left-handed individuals. We demonstrate that cortical communication is strictly scale-dependent regardless of handedness: ipsilateral dominance occurs primarily in general, network-wide interactions, whereas inter-hemispheric interactions maintain a contralateral profile. Crucially, left-handed individuals exhibit inter-hemispheric information dynamics that are functionally equivalent to right-handers when executing tasks with the same anatomical hand, refuting assumptions of mirrored or atypical lateralization. By demonstrating that human cortical lateralization relies on dynamical principles conserved across manual preferences, these findings suggest that functional asymmetries reflect adaptive optimizations governed by epigenetic or social flexibility, rather than fixed structural divergences.

</details>


### [227] [The Platonic brain bridge hypothesis: human brain networks as an architectural prior for omni models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10947)
*Pengfei Zhang,Biao Tian,Xiangang Li,Li Liu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出"柏拉图式脑桥假说"：像大脑一样联合处理视频、音频和文本的全模态模型会收敛到类脑表征，且这种对应关系是双向的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证全模态模型与大脑表征之间是否存在双向的、稳定的对应关系，并探索大脑网络作为全模态模型架构先验的可用性。

Method: 基于七个全模态模型的内部隐状态构建编码模型，并在 Algonauts 2025 分布外榜单上进行评估；提出三个从脑到模型的方法：Brain-MoE（为七个皮层网络各配置一个脑预训练专家）、Brain-AVQA（依据最响应脑网络标注的视频片段构建问答）、Brain-Scope（用稀疏自编码器定位对应关系子集）。

Result: 全模态模型的类脑性在受试者间稳定，编码模型在 Algonauts 2025 分布外榜单排名第一；Brain-MoE 在全部 15 个模型-基准组合上平均提升 6.42 个百分点；Brain-AVQA 中真实网络-专家映射在三个模型上均优于打乱映射；Brain-Scope 定位的小子集被移除后，三个基座模型的脑预测能力均减弱。

Conclusion: 人脑网络可作为全模态模型的有效架构先验，模型与大脑之间的对应关系是双向且可定位的。

Abstract: We propose the Platonic brain bridge hypothesis: omni models, which process video, audio and text jointly like the brain, converge on brain-like representations, and the correspondence is bidirectional. From model to brain, brain-likeness of seven omni models is stable across participants, and our encoding models on their internal hidden states rank first on the Algonauts 2025 out-of-distribution leaderboard. From brain to model, three contributions follow. Brain-MoE gives seven cortical networks one brain-pretrained expert each and raises held-out accuracy in all 15 model-benchmark pairs by 6.42 percentage points on average. Brain-AVQA builds questions from video clips labelled by the most responsive brain network; the real network-to-expert map exceeds shuffled maps in-domain on all three models. Brain-Scope uses sparse autoencoders to localize the correspondence to a small subset whose removal weakens brain prediction in all three bases tested. Human brain networks are therefore a usable architectural prior for omni models.

</details>


### [228] [Degeneracy along the sensorimotor hierarchy: motor control within a framework larger than redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11325)
*Florent Paclet,Paul Duprat*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文重新界定运动控制中"冗余"与"简并"的区别，提出以"非选择情境下的分化"作为可操作判据，并设计在神经力学闭环系统中量化简并度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动控制领域长期沿用"冗余"一词描述神经系统可用的多余解，但冗余暗含元素可互换、难以差异化适应；而生物学早在25年前提出"简并"概念，指非同一却对给定输出等功能的元素，其非同一性带来适应性。运动控制与电路神经科学在术语上分裂，缺乏统一的对象类别与量化手段。

Method: 将脊髓回路与肌骨系统置于闭环中的神经力学模型，把两大传统归入同一类对象；重述Edelman与Tononi的区分并推导出操作化要求——不是多解的存在，而是这些解在其未被选择的情境中的分化。对三项有影响力的研究进行检验，并论证简并与冗余沿感觉运动层级以连续变化的比例共存。

Result: 三项研究各自满足该要求的一部分，但没有一项满足全部。提出可测量的比例：对同一构型计算两次简并度，一次以肌肉激活为输出，一次以产生的运动为输出，从而分离出肌骨系统的贡献。由此得出五项预测及唯一可反驳该提案的结果。

Conclusion: 提出在非线性、非平稳、闭环系统中实施该测量所需的调整，并指出一旦不再假定各解等价，运动控制的核心问题将从"哪条规则选择一个指令"转向"系统仍然允许什么样的解集"。

Abstract: Motor control has described the surplus of solutions available to the nervous system as redundancy, a term that names duplication: interchangeable elements, robust to loss but incapable of differential adaptation. Biology has had a second term for twenty-five years. Degeneracy names elements that are not interchangeable and are nonetheless isofunctional with respect to a given output, and it supports adaptability, since non-identical elements necessarily diverge in some context. Circuit neuroscience has relabeled its own results accordingly, while motor control has kept the older vocabulary. Neuromechanical models, by placing a spinal circuit in the loop with a musculoskeletal apparatus, bring the two traditions onto the same class of objects. We restate the Edelman and Tononi distinction for sensorimotor systems and derive an operational requirement: not the existence of multiple solutions, but their divergence in contexts they were not selected for. Three influential studies each meet part of that requirement and none meets all. We then argue that degeneracy and redundancy coexist along the sensorimotor hierarchy in a proportion that varies continuously, and that this proportion is measurable: computing degeneracy twice for the same configuration, once with muscle activation as the output and once with the movement produced, isolates what the musculoskeletal apparatus contributes. Five predictions follow, with the single outcome that would refute the proposal. We set out the adaptations the measurement requires in a nonlinear, non-stationary, closed-loop system, and what changes for motor control once solutions are no longer assumed equivalent: the question shifts from which rule selects a command to what the repertoire of the system still allows.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Sequence-Informed Geometric Evaluation of RNA 3D Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10644)
*Andrea Zerio,Yighua Yao,Alessandro Micheli,Roland G. Huber,Mile Sikic,Samir Bhatt,Andres R. Masegosa,Yuangang Pan*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: SIRGE：一种序列信息驱动的RNA几何评估器，用预训练RNA语言模型的核苷酸嵌入条件化结构表示，在候选构象排序任务上优于已有评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算RNA结构流程会为同一序列生成大量候选构象，可靠评估不仅要判断几何是否合理，还需判断该几何是否与序列相容；现有评估器主要基于几何，缺乏序列—结构相容性的直接建模。

Method: 提出SIRGE（sequence-informed geometric evaluator），将结构表示以预训练RNA语言模型输出的核苷酸嵌入为条件进行建模；并设置与纯几何模型匹配的受控对比实验，以隔离序列条件化的作用。

Result: 初步结果显示SIRGE在Kendall-τ排序一致性、Top-1选择与Top-3排序指标上优于已有评估器；受控对比表明序列条件化可纠正同构几何模型的错误，并改善目标层级（target-level）的秩结构。

Conclusion: 预训练序列表示能提供与几何推理互补的排序信息，为RNA候选构象评估引入序列—几何联合建模提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Computational RNA structure pipelines generate many candidate conformations for the same sequence. Reliable evaluation therefore requires more than recognising plausible geometry, it requires determining whether that geometry is compatible with the sequence. We introduce SIRGE, a sequence-informed geometric evaluator that conditions structural representations on nucleotide embeddings from a pretrained RNA language model. Early results show that SIRGE outperforms established evaluators in Kendall--$τ$ alignment, Top-1 selection, and Top-3 ranking. Controlled comparisons further show that sequence conditioning corrects errors made by an otherwise matched geometric model and improves target-level rank structure. These findings provide initial evidence that pretrained sequence representations supply ranking information that complements geometric reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [When Information is Worth the Risk: Behavioral Valuation for Hazardous Robotic Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10726)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Aamodh Suresh,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将危险环境探索建模为"估值层"问题：固定信念更新、传感器模型、风险模型与有限时域规划器，仅改变用于排序可行路径的标量目标，提出基于Prelec概率加权的风险增强行为信息（Behavioral Information）目标，并证明该估值参数可在高信息高风险与低信息低风险路径间产生切换边界、重塑Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险机器人探索中，采集信息本身可能导致任务失败（如不安全地形、辐射、火灾、地雷或结构损伤）。高信息量路径可能使机器人暴露于危险、终止执行并阻断后续观测。因此不仅要判断何处不确定性最大，还要判断降低该不确定性是否值得承担相应风险，而现有信息规划多以纯信息量（如Shannon信息）为准则，缺乏对"信息-风险"权衡的可解释估值机制。

Method: 提出估值层视角：保持belief update、传感器模型、物理风险模型和有限时域信息规划器不变，仅替换用于对可行路径排序的标量目标。在该框架下引入基于Prelec概率加权的风险增强Behavioral Information目标，形成从保守到激进的可解释信息-风险估值族。理论上分析估值参数如何产生高信息/高风险与低信息/低风险路径间的切换边界，并导出可行探索策略上变换后的Pareto前沿结构。通过大规模失败截断（failure-truncated）栅格世界实验进行验证。

Result: 大规模失败截断栅格世界实验表明，仅凭估值即可重塑信息-风险前沿：Shannon信息规划仍是强原始信息基线，而风险感知目标可通过避免截断未来感知的失败来降低危险暴露与机器人损失；风险增强Behavioral估值与标准风险感知基线相比具有Pareto竞争力，并提供可解释的保守与中间机制。

Conclusion: 结果支持这样一种框架：机器人不仅推理某动作能降低多少不确定性，还应推理获得该不确定性降低是否值得所需承担的风险。估值层的设计（而非规划器或模型本身）足以系统性调节探索的保守—激进程度并改善信息-风险权衡。

Abstract: Hazardous robotic exploration requires robots to map spatial risks, such as unsafe terrain, radiation, fire, mines, or structural damage, while operating where collecting information can itself cause failure. A highly informative path may expose the robot to hazards, terminate execution, and prevent future observations. Hazardous exploration therefore requires deciding not only where uncertainty is largest, but when reducing it is worth the risk. This paper introduces a valuation-layer view of this problem. We keep the belief update, sensor model, physical risk model, and finite-horizon informative planner fixed, and change only the scalar objective used to rank feasible paths. Within this framework, we introduce a risk-augmented Behavioral Information objective based on Prelec probability weighting, yielding an interpretable family of conservative-to-aggressive information-risk valuations. Theoretically, we show that valuation parameters create switching boundaries between high-information/high-risk and lower-information/lower-risk paths, and induce a transformed Pareto-frontier structure over feasible exploration policies. Large-scale failure-truncated grid-world experiments show that valuation alone reshapes the information-risk frontier. Shannon information planning remains a strong raw-information baseline, while risk-aware objectives can reduce hazard exposure and robot losses by avoiding failures that truncate future sensing. Risk-augmented Behavioral valuation is Pareto-competitive with standard risk-aware baselines and provides interpretable conservative and intermediate regimes. These results support a framework in which robots reason not only about how much uncertainty an action reduces, but whether that reduction is worth the risk required to obtain it.

</details>


### [231] [Planning along Differentiable Charts of Constraint Manifolds with General-Purpose IK Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10905)
*Thomas Cohn,Seiji Shaw,Harel Biggie,Travis Manderson,Nicholas Roy,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出利用逆函数定理从正向运动学雅可比矩阵中恢复解析逆运动学参数化的梯度，并给出最小二乘域扩展与可达性约束的可优化描述，实现受约束机械臂轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械臂在运动学等式约束下的轨迹规划，其可行运动被限制在构型空间的零测度子流形上，需要特殊算法处理。用解析逆运动学(IK)对可行构型集参数化是可行思路，且梯度优化需要该参数化可微；但绝大多数IK函数由IKFast等自动元求解器生成，难以修改以获得可微性。

Method: 1) 借助逆函数定理，从普通的正向运动学雅可比矩阵恢复解析IK参数化所需梯度，无需修改IK求解器本身；2) 提出最小二乘形式的定义域扩展，使参数化在可达工作空间之外仍保持梯度信号；3) 给出利于优化的可达性约束描述形式。

Result: 通过数值实验与下游任务验证了方法有效性，包括RB-Y1机器人抓取箱子并放置到桌面上的硬件演示。

Conclusion: 该方法使自动化生成的解析IK函数可用于基于梯度的轨迹优化，并通过域扩展与可达性约束处理保持了工作空间外的梯度信息，在实际硬件任务中得到验证。

Abstract: Planning trajectories for robot manipulators under kinematic equality constraints restricts feasible motions to a measure-zero submanifold of the configuration space, requiring special algorithmic treatment. A promising strategy is parametrizing the set of feasible configurations using analytic inverse kinematics (IK). Bespoke analytic IK functions can be written to be differentiable, a necessary property for gradient-based trajectory optimization. But the vast majority of IK functions are computed by automated meta-solvers like IKFast, and are difficult to modify for differentiability. We present a new approach for computing gradients of analytic IK parameterizations: we leverage the inverse function theorem to recover the desired gradients from the ordinary forward kinematic Jacobian. Furthermore, we present a least-squares domain extension and an optimization-amenable description of the reachability constraint, which preserves gradient signal outside the reachable workspace. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through numerical experiments and downstream tasks, including a hardware demonstration of an RB-Y1 picking up a box and placing it on a table. Project website: https://cohnt.github.io/inverse-function-theorem-parameterization/

</details>


### [232] [IMLE-VLA: Fast Single-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10915)
*Kian Hosseinkhani,Qinhe Peng,George Shramko,Mehran Aghabozorgi,Jianing Qian,Tristan Engst,Alireza Moazeni,Dinesh Jayaraman,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMLE-VLA 用单步条件生成器（cIMLE）替换 VLA 中扩散/流匹配的迭代动作头，在保持多模态与泛化能力的同时消除多步采样，实现 3.67 倍推理频率提升与更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略通常将预训练视觉-语言骨干与连续动作头（扩散或流匹配）耦合，但这类动作头依赖多步迭代采样（如 π0.5 需 10 步 Euler 采样），造成推理瓶颈，导致机器人运动出现走走停停、任务完成变慢。因此需要一种既保留多模态动作覆盖与泛化能力、又能单步生成动作的高效动作头。

Method: 提出 IMLE-VLA：以单步条件生成器替换迭代动作头，采用条件隐式最大似然估计（cIMLE）目标进行训练。cIMLE 目标促进多模态动作覆盖，避免朴素回归头的模式坍缩，同时完全消除多步采样。将该动作头应用于 π0.5 进行验证。

Result: 应用于 π0.5 时，推理频率提升 3.67 倍（55 Hz vs. 15 Hz），动作吞吐量最高提升 11 倍。在 40 任务的 LIBERO 基准上，平均成功率最高（98.0%），且在推理频率上领先所有基线。在 LIBERO-plus 测试时扰动下，IMLE-VLA 保持 π0.5 的鲁棒性，而其他基线显著退化。真实机器人（Franka Emika Panda）四个任务上运动更平滑（jerk 降低 2.2–3.0 倍），任务完成更快，每个 episode 平均 VLA 推理时间减少 3.9–6.6 倍。

Conclusion: 单步 cIMLE 动作头可在不牺牲多模态覆盖与泛化鲁棒性的前提下，彻底消除迭代采样瓶颈，显著提升推理频率、动作吞吐与真实机器人运动平滑度，是替代扩散/流匹配动作头的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language backbones to achieve strong cross-task generalization. A leading design couples this backbone with a dedicated continuous action head trained via diffusion or flow matching. However, such heads rely on iterative multi-step sampling, for example 10 Euler steps in $π_{0.5}$. This creates an inference bottleneck that produces stop-and-go movement in the robot and slower task completion. We introduce IMLE-VLA, which replaces the iterative action head with a single-step conditional generator trained via conditional Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (cIMLE). The cIMLE objective promotes multimodal action coverage, avoiding the mode collapse of naive regression heads while eliminating multi-step sampling entirely. When IMLE-VLA is applied to $π_{0.5}$, it increases inference frequency 3.67x (55 Hz vs. 15 Hz), enabling up to 11x higher action throughput. On the 40-task LIBERO benchmark, IMLE-VLA achieves the highest average success rate (98.0%) among all baselines while leading in inference frequency. Under the test-time perturbations of LIBERO-plus, IMLE-VLA retains $π_{0.5}$'s robustness while other baselines degrade sharply, confirming that the cIMLE head preserves generalization. Real-world experiments on a Franka Emika Panda across four tasks demonstrate smoother motion (2.2x to 3.0x lower jerk) and faster task completion, with IMLE-VLA outperforming $π_{0.5}$ on every task and reducing average VLA inference time per episode by 3.9x to 6.6x. Videos and code are available at https://kianhk6.github.io/IMLE-VLA/

</details>


### [233] [ObstaDiff: Generalizable Diffusion Policy Learning via Obstacle-aware Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10918)
*Jiawen Wang,Kevin Yao,Khalid Jawed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ObstaDiff：一种分解式扩散策略框架，结合轻量级障碍感知视觉编码器，在杂乱农业场景中提升模仿学习的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习在机器人操作中表现优异，但多数方法假设干净背景，且缺乏显式的障碍物感知运动生成机制，难以泛化到非结构化障碍物密集的真实杂乱场景。

Method: 提出 ObstaDiff，采用分解式扩散策略框架与轻量级障碍感知视觉编码器，提取"目标-障碍物-背景"结构化表示；下游对齐策略据此生成朝向目标中心瓶颈位姿的末端执行器轨迹，并同时推理周围障碍物。

Result: 在真实温室场景中进行评估，每种方法61次试验，共366次执行。ObstaDiff 取得75.41%的平均任务成功率与8.20%的平均障碍物碰撞率，优于代表性模仿学习基线。

Conclusion: ObstaDiff 通过分解式扩散策略与障碍感知表示，有效提升了杂乱农业场景下的泛化能力与操作安全性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has achieved impressive results in robotic manipulation, yet most existing approaches assume clean backgrounds and lack explicit mechanisms for obstacle-aware motion generation. Extending such policies to cluttered, real-world scenes with unstructured obstacles remains a key generalization challenge. We present ObstaDiff, a decomposed diffusion-policy framework with a lightweight obstacle-aware visual encoder. ObstaDiff extracts a structured target-obstacle-background representation, enabling the downstream alignment policy to generate end-effector trajectories toward a target-centered bottleneck pose while reasoning about surrounding obstacles. We evaluate ObstaDiff on 61 real-robot greenhouse trials per method (366 executions in total). ObstaDiff achieves 75.41% average task success and 8.20% average obstacle collision rate, outperforming representative imitation-learning baselines and improving generalization in cluttered agricultural scenes.

</details>


### [234] [LTLDiff: Finite Linear Temporal Logic-Guided Data Generation and Diffusion Policies for Multi-agent Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11043)
*Chuhan Meng,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LTLDiff 将 LTLf 逻辑规范学习与扩散策略结合，用语言模型将自然语言指令转为 LTLf 公式并通过抽象语法树嵌入作为条件，实现逻辑引导的数据采集与策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体操作需满足时序、逻辑与安全约束，现有扩散策略存在去同步、动作顺序错误及协同失败等问题。

Method: 用大语言模型从自然语言指令中学习每个任务的 LTLf 公式；通过抽象语法树表示实现固定维度的逻辑嵌入；将该嵌入作为条件用于逻辑引导的数据采集和扩散策略训练，使轨迹符合所需顺序与协同要求。

Result: 在多智能体 LTLDiff 操作任务上，任务成功率相比基线有所提升。

Conclusion: LTLDiff 能有效支持协同多智能体操作，验证了逻辑规范引导扩散策略的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent robotic manipulation tasks require coordination among agents to satisfy task-level temporal, logical, and safety constraints. Recently, diffusion policies have been used to perform the task. However, they still suffer from desynchronization, incorrect action ordering, and coordination failures in tasks that require simultaneous or sequential multi-agent interaction. Therefore, LTLDiff is proposed as a framework that combines Finite Linear Temporal Logic (LTLf) specification learning for both the generation of demonstrations and learning via diffusion policies. Each task has a specific LTLf formula that is learned from a set of natural language instructions using a large-scale language model. To enable a fixed-dimensional vector embedding of the learned specification from the language model, LTLf uses an abstract syntax tree representation scheme. This embedding of logic serves as a condition for (i) logic-guided data collection and (ii) diffusion-based policy training, encouraging trajectories that are consistent with the desired ordering and coordination requirements. Experiments on multi-agent LTLDiff manipulation tasks demonstrate improved task success rates compared to the baseline. Together, these contributions demonstrate the effectiveness of LTLDiff for coordinated multi-agent manipulation.

</details>


### [235] [RIDE: Relocalization-Informed Depth Estimation with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11079)
*Jiarong Lian,Zhe Xiao,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Li,Ruizhi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RIDE，利用render–match–PnP重定位中产生的稀疏度量深度观测，结合预训练视频深度模型的几何先验，从机器人RGB流中估计稠密度量深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Render–match–PnP重定位可在查询图像像素与3D地图点之间建立对应关系用于相机位姿恢复，但其支撑稠密深度估计的潜力常被忽视。作者希望利用这一几何信息，在无需重复建图的条件下为机器人提供稠密度量深度感知。

Method: RIDE以度量尺度的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）模型为基础，将PnP-RANSAC内点对应关系导出的稀疏度量深度观测与预训练视频深度模型的几何先验相融合。针对观测不均且间歇的特点，方法引入全局与局部深度校正以及时序记忆机制，在度量尺度初始化后支持跨越短暂观测缺口的深度估计。模型在公开RGB-D视频上训练，并在机器人序列上零微调评估。

Result: 实验表明，相比仅做尺度标定的基线，RIDE在深度精度和时序一致性上均有提升，验证了定位几何同时支持位姿恢复与稠密机器人感知的可行性。

Conclusion: RIDE显示render–match–PnP重定位中的对应关系可被复用为稠密度量深度估计的监督信号，定位几何能够服务于机器人稠密感知，而不仅限于位姿恢复。

Abstract: Render--match--PnP relocalization establishes correspondences between query image pixels and 3D map points for camera pose recovery, but their potential to support dense depth estimation is often overlooked. To exploit this geometric information, we present RIDE, which estimates dense metric depth from a robot's RGB stream. Given a metrically scaled 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, RIDE combines sparse metric depth observations derived from PnP-RANSAC inlier correspondences with the geometric prior of a pretrained video-depth model. To handle uneven and intermittent observations, it integrates global and local depth correction with temporal memory, supporting depth estimation through short observation gaps after metric scale initialization. Trained on public RGB-D videos, RIDE is evaluated on robot sequences without fine tuning. Experiments show improved depth accuracy and temporal consistency over scale-only calibration, demonstrating how localization geometry can support both pose recovery and dense robot perception.

</details>


### [236] [Beyond Noise Steering: Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning for Generative Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11270)
*Pengfei Zhang,Teng Sun,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双潜在空间强化学习框架DLSRL，在冻结生成器中结合初始噪声引导与表示级控制，以加速机器人策略在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练生成式机器人策略虽能从示范中学习表达性动作先验，但现有强化学习方法仅调节噪声空间，无法在生成过程中调制中间动作表示，导致性能下降与效率低下。

Method: DLSRL框架中，actor网络预测两个潜在变量：引导行为生成的初始噪声潜在变量，以及用于中间特征调制的动作表示潜在变量；后者映射为适配器特征，通过残差连接注入中间动作token的隐藏状态，实现双控控制，无需更新基础策略。

Result: 在多种生成式策略架构与机器人操作任务上的实验表明，DLSRL能有效加速在线机器人策略自适应，并取得有竞争力的性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过在冻结生成器内部引入表示级控制，与初始噪声引导互补，DLSRL实现了对动作表示的直接调整，提升了生成式机器人策略的在线自适应效率与性能。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies learn expressive action priors from demonstrations. However, existing reinforcement learning methods only steer the noisy space but fail to modulate intermediate action representations during the generation process, resulting in performance degradation and inefficiency. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning (DLSRL) framework, which complements initial-noise steering with representation-level control inside the frozen generator. Specifically, our actor network predicts two distinct latent variables: an initial-noise latent variable that steers behavior generation, and an action-representation latent variable for intermediate feature modulation. Moreover, this representation latent variable is mapped to adapter features and ingeniously injected into the hidden states of intermediate action tokens via residual connections. Our dual-control design enables direct adjustment of action representations without updating the base policy. Experiments across generative policy architectures and robotic manipulation tasks show that DLSRL effectively accelerates online robot policy adaptation and achieves competitive performance. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}.

</details>


### [237] [2AM: Grounding Agent-Side Memory as Guidance for Steerable Action Models in Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11308)
*Yutong Hu,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2AM提出由多模态Agent独占任务记忆、单一RGB无状态动作模型负责执行的分工设计，在无深度/几何/规划器的条件下于LIBERO-Mem取得76.3%平均完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要记忆，但记忆不必置于动作策略内部。现有VLAs+规划器+几何工具的系统存在归因混淆：性能提升可能来自更丰富的观测或额外运动工具，失败也可能源自策略或语言接口欠定义。作者希望剥离这些混杂因素，检验记忆归属与接口带宽的作用。

Method: 采用刻意约束的设计：减少工具广度、提升接口带宽。多模态Agent作为任务记忆的唯一持有者，单一基于RGB、情节性无状态的动作模型作为任务相关运动的唯一执行者。Agent将交互历史编译为子任务语言及可选2D抓取、放置、移动提示，在不同时间尺度上绑定物理意图。为让VLA具备可操控性，扩充演示数据加入结构化提示标签，并在条件dropout、空间噪声和时间抖动下训练以容忍Agent输出的不完美。

Result: 在LIBERO-Mem上，不使用深度、在线几何或基于规划器的物体运动，2AM达到76.3%平均完成率，较最强基线14.8%提升61.5个百分点；同时取得63.0%松弛成功率和11.8%严格成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明任务记忆可以保留在Agent侧。进一步说明动作模型的能力不仅取决于策略学到了什么，还取决于Agent能多精确地操控它。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires memory, but not necessarily inside the action policy. To address such tasks, current agentic systems often combine VLAs with planners and geometric tools, sometimes using additional depth or calibrated geometry. These systems confound attribution: gains may come from richer observations or alternative motor tools, while failures may stem from either the policy or an under-specified language interface. We isolate this question through a deliberately constrained design: less tool breadth, but greater interface bandwidth. 2AM makes a multimodal Agent the sole holder of task memory and a single RGB-based, episodically stateless Action Model the sole executor of task-relevant motion. The Agent compiles interaction history into subtask language and optional 2D grasp, place, and move hints that bind its physical intention at different time scales. To teach this steerability to the VLA, we augment demonstrations with structured hint labels and train under condition dropout, spatial noise, and temporal jitter to tolerate imperfect Agent outputs. On LIBERO-Mem, without depth, online geometry, or planner-based object motion, 2AM reaches 76.3% average completion, a 61.5-point improvement over the strongest reported baseline of 14.8%, together with 63.0% relaxed and 11.8% strict success. These results show that task memory can remain Agent-side. They further show that Action Model capability depends not only on what the policy has learned, but on how precisely the Agent can steer it.

</details>


### [238] [Modular Kinematic Reduction of Closed-Chain Mechanisms Using Path Assembly and Defect Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11338)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出路径装配闭链微分映射（PACDM）框架，实现模块化闭链求解与运动学降维，在七自由度重载机械臂上验证，轨迹RMS误差低于8.5×10^-10 rad，预测-校正延拓比逐点同伦法快约45.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭链机构通过非线性闭合约束耦合主动与被动坐标，给模块化建模带来困难，需要一个能够模块化处理闭合约束并实现运动学降维的统一框架。

Method: 将每个闭合单元建模为两条具有共同端点的有序变换路径，路径失配用SE(3)上的对数映射表示，并由局部变换导数装配对应雅可比；由最小成对闭合单元构造多路径模块，通过秩揭示分析选取局部独立的标量约束；采用缺陷同伦沿可行且正则的延拓路径恢复满足闭合一致性的被动坐标；在正则位形下用隐式微分得到局部主动-被动微分映射，并用于预设运动的预测-校正延拓。

Result: 在含双路径与三路径闭链模块的七自由度重载机械臂上评估，与Simscape Multibody对比，轨迹均方根误差低于8.5×10^-10 rad；预测-校正延拓比在每个轨迹采样点都使用缺陷同伦法快约45.8倍。

Conclusion: PACDM通过路径装配与微分映射，为模块化闭链求解和运动学降维提供了高精度、高效率的统一方案，适合用于闭链机械臂的建模与轨迹延续计算。

Abstract: Closed kinematic chains complicate modular modeling by coupling active and passive coordinates through nonlinear closure constraints. This paper presents a Path-Assembled Closure Differential Mapping (PACDM) framework for modular closure resolution and kinematic reduction. Each closure element compares two ordered transformation paths with common endpoints, with their mismatch expressed through the logarithm on SE(3) and the corresponding Jacobian assembled from local transformation derivatives. Multi-path modules are constructed from a minimal set of pairwise closure elements, while rank-revealing analysis selects locally independent scalar constraints. A defect homotopy recovers closure-consistent passive coordinates from approximate estimates along a feasible and regular continuation path. At regular configurations, implicit differentiation yields the local active-to-passive differential mapping, which is subsequently used in a predictor-corrector continuation procedure for prescribed motion. The framework is evaluated on a seven-degree-of-freedom heavy-duty manipulator containing two-path and three-path closed-chain modules. Comparison with Simscape Multibody yields trajectory root-mean-square errors below 8.5 x 10^-10 rad, while predictor-corrector continuation is approximately 45.8 times faster than applying defect homotopy at every trajectory sample.

</details>


### [239] [Morphology-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Wheeled-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11357)
*Chenbo Xia,Chao Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套可复现流水线，将多数据集SMPLX人体动作转换为Galaxea R1 Pro轮式人形机器人的可执行移动-操作行为，涵盖形体预处理、平面基座归一化、形态感知差分逆运动学、肩部层级手臂重定向与连续躯干替换，并通过参考扭转驱动规划与Isaac Lab中21维BaseDecode策略实现物理可跟踪的轮式人形移动-操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类到人形机器人的动作重定向研究多集中于足式平台，轮式人形系统在从通用人体动作中同时支持耦合的移动与操作方面研究较少。R1 Pro缺少腿部关节，必须将人体下肢动作重新分配到基座运动与躯干姿态上，同时保持手臂操作的几何关系，这构成了核心挑战。

Method: 基于GMR的可配置通用动作重定向与BeyondMimic的物理仿真R1 Pro学习框架，构建流水线：规范形体预处理、平面基座归一化、形态感知差分逆运动学、以肩部为根的分层手臂重定向、面向弯腰与下蹲的连续躯干替换；随后由参考扭转驱动的规划层在滞回、运动学连续性、加速度与执行器速率约束下，将平面基座运动解码为连续三轮转向与滚动指令；最后在Isaac Lab中训练具有方向性关节限位缩放、聚焦上身跟踪与分阶段车轮接触奖励的21维BaseDecode策略。

Result: 系统形成了从人体动作数据到物理可跟踪的轮式人形移动-操作行为的完整桥梁，而非仅用于可视化的重定向器。摘要未给出定量策略对比结果，相关量化比较被安排至后续修订版本。

Conclusion: 该工作为轮式人形机器人的耦合移动-操作提供了一条可复现的人体动作重定向与仿真学习流水线，弥补了足式平台研究之外的空白；未来工作包括补充定量策略比较，以进一步完善验证。

Abstract: Human-to-humanoid retargeting has largely been studied on legged platforms, while comparatively few wheeled-humanoid systems support coupled locomotion and manipulation from general human motion. Building on GMR's configurable general-motion retargeting and BeyondMimic's physically simulated R1 Pro learning framework, we present a reproducible pipeline that converts multi-dataset SMPLX motion into executable loco-manipulation behavior for the Galaxea R1 Pro wheeled humanoid. The robot has a planar three-wheel base, a serial torso, and two arms but no leg joints, so human lower-body motion must be redistributed across base motion and torso posture without sacrificing manipulation-relevant arm geometry. Our pipeline combines canonical body-shape preprocessing, planar-base normalization, morphology-aware differential inverse kinematics, shoulder-rooted hierarchical arm retargeting, and continuous torso substitution for bending and squatting. A reference-twist-driven planning layer then decodes planar base motion into continuous three-wheel steering and rolling commands subject to hysteresis, kinematic continuity, acceleration, and actuator-rate limits. Finally, a 21-dimensional BaseDecode policy is trained in Isaac Lab with directional joint-limit scaling, focused upper-body tracking, and a staged wheel-contact reward. The resulting system provides a complete bridge from human motion data to physically trackable wheeled-humanoid loco-manipulation rather than a visualization-only retargeter; quantitative policy comparisons remain scheduled for a later revision.

</details>


### [240] [GeoTrussRover: Morphological Computation with Contact-Semantic Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11361)
*Muyuan Ma,Yi Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuanyan Zheng,Ruiqi Hu,Boxuan Ke,Zhenyu Chen,Yicong Lin,Xin Hao Yang,Daliang Xiao,Zhinan Hou,Wanhao Niu,Yuan Sun,Yan Yang,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoTrussRover，将电驱动可变几何桁架（VGT）与轮式移动底座结合，通过接触语义形态规划与控制解决高维协调问题，实现低维任务表示与高效复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构机器人可通过改变接触几何来跨越固定躯体无法通过的障碍，VGT能将形态变化分布于承载结构中，但与移动底座耦合后会产生高维协调难题，亟需一种高效的规划与控制方法。

Method: 求解单一源穿越任务并提取四个接触语义基元，描述21个构件的协调关系；采用物理约束投影将基元适配到具有相同接触拓扑的新台阶高度；当所有阶段可行时不重算完整运动，若某阶段违反新物理约束则仅重算该阶段；最后用全空间QP跟踪适配运动并修正构件与轮子误差。

Result: 在0.10m到0.075m的迁移中，相比完全重算，目标函数评估次数减少63.7%；接触阶段可行性分析覆盖台阶高度0.10至0.46m（即1.08至4.97倍轮半径），上限接近理论可行边界；电驱动样机可穿越2.11倍轮半径。

Conclusion: 该方法以低维表示在超冗余承载形态中存储任务协调信息，并在运动过程中复用，验证了接触语义形态规划在变几何桁架移动机器人上的有效性。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots can change their contact geometry when a fixed body cannot negotiate an obstacle. A variable-geometry truss (VGT) distributes this shape change through a load-bearing structure, but coupling it to a mobile base creates a high-dimensional coordination problem. GeoTrussRover combines an electrically actuated VGT, a wheeled base, and contact-semantic morphology planning and control. We solve one source traversal and extract four contact-semantic primitives that describe coordination among 21 members. Physics-constrained projection adapts them to unseen step heights with the same contact topology. When every phase remains feasible, adaptation does not recompute the complete motion. If one phase violates the new physical constraints, only that phase is recomputed. A full-space QP then tracks the adapted motion and corrects member and wheel errors. For transfer from 0.10m to 0.075m, the method reduces objective-function evaluations by 63.7% relative to full recomputation. Contact-phase feasibility analysis covers step heights from 0.10 to 0.46m, or 1.08 to 4.97 wheel radii, with the upper value near the theoretical feasible boundary. The electric prototype traverses 2.11 wheel radii. The resulting low-dimensional representation stores task coordination in a hyper-redundant, load-bearing morphology and reuses it during locomotion.
