</details>


### [241] [SwarmNxt: Open-source Software-Hardware Platform for Fast and Agile Aerial Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11382)
*Charbel Toumieh,Niel Mistry,Benjamin Jarvis,Simon Jeger,Peize Liu,Shaojie Shen,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SwarmNxt——一个基于开源 OmniNxt 无人机硬件的开源软件平台，提供硬件装配教程、并行部署与集群升级自动化工具，以及基于 ROS 2 的自主导航框架，并通过六机去中心化规划避碰与四机机载深度估计集群飞行两项实机实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人集群有望变革时间敏感的安全、安保与搜救任务，通过多机协同可快速勘测灾区、建图 GPS 拒止环境并搜索杂乱区域，从而缩短响应时间、降低一线人员风险。但实现该潜力需要鲁棒的自主集群导航，这仍是活跃的研究难题；同时现有平台制约进展：商用无人机多为闭源，或缺乏敏捷视觉集群飞行所需的机载算力；此外在多台空中机器人上开发、部署与维护软件需要大量工程投入。

Method: 提出 SwarmNxt，一个构建于开源 OmniNxt 无人机硬件之上的开源软件平台。其提供端到端工具链：含视频教程的详细硬件装配说明；用于并行软件部署与集群级更新的自动化工具；以及基于 ROS 2 的自主导航框架。平台将先进的控制、规划与深度估计集成到单一 ROS 2 多智能体系统中，为实体集群实验提供开放研究基础设施。

Result: 通过两项真实世界实验验证：一是六机集群执行去中心化规划，并实现高速机间避碰；二是四机集群在充满障碍的环境中执行带机载深度估计的集群飞行。两项实验均在室内进行，全局位置来自外部动捕系统；感知、规划与控制均在机载运行。

Conclusion: SwarmNxt 以开源软硬件一体化的方式降低了空中集群研究与实验的门槛，为自主集群导航提供了可复用的开放研究基础设施；实验表明其在真实场景中可支撑去中心化规划、高速避碰与机载深度估计下的集群飞行。

Abstract: Aerial robot swarms have the potential to transform time-critical safety, security, and search-and-rescue operations. By coordinating multiple robots, they can rapidly survey disaster sites, map collapsed or GPS-denied environments, and search cluttered areas faster than a single robot, reducing response times and minimizing risks to first responders. Realizing this potential, however, requires robust autonomous swarm navigation, which remains an active research challenge. Progress is further constrained by existing platforms, as commercial drones are often closed-source or lack the onboard computational resources needed for agile, vision-based collective flight. Moreover, developing, deploying, and maintaining software across multiple aerial robots requires significant engineering effort. To address these challenges, we present SwarmNxt, an open-source software platform built on the open-source OmniNxt drone hardware. SwarmNxt provides an end-to-end toolkit, including detailed hardware assembly instructions with a video tutorial, automation tools for parallel software deployment and swarm-wide updates, and a ROS 2-based framework for autonomous navigation. The platform integrates state-of-the-art control, planning, and depth estimation into a single ROS 2 multi-agent system, providing an open research infrastructure for physical swarm experimentation. We validate SwarmNxt through two real-world experiments: a six-drone swarm performing decentralized planning with high-speed inter-drone collision avoidance, and a four-drone swarm executing collective flight with onboard depth estimation in an obstacle-filled environment. Both experiments were run indoors with global position from external motion capture; perception, planning, and control run onboard.

</details>


### [242] [Safety-aware Skill Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11433)
*A K M Nadimul Haque,Sheila Sutjipto,Marc G. Carmichael,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Dist-GPRL，一种距离感知与安全引导的强化学习框架，基于高斯过程对技能参数化，通过顺序自适应稀疏轨迹路点的局部窗口实现结构化机器人技能自适应，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证其在成功率、碰撞率与学习稳定性上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的技能自适应框架为维持稳定性往往需要固定观测或严格控制的探索调度等限制性假设。在杂乱且动态的环境中，无约束探索易导致不安全行为与学习不稳定，尤其当任务相关观测靠近障碍物或涉及运动物体时。因此需要一种兼顾安全性、局部性与时序一致性的技能自适应方法。

Method: 构建于基于高斯过程（GP）的技能参数化之上，顺序自适应稀疏轨迹路点的重叠局部窗口，而非在每个策略步修改完整技能；通过 GP 协方差结构关联原始策略输出，产生时序连贯的轨迹更新，并降低动作空间维度与全局轨迹自适应的信用分配难度。安全引导包含两部分：由 Hausdorff Approximation Planner（HAP）导出的安全子空间先验，将策略探索偏向可行区域；动态更新的距离场间隙与梯度奖励提供局部障碍感知。此外引入轨迹运动学相似性正则项，在自适应过程中保持示教的速度与加速度特性。

Result: 在两个动态物体操作任务的仿真中进行评估，并将学习到的策略迁移到真实机器人执行。实验结果表明，相比基线方法，该框架具有更高的任务成功率、更低的碰撞频率和更稳定的学习过程，同时保持示教技能的运动学特性。

Conclusion: Dist-GPRL 通过 GP 技能参数化、局部窗口顺序自适应、安全子空间先验、动态距离场奖励与运动学相似性正则化的结合，有效缓解了动态杂乱环境下探索不安全与学习不稳定问题，并具备从仿真到真实机器人的迁移能力。

Abstract: Skill adaptation frameworks based on reinforcement learning often require restrictive assumptions to maintain stability, such as fixed observations or tightly controlled exploration schedules. In cluttered and dynamic environments, however, unrestricted exploration can lead to unsafe behaviour and unstable learning, particularly when task-relevant observations lie near obstacles or involve moving objects. In this work, we present Dist-GPRL, a distance-aware and safety-guided reinforcement learning framework for structured robot skill adaptation. Building upon Gaussian Process (GP)-based skill parameterisation, our framework sequentially adapts overlapping local windows of sparse trajectory via-points rather than modifying the complete skill at every policy step. Raw policy outputs are correlated through the GP covariance structure, producing temporally coherent trajectory updates while reducing the action-space and credit-assignment difficulties associated with global trajectory adaptation. Safety is incorporated through two complementary forms of guidance. A safe-subspace prior derived from the Hausdorff Approximation Planner (HAP) biases policy exploration toward feasible regions, while dynamically updated distance field clearance and gradient rewards provide local obstacle awareness. A trajectory-kinematics similarity regulariser further preserves the demonstrated velocity and acceleration characteristics during adaptation. We evaluate the framework on two dynamic object-manipulation tasks in simulation and transfer the learned policy to real-world robot execution. Experimental results demonstrate higher task success, lower collision frequency, and more stable learning than the baselines, while preserving the kinematic characteristics of the demonstrated skill.

</details>


### [243] [CARLAverse: A Highly Modular, Distributed, and Multimodal Framework for Human-in-the-Loop Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11478)
*Patrick Rebling,Philipp Nenninger,Reiner Kriesten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARLAverse：面向混合交通场景的开源多模态人在环仿真生态，通过分布式物理架构解耦触觉控制回路与网络瓶颈，实现跨机构高保真交互实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需在涉及弱势道路使用者（VRU）的混合交通场景中充分测试，而纯人工代理难以复现真实的人类社会协商行为；现有多智能体人在环（HITL）仿真平台难以同时满足高保真力反馈所需的网络延迟与同步约束。

Method: 提出开源多模态仿真生态CARLAverse，基于模块化硬件抽象，将驾驶（DrivoCARLA）、骑行（CycloCARLA）与行人（WalkoCARLA）仿真器集成到同一虚拟环境；核心为分布式物理架构：延迟敏感的自身车辆动力学与高频力反馈在客户端本地计算，中央CARLA服务器负责NPC物理与全局交通编排。

Result: 通过将触觉控制回路与网络瓶颈解耦，CARLAverse 在不牺牲物理沉浸感的前提下支持可扩展的跨机构 HITL 实验。

Conclusion: CARLAverse 为混合交通中人类社会协商研究提供了可扩展、跨机构的多模态 HITL 仿真基础设施，代码与文档已开源。

Abstract: The development of autonomous driving demands comprehensive testing in mixed-traffic scenarios involving vulnerable road users (VRUs), where purely artificial agents often fail to capture authentic human social negotiations. While human-in-the-loop (HITL) simulators enable safe investigation of these interactions, existing multi-agent platforms struggle with the network latency and synchronization constraints required for high-fidelity haptic feedback. To resolve this, we present CARLAverse, an open-source, multimodal simulation ecosystem. Extending modular hardware abstraction, CARLAverse integrates driving (DrivoCARLA), cycling (CycloCARLA), and pedestrian (WalkoCARLA) simulators into a shared virtual environment. Its core methodological contribution is a distributed physics architecture: latency-critical ego dynamics and high-frequency force feedback are computed locally on client nodes, while a central CARLA server orchestrates non-player character (NPC) physics and global traffic. By decoupling haptic control loops from network bottlenecks, CARLAverse enables scalable, cross-institutional HITL experiments without compromising physical immersion. Code and documentation: https://git.ieem-ka.de/simulator-environments/carlaverse

</details>


### [244] [Memory as Plans: World-Action Modeling with Memory-Grounded Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11561)
*Sizhe Zhao,Haozhe Xie,Weiyu Zhao,Chenchu Zhang,Huan Wang,Chenyang Wang,Qinglin Liu,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MaP-WAM将记忆依赖的世界-动作建模分解为"记忆驱动的规划"与"计划条件的执行"，用长期多模态情景上下文作为规划期证据，使执行器上下文长度保持固定，在RMBench达83.3%、真机达78.0%成功率，且推理延迟随历史增长近似恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流机器人策略多采用马尔可夫式建模范式，但许多真实世界复杂操作任务本质上是非马尔可夫的，需要超越当前观测的长期记忆。现有记忆机制或依赖语言摘要、或使用不断增长的视觉窗口及其组合，容易丢失细粒度视觉证据，或在历史覆盖度与执行效率之间面临权衡。

Method: 提出Memory-as-Plans（MaP-WAM）框架，将记忆依赖的世界-动作建模分解为记忆驱动的规划与计划条件的执行。将记忆表示为包含语言指令与稀疏视觉上下文的已完成片段记录（情景记忆），并转换为紧凑计划，包括下一片段级语言计划及对应视觉引导。由World-Action-Progress（WAP）模型在推理时联合预测动作块与相应执行进度，以未知时长执行每个计划；通过计划-观测对齐校准预测进度，实现自适应片段切换与闭环上下文更新。结构化注意力支持规划与执行两阶段的键值缓存。

Result: 在RMBench上取得最先进性能，成功率83.3%；真实机器人任务成功率达78.0%；随任务历史增长，执行器推理延迟保持近似恒定。

Conclusion: 通过在规划期注入长期多模态情景记忆、在执行期以固定长度上下文运行，MaP-WAM在不牺牲历史覆盖度的前提下兼顾了细粒度视觉证据保留与执行效率，验证了"记忆即计划"分解在长时程非马尔可夫操作任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Mainstream robotic policies often adopt a Markovian formulation, but many complex real-world manipulation tasks are inherently non-Markovian, requiring long-horizon memory beyond the current observation. Existing memory mechanisms often rely on language summaries, growing visual windows, or their combinations, and may therefore lose fine-grained visual evidence or face a trade-off between history coverage and execution efficiency. We introduce MaP-WAM, a Memory-as-Plans framework that decomposes memory-dependent world-action modeling into memory-grounded planning and plan-conditioned execution, and uses long-term multimodal episodic context as planning-time evidence rather than repeatedly conditioning the executor on the full history. MaP-WAM represents memory as completed segment records containing language instructions and sparse visual context, and converts this episodic memory into compact plans comprising the next segment-level language plan and corresponding visual guidance. A World-Action-Progress (WAP) model executes each plan over an unknown duration by jointly predicting action chunks and corresponding execution progress at inference time, calibrating predicted progress through plan-observation alignment for adaptive segment transitions and closed-loop context updates. MaP-WAM keeps the executor context length fixed, while structured attention further enables key-value caching in both planning and execution. MaP-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RMBench with an 83.3% success rate and attains 78.0% success on real-robot tasks, while maintaining approximately constant executor inference latency as task history grows.

</details>


### [245] [Quasi-static analysis of passive stability in a novel underactuated multi-finger hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11579)
*Léonie Plancoulaine,Sylvain Guégan,Franck Plestan,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种准静态解析方法评估欠驱动多指手的被动稳定性，并设计集成差动弹簧滑块机构的三指手，分析其对抓取行为及物体尺寸对稳定平衡位姿的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动机械手虽能以减少的驱动器数量实现自适应、鲁棒的抓取，但预测被抓物体的稳定平衡位姿仍是重大挑战。

Method: 引入准静态解析方法评估被动稳定性；设计新型三指手架构，集成差动弹簧加载滑块机构；聚焦差动机构对整体抓取行为的影响，并针对圆柱形与球形两种典型抓取类型分析物体尺寸对稳定平衡构型的作用。

Result: 分析揭示了差动机构如何影响整体抓取行为，并给出物体尺寸对圆柱形与球形抓取稳定平衡构型的影响规律。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够评估欠驱动多指手的被动稳定性，差动弹簧滑块机构有助于实现多样且自适应的抓取，物体尺寸显著影响稳定平衡位姿。

Abstract: Underactuated robotic hands achieve adaptive and robust grasping with a reduced number of actuators, but predicting the stable equilibrium pose of the grasped object remains a significant challenge. This paper introduces a quasi-static analytical approach to assess passive stability in underactuated multi-finger hands. A novel three-finger hand architecture integrating a differential spring-loaded slider mechanism is introduced, enabling versatile and adaptive grasping. The study focuses on how the differential mechanism influences the overall grasp behavior and analyzes the effect of object size on the stable equilibrium configurations for two canonical grasp types: cylindrical and spherical.

</details>


### [246] [ActSafeGuard: Differentiable and Training-Aligned Constraint Enforcement for Flow-Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11697)
*Jianming Ma,Rongjun Jin,Xiaxi Si,Yang Zhang,Yiheng Li,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ActSafeGuard——一种可微且与训练对齐的流匹配策略安全防护层，将硬动作可行性约束融入策略学习，通过解析式射线缩放算子实现边界感知梯度，在多个骨干网络上实现100%逐步安全率且保持甚至提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA与WAM在通用机器人操作中能力强劲，但生成的动作可能违反硬物理约束，导致部署时不安全或不可行。现有安全方法要么仅优化统计安全目标而缺乏确定性的逐步保证，要么仅在推理阶段纠正不安全动作，造成策略训练与执行之间的不一致。

Method: 提出ActSafeGuard，一种面向流匹配（flow-matching）策略的可微、训练对齐安全防护层。其核心在于将硬动作可行性集成进策略学习而非作为推理时的外部组件；采用解析式射线缩放（ray-scaling）算子设计，产生边界感知梯度，引导模型自然学习受约束流形。

Result: 在多个标准基础骨干网络（π_{0.5}与Fast-WAM）及多种任务上开展大量实验，ActSafeGuard持续实现100%的逐步安全率，同时完全保持甚至提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: ActSafeGuard为安全具身智能部署提供了可扩展且侵入性最小的解决方案，弥合了训练与执行之间的安全鸿沟。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World-Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet their generated actions may violate hard physical constraints and therefore be unsafe or infeasible for deployment. Existing safety approaches either optimize statistical safety objectives without deterministic per-step guarantees or correct unsafe actions only during inference, creating a mismatch between policy training and execution. We introduce ActSafeGuard, a differentiable and training-aligned safeguard layer for flow-matching based policies. ActSafeGuard integrates hard action feasibility into policy learning, not merely treating safety as an inference-time external component. Through an analytical ray-scaling operator design, ActSafeGuard enables boundary-aware gradients to guide the model to naturally learn constrained manifolds. Extensive experiments on multiple standard foundation backbones ($π_{0.5}$ and Fast-WAM) across various tasks demonstrate that ActSafeGuard consistently achieves a $100\%$ step safety rate while fully preserving or even boosting task success rates, providing a scalable and minimally invasive solution for safe embodied AI deployment.

</details>


### [247] [Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular Reinforcement Learning for Muscle-Driven Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11733)
*Jian Zhou,Xingyu Zhang,Rui Ma,Yu Cao,Shane Xie,Zhi-qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"反射引导的神经肌肉强化学习"框架，用固定相位依赖反射控制器作为底层机制，强化学习策略输出四个生物力学残差参数调节反射增益与阈值，实现生理可信且鲁棒的肌肉驱动运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉驱动运动虽能生成物理上合理的真实人体运动，但难以同时兼顾生理合理性与对肌肉骨骼能力变化及外界扰动的适应性，这是该领域的基本挑战。

Method: 构建Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular Reinforcement Learning框架：底层为固定的相位依赖反射控制器作为神经肌肉控制机制；上层强化学习策略根据当前状态产生四个具有生物力学意义的残差参数，用于调节与髋关节摆动、膝关节支撑和踝关节推进相关的关键反射增益和阈值。

Result: 在正常行走条件下，该框架生成生理合理的运动，具有更高的运动学精度和动力学一致性，以及更好的双侧对称性和步态间一致性；在肌肉无力和外部扰动下，学习到的策略无需重新训练仍保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 将固定反射控制器与强化学习残差调节相结合，可有效兼顾肌肉驱动运动的生理可信性与对肌肉能力变化和外部扰动的适应性，且具备免重训的泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Muscle-driven locomotion provides a physically grounded approach to generating realistic human movement. However, achieving both physiological plausibility and adaptability to changes in musculoskeletal capacity and external disturbances remains a fundamental challenge. To address this limitation, we propose a Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular Reinforcement Learning framework for muscle-driven locomotion. Within this framework, a fixed phase-dependent reflex controller serves as the underlying neuromuscular control mechanism, while the reinforcement learning policy produces four biomechanically meaningful residual parameters to modulate key reflex gains and thresholds associated with hip swing, knee support, and ankle propulsion according to the current state. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework generates physiologically plausible locomotion with improved kinematic accuracy and dynamic consistency, as well as better bilateral symmetry and stride-to-stride consistency under nominal walking conditions. The learned policy remains robust under muscle weakness and external perturbations without retraining.

</details>


### [248] [SEED-UMI: Sharing the Exoskeleton between human and robot for onE-to-one Dexterous demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11753)
*Tengbo Yu,Jiahao Wu,Daohan Li,Bingxu Chen,Hao Liu,Xiaojian Ma,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SEED-UMI框架，让人类与机器人佩戴相同外骨骼，将重定向转化为配对跨具身监督，实现接触丰富任务的高效模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手模仿学习受限于难以采集能够忠实迁移到机器人的接触丰富示范数据。已有可穿戴外骨骼系统仅记录人类一侧数据，并通过在自由空间标定的开环映射进行重定向，在接触场景下性能退化。

Method: 人类与机器人佩戴同一外骨骼：关节编码器成为物理共享的测量量；安装于外骨骼的腕部相机在人类数据采集与机器人策略部署期间观测相同的外部机构。由此将重定向转化为配对的跨具身监督，并使策略可直接在原始以骨骼为中心的腕部图像上训练，无需分割或图像修复。

Result: 在五项接触丰富任务上，SEED-UMI的数据采集效率比基于外骨骼的遥操作高3.0倍，平均部署成功率达70.0%。

Conclusion: 共享外骨骼的配对跨具身监督可有效缓解接触下的重定向退化问题，提升灵巧手模仿学习的数据效率与部署成功率。

Abstract: Imitation learning for dexterous hands is bottlenecked by the difficulty of collecting contact-rich demonstrations that transfer faithfully to the robot. Prior wearable-exoskeleton systems record only on the human side and retarget via open-loop mappings calibrated in free space, which degrade under contact. We present SEED-UMI, a framework in which both the human and the robot wear the same exoskeleton: joint encoders become a physically shared measurement, and wrist cameras mounted to the exoskeleton observe the same outer mechanism during both human data collection and robot policy rollouts. This turns retargeting into paired cross-embodiment supervision and lets policies train directly on raw exoskeleton-centric wrist images, without segmentation or inpainting. On five contact-rich tasks, SEED-UMI achieves 3.0x greater data collection efficiency than exoskeleton-based teleoperation and a 70.0% average rollout success rate.

</details>


### [249] [Visual-SLAM for the detection of hidden tomatoes in greenhouses by Hierarchical Localization and GLOMAPfor robotized harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11766)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco,Francisco Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单目相机的低成本视觉SLAM系统，面向温室番茄作物三维建图，结合GLOMAP（SfM）与分层定位工具箱，实现粗到精的定位与重建，并用手工真值验证了几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室作物监测日益重要，但高性能传感器（LiDAR、立体相机）成本高昂，限制了大规模农业应用，因此需要一种更具成本效益的作物建图方案。

Method: 在Agroconnect实验温室中对真实番茄串进行测试；开发ROS 2 Humble节点采集图像并离线处理；将基于Structure-From-Motion的GLOMAP建图器与Hierarchical Localization工具箱集成；采用粗到精的分层定位策略，先全局检索生成位置假设，再在候选区域内匹配局部特征。

Result: 系统正确识别出番茄簇，并能有效表征被遮挡、传统视觉技术难以获取的番茄；重建的3D模型经果实尺寸、质心位置和方向的手工地面真值测量验证，确认了几何精度。

Conclusion: 该低成本单目视觉SLAM流水线可为温室作物提供几何准确的三维建图，为后续生长模式分析与农业管理优化算法奠定基础。

Abstract: Advanced crop monitoring inside greenhouses is becoming one of the primary objectives of research centers. High-performance sensors, such as LiDAR or stereo cameras, have traditionally been employed for this purpose, though these often have a high cost. This work proposes a Visual-SLAM system using a monocular camera, which is significantly more cost-effective and specifically tailored for agricultural applications, such as mapping tomato crops in a greenhouse. Tests were carried out on a real tomato bunch, located in the Agroconnect experimental greenhouse. A ROS 2 Humble node was developed to run on the robot in order to capture images of these crops, which were then stored for offline processing. To generate a 3D mapped model for the crop in the greenhouse, the GLOMAP mapper, based on Structure-From-Motion, was integrated with the Hierarchical Localization toolbox. This initial mapping is a foundation for future, more advanced algorithms to analyze growth patterns, and optimize agricultural management. The system leverages a hierarchical localization paradigm based on a coarse-to-fine strategy: it first performs global retrieval to generate location hypotheses, then combines local features within the identified candidate regions. The results show a correct identification of the tomato cluster, correctly characterising the tomato that is occluded and inaccessible by classical vision technologies. The reconstructed 3D model was further validated against manual ground-truth measurements of fruit size, centroid position, and orientation, confirming the geometric accuracy of the proposed low-cost monocular pipeline.

</details>


### [250] [Rapid Learning of Dexterous In-Hand Pen Writing through Real-Time Jacobian Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11775)
*Kai Stewart,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于实时雅可比估计的具身控制方法，无需解析手-物模型、仿真训练或预采集示教，即可让拟人手通过纯手内运动握笔书写任意单笔轨迹，在物理机器人上实现亚毫米级平面精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟人手手内操作是机器人灵巧性的未解前沿。手-物交互接触丰富且高度动态，学习类方法（RL/IL）通常需要大量建模或数据采集：现代仿真器难以复现所需接触复杂度，而采集灵巧示教本身也是开放难题。因此作者希望绕开计算与数据密集的范式，寻找计算简单、数据高效且与具体本体无关的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于物理机器人上手-物组合系统实时任务雅可比估计的具身控制方法。控制器在笔记本电脑上仅用 CPU 运行，约 18 秒初始化后即开始手内书写，并在无解析手-物运动学/接触模型、无仿真训练、无预采集任务示教的条件下在线自适应。同一估计器/控制器形式在三种拟人机械手系统（一台物理、两台仿真）上验证，实现本体无关的表述。

Result: 在物理机器人上，空中与纸上书写的字母和图形均达到亚毫米级平面精度（各次运行平均 0.6 mm）。同一估计器/控制器在三套拟人手系统上均有效，展现出人似的握笔手内关节运动能力。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首次展示拟人手仅通过手内运动、握笔书写任意单笔轨迹。该工作表明，通过计算简单、数据高效的算法即可实现灵巧操作，为 RL、IL 等计算与数据密集型方法提供了替代思路。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation of a grasped object with an anthropomorphic hand is an unsolved frontier for robot dexterity. The contact-richness and highly dynamic nature of object-hand interactions tend to require extensive modeling or data-collection efforts for learning-based approaches. Modern simulators used for reinforcement learning (RL) cannot fully replicate the required contact complexity, while collecting dexterous demonstrations for imitation learning (IL) remains an open problem. In this research, we present an embodied control approach based on real-time task Jacobian estimation of the combined hand and object system on the physical robot. Using only the CPU on a laptop, the proposed controller begins in-hand pen writing after approximately 18 s of initialization and continues to adapt online, without an analytic hand--object kinematic/contact model, simulation training, or precollected task demonstrations. We demonstrate that the same estimator/controller formulation works on three anthropomorphic robotic hand systems (one physical, two simulated) to show human-like, in-hand articulation of a grasped pen by an embodiment-independent formulation. Sub-millimeter in-plane precision (mean 0.6 mm across runs) is achieved across letters and shapes written in the air and on paper on a physical robot. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an anthropomorphic hand writing arbitrary single-stroke trajectories with a grasped pen through purely in-hand motion, and it showcases an alternative to compute- and data-heavy approaches such as RL and IL for achieving dexterous manipulation through computationally simple and data-efficient algorithms.

</details>


### [251] [EVPeriscope: Extended Perception across Aerial and Ground Vehicles with Event-based Propeller Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11920)
*Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EVPeriscope——一种基于事件相机的空地对相对感知系统，通过地面机器人上的朝上事件相机检测四旋翼螺旋桨高频视觉特征，实现检测、定位与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧密协同的异构空地团队需要可靠的空-地相对定位，但传统帧式相机与基准标记易受运动模糊、光照变化和载荷约束影响，在野外复杂环境中难以稳定工作。

Method: 在地面机器人上安装朝上事件相机，利用螺旋桨旋转产生的高频视觉特征对四旋翼进行检测与定位，并据此设计闭环控制器；系统全部基于机载感知与计算运行于200 Hz。

Result: 在最高15 mph风速的恶劣野外条件下，于白天和夜间均完成实验；实现密集植被遮挡下地面机器人传感器失效时的定位与闭环导航。

Conclusion: 事件相机可有效克服帧式相机在运动模糊和光照变化下的局限，使四旋翼成为地面机器人的扩展感知平台，验证了"袋鼠式"空地协同系统在复杂环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Reliable relative localization between aerial and ground robots is a key requirement for tightly coordinated heterogeneous teams. This can be difficult to do using conventional frame-based cameras and fiducial markers because they are sensitive to motion blur, lighting variations, and payload constraints. This paper presents EVPeriscope, an event-based perception system that enables detection, localization and control of a quadrotor using an upward-facing event camera on a ground robot by detecting the high-frequency visual signature of its propellers. This system allows the quadrotor to function as an extended perception system for the ground robot when onboard sensors exhibit degradation or occlusion. We demonstrate the capabilities of this marsupial ground-aerial system via experiments in challenging field conditions with wind speeds of up to 15 mph, in both daylight and at night. We show that the system supports localization and closed-loop navigation through dense foliage where the ground robot's sensors are occluded. Our control system for the quadrotor operates at 200 Hz entirely with onboard sensing and computation. More details and experiment videos can be found on the project page: https://ongdexter.github.io/evperiscope.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [The Internet of Collaborating Things: Agentic Edge AI for Autonomous Cross-Domain Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10542)
*Walid A. Hanafy,Nader Sehatbakhsh,David Irwin,Mani Srivastava,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"协作物联网"(IoCT)新范式，主张IoT设备由纵向卸载转向横向直接协作，并指出其实现需可移植安全执行底座与具备上下文推理、瞬时信任建立和开放世界适应能力的智能体控制平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT正迈向万亿美元级、跨域部署的互联设备规模。这些设备已不再是简单传感与执行端点，而是具备嵌入式处理与设备端智能服务的移动平台。主流的"将计算卸载至云/边缘服务器"的纵向设备—服务器交互模型无法随此趋势扩展，因此需要一种横向范式，让设备直接通信协作，汇聚算力、感知与执行能力，形成动态跨域集群。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文(position paper)，非实验性研究。方法上首先定义IoCT这一通信、计算与协作新范式；其次阐明智能体边缘AI(agentic edge AI)在实现该范式中的作用；最后梳理研究挑战与未来方向。

Result: 论文识别出实现IoCT的两大关键需求：一是面向异构硬件的可移植且安全的执行底座(portable and secure execution substrate)；二是智能体控制平面(agentic control plane)，需在设备协作全生命周期中无需人工干预地完成上下文推理、瞬时信任建立(transient trust establishment)与开放世界适应(open-world adaptation)。

Conclusion: 论文呼吁面向可信、自主的IoCT协作开展进一步研究，并给出了相应研究挑战与未来方向，旨在推动IoT从纵向卸载模式向横向设备协作模式演进。

Abstract: The Internet of Things is on a trajectory toward a trillion connected devices deployed across multiple domains. These devices are no longer simple sensing and actuation endpoints; they are mobile platforms with embedded processing and intelligent on-device services. The dominant paradigm of offloading computation to cloud and edge servers is a vertical device-to-server interaction model that cannot scale with this trajectory. What is needed instead is a horizontal paradigm in which devices communicate and collaborate directly, pooling their compute, sensing, and actuation into dynamic, cross-domain clusters rather than offloading to centralized infrastructure. We refer to this paradigm as the Internet of Collaborating Things (IoCT). Realizing this vision requires both a portable and secure execution substrate for heterogeneous hardware and an agentic control plane capable of contextual reasoning, transient trust establishment, and open-world adaptation throughout the device collaboration lifecycle without human intervention. In this position paper, we define this new communication, compute, and collaboration paradigm, articulate the role of agentic edge AI in realizing it, and outline research challenges and future directions toward trustworthy, autonomous IoCT collaboration.

</details>


### [253] [MUC-FL: Block-Wise Marginal Utility Contribution for Communication-Efficient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10545)
*Akshay Mhatre,Vikram Karthick,Deepti Gupta,Jia Zou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出分块边际效用贡献（MUC）框架，在联邦学习中仅传输对模型性能贡献最大的数据块，在MIMIC多模态数据上以1.76%的块实现45-50%通信量削减并保持或提升模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可在不集中数据的前提下进行分布式训练，但通信开销高，需要减少传输量。

Method: 提出Block-Wise Marginal Utility Contribution (MUC)框架，基于数据块对模型性能的贡献进行选择性传输，并采用基于去重的块选择策略；在集成多个MIMIC临床数据集的多模态数据集上评估。

Result: 1,135个候选块中仅24个（1.76%）携带有效改进信号，可实现45-50%的通信削减；去重块选择的宏F1为0.8566，优于标准联邦优化的0.8155，且在代表性不足的类别上提升明显。

Conclusion: 选择性传输数据块可在降低通信开销的同时保持甚至提升模型性能，尤其有利于少数类。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed model training without centralizing data but suffers from high communication overhead. To address this, we propose Block-Wise Marginal Utility Contribution (MUC), a framework that selectively transmits only the most impactful data blocks based on their contribution to model performance. To evaluate our framework, we apply it to a multimodal dataset integrated from multiple MIMIC clinical datasets and show that only 24 out of 1,135 candidate blocks (1.76%) carry meaningful improvement signals, enabling a potential communication reduction of 45-50% while maintaining or improving model quality. Our deduplication-based block selection achieves a macro F1 score of 0.8566 compared to 0.8155 for standard federated optimization, demonstrating that selective transmission can improve performance, particularly in underrepresented classes.

</details>


### [254] [Design and Operation of a Federated GPU Cluster for Digital Humanities within DHinfra.at](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10552)
*Florian Atzenhofer-Baumgartner,David Fleischhacker,Max Resch,Lukas Waldhofer,Michael Otto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一份关于奥地利 DHinfra.at 项目中面向数字人文（DH）研究的小型联邦 GPU 集群的技术报告，描述了其设计、实现与运行经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数字人文研究构建专用 GPU 计算平台，在有限预算、固定功耗、小团队（约1.5 FTE）及欧盟采购等约束下，满足跨校研究者对大语言模型与算力的需求。

Method: 系统跨两所大学站点部署，通过国家级身份联邦进行登录代理，提供交互式笔记本、SSH 和 OpenAI 兼容推理 API 三种接口；核心为控制平面，将联邦身份、按项目配额与模型目录映射到自助服务界面，并采用请求-审批队列管理特权操作，以及结合常驻服务与按需模型切换的两级模型服务架构。

Result: 实现了跨站点联邦集群与统一控制平面，使研究者无需集群账户即可经 HTTPS 使用大语言模型；报告记录了功耗预算、欧盟采购、团队规模、优先访问与利用率关系等实际约束，以及未采纳替代方案的主要构建决策；平台以 Apache License 2.0 发布。

Conclusion: 提出面向领域专用、低预算 GPU 集群建设的实践指南（playbook），并以持续版本化的活技术报告形式随平台演进更新。

Abstract: We describe the design, implementation, and operation of a small federated GPU cluster built for Digital Humanities (DH) research within the Austrian DHinfra.at project. The system spans two university sites, brokers logins from a national identity federation, and exposes compute through three interfaces: interactive notebooks, SSH, and an OpenAI-compatible inference API. The core of the platform is a control plane that maps federated identity, per-project quotas, and a model catalogue onto self-service interfaces. We document this control plane in detail, including the request-and-approval queue that mediates every privileged cluster effect and the two-tier model-serving setup, which combines always-on services with on-demand model swapping and lets researchers consume large language models over HTTPS without holding a cluster account. We report the constraints that shaped the build: a fixed power budget, EU-wide procurement, a team of about 1.5 full-time equivalents, and the relationship between prioritized access and utilization. We also record the main build decisions against the alternatives we researched and did not adopt. The platform is released under the Apache License 2.0. We close with a playbook for teams building a domain-specific GPU cluster on a limited budget. This is a living technical report, versioned and updated as the platform evolves.

</details>


### [255] [Composable CXL Memory as a Kubernetes-Native Shared Memory for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10790)
*Hongjian Fan,Kevin Zhang,David Habinsky,Sean Dykstra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一个Kubernetes DRA驱动，将可组合CXL内存变为可调度的集群资源，并用于LLM服务中跨节点KV-cache复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中KV-cache复用通常局限于节点本地（GPU前缀缓存、CPU-DRAM卸载），跨节点复用缺乏可调度、可共享的内存介质；本文探索以CXL内存分片实现内存解耦，使跨节点前缀复用成为可能。

Method: 实现Kubernetes DRA驱动：按需组合CXL region，在各主机上物化为DAX设备，并以单一CDI名称注入Pod，使不同节点的Pod访问同一物理区域；为vLLM/llm-d提供共享内存连接器，将CXL区域作为KV-cache层级，并在共享介质内嵌入slot目录，免除外置元数据服务。

Result: 两节点集群、512 GiB CXL设备、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct：外部命中率95.4–99.5%时，跨节点前缀复用使TTFT降低5.5×–36.6×；节点本地层级则退化为完全重算。跨节点与同节点复用的“共享差距”为1–4%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明CXL可组合内存可作为可调度的集群级共享KV-cache层级，跨节点复用相对同节点复用的额外延迟很小；两个副本均运行完整引擎，属于内存解耦而非预填充/解码解耦，定位为可行性研究而非性能评测。

Abstract: We present a Kubernetes Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA) driver that makes composable CXL memory a schedulable cluster resource, and evaluate the resulting shared-memory tier for cross-node KV-cache reuse in LLM serving. The driver composes CXL regions on demand, materializes them as DAX devices on each participating host, and injects them into pods under a single Container Device Interface (CDI) name so that pods on different nodes access the same physical region. A shared-memory connector for vLLM/llm-d uses that region as a KV-cache tier with a slot directory embedded inside the shared medium, which eliminates the need for an external metadata service. On a two-node cluster with a 512\,GiB CXL appliance and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, cross-node prefix reuse reduces TTFT by 5.5$\times$--36.6$\times$ at an external hit rate of 95.4--99.5\,\%, while node-local tiers (GPU prefix caching, CPU-DRAM offload) fall back to full recompute. The sharing gap, defined as the latency ratio between cross-node and same-node reuse, is 1--4\%, indicating that cross-node reuse incurs little additional latency relative to same-node reuse on our testbed. Both replicas run full engines; the study demonstrates memory disaggregation rather than prefill/decode disaggregation. We report this as a feasibility study rather than a performance evaluation.

</details>


### [256] [ExaServe: Large-Scale LLM Serving on Exascale HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10812)
*Wenyi Wang,Shu Shi,Yadu Babuji,Ian Foster,Kyle Chard*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ExaServe 是一个可通过 pip 安装的框架，可将声明式 YAML 配置转化为可复现的大规模 LLM 服务部署，并在 ALCF Aurora 上实现 1–256 节点的部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生 LLM 服务框架在数据中心已普及，但在顶级超算上部署面临调度器集成、MPI 启动、加速器选择、节点本地权重暂存和平台专用补丁等工程难题，缺乏实用且可复现的部署路径。

Method: 提出 ExaServe 框架，基于声明式 YAML 规范自动生成可复现的大规模 LLM 服务部署；在 ALCF Aurora 上从 1 扩展到 256 节点（3072 个 vLLM 副本），并对非流式推理、token 流式推理及控制平面行为进行测量与分析。

Result: 非流式推理近乎线性扩展至 256 节点，达到 27.1k 请求/秒（3.8M tokens/秒）；token 流式推理表现不同，集中式代理在约 4.7k 请求/秒处进入平台期，尽管模型服务器仍满足服务级目标；同时发现 O(N²) 的 Ray Serve 控制平面瓶颈，使 256 节点时集群启动时间增至约 30 分钟。

Conclusion: ExaServe 提供了实用且可复现的部署路径，同时揭示了未来百亿亿次（exascale）级 LLM 服务的关键障碍，包括流式推理的代理瓶颈与控制平面扩展性问题。

Abstract: Cloud-native LLM serving frameworks have made deployment routine in data centers, yet deploying them on leadership-class supercomputers remains an engineering challenge requiring scheduler integration, MPI launch, accelerator selection, node-local weight staging, and platform-specific patches. We present \textit{ExaServe}, a pip-installable framework that transforms a declarative YAML specification into a reproducible large-scale LLM serving deployment. Using ExaServe, we deploy LLM serving on ALCF Aurora from 1 to 256 nodes (3072 vLLM replicas). Non-streaming inference scales nearly linearly to 256 nodes, reaching 27.1\,k requests/s (3.8\,M tokens/s). Token streaming scales differently: a centralized proxy plateaus at $\sim$4.7\,k requests/s despite the model servers remaining within the service-level objective. We also identify an \emph{O}(\emph{N}\textsuperscript{2}) Ray Serve control-plane bottleneck that increases cluster bring-up to $\sim$30 minutes at 256 nodes. ExaServe provides a practical, reproducible deployment path while exposing key barriers to future exascale LLM serving.

</details>


### [257] [Online Treasure Hunt in Vertex-Permuted Dynamic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11013)
*Kamran Ayoubi,Bernard Mans,Lata Narayanan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究顶点置换动态环（VP）上k个智能体的寻宝问题，刻画了可解性条件、最优搜索时间与竞争比，并给出随机置换（R-VP）下的期望步数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环中顶点位置不断变化，传统静态或边置换模型的结果不适用，需明确在何种置换约束下寻宝可行，以及所需智能体数量、搜索时间下界与最优算法。

Method: 定义VP模型（每步顶点在环上被置换）与VP(δ)模型（任一对顶点所连边每δ步内出现）；证明不可解性与可行性阈值；针对单智能体给紧界与在线算法竞争比分析；为k智能体设计最优算法；在R-VP（每步随机置换成环）下分别针对遗忘型与自适应对手分析期望步数。

Result: 无置换序列限制时，k≤n−3个智能体无法完成寻宝；VP(δ)仅当δ≥⌈(n−1)/2⌉时可行；单智能体在δ≥2n时最坏搜索时间与任意在线算法竞争比均为Θ(δn)；k智能体最优算法可实现k倍最坏搜索时间加速；R-VP下期望搜索时间为Θ(n)（遗忘型对手）与Θ(n log n)（自适应对手）。

Conclusion: 顶点置换动态环的寻宝可行性由置换约束强度决定；VP(δ)给出紧的可行性阈值与最优搜索性能，多智能体可线性加速，随机置换模型下期望复杂度依赖对手类型。

Abstract: We study the problem of treasure hunt by a group of $k \geq 1$ agents in vertex-permuted dynamic rings (VP). In this model, the $n$ vertices remain on a ring but are permuted at each time step. We first show that treasure hunt is impossible for any $k \leq n-3$ agents, if there are no restrictions on the sequence of permutations used in the dynamic ring. We then study the $VP(δ)$ setting, in which for every pair $i, j$ of vertices, the edge $(i, j)$ is guaranteed to appear within $δ$ steps. We show that the class $VP(δ)$ is feasible only for $δ\geq \left\lceil \frac{n-1}{2}\right\rceil$. For the one-agent case, we show a tight bound of $Θ(δn)$ on the worst-case search time as well as competitive ratio of any online algorithm for treasure hunt, provided $δ\geq 2n$. We then give an optimal algorithm for $k$ agents, thereby showing that $k$ agents can obtain a speedup of $k$ on the worst-case search time. Finally, in the R-VP setting, in which in every step, the vertices are arranged as a ring according to a random permutation, we show that treasure hunt takes expected $Θ(n)$ steps against an oblivious adversary and $Θ(n \log n)$ steps against an adaptive adversary.

</details>


### [258] [Entwine: Coordinating Tiled Computation and Fine-Grained Communication across GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11562)
*Kai Ma,Quanfeng Lv,Jingguo Ge,Bowei Dai,Kefan Ruan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Entwine，通过协调 tile 计算顺序、细粒度通信与 SM 资源分配，减少计算与通信进展不匹配带来的开销，在张量并行 LLM 负载上平均加速 1.232x（最高 1.433x），优于 cuBLAS+NCCL 及现有重叠基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高性能 GPU 计算将张量分块以利用数据复用与并行性，单个 tile 计算早于整体完成，为计算与通信重叠带来机会。但计算与通信进展不匹配会限制这些机会：通信在无数据就绪时停顿，数据突发到达时滞后，且通信占用共享资源拖慢计算，抵消重叠收益。

Method: Entwine 协调 tile 计算顺序、细粒度通信与 SM 资源分配。它重排 tile 计算顺序，使数据以更规律的节奏产生以支持通信；将该调度与基于 SM 的细粒度通信耦合，以低延迟低开销处理 tile 结果；由于通信内核也消耗 SM 资源，Entwine 协调二者分配，在通信进展与计算减速之间取得平衡。

Result: 在代表性张量并行 LLM 负载上，Entwine 相对 cuBLAS+NCCL 取得几何平均 1.232x 加速（最高 1.433x），并在几何平均上优于当前最优重叠基线 3.1-9.8%。

Conclusion: 通过协同调度 tile 计算顺序、细粒度 SM 通信与资源分配，可有效解决计算-通信进展不匹配问题，显著提升张量并行工作负载性能。实现将在论文发表后开源。

Abstract: Modern high-performance GPU computations partition tensors into tiles to exploit data reuse and parallelism. Individual tile computations complete earlier than the full tensor computation, creating opportunities to overlap computation and communication. However, a mismatch between computation and communication progress can limit these opportunities. Communication stalls when no data is ready, and may lag when data arrives in bursts. Communication can also slow computation by consuming shared resources, offsetting the benefits of overlap.
  We present Entwine, which coordinates tile computation order, fine-grained communication, and SM resource allocation to minimize overall completion time. Entwine reorders tile computation to produce data for communication at a more regular pace. Entwine couples this schedule with fine-grained SM-based communication to process tile results with low latency and low overhead. Since the communication kernel also consumes SM resources, Entwine coordinates their allocation to balance communication progress against computation slowdown. Across representative tensor-parallel LLM workloads, Entwine achieves a geomean speedup of 1.232x (up to 1.433x) over cuBLAS+NCCL, and outperforms state-of-the-art overlap baselines by 3.1-9.8% in geomean. We will open-source our implementation upon publication.

</details>


### [259] [The Computing Channel: How Modulation Programs the Airwaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11145)
*Saeed Razavikia,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"数字函数导向通信"框架：联合设计有限字母符号表示与接收机判决，使多址叠加直接编码目标函数，无需恢复各用户输入，从而将空口计算（OAC）与数字通信流程相匹配，并以联邦边缘学习为例说明其可减少依赖用户数的数据承载资源、直接处理量化模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算与分布式人工智能需频繁交换中间结果，但许多应用只需聚合值而非各设备单独消息。传统系统先恢复每条消息再聚合，空口计算（OAC）利用同时传输直接获得聚合值。然而主流OAC实现依赖模拟信令，与有限精度数据和数字通信流程存在不匹配。

Method: 提出数字函数导向通信：联合设计有限字母（finite-alphabet）符号表示与接收机判决，使多址叠加（multiple-access superposition）直接编码所需函数而不恢复个体输入。文中给出其计算星座（computational-constellation）原理、主要设计方法、扩展方向及实现挑战。

Result: 以联邦边缘学习为例，展示该框架可直接作用于量化模型更新，并减少依赖用户数的数据承载资源。

Conclusion: 数字函数导向通信为OAC提供与数字通信流程兼容的实现路径，通过联合设计符号与判决实现"通信即计算"，具有可扩展性与应用潜力，但仍面临实现层面的挑战。

Abstract: Distributed computing and distributed artificial intelligence require frequent exchanges of intermediate results, although many applications need only an aggregate rather than messages from individual devices. Conventional systems recover each message before computing the aggregate, whereas over-the-air computation (OAC) exploits simultaneous transmission to obtain it directly. However, dominant OAC implementations rely on analog signaling, creating a mismatch with finite-precision data and digital communication procedures. This article presents digital function-oriented communication, in which finite-alphabet symbol representations and receiver decisions are jointly designed so that multiple-access superposition encodes the desired function without recovering individual inputs. We introduce its computational-constellation principle, main design approaches, extensions, and implementation challenges. Federated edge learning illustrates how the framework can reduce user-dependent data-bearing resources while operating directly on quantized model updates.

</details>


### [260] [Can AI Remediate Backend Failures Safely? GuardedAct with Blast-Radius-Aware Sandboxing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11264)
*Wanrong Cai,Tianyu Yu,Shaorui Pi,Xiaoxuan Sun,Wenrui Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GuardedAct 是一种"沙箱优先"的微服务修复框架，在 LLM 动作生成器与生产环境之间插入爆炸半径感知的验证层，通过数字孪生沙箱仿真与回滚置信门控，仅自动执行低风险动作，从而在保持高恢复率的同时显著降低连带损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽能为微服务故障生成修复动作，但直接在生产环境执行 AI 生成的修复动作存在级联连带损伤风险，缺乏执行前的安全验证机制。

Method: GuardedAct 采用四阶段流程：(1) 摄取诊断报告、实时系统拓扑与近期遥测数据；(2) 提示 LLM 生成排序后的候选修复动作列表；(3) 在轻量级数字孪生沙箱中仿真每个动作，估计爆炸半径并赋予风险标签；(4) 通过回滚置信门控，仅自动执行低风险动作，高风险动作升级交由人工审核。

Result: 在 DeathStarBench 社交网络应用中注入的五种故障场景上，GuardedAct 整体恢复率达 87.4%，连带损伤较直接 LLM 执行降低 79.7%（从 25.6% 降至 5.2%），代价是沙箱引入的平均恢复时间增加约 8 秒。消融实验证实各组件对安全—速度权衡均有实质性贡献。

Conclusion: GuardedAct 表明将爆炸半径感知的沙箱验证与风险门控引入 LLM 修复流程，可在可接受的时间开销下大幅提升生产环境修复的安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising capabilities in generating remediation actions for microservice failures. However, directly executing AI-generated repair actions in production risks cascading collateral damage. We propose GuardedAct, a sandbox-first remediation framework that interposes a blast-radius-aware verification layer between the LLM action generator and the production environment. GuardedAct operates in four phases: (1) ingesting a diagnosis report together with the live system topology and recent telemetry, (2) prompting an LLM to produce a ranked list of candidate remediation actions, (3) simulating each action in a lightweight digital-twin sandbox that estimates the blast radius and assigns a risk label, and (4) enforcing a rollback-confidence gate that auto-executes only low-risk actions while escalating high-risk ones for human review. We evaluate GuardedAct on five fault scenarios injected into the DeathStarBench social-network application. Experimental results show that GuardedAct achieves an overall recovery rate of 87.4% while reducing collateral damage by 79.7% relative to direct LLM execution (from 25.6% to 5.2%), at the cost of a modest sandbox-induced increase in mean time to recovery (approximately 8 s). Ablation studies confirm that each component contributes meaningfully to the safety-speed trade-off.

</details>


### [261] [Rethinking Sparse Formats for RISC-V: A Hierarchical Approach to High-Performance SpMV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11352)
*Anna Pirova,Anastasia Vodeneeva,Konstantin Kovalev,Alexander Ustinov,Maksim Zagriadskov,Daniil Litvyakov,Arthur Kulik,Evgeny Kozinov,Valentin Volokitin,Iosif Meyerov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对RISC-V处理器上的稀疏矩阵-向量乘法（SpMV），比较了8种稀疏矩阵存储格式的向量化实现，并提出一种新的CSR改进格式——分层CSR（HCSR），基于RVV 1.0 intrinsics实现并开源为RVVLASparse库。实验表明，合适格式选择平均加速1.6倍，HCSR在广泛稀疏矩阵上执行时间最短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpMV是线性代数的基础计算内核，广泛用于科学与工程仿真中的迭代求解器。RISC-V平台上的SpMV向量化优化及存储格式选择尚缺乏系统研究，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 采用RVV 1.0 intrinsics对8种已有稀疏矩阵存储格式的SpMV进行向量化实现，并提出CSR的新变体HCSR（Hierarchical CSR）以提升RISC-V上的性能；实现以开源C++库RVVLASparse发布，并在SpacemiT K1和K3 RISC-V开发板上进行实验。

Result: 实验显示，选择合适的矩阵存储格式平均可使SpMV计算加速1.6倍；所提HCSR格式在广泛稀疏矩阵类型上均取得最短执行时间。

Conclusion: 存储格式选择对RISC-V上SpMV性能影响显著，HCSR作为对CSR的有效改进，可作为RISC-V平台上SpMV的优选格式，相关实现已开源以供复用与验证。

Abstract: The sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) algorithm is a fundamental computational kernel of linear algebra and serves as a building block for numerous applications, primarily iterative solvers for systems of linear equations used in scientific and engineering simulations. This paper compares vectorized implementations of the SpMV algorithm across eight established sparse matrix storage formats and proposes a novel modification of the CSR format, Hierarchical CSR (HCSR), which enhances SpMV performance on RISC-V processors. Our SpMV implementations utilize RVV 1.0 intrinsics and are publicly available as an open-source C++ library named RVVLASparse. Computational experiments conducted on SpacemiT K1 and K3 RISC-V boards demonstrate that selecting an appropriate matrix storage format accelerates SpMV computations by an average of 1.6x, while the proposed HCSR format achieves the shortest execution time among all considered formats across a broad class of sparse matrices.

</details>


### [262] [Taming Bitwise Behavior in GPU Kernels with Tensor Core: Black-Box Reconstruction, Compiler Enforcement, and Static Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11356)
*Ziteng Yang,Nicholas J. Riasanovsky,Warren Deng,Vivek Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究GPU核函数的确定性数值可复现性问题，聚焦浮点归约顺序、部分和精度、FMA及舍入位置等导致逐位差异的因素。工作包括：提出GEMM归约顺序描述符并首次对闭源库进行黑盒算术重构；在Triton降低阶段强制平衡树归约并引入数据布局优化；开发跨NVIDIA PTX与AMD GCN的逐位等价静态检查器并集成到自动调谐器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统对GPU核函数的确定性与数值可复现性需求日益增长，但同一核函数的确定性实现仍可能存在逐位差异，根源在于浮点归约顺序等因素。这些算术选择可能由手工编码、Triton等块级语言选定，或隐藏于cuBLAS/rocBLAS等闭源库中。为性能选择的tile形状同时决定了算术行为，可能破坏批次不变性。保持固定顺序性能损失最高可达20%，而自动调谐器无法识别哪些配置逐位等价。

Method: 1) 提出GEMM归约顺序的描述符（含split-K GEMM中K维的分区方式），据此首次对闭源库的算术进行黑盒重构；2) 在Triton lowering期间强制平衡树归约，并引入数据布局优化；3) 开发健全的逐位等价静态检查器，涵盖NVIDIA PTX与AMD GCN。

Result: Triton GEMM系列在Blackwell与Hopper上所有测试用例中均与NVIDIA cuBLAS匹配；对于带融合epilogue的真实LLM形状，性能匹配或超过torch.compile。归约优化使GB300与H100上27个核函数中的19个在自由顺序性能的10%以内。逐位等价检查器集成进Triton自动调谐器后，将搜索限制在单一逐位等价类中。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了归约与GEMM的逐位行为决定因素，实现了对闭源库算术的黑盒重构、平衡树归约与数据布局优化带来的性能提升，以及跨厂商（NVIDIA/AMD）的逐位等价静态检查，并将其用于约束自动调谐搜索空间，兼顾确定性与性能。

Abstract: Determinism and numerical reproducibility are increasingly required of GPU kernels in machine learning systems, yet deterministic implementations of the same kernel can still differ bit for bit. Floating-point reduction order is the primary cause, alongside partial-sum precision, fused multiply-add operations, and rounding placement. These choices may be hand-coded, selected by a block-level language such as Triton, or hidden inside a closed-source library such as cuBLAS or rocBLAS. A tile shape chosen for performance therefore also determines the arithmetic, potentially breaking batch invariance. Preserving a fixed order can cost up to 20 percent, while an autotuner cannot identify which configurations are bitwise equivalent.
  We characterize the factors determining the bitwise behavior of reductions and general matrix multiplication (GEMM). First, we introduce a descriptor of GEMM reduction order, including the partitioning of K in split-K GEMM. Using it, we perform the first black-box reconstruction of a closed-source library's arithmetic for bit-level correctness. Our family of Triton GEMMs matches NVIDIA cuBLAS in all tested cases on Blackwell and Hopper. For realistic LLM shapes with fused epilogues, it matches or exceeds torch.compile performance. Second, we enforce balanced-tree reduction during Triton lowering and introduce a data-layout optimization that brings 19 of 27 kernels on GB300 and H100 within 10 percent of free-order performance. Third, we develop sound static checkers for bitwise equivalence between compiled GPU kernels, including the first checker spanning NVIDIA PTX and AMD GCN. Integrated into Triton's autotuner, the checker restricts search to a single bit-equivalence class.

</details>


### [263] [GPU-CFR: 80x Faster Counterfactual Regret Minimization by Compiling the Game to Static Dataflow and CUDA Graph Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11923)
*Boning Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出GPU-CFR，将博弈树一次编译为静态数据流（扁平化边/信息集数组、预计算索引、按深度分批），使形状、索引与缓冲区地址在迭代间不变，再用CUDA Graph Replay单次启动回放整个迭代。在A100上，八款博弈套件中最快较此前最优GPU CFR提升29.8–80.4倍，四个最大博弈上较LiteEFG（快速开源CPU实现）提升14–258倍，且不改变更新规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实后悔最小化（CFR）是少数在CPU上仍快于GPU的大规模数值负载之一。每次迭代需遍历含数十亿状态、通过通用树接口发出数百万次微小且相互依赖的gather/scatter步：GPU上每个kernel仅数微秒，kernel启动与框架调度成为瓶颈，导致已有GPU实现反而不如优化后的CPU代码。作者观察到：对固定博弈，CFR迭代中除数值外的所有信息在首轮迭代前即已确定，因而可将全部控制流静态化。

Method: 1) 将任意博弈一次性编译为静态数据流：扁平的边数组与信息集数组、预计算索引、按深度层级批处理的pass，固定整个操作序列，迭代间只改变求解器状态。2) 三项优化——静态机会节点折叠（static chance folding）、按深度层级的执行块（depth-level execution blocks）、双通道reach缓冲区（dual-lane reach buffer），将框架操作数最多减少18.1倍。3) 由于形状、索引与缓冲区地址恒定，使用CUDA Graph Replay记录一次迭代并以单次图启动重放。4) CPU路径复现参考迭代的按位一致结果；树构建与图捕获成本在首次求解内即被摊销。

Result: 在单张A100、覆盖牌类/骰子类/棋类共八款博弈的套件上：较同一加速器上最快的先前GPU CFR实现快29.8–80.4倍；在四个最大博弈上较LiteEFG快14–258倍。仅用编译表示、八线程CPU且无加速器时，已比GPU基线快2.2–51.1倍。GPU-CFR在中大型博弈上击败所有CPU与GPU基线，且未改变更新规则。

Conclusion: 对固定博弈预先可知的控制流进行编译与静态化，能有效消除GPU上kernel启动与框架调度开销；编译表示本身即携带大部分性能优势，配合CUDA Graph Replay可实现单次图启动的高效迭代。该方法在不修改CFR更新规则的前提下，全面超越CPU与GPU基线。

Abstract: Counterfactual regret minimization (CFR) is one of the few large numerical workloads that still runs faster on CPUs than on GPUs. Each iteration sweeps a game tree with up to billions of states in millions of small, interdependent gather and scatter steps issued through a generic tree interface. On a GPU every kernel finishes in microseconds, so kernel launches and framework dispatch dominate the run time, and prior GPU implementations have lost to optimized CPU code. We observe that for a fixed game, everything about a CFR iteration except the numerical values is known before the first iteration runs. We propose GPU-CFR, a compiler and runtime built on this observation. It compiles any game once into static dataflow: flat edge and information-set arrays, precomputed indices, and depth-level batched passes fix the entire operation sequence, and only solver state changes between iterations. Static chance folding, depth-level execution blocks, and a dual-lane reach buffer cut the number of framework operations by up to 18.1x. Because shapes, indices, and buffer addresses never change, CUDA Graph Replay records the iteration once and replays it with a single graph launch. On one A100, across an eight-game suite that spans card games, dice games, and board games, GPU-CFR runs 29.8--80.4x faster than the fastest prior GPU CFR on the same accelerator, and 14--258x faster than LiteEFG, one of the fastest open-source CPU implementations, on the four largest games. The compiled representation carries most of that margin: on eight CPU threads with no accelerator it is already 2.2--51.1x faster than the GPU baseline. On the CPU the optimized path reproduces the reference iterates bitwise, and tree construction and graph capture pay for themselves within the first solve. GPU-CFR beats every CPU and GPU baseline on the mid-to-large games of the suite without changing the update rule.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [REACH: Controller-Managed Long-Span ECC for HBM AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10861)
*Rui Xie,Yunhua Fang,Asad Ul Haq,Linsen Ma,Sanchari Sen,Swagath Venkataramani,Liu Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: REACH是一种HBM控制器微架构，通过内码纠正常见错误、外码（长跨度码）处理已知擦除的差分奇偶校验方案，在LLM解码读主导场景下以更低面积与功耗实现高带宽纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBM成本高，需更强控制器保护以容忍更宽范围的器件错误率。长跨度纠错码在相近码率下保护更强，但直接实现会将小访问与跨度级状态耦合，并在HBM带宽下需要高代价解码。

Method: REACH采用控制器微架构：用成熟内码纠正常见错误并识别未解决的数据块（chunk），仅对已知擦除保留长外码修复；差分奇偶校验（differential parity）限制写流量；协同设计的端点保持32B事务而无需额外数据突发。评估用Ramulator2仿真，并基于ASAP7综合内核做面积/功耗分析。

Result: 在最高错误压力下Ramulator2维持1.88 TB/s应用流量；分离全接口尺寸设计在ASAP7综合内核下支持2.69 TB/s应用目标。在该分析目标下，REACH标称组成比所评估的mean-work direct-long设计减少55.8%控制器面积、57.7%建模功耗。

Conclusion: 将长跨度恢复仅保留给异常请求，可在LLM解码读主导场景中以更低面积与功耗获得强纠错与高带宽，优于直接长码实现。

Abstract: High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) cost motivates stronger controller protection that can support a wider range of device error rates. Long-span error-correcting codes provide stronger protection at a comparable code rate, but a direct implementation couples small accesses to span-wide state and requires costly decoding at HBM bandwidth. Read-dominated LLM decode offers a favorable setting: sequential reads support span aggregation, while sparse writes limit parity-update traffic. This paper presents REACH, a controller microarchitecture that uses established inner codes to correct common errors and identify unresolved chunks, reserving a long outer code for known-erasure repair. Differential parity bounds write traffic, and a co-designed endpoint preserves 32\,B transactions without an extra data burst. Ramulator2 sustains 1.88\,TB/s of application traffic at the highest error stress, while separate full-interface sizing supports a 2.69\,TB/s application target using ASAP7-synthesized kernels. At this analytical target, REACH's nominal composition uses 55.8\% less controller area and 57.7\% less modeled power than the evaluated mean-work direct-long design, showing the benefit of reserving long-span recovery for exceptional requests.

</details>


### [265] [Fengshui: Demystifying Chiplet Ecosystem and Bespoke Neural Network Accelerator Codesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10970)
*Haoran Jin,Jirong Yang,Zhiheng Zhang,Justin Shin,Barry Lyu,Kangqi Zhang,Yunpeng Liu,Nathan Bleier*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fengshui——一个 chiplet 生态与加速器协同设计框架，通过算子级解聚联合优化 chiplet 池构成与定制 ASIC（BASIC）设计，在 8 个精选 chiplet 下显著降低能耗与多项能效指标，并逼近无约束异构设计的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 ML 负载对延迟与能耗要求严苛，在同构通用硬件上高效运行愈发困难。算子级解聚可针对各算子定制微架构、批处理与存储层次，但高度定制化加速器带来难以承受的 NRE 成本。基于 chiplet 的集成可分摊 NRE，然而"构建哪些 chiplet、如何组合成加速器"存在循环依赖：chiplet 池的价值取决于所构建的加速器，而加速器质量又受可用 chiplet 限制。

Method: Fengshui 通过算子级解聚构建 BASIC，协同探索 chiplet 与存储异构性、张量融合，以及流水线/张量/专家并行，并结合布局布线验证以保证物理可实现性，从而联合优化 chiplet 池构成与 BASIC 设计。

Result: 仅用 8 个精选 chiplet（含网络交换机、存内计算单元及多样微架构加速器），Fengshui 生成的 BASIC 相比同构加速器在 energy、EC、EDP、EDPC 上分别降低 48.5%、88.1%、93.0%、97.8%，且在多种神经网络上距无约束异构设计仅差 4.1%。面向数据中心 MoE 与稠密 LLM 服务，prefill 能耗与 EC 分别最高降低 16.8% 与 28.7%；面向边缘自动驾驶感知，在实时延迟约束下实现 12.0% 能耗与 23.6% EC 降低。

Conclusion: chiplet 生态与加速器的协同设计能够在有限 chiplet 集合下有效逼近无约束异构设计的能效，为受延迟与能耗约束的 ML 负载提供了可行的定制加速路径。

Abstract: Modern ML workloads, with stringent latency and energy constraints, are increasingly hard to run efficiently on homogeneous commodity hardware. We argue that operator-level disaggregation--tailoring microarchitecture, batching, and memory hierarchy to each operator--is essential to overcome these limitations, though the resulting highly bespoke accelerators incur prohibitive Non-Recurring Engineering (NRE) costs. Chiplet-based integration amortizes NRE across applications, but choosing which chiplets to build and how to compose them into accelerators is circularly dependent--a chiplet pool's value depends on the constructed accelerators, while accelerator quality is constrained by available chiplets. This paper introduces Fengshui, a chiplet ecosystem and accelerator co-design framework that jointly optimizes chiplet pool composition and bespoke application-specific integrated circuit (BASIC) design. Fengshui constructs BASICs through operator-level disaggregation, co-exploring chiplet and memory heterogeneity, tensor fusion, and pipeline/tensor/expert parallelism with place-and-route validation for physical implementability. With just 8 strategically selected chiplets, encompassing network switches, processing-in-memory units, and accelerators with diverse microarchitectures, Fengshui-generated BASICs achieve 48.5%, 88.1%, 93.0%, and 97.8% reductions in energy, energy-cost product (EC), energy-delay product (EDP), and energy-delay-cost product (EDPC) over homogeneous accelerators, while scoring within 4.1% of unconstrained heterogeneous designs across diverse neural networks. For datacenter MoE and dense LLM serving, Fengshui reduces prefill energy and EC by up to 16.8% and 28.7%, respectively; for edge autonomous vehicle perception, it achieves 12.0% energy and 23.6% EC reductions under real-time latency constraints.

</details>


### [266] [BEACON: A Versatile Accelerator for Computational Pathology Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11044)
*Sumanth Gudaparthi,Ananth Krishna Prasad,Lin Jia,Rajeev Balasubramonian,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出"AI+X"思路：以基线AI加速器为基础，仅添加最少逻辑即可支持新领域算子，并以计算病理学验证，得到BEACON加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器已获商业成功，但其他专用领域难以复制该成功，新加速器面临诸多壁垒。作者主张从基线AI加速器出发，仅增加少量逻辑来支持新领域所需的新算子，从而形成可高量产、可跨多类应用部署的通用芯片。

Method: 以计算病理学为目标领域，其多阶段流水线包括分割与特征提取、基于kNN的图构建、以及交替进行Aggregation与Combination的迭代GCN推理。分析这些阶段在基线CPU、GPU、AI及GCN加速器上的低效性，并通过软件重构与对基线脉动阵列AI加速器的小幅修改来应对。具体做法是提供处理单元间灵活数据通路与寄存器访问机制，将多数内核映射到脉动阵列，并增加特征聚合、负载均衡执行、欧氏距离计算、分箱（binning）与计数器聚合等支持。

Result: 相比基线CPU与GPU平台，BEACON在计算病理学任务上取得超过一个数量级的吞吐提升；相比基线AI芯片，其面积仅增加1.1倍。性能提升源于规避内存墙并提供高并行度。

Conclusion: AI+X思路可行：以极小面积代价扩展基线AI加速器，即可高效支持计算病理学等新领域，为降低新加速器门槛提供了实证案例。

Abstract: While accelerators for AI have seen great commercial success, it is challenging to replicate that success for other specialized domains due to a number of factors. We make the case that barriers for new accelerators can be lowered by starting with a baseline AI accelerator, and adding minimal logic to support new operators demanded by new specialized domains. This leads to a versatile chip that can be manufactured at high volume and deployed for a range of popular applications. We refer to this as the AI+X approach. This paper explores its potential for the emerging domain of Computational Pathology, which involves analysis of large whole-slide tissue images with a multi-stage pipeline. The pipeline requires support for a number of different kernels and operators - early stages perform segmentation and feature extraction, followed by graph creation with k nearest neighbor (kNN) algorithms, and finally inference with an iterative graph convolutional network (GCN) that alternates between Aggregation and Combination. We show that these stages execute inefficiently on a range of baseline CPU, GPU, AI, and GCN accelerators. That inefficiency is addressed with a combination of software re-structuring and small modifications to a baseline systolic AI accelerator. Many of the above kernels can be mapped to a systolic accelerator by offering a flexible datapath between processing elements and register access mechanisms. We add support for feature aggregation, load balanced execution, Euclidean distance calculation, binning, and counter aggregation. This additional flexibility and logic grows the area of a baseline AI chiplet by 1.1x, but by avoiding the memory wall and offering high parallelism, the proposed accelerator BEACON yields over an order of magnitude higher throughput for Computational Pathology than baseline CPU and GPU platforms.

</details>


### [267] [Bio-inspired Learning and Decision-Making with Probabilistic In-Memory Computing Hardware: Part 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11288)
*Thomas Dalgaty,Eiji Kawasaki,Miguel de Prado,Tommaso Salvatori,Germain Haugou,Eric Flamand*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本报告（Part 2）在Part 1提出的基于能量的不确定性学习与决策模型基础上，指出其规模化瓶颈并非模型本身，而是GPU依赖HBM接口的架构限制；通过事务级建模证明，采用概率模拟存内计算（AIMC）架构可消除HBM接口，实现超过1000倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Part 1提出的基于能量的模型使用随机Langevin动力学连续演化神经元状态与权重的近似概率分布，但在GPU实现中暴露出严重的可扩展性问题，主要表现为执行延迟过高。作者认为该延迟源于"大规模并行、低算术强度"的模型与依赖HBM接口的高带宽内存GPU架构之间的根本性错配，因此需要重新审视瓶颈的归属，并探索更适合此类模型的处理器架构。

Method: 作者构建了概率模拟存内计算（AIMC）处理器的详细事务级模型（TLM），在该架构下取消HBM接口，将计算直接在片上存储器内完成，并以此运行与Part 1相同的基于能量的模型，进而与数据中心级GPU硬件进行性能对比分析。

Result: 基于TLM的仿真结果表明，同一基于能量的模型在AIMC架构上的执行速度可超过数据中心级硬件的1000倍以上。由此说明，此前GPU上观察到的"不可扩展"现象是由HBM接口带来的串行化约束造成的假象，而非模型内在缺陷。

Conclusion: 大规模概率型基于能量的模型并非本质上不可扩展，真正不具备可扩展性的是依赖HBM接口的GPU架构。通过消除HBM接口、在片上内存内直接计算（AIMC），可以有效突破该瓶颈，为这类模型提供可行的扩展路径。

Abstract: This report extends our previous work (Part 1), which introduced an energy-based model for learning and decision-making under uncertainty. The model leverages stochastic Langevin dynamics to continuously evolve approximate probability distributions over neuron states and model weights. However, as noted in Part 1 and confirmed through GPU-based implementations, large-scale probabilistic energy-based models of this nature face significant scalability challenges due to excessive execution latency. This latency stems from a fundamental mismatch: massively parallel models with low arithmetic intensity (such as energy-based models) are being executed on processor architectures like GPUs that rely on high-bandwidth memory (HBM) interfaces. The HBM imposes brutally sequential execution constraints on inherently parallelizable models, creating the false impression that such models are unscalable. In reality, it is the GPU architecture itself, with its dependence on HBM interfaces, that is not a scalable processor architecture for this class of AI model. In this report, we demonstrate using a detailed transaction-level model (TLM) of a probabilistic analogue in-memory computing (AIMC) processor that the same energy-based model can execute well over 1000x faster than data-center-grade hardware by eliminating the HBM interface and performing computation directly within on-chip memory.

</details>


### [268] [PATTON: Enabling Commodity PIM for Production LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11392)
*Hangyeol Kim,Sanghyun Lee,Teokkyu Suh,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PATTON 是一个 PIM 运行时，通过在商品化 PIM 上实现分层粒度分配与 Commit Zone 机制，解决了 Value 缓存在 GEMV 效率、单 token 写入效率与内存容量之间的冲突，从而支持生产级 LLM 服务的完整 KV cache 生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PIM 方案仅加速 decode attention，无法满足生产级 LLM 服务中 KV cache 块的动态分配、填充、共享、缓存与回收需求。在商品化 PIM 上支持该生命周期，需要高效的物理内存分配、块到地址映射与命令生成，而 Value cache 的物理布局在 GEMV 效率、单 token 写入效率和内存容量三者之间存在根本性冲突。

Method: PATTON 引入分层粒度分配：块大小的 Key/Value 粒度与逻辑 token 块一一对应，固定其物理位置与命令；更粗的粒度将多个块分组，以实现高效 GEMV 执行与内存利用。Commit Zone 暂存部分 Value 块，在提交到 GEMV 优化位置之前完成高效的单 token 写入。PATTON 跟踪这些位置以生成 KV cache 写入及 QK-transpose/SV 命令。该方案无需修改 PIM 处理单元。

Result: 在 attention 执行及运行时触发的 prefill 重计算中，PATTON 相比评估基线平均实现 1.95 倍加速和 4.83 倍能效提升，且无需修改 PIM 处理单元，同时保持与 vLLM 原生 GPU KV cache 相当的命中率。

Conclusion: PATTON 通过分层粒度分配与 Commit Zone 机制，在商品化 PIM 上有效支撑了生产级 LLM 服务的 KV cache 生命周期管理，在不改动 PIM 处理单元的前提下显著提升性能与能效，并维持与原生 GPU KV cache 相当的命中率。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) is promising for accelerating memory-bound decode attention, but attention acceleration alone is insufficient for production LLM serving, where engines dynamically allocate, populate, share, cache, and reclaim logical KV cache blocks. Supporting this lifecycle on commodity PIM requires efficient physical memory allocation, block-to-address mapping, and command generation. For the Value cache, these requirements create a fundamental conflict among GEMV efficiency, single-token write efficiency, and memory capacity: GEMV-optimized layouts scatter newly generated Value vectors across rows, making writes costly, while finer-grained memory sharing improves capacity utilization but fragments GEMV reductions. We present PATTON, a PIM runtime that integrates production LLM serving engines with commodity PIM. PATTON introduces hierarchical granule allocation: block-sized Key and Value granules map one-to-one to logical token blocks, fixing their physical placements and commands, while coarser granules group blocks for efficient GEMV execution and memory utilization. A Commit Zone stages partial Value blocks for efficient single-token writes before committing them to GEMV-optimized locations. PATTON tracks these placements to generate KV cache writes and QK-transpose/SV commands. Across attention execution and runtime-induced prefill recomputation, PATTON achieves an average 1.95x speedup and 4.83x higher energy efficiency over evaluated baselines, requires no PIM processing-unit modifications, and maintains a KV cache hit rate comparable to the native GPU KV cache in vLLM.

</details>


### [269] [CHERI-D Reincarnate: efficient multicore CHERI temporal memory safety through allocation reincarnation (draft version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11590)
*Yuecheng Wang,Jonathan Woodruff,Simon W. Moore*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reinc，一种面向 CHERI 的可扩展且高效的时序内存安全架构扩展方案。其核心思想是"分配再生"：ID 耗尽时不再隔离内存对象，而是为同一分配槽动态分配新 ID，仅隔离并回收 ID，从而使内存可持续即时复用，并结合一致性 ID 缓存与多核反向映射机制实现软硬件协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作 CHERI-D 将有限宽度的 generation ID 存放在固定位置，当对象 ID 耗尽时不得不在整个 ID 生命周期内对该对象（内存）进行隔离。这导致两个问题：一是内存清扫（memory-sweep）频繁，二是隔离带来的内存开销显著，制约了方案的可扩展性与实际可用性。

Method: 1) 分配再生（allocation reincarnation）：ID 耗尽时动态为同一内存槽分配新 ID，被隔离的是 ID 而非内存，底层内存可立即复用；2) 一致性 ID 缓存（coherent ID caching）：保持完全去中心化的 ID 组织，时态元数据与被保护内存同位存放以保留局部性；3) 多核支持：通过轻量级反向映射（reverse-map）与过滤器（filter）机制，将物理一致性事件连接到虚拟编址的 ObjID 缓冲区；4) 软硬件协同实现，覆盖 CHERI-Toooba（超标量 FPGA 软核）、QEMU、LLVM/Clang 与 CheriBSD。

Result: 在所评测的工作负载中，Reinc 大幅降低了内存清扫频率与内存隔离开销，同时仅引入较低的运行时性能开销和硬件开销。

Conclusion: Reinc 通过将隔离对象从内存转为 ID，在保留 CHERI-D 时序内存安全（如 use-after-free 缓解）能力的同时实现了内存的持续即时复用，为可扩展、高效的时序内存安全提供了一条可行的架构路径。

Abstract: We propose CHERI-D Reincarnate (Reinc), an architectural extension to CHERI for scalable and efficient temporal memory safety. Prior work CHERI-D has a finite-width generation ID stored at a fixed location, requiring an object to be quarantined when its ID is exhausted. Reinc further provides use-after-free mitigation while permitting immediate freed memory reuse for objects through allocation reincarnation: rather than quarantining an allocation slot upon ID exhaustion, Reinc dynamically assigns a new ID to that slot when its current ID is exhausted. Exhausted IDs are quarantined and later reclaimed, while the underlying memory remains available for immediate reuse. By quarantining IDs rather than memory, Reinc enables continuous reuse of memory in the common case, substantially reducing both memory-sweep frequency and quarantine memory overhead.
  Reinc further introduces coherent ID caching while retaining a fully decentralized ID organization. Temporal metadata remains colocated with the memory it protects, preserving locality while avoiding centralized metadata structures. To support multicore execution, Reinc connects physical coherence events to the virtually addressed ObjID buffer using lightweight reverse-map and filter-based mechanisms.
  We implement Reinc as a hardware-software co-design spanning CHERI-Toooba (superscalar FPGA softcore), QEMU, LLVM/Clang and CheriBSD. Across our evaluated workloads, Reinc substantially reduces memory-sweep frequency and memory quarantine while incurring low performance and hardware overhead.

</details>


### [270] [AccelForge: Comprehensive Modeling and Co-Design Framework for AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11906)
*Tanner Andrulis,Michael Gilbert,Vivienne Sze,Joel S. Emer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出AccelForge，一个统一且可扩展的加速器建模框架，通过可组合的硬件模型与快速映射器，实现高效、准确的张量代数工作负载加速器评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量代数工作负载（如深度神经网络）在现代数据中心和边缘部署中能耗极高，需要加速器来提升能效与吞吐。为了快速评估和迭代加速器设计，需要一个能够同时刻画器件、电路、架构、工作负载以及工作负载到硬件映射优化等关键属性的建模框架。

Method: AccelForge将多项既有工作统一到一个框架中，包含三方面：(1) 可组合、用户可定义且可修改的器件、电路与架构模型；(2) 快速映射器，可在数量级更少的计算与人力时间内完成准确评估；(3) 易用、易扩展且高性能的Python实现的模型与映射器，便于快速研究和扩展至新型优化。

Result: AccelForge在能力、速度和易用性上均优于现有加速器建模框架。

Conclusion: AccelForge通过统一框架、可组合模型、快速映射器及高性能Python实现，为加速器设计提供了高效、准确且易扩展的评估手段。

Abstract: Tensor algebra workloads, of which deep neural networks are prominent examples, are energy-intensive workloads in modern datacenter and edge deployments, making accelerators necessary to achieve energy efficiency and high throughput. To quickly evaluate and iterate on accelerator designs, we need an accelerator modeling framework that captures salient attributes of devices, circuits, architectures, workloads, as well as optimizing the mapping of the workload onto the hardware.
  In this paper, we introduce AccelForge, which improves upon existing accelerator modeling frameworks in capabilities, speed, and ease-of-use. AccelForge unifies and multiple works into one framework, and it includes (1) composable user-defined and user-modifiable models of devices, circuits, and architectures, (2) fast mappers that enable accurate evaluation in orders of magnitude less (computer and human) time, and (3) easy-to-use and easy-to-extend, yet still high performance, Python implementations of both the model and mapper to enable rapid research and extension to novel optimizations.

</details>
